# Constitution of Zimbabwe (2013) — Xhosa translation

This is a translation of the original 2013 text of theConstitution of Zimbabwe. It does not include amendments made by Acts of Parliament in 2017 and 2021; the validity of those Acts is still the subject of litigation in the Constitutional Court.

Isilungiso Somgaqo-Siseko Wasezimbabwe (esesi, 20)

2013

ZIMBABWE

UMTHETHO Ukukurhoxiswa nokubambela uMgaqo-siseko waseZimbabwe. UQINGQWE nguMongameli nePalamente yaseZimbabwe. 1


Isihloko esifutshane

LoMthetho unokucatshulwa njengoMgaqo-siseko waseZimbabwe isilungiso

(20) uMthetho, ka 2013. 2

Utoliko KuloMthetho— “uMgaqo-Siseko okhoyo” uchaza uMgaqo-Siseko waseZimbabwe owaqala ukusebenza ngomhlaka 18 KaTshazimpunzi 1980, waze walandelwa sisilungiso; “uMgaqo-Siseko omtsha” kuxelwa uMgaqo-Siseko waseZimbabwe okwiShedyuli.



Ukukurhoxiswa koMgaqo-Siseko okhoyo nguMgaqo-Siseko omtsha

(1) Ngokwecandelwana (2) uMgaqo-Siseko okhoyo urhoxisiwewabanjelwa nguMgaqo-siseko omtsha. 15

(2) Ukuze kungabikho kuthandabuza loMthetho uthi— a.

uMgaqo-Siseko omtsha uyakuqingqwa “ngosuku lokupapashwa kwawo” njengoko kutshiwo kwiShedyuli yesi 6 yalaMgaqo-Siseko, okutsho ukuba, ngomhla okuyakuthi kupapashwe ngawo loMthetho kwiGazethi njengokutsho kwecandelo 51(5) woMgaqo-Siseko okhoyo; kwaye Printed by the Government Printer, Harare. 1

b.

2013

ukukuqala ngosuku lokuqingqwa kwawo uMgaqo-Siseko omtsha njengoko kutshiwo kumhlathi (a), uMgaqo-Siseko okhoyo usasebenza unamandla axelwe kwishedyuli yesi thandathu kuloMgaqo-Siseko omtsha.

## Section 59 — Amalungelo Asisiseko Nenkululeko yaBantu

Ilungelo lokuphila. Ilungelo lokukhululeka. Amalungelo wabantu ababanjiweyo kunye nabavalelweyo. Ilungelo lokuba nesidima somntu. Ilungelo lokhuseleko lomntu. Inkululeko yokungathuthukiswa nokukhohlakalelwa okungenabuntu nesohlwayo esithoba isidima. Inkululeko yokungakhobokiswa. Inkululeko yokunganyanzelwa ngomsebenzi. Ukulingana nokungacaluli. Ilungelo lobomi obubobakho Inkululeko yokuhlangana nokuzimanya. Ilungelo lokubonisa ukungeneliseki kunye noxwebhu lezikhalazo. 4

## Section 103

UMongameli. Amagunya nemisebenzi kaMongameli. Iimfuno zokunyulwa njengoMongameli kunye nesekela likaMongameli. Unyulo lukaMongameli kunye neSekela likaMongameli. Ukuphikisana nokunyulwa kukaMongameli. Ukukuqala umsebenzi kukaMongameli nooSekela-Mongameli. Ixesha elibekiweyo lokuba sesihlalweni kukaMongameli kunye nooSekelaMongameli. Ukushiya isikhundla sobuMongameli okanye uSekela-Mongameli. Ukususwa kukaMongameli okanye iSekela likaMongameli kwi-ofisi. Ukhuseleko lukaMongameli emthethweni. Imisebenzi yooSekela-Mongameli. IBambela likaMongameli. Ukungenwa kwesikhundla ngemva kokufa, ukushiya umsebenzi okanye ukoyisakala kokusebenza kukaMongameli okanye esikaSekela-Mongameli. Irholo likaMongameli kunye nooSekela-Mongameli. UMongameli kunye nooSekela Mongameli kwa nabo babeke babamba ezizikhundla abavumelekanga ukuba babe kwesinye isihlalo okanye benze omunye umsebenzi ebuRhulumenteni. INXALENYE 3 Abaphathiswa, Osekela - Phathiswa Nekhabhinethi

Iziqinisekiso zokuqeshwa kweejaji zeNkundla yoMgaqo. Iziqinisekiso zokuqeshwa kweejaji zeNkundla Ephezulu. Iziqinisekiso zokuqeshwa kweejaji zeNkundla Ephakamileyo, iNkundla yaBasebenzi, neNkundla Zolawulo. Ukuqeshwa kweejaji. Ukuqeshwa kwamabambela abagwebi. Ukuqeshwa koomantyi namanye amalungu ejaji. Amalungu abagwebi angenakuqeshelwa kwinkundla engaphezu kwenye. Ukuqeshwa kweejaji kufuneka kubonise uluntu. Isifungo sewofisi. Ixesha lengqesho yeejaji. Ukususwa kwejaji ewofiSini. Ukusetyenziswa kwenxalenye 3. Imigaqo emayisetyenziswe ekutolikeni uMgaqo-siseko. Iinkcazelo. Iimbekiselo zezahluko, amacandelo nokunye okunjalo. Amagama obunye aquka nobuninzi nawobuninzi achaza ubunye. Iitafile neezihloko. Iimbekiselo ngexesha. Iimbekiselo kwabasebenzela iiofisi. Iimbekiselo zePalamente. Ingcebiso nothethwano. Ukuqeshwa. Ukurhoxa. Ukusetyenziswa kwemisebenzi, nokunjalo. Umntu uthathwa njengomntu osebenzela iofisi kawonke-wonke xa kutheni. Ikhoramu nemisebenzi yezithuba kwiibhodi zoMgaqo-siseko. Ukungangqinelani kwemibhalo kuloMgaqo-siseko. ISHEDYULI YOKUKUQALA: Iflegi Yesizwe, uMhobe weSizwe, uPhawu lweSizwe. ISHEDYULI YESIBINI: Ukufakwa Kwemida Kumalungelo ngeXesha lika Xakeka kaWonke-wonke. ISHEDYULI YESITHATHU: Izifungo neziQinisekiso. ISHEDYULI YESINE: Iziqinisekiso zabaVoti. ISHEDYULI YESIHLANU: Inkqubo zeeBhili neminye imicimbi ePalamente. ISHEDYULI YESITHANDATHU: Ukukuqala kokusebenza kwaloMgaqosiseko.

INTSHAYELELO _________ Thina bantu base Zimbabwe, Simanyene ngaphezu kweyantlukwano yethu ngenjongo yokufuna inkululeko, ubulungisa nolingano kwanokulwisa ukulawulwa ngabanye abanye abantu, ikhethane ngokwebala lomntu, nezinye zonke iindlela zengcinezelo, Sizukisa nokuphakamisa lamadoda nababafazi abakhaliphileyo abancama ubomi babo ngexesha ledabi lenkululeko, Sihlonipha kwanabobantu bathe bazinikela kwinkqubela yeLizwe lethu, Siqaphela imfuno yokumisela idemokrasi, kwanolawulo loMthetho olufanelekileyo olunolingano olungenamkhethe nolawulo olulungileyo, Singqina ukuzubophelela ekuphumezeni nasekugcineni amalungelo abantu asiseko kwanenkululeko yabantu, Sivuma ubutyebi bomhlaba wethu, Sibhiyozela ubudlamka benkcubeko yethu, Sizimisele ukoyisa zonke izingeni ezibuysela inkqubela yethu emva, Sixabisa inkululeko, ulingano, ukuthula, impumelelo nokuzinikela ekufuneni imizamo enokusiphumeza, Sixabisa ubukhulu bukaThixo, onguye ophethe ikamva lethu, Sisebenzisa imibono yaloMgaqo-siseko ukwakha, iSizwe esimanyeneyo nesinokuthula esiphumeleleyo, esisekelwe phezu kolingano, inkululeko, ubulungisa kwanentlonelo yokusebenza ngamandla, Kwaye sicenga ukukhokhelwa nguThixo, ngoko ke senze loMgaqo-siseko saze sazinikela kuwo njengoMthetho osiseko kulomhlaba wethu siwuthandayo.

ISAHLUKO 1 IZIBONELELO EZINGUNDOQO 1

Iriphabliki IZimbabwe liLizwe elimanyeneyo elinobunganga nelinedemokhrasi .


Ubukhulu boMgaqo-siseko

(1) Lo Mgaqo-siseko nguwona Mthetho wongamileyo eZimbabwe, uMthetho amasiko okanye izenzo ezingangqinelani nawo azivumelekanga kwaphela, kwaye izimiselo ezimiselwe nguwo kufuneka zizalisekisiwe.

(2) Zonke izibopho ezixelwe kuloMgaqo-siseko zibandakanya wonke umntu, owenyama kunye nowenziwe nguMthetho, kuquka uRhulumente kunye neenkokheli zakhe, amaziko oMthetho kunye neearhente zikarhulumente kumazinga onke, kufuneka zizalisekise isibopho salo Mgaqo-siseko. 3

Izithethe zoluntu nemigaqo esisiseko

(1) iZimbabwe yakhelwe yaye iyazihlonela ezizithe nemigaqo ingundoqo eilandelayo—

(a)

ubukhulu boMgaqo-siseko;

(b) ukulawula koMthetho;

(c)

amalungelo asiseko kunye nenkululeko yoluntu;

(d) inkcubeko, izithethe nokunqula okwahlukeneyo;

(e)

ukubalulekisa isidima nesithunzi somntu wonke;

(f)

ukubalulekisa ukulingana komntu wonke;

(g) ukulingana ngokweSini;

(h) ululawulo oluhle; kunye

(i)

nokubalulekisa kwanokuhlonela idabi eleza nenkululeko.

(2) Imigaqo yolawulo oluhle ebophelela iSizwe nayo yonke imibutho karhulumente, ibalisela le:—

(a)

ukuvunyelwa kwamaqela ahlukeneyo ezepolitiki ukuba athathe inxaxheba kwezepolitiki;

(b) inkqubo yonyulo esekwe—

(i) ngumntu wonke omdala onelungelo elilinganayo lokuvota;

(ii) l unyulo olungenamkhethe, kwaye

(iii) nokumela ngokwaneleyo kwabavoti;

(c)

indlela enocwangco yokwedluliselwa kwamandla ngemva konyulo;

(d) ukuhlonipha amalungelo amaqela onke ezepolitiki;

(e)

ukugcina umgaqo wokwe hlukana kwamagunya;

(f)

ukuhlonipha abantu baseZimbabwe, kuba igunya lolawulo lithathelwa kubo;

(g) ukungafihli, ubulungisa, ukwamkela nokuphendula;

(h) ukukhuthaza umanyano, uxolo nokuzinza kubalulekiswa iyantlukwano, namasiko kunye nezithethe;

(i)

ukubalulekisa amalungelo—

(i) obuhlanga, enkcubeko yosiko, eelwimi nawokunqula

(ii) abantu abakhubazekileyo;

(iii) abantu basetyini, abakhulileyo, ulutsha nabantwana;

(iv) abalweli bedabi lenkululeko;

(j)

ukwabelana ngokulingana ubutyebi beLizwe kuquka umhlaba;

(k) ukuhlonitshwa kwamalungelo onke; kunye

(l) 4

ukunikelwa kwamandla olawulo nemisebenzi kaRhulumente kwiindawo zonke.

Iflegi yeSizwe, uMhobe weSizwe, iTywina likaWonke-wonke neMbasa yeSizwe

IZimbabwe ineflegi yobuzwe, mhobe weSizwe, ityinwa lika wonke wonke neMbasa yeSizwe, okuchazwe nje konke kwishedyuli yokuqala. 5

Amanqanaba kaRhulumente Amanqanaba ka Rhulumente ngala—

(a)

uRhulumente weLizwe;

(b)

amabhunga oombaxa nawamaphondo; kwa

(c)

noRhulumente wasekuhlaleni, kuquka—

(i) amagunya asekuhlaleni kwasezidolophini kungakhathalekile nokuba abizwa ngeliphi na igama emela kwaye elawula iinkqubo zabantu ezidolophini;

(ii) kwa namabhunga asemaphandleni, nokuba abizwa ngeliphi na igama, amela kwaye elawula iinkqubo zabantu basemaphandleni ngaphakathi kwamaphondo ahluliweyo.


Iilwimi

(1) Iilwimi ezisemthethweni kweliLizwe lase Zimbabwe zezi zilandelayo Chewa, ChiBarwe, English, Kalanga, Koisan, Nambya, Ndau, Ndebele, Shangani, Shona, Sign Language, Sotho, Tonga, Tswana, Venda nesi Xhosa.

(2) UMthetho wasePalamente unako ukuxela ezinye iilwimi njengeenlimi ezamkelekileyo eburhulumenteni beliLizwe.

(3) Urhulumente kunyenawo onke amaziko namasebe akhe nabameli bakarhulumente bamelwe kukwenjenje—

(a)

ukuqinisekisa ukuba zonke iilwimi ezisemthethweni ziphathwa ngokufanayo; kwaye

(b) aqwalasele ukukhethwa kolwimi kubantu abachaphazelekayo ngamanyathelo karhulumente okanye kwiintetho zonxibelelwano.

(4) Urhulumente kufuneka akhuthaze kwaye aqhube ukuphuhliswa nokusetyenziswa kwazo zonke iilwimi zaseZimbabwe ezisemthethweni, kuquka nolwimi lwentetho yezandla, kwaye adale iimeko zokuphuhlisa 7

Ukukhuthazwa koluntu ngokuqonda uMgaqo-siseko

Urhulumente kufuneka aphakamise ulwazi lomphakathi ngalo Mgaqo-siseko, ngakumbi ngo—

(a)

kuwuguqulelela kwiilwimi ezisemthethweni zonke zeliLizwe nokuwusasaza ngokubanzi kangangoko kunokwenzeka;

(b) ngokufuna ukuba loMgaqo-siseko ufundiswe ezikolweni kwaye ubeyinxalenye yemigaqo yokuqeqesha amalungu eenkonzo zokhuseleko, namalungu enkonzo yoluntu kwanamalungu amaziko kawonke-wonke; kwaye

(c)

akhuthaze bonke abantu kunye nemibutho kuquka imibutho yoluntu ukuba ibhengeze ulwazi lwaloMgaqo-siseko kuso sonke iSizwe. ISAHLUKO 2


Iinjongo Zesizwe Injongo yokuqhuba iSizwe kwanamaziko onke kwaneearhente ziphela zikaRhulumente

(1) Injongo exelwe kwesisaHluko iqhuba iSizwe kunye nawo onke amasebe neearhente zikarhulumente kuwo onke amazinga ekuqulunqeni nasekuphunyezweni kwemithetho kunye nezigqibo zomgaqo nkqubo ezizakubakhokelela ekumisweni, ekuphuculweni nasekukhuthazweni kokuzinzileyo, ngokukhawuleza, ngokukhululeka kunye noluntu lwentando yeSinintsi oko okuzakwenza nabantu banambithe ubomi obucebileyo nobucwayithileyo.

(2) Kufuneka kuhoywe inongo echazwe kwesi sahluko xa kuguqulelwa izibopho zombuso phantsi kwalo Mgaqo-siseko kunye nawuphi na omnye uMthetho. 9

Ululawulo oluhle

(1) Urhulumente kufuneka amkele kwaye aphumeze imigaqo nkqubo kunye noMthetho ukuphucula ukusebenza kakuhle, ubuchule, ukuphendula, ukusabalalisa, ukutheka komntu kunye neengxaki zemali kuwo onke amaziko kunye nee arhente zikarhulumente kuwo onke amanqanaba nakuwo onke amaziko karhulumente, kwaye ngakumbi —

(a)

ukuqeshwa kwabaqeshwa kwiiofisi zaseburhulumenteni kufuneka kwenziwe ngokusisiseko sokufanelekileyo ngokwalomsebenzi

(b) amanyathelo afanelekileyo kufuneka athathwe ukuveza, ukulwa nokutshabalalisa zonke iindidi zosetyenziso lorhwaphilizo ngabo baphethe iiofisi zezepolitiki kunye nezikarhulumente.

(2) Urhulumente kufuneka aqinisekise ukuba onke amaziko kunye neearhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba, ngokukodwa iiKhomishini kunye namanye amabhunga asekwe phantsi okanye phantsi kwalo Mgaqo-siseko anikezelwa ngezibonelelo zemali nezinye izibonelelo ezifanelekileyo ukuze akwazi ukuqhuba izicwangciso zawo ngokunyanisekileyo, ngokufanelekileyo nangokuqinisekileyo.

2013

Umanyano lweSizwe, uxolo nokuzinza

Urhulumente kunye naye wonke umntu kubandakanya nabantu bomthetho kunye nawo onke amaziko nee-arhente zikarhulumente kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka bakhuthaze umanyano eLizweni, uxolo nokuzinza. 11

Ukukhuthaza amalungelo asisigxina kunye nenkululeko

Urhulumente kufuneka athathe onke amanyathelo asebenzayo ekukhuseleni amalungelo kunye nenkululeko enemimiselo ebhalwe kwiSahluko 4 kunye nokukhuthaza ukuzalisekiswa kwawo ngokupheleleyo. 12

Inkqubo ejongene nemicimbi yangaphandle

(1) Imicimbi yeLizwe elingaphandle kwe Zimbabwe kufuneka usekelwe kwimigaqo elandelayo—

(a)

ukuphakanyiswa nokukhuselwa kweenjongo zeSizwe sase Zimbabwe;

(b) ukuhlonipha iimithetho yehlabathi;

(c) ukuphilisana ngoxolo namanye amazwe; kunye

(d) nokusombulula ingxaki zamanye amazwe ngoxolo.

(2) URhulumente kufuneka akhuthaze ukuphucula intsebenziswano kwiinkcubeko, uqoqoqsho nezepolitiki eAfrika kwaye kufuneka athathe inxaxheba kwimibutho yehlabathi neyase Afrika ezakube imele ukuphakamisa uxolo nenkqubela 13

UPhuhliso lweSizwe

(1) Urhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka athathe imigangatho efanelekileyo ukuphuhlisa ngokukhawulezileyo nangokulinganayo kwaye kufuneka athathe amanyathelo —

(a)

akukhuthaza ukuzenzela nokuzethemba;

(b) aphuhlisa ukulima, ushishino, iindastri, thekinoloji nophuhliso lwesayensi;

(c)

akukhuthaza ukuphuhliswa kwamashishini kunye nezorhwebo ukwenzela ukuxhobisa abemi base Zimbabwe; kunye

(d)

nokuzisa uphuhliso olulinganisiweyo lweenginqi ezahlukeneyo zaseZimbabwe, ingakumbi ukuphuhlisa ngokulinganisiweyo kwimimandla yasezidolophini naseMaphandleni

(2) Amanyathelo okubhekiswe kuwo kwelicandelo kufuneka abandakanye abantu ekuqulunqweni nasekuphunyezweni kwezicwangciso zophuhliso kunye neenkqubo ezibachaphazelayo

(3) Amanyathelo okubhekiswe kuwo kwelicandelo kufuneka akhusele kwaye akhuthaze ukunyanzekiswa kwamalungelo oluntu, ingakumbi abantu ababhinqileyo , ukuba nabo bafumane izithuba ezilingana notate kwimiba yophuhliso.

(4) Urhulumente kufuneka aqinisekise ukuba uluntu luyancedakala kwimveli nakwizibonelelo zikumimandla bakuyo. 14

Ukuxhotyiswa kunye nokudalwa kweimisebenzi

(1) Urhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko aseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka aphephe ukulungiselela kwaye athathe amanyathelo okuxhobisa

ngokufanelekileyo, ngokucacileyo, ngokulungileyo kunye nokulungelelanisa nje, onke amaqela ahlukeneyo noluntu lonke lwase Zimbabwe.

(2) Ngamaxesha onke uRhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo okudala imisebenzi kubemi base Zimbabwe, ingakumbi abantu ababhinqileyo nolutsha. 15

Ukhuselo lokutya Urhulumente kufuneka —


(a)

akhuthaze abantu ukuba balime kwaye bagcine ukutya okwaneleyo;

(b)

ukukhusela indawo zokugcinela ukutya; kunye

(c)

nokukhuthaza nokuphakanyiswa kokutya okunesondlo, oko kusenziwa ngokufundisa uluntu mayelana noko.

Inkcubeko

(1) URhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka akhuthaze aze alondoloze ixabiso lenkcubeko yosiko kunye nezenzo ezikhuthaza isithunzi, impilo kunye nokulinganakubo bonke abemi base Zimbabwe.

(2) URhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka azame ukugcina nokukhusela ilifa le Zimbabwe.

(3) URhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka aqinisekise ukuhlonelwa kwesidima samaziko emveli. 17

Ukulingana ngokweSini

(1) URhulumente kufuneka akhuthaze ulingano ngokweSini kwiSizwe sonke sase Zimbabwe, ingakumbi—

(a)

uRhulumente kufuneke akhuthaze ukuthatha inxaxheba ngokupheleleyo kwabo bangabafazi kuzo zonke iindawo zase Zimbabwe nokumalunga nokulingana namadoda

(b) URhulumente kufuneka athathe onke amanyathelo, kuquka amanyathelo oMthetho ukuqinisekisa ukuba—

(i) umntu nokokuba ngowasiphi iSini uyamelwa ngokulinganayo kuwo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zikarhulumente kuwo onke amanqanaba; kwaye

(ii) abantu ababhinqileyo bakha isigqeba sobulungu bazo zonke iiKhomishini kunye namanye amabhunga karhulumente akhethiweyo nanyuliweyo okanye aphantsi kwalo Mgaqo-siseko okanye nawuphi na uMthetho wePalamente; kwaye

(c)

uRhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo ukuqinisekisa ukuba abafazi banokufikelela kwizibonelelo zemveli, kuquka umhlaba, ngokulinga namadoda.

(2) URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo alungileyo ekulungiseni ukucalulwa kweSini kunye nokungalingani okwabangelwa yimigaqo nemigaqo-nkqubo yangaphambilini.

1 8 Ukumelwa kommandla ngokulinganayo

(1) URhulumente kufuneka akhuthaze ukumelwa ngokulingana kommandla wonke waseZimbabwe kuwo onke amaziko nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba.

(2) Urhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kunye noorhulumente kuwo onke amacandelo kufuneka athathe amanyathelo okusebenza ukuqinisekisa ukuba zonke iindawo zenginqi zifumana ukufikelela ngokulinganayo kwizixhobo zokukhuthaza ukuphuhliswa kwazo. 19

Abantwana

(1) Urhulumente kufuneka amkele imigaqo –nkqubo kunye nemilinganiselo ukuqinisekisa ukuba kwimicimbi enxulumene nabantwana , iimfuno ezifanelekileyo zabantwana zibaluleke kakhulu.

(2) Urhulumente kufuneka amkele imigaqo-nkqubo kunye nemilinganiselo efanelekileyo kusetyenziswa, izibonelelo ezikhoyo, ukuqinisekisa ukuba abantwana —

(a)

babonwabele ubulungu bosapho kwanenkathalo yabazali babo, okanye inkathalo efanelekileyo xa bethe basuswa kwimeko yosapho;

(b) banendawo yokuhlala nokutya okunesondlo, bayafikelela kwinkonzo zeempilontle nezentlalo;

(c) bayakhuselwa ekungaphathweni kakuhle, ukunganakwa kunye nayiphi na intlobo yoxhatshazo; kwaye

(d) anokufikelela kwimfundo noqeqesho ulufanelekileyo.

(3) uRhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo oMthetho kunye namanye amanyathelo —

(a)

ukukhusela abantwana ukuba bangasebenzi besebancinci; kwaye

(b) ukuqinisekisa ukuba abantwana abavunyela ukusebenza —

(i) imisebenzi engalinganiselwanga iminyaka yabo; okanye

(ii) imisebenzi ebeka ubomi babo engozini, exhaphaza ilungelo labo lokufunda, elokukhula kakuhle ngokwesimo senyama, umoya, ingqondo nokuphuhla ngokwentlalo. 20

Ulutsha

(1) Urhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo, kuquka iinkqubo zokwenza amanyathelo, ukuqinisekisa ukuba ulutsha, oku kuthetha abantu abaneminyaka ephakathi kweshumi elinantlanu ukuya kumashumi amathathu anesihlanu —

(a) bayadikelela kwimfundo noqeqesho olufanelekileyo;

(b) banamathuba okudibana kunye nokumelwa kwaye bathathe inxaxheba kwezepolitiki, ezentlalo, ezoqoqosho kunye nezinye iinkalo zobomi;

(c)

bafumana amathuba okuqeshwa kunye nezinye iindlela zokuzixhobisa;

(d) banethuba lokuzonwabisa kunye nokufikelela kwiindawo zokuzonwabisa;

(e)

bakhuselwe kwiinkqubo zenkcubeko yosiko , ukuxhatshazwa kunye nazo zonke iindlela zokusetyenziswa kakubi.

(2) UMthetho wasePalamente usenokuvela nenkqubo engabe inye nangaphezulu ezolutsha.

(3) Amanyathelo kunye neenkqubo ezixelwe kwicandelwana 1 kunye no 2 kufuneka zibandakanye, engabandakanyekanga kwaye zibe nobuzwe. 21

Abantu abadala

(1) Urhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo, kuquka amanyathelo oMthetho, ukukhuselwa, ukuxhasa nokukhusela abantu abadala kunye nokubenza bakwazi ukuthatha inxaxheba ebomini babo.

(2) Urhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuzo zonke iinjongo kufuneka aziphele ukwenza oku kulandelayo kusetyenziswa izibonelelozemveli ezikhoyo —

(a)

ukukhuthaza abantu abadala ukuba bathathe inxaxheba kwizinto ezenzeka ekuhlaleni;

(b) ukubonelela abantu abadala abasweleyo, ngeendawo, ukutya nentlalo yoluntu;

(c)

ukuphuhlisa iinkqubo zokunika abantu abadala ithuba lokuba bahlanganyele emsebenzini ovelisayo ngokwamakhono abo kunye neminqweno yabo; kwaye;

(d) ukukhuthaza imibutho yentlalo ejoliswe ekuphuculeni umgangatho wobomi babantu abadala. 22

Abantu abakhubazekileyo

(1) URhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka aqonde amalungelo abantu abanokukhubazeka ngokomzimba okanye ngokwengqondo, ngokukodwa ilungelo labo lokuphathwa ngokuhlonipha nesithunzi.

(2) URhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka ngokwezibonelelo ezikhoyo, bancedise abantu abakhubazeke ngokwenyama okanye ngokwengqondo ukuba bakwazi ukufezekisa ngokupheleleyo iminqweno yabo kunye nokunciphisa ubunzima ababuvayo.

(3) Ngokukodwa, uRhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka—

(a)

aphuhlise iinkqubo zentlalontle yabantu abakhubazekileyo ngokwenyama okanye ngokwengqondo, ngokukodwa iinkqubo ezihambelana nezikhundla zabo kwaye zisamkeleka kubo okanye kubameli babo kwezoMthetho;

(b) akuqwalasele iimfuno ezithile zabantu abaneendlela zonke zokukhubazeka njengenye yezinto ezibalulekileyo kwisicwangciso sophuhliso;

(c)

ukukhuthaza ukusetyenziswa kunye nophuhliso lweendlela zokunxibelelana ezifanelekileyo kubantu abakhubazekileyo ngokwenyama nangokwe ngqondo; kwaye

(d) kukhuthazwe imibutho yentlalo ejolise ekuphuculeni umgangatho wobomi babantu abanentlobo zonke zokukhubazeka.

(4) URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo ukuqinisekisa ukuba izakhiwo kunye nezibonelelo ezibhekiselele kuluntu ziyafikeleleka kubantu abanokukhubazeka. 23 Amaqhawe edabi lenkululeko

(1) URhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka amkele intlonelo, inhlonipho nesidima esiphiwa aba bathathinxaxheba kwidabi lenkululeko, oko kubalisela abo balwayo kwidabi lenkululeko; (a ) abo ababanjwayo kumfazo yenkululeko

(b) bancedisa abalwi ngexesha lemfazwe yenkululeko; kwa

(c)

nabo baye babanjwa bayokuvalelwa entilongweni bebanjelwa ukuba bethatha inxaxheba ekukhululeni iLizwe.

(2) URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo kubandakanywa amanyathelo oMthetho ukuphuhlisa intlontle nokuxhotyiswa ngokwezoqoqosho kwabo balwela inkululeko yeSizwe. 24

Umsebenzi nobudlelwano emsebenzini

(1) URhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka athathe imigaqo-nkqubo efanelekileyo kunye nemilinganiiselo ngezibonelelo ezikhoyo, ukupha wonke umntu ithuba lokusebenza emsebenzini azikhethele wona , ukuzilungiselela ukufumana ubomi obufanelekileyo kunye nosapho lwakhe.

(2) URhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka aqhubeke ngo—

(a)

kupha umsebenzi osisigxina;

(b) kususwa kwezithintelo ezingabalulekanga okanye ezithintela abantu ukuba bangasebenzi kakuhle kunye nokubandakanyeka kwimisebenzi yezoqoqosho

(c)

kupha izikhokelo zentlalo kunye nophuhliso lweenkqubo uzoqeqesho nezokuqeqesha, kubandakanya kunye nabo bantu bakhubazekileyo;

(d) ukuphunyezwa kwamanyathelo afana nokuhoywa kweentsapho khona ukuze abafazi bafumane ithuba lokonwabela ukuba semsebenzini. 25

Ukhuseleko lwentsapho

URhulumente kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhrnte zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka akhusele kwaye akhuthaze ubukho bentsapho kwaye ngokukodwa aphucule kusetyenziswa izibonelelo ezikhoyo kuzo, ukuba kwamkelwe amanyathelo—

(a)

ukubonelelwa ngononophelo nokuncediswa koomama, ootata namanye amalungu entsapho ajongene nabantwana; kwaye

(b) ukuthintela ubundlobongela bekhaya. 2 6 Umtshato URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo ukuqinisekisa ukuba—

(a) akukho mntu unokungena emtshatweni ngaphandle kwemvume ekhululekileyo kunye nepheleleyo yeenjonjo zokutshata;

(b) abantwana abanyanzelwa ukungena emitshatweni besebancinci;

(c)

kukho ukulingana kwamalungelo kunye neembophelelo zabatshatileyo ngexesha lomtshato kunye nasekuchithweni kwawo; kwaye

(d) xa kunokwenzeka umtshato uchithwe, nokuba ngaba kungokufa okanye umtshato uchitheke ngokwehlukana, kunikezelwa ukulungiselela ukhuseleko olufanekayo lwabantwana nabaphina kunye nabatshatileyo. 27

Imfundo

(1) URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo ukuqinisekisa ukuba—

(a)

wonke umntwana uyayifumana imfundo esisiseko; kwaye

(b) wonke ubani uyafikelela kwimfundo ephakamileyo neyemisebenzi .

(2) URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo okuqinisekisa ukuba amantombazana afunyaniswa amathuba afanayo namakhwenkwe awokuba bafumane imfundo kuwo onke amanqanaba. 28

Indawo yokuhlala

URhulumenete kunye nawo onke amaziko kunye nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba kufuneka athathe amanyathelo oMthetho kunye namanye amanyathelo, kusetyenziswa izibonelelo ezikhoyo kubo ukwenzela ukuba wonke umntu abe nokufikelela kwindawo yokuhlala enesizotha. 29

Iinkonzo zezempilo

(1) URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo okuqinisekisa ukuba ubonelela abantu ngeenkonzo ezisemgangathweni, ezifumanekayo nezaneleyo zezempilo kulo lonke elase Zimbabwe.

(2) URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo, alungileyo namkelelkileyo awokuqinisekisa ukuba nabani na uyalufumana unyango olungxamisekileyo noba kukweliphi na iziko lezempilo.

(3) URhulumente kufuneka athathe onke amanyathelo okukhusela kusetyenziswa izibonelelo ezikhoyo, kubandakanywa imfundo kunye nokwaziswa koluntu ngokusasazeka kwezifo. 30

Intlalo ntle

URhulumente kufuneka athathe onke amanyathelo asebenzayo, kusetyenziswa izibonelelo ezikhoyo, ukubonelela ngononophelo uluntu nenkathalo kubantu abanesidingo. 31

Uncedo lwezoMthetho

URhulumente kufuneka athathe onke amanyathelo asebenzayo, kusetyenziswa izibonelelo ezikhoyo, ukubonelelo ngokokumelwa ngokoMthetho kwabo baxoxiswa amatyala ezinkundleni zamatyala ,befuna abameli kodwa bengenelisi ukuzifunela abameli bezoMthetho abazikhethela bona ngokunokwabo.

2013

Izakhiwo zemidlalo kunye nezokuzonwabisa

Urhulumente kufuneka athathe onke amanyathelo okukhuthaza ezemidlalo nokuzonwabisa, kubandakanywa nokubonelelwa ngezakhiwo ezifanelekileyo zemidlalo nokuzonwabisa kubantu bonke. 33

Ukugcinwa kolwazi lwemveli

URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo okugcina, ukukhusela nokukhuthaza iinkqubo ezingenasiphelo, kuquka nolwazi lwamachiza kunye nezinye iimpawu zezityalo nezilwanyana ezilawulwa luluntu nenginqi zasekuhlaleni. 34

Ukuhoywa kweemithetho nezigqibo zamazwe ngamazwe

URhulumente kufuneka aqiniseke ukuba zonke iindibano, izivumelwano nezibopho zamazwe ngamazwe ezibandakanya iZimbabwe zibayimithetho yasekhaya. ISAHLUKO 3 35

Ubumi baseZimbabwe

Ubumi

(1) Abantu ngabemi beSizwe sase Zimbabwe ngokuzalwa, inzala okanye ngokubhalisa.

(2) Bonke abemi base Zimbabwe banelungelo elifanelekileyo lokufumana amalungelo nokuxhamla ukuba ngabemi kwaye baxhomekeka ngokufanelekileyo ekusebenziseni nasekuzibopheleleni kobumi.

(3) Bonke abemi base Zimbabwe banamalungelo nenzuzo ezilandelayo, ngaphezu kweyiphi na enikezwe kubo —

(a)

ukukhuselwa ngurhulumente nokokuba baphi na;

(b) ukuba nepaspoti kunye namanye amaxhwebhu okuhamba; kwaye

(c)

ukuba nesiqinesekiso sokuzalwa kunye nezinye iinkcukacha zokuzazisa ezikhutshwa ngurhulumente.

(4) Abemi base Zimbabwe banemisebenzi elandelayo, ngaphezu kweyiphi na abayinikwe nguMthetho—

(a)

ukunyaniseka nokwethembeka eLizweni lase Zimbabwe;

(b) ukugcina loMgaqo-siseko nokuhlonela iinjongo kunye namaziko awo;

(c)

ukuhlonela iflegi yeSizwe kunye nomhobe weSizwe; kwa

(d) kangangoko benokwazi ukukhusela iZimbabwe kunye nolongamo lwayo. 36

Ubumi ngomnombo

(1) Abantu abangabemi base Zimbabwe ngabantu abazalelwe e Zimbabwe kwaye bezalwa —

(a)

ingaba ngunina okanye uyise ongummi wase Zimbabwe okanye

(b) nawuphina wootatatomkhulu babo xa wayengummi waseZimbabwe ngokuzalwa okanye ngenzalo

(2) Abantu abangaphandle kweLizwe lase Zimbabwe babangabemi base Zimbabwe xa bezalwa ngabazali okanye omnye wabazali ebengummi wase Zimbabwe ngokuzalwa okanye.—

(a)

ebengumhlali wase Zimbabwe; okanye

(b)

ebesebenzela ngaphandle kweLizwe lase Zimbabwe esebenzela uRhulumente okanye imibutho yamazwe ngamazwe.

(3) Umntwana ofumaneka eZimbabwe oneminyaka okanye obonakala eneminyaka engaphantsi kweminyaka elishumi elinantlanu ubudala kwaye ubuzwe okanye abazali bakhe bengaziwa, uba ngummi wase Zimbabwe ngokuzalwa. 37

Ubumi ngenzalo

Kuxhomekeka kwicandelo 36(2), abantu abazalelwe phandle kweLizwe lase Zimbabwe ngabemi base Zimbabwe ngenzalo, xa bezalwa—

(a)

ngomnye wabazali okanye omnye wootatomkhulu babo ongummi wase Zimbabwe ngokuzalwa okanye ngenzalo, okanye

(b) omnye wabazali ebengummi wase Zimbabwe ngokubhalisa; kwaye ukuzalwa kubhalisiwe eZimbabwe ngokuhambelana noMthetho ophathelene nokubhaliswa kokuzalwa. 38

Ubumi ngokubhalisa

(1) Nawuphi na umntu otshate nommi waseZimbabwe iminyaka engaba mihlanu, nokuba ngaba ngaphambi okanye ngemva komhla wokuqalisa kwaloMthetho, kwaye esenelisa imiqathango ebekwe nguMthetho wasePalamente, unelungelo, ngokwesicelo sokubhalisa ukuba abe ngummi waseZimbabwe

(2) Nawuphi na umntu obeqhubeka ehleli kweli laseZimbabwe ngokusemthethweni okweminyaka engaba lishumi, nokuba ngaba ngaphambi okanye ngemva komhla wokuqalisa kwaloMthetho, kwaye eseneza imiqathango ebekwe nguMthetho wasePalamente, unelungelo ngokwesicelo sokubhalisa ukuba abe ngummi waseZimbabwe.

(3) Umntwana ongengommi waseZimbabwe kodwa amkelwe njengomntwana wabemi baseZimbabwe, nokuba ngaphambi okanye ngemva komhla wokuqalisa kwaloMthetho, unelungelo ngokwesicelo sokubhaliswa ukuba abe ngummi waseZimbabwe. 39

Ukurhoxiswa kobummi

(1) Ukuba ngummi waseZimbabwe ngokubhalisa kungaba nako ukurhoxiswa

xa—

(a)

umntu ochaphazelekayo efunyaniswa ukuba wafumana ubummi ngobuqhetseba, esebenzisa ubuxoki okanye ukufihlwa kwezinto eziphathekayo; okanye

(b) ukuba ngaba ngexesha lemfazwe yaseZimbabwe lomntu uchaphazelekayo wathengisela okanye wasebenzisana notshaba olu lwalulwiswa okanye wazibandakanya kushishino oluhambisana notshaba ngendlela yokuncedisana notshaba olo ukulwisa iSizwe.

(2) Ukuba ngummi waseZimbabwe ngokuzalwa kungaba nako ukukurhoxiswa xa—

(a)

ubummi obo bafunyanwa ngobuqhetseba, kusetyenziswa ubuxoki okanye ukufihlwa kwezinto eziphathekayo; okanye

(b) kwimeko yomntu okubhekiswe kuyo kwicandelo 36(3), ukuba ubuzwe okanye umzali womntu lowo buye baziwa, kwaye kubonisa ukuba umntu wayengummi weLizwe elithile.

(3) Ubumi bobuzwe baseZimbabwe abufanelekanga burhoxiswe phantsi kwelicandelo ukuba umntu lowo uyakushiyeka engenabo ubummi emva koko. 40

Ukugcinwa kobummi nangona kukho umtshato okanye umtshato sowuchithekile

Ubummi baseZimbabwe abulahleki ngenxeni yomtshato okanye ngokuchitheka kwawo umtshato. 41 Ubummi nebhodi yokufuduka UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka unikezele ukusekwa kweBhodi yoBumi neMfuduko, okubalisela usihlalo wayo namanye amalungu amabini atyunjwa ngumongameli, ukuba enze lomsebenzi—


(a)

ukunika nokwaxutha ubumi ngokubhalisa;

(b)

ukuvumela abantu, ngaphandle kwabemi, ukuba bahlale basebenze e Zimbabwe, kwaye balungise imimiselo neemeko abazakuhlala kwaye basebenze ngaphantsi kwazo; kwaye

(c)

ukusebenza nayiphi na imisebenzi enokunikezwa okanye ukumiselwa yiBhodi okanye phantsi koMthetho wasePalamente.

Amandla ePalamente malunga nobumi

UMthetho wasePalamente unokwenza isibonelelo, ngokuhambelana nesahluko, ukwenza oku—


(a)

iinkqubo zokuncedisa abo bangabemi base Zimbabwe ngokubhalisa;

(b)

ukukurhoxiswa ngokuzithandela kobummi baseZimbabwe;

(c)

iindlela zokurhixiswa kobummi baseZimbabwe ngokubhalisa;

(d)

ukubuyiselwa kobummi base Zimbabwe;

(e)

ukuthintela ubumi obubini ngokubhekiselele kwabo bangabemi ngokwenzala okanye ngokubhalisa; kwaye

(f)

ukuchaza ngokubanzi ukusebenza kwesisahluko.

Ukuqhubekeka nokubuyiselwa kobummi bangaphambili

(1) Wonke umntu, ngokukhawulezileyo ngaphambi komhla wokupapashwa ubengummi waseZimbabwe, uyaqhubeka engummi waseZimbabwe nasemva kwaloo mhla.

(2) Wonke umntu ozalelwe eZimbabwe ngaphambi komhla wokupapashwa, ungummi waseZimbabwe ngokuzalwa xa—

(a)

omnye umzali okanye bobabini bengabemi beLizwe elaba lilungu lombutho wophuhliso lwamazwe asezantsi Afrika owe Southern African Development Community owaqulungqwa ngomqophiso awasayinelwa eWindhoek kwiRephabliki yaseNamibia ngomhla we 19 Agasti 1992, kwaye.

(b) ubengumhlali waseZimbabwe ngomhla wokupapashwa.

ISAHLUKO 4 Isibhengezo Samalungelo ISAHLUKWANA 1 Ukusetyenziswa Nokutolikwa Kwesahluko 4 44

Uxanduva lokuhlonela amalungelo asisigxina kunye neenkululeko zabantu

URhulumente kunye naye wonke umntu, kuquka naboMthetho, kunye nawo onke amaziko nee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kumanqanaba onke kufuneka ahloniphe, akusele, akhuthaze kwaye azalisekise amalungelo kunye nenkululeko achazwe kwesisahluko. 45

Inkcazelo yesaSahluko 4

(1) Esi Sahluko sibophelela uRhulumente kunye nazo zonke izigqeba zolawulo, amaziko oMthetho kunye namagqwetha kwanee-arhente zaseburhulumenteni kuwo onke amanqanaba.

(2) Esi Sahluko sibophelela abantu nje bonke kunye naboMthetho ngendlela esisebenza ngayo kubo, kuqwalaselwa uhlobo lwelungelo okanye inkululeko echaphazelekayo kwaye nawuphi na umsebenzi owenziwe yiyo

(3) Abantu boMthetho kunye nabantu nje bonke banelungelo lokufumana amalungelo kunye nenkululeko ezichazwe kwesisahluko kusiya ngokwendlela ilungelo okanye inkululeko elibaluleke ngayo kubo. 46

Ukuchazwa kweSahluko 4

(1) Xa kuchazwa esisahluko, iinkundla zokuxoxiswa kwamatyala neemibutho yabameli kuzakufuneka zenzenje—

(a)

zihoye ngokupheleleyo amalungelo nenkululeko ezichazwe kwesisahluko;

(b) zikhuthaze ixabiso kunye nemigaqo ebeka uluntu lwentando yeSininzi olususela ekuhleni, ubulungisa, isithunzi somntu, ukulingana nenkululeko, ngokukodwa imilinganiselo kunye nemigomo echazwe kwicandelo 3;

(c)

ziqwalasele uMthetho wamazwe ngamazwe kunye nazo zonke iintetho kunye neendibano apho iZimbabwe ithatha inxaxheba khona;

(d) kufuneka zithobele ngokubhekiselele kuwo onke amalungiselelo alo Mgaqo-siseko, ngokukodwa imigaqo kunye neenjongo ezichazwe kwiSahluko 2; kwaye

(e)

zisenokuqwalasela uMthetho wangaphandle;

ukongezelela ukuqwalasela zonke ezinye izinto ezifanelekileyo ezizakuthathwa ingqalela ekuchazeni loMgaqo-siseko.

(2) Xa kutolikwa umyalelo, kwaye xa kuphakanyiswa uMthetho oqhelekileyo kunye noMthetho wesintu, zonke iinkundla neebhodi zokuzozwa kwamatyala kufuneka zikhuthazwe kwaye zikhokelwe ngumoya kunye neenjongo zesiSahluko.

2013

Isahluko 4 asithinteli ekubeni amanye amalungelo angahoywa

Esi Sahluko asikuthinteli ubukho bamanye amalungelo kunye nenkululeko enokuthi ibonwe okanye inikezwe nguMthetho, ukuba ziyahambisana nalo Mgaqosiseko. ISAHLUKWANA 2 Amalungelo Oluntu Asiseko Kunye Neenkululeko 48

I Lungelo lokuphila

(1) Wonke umntu unelungelo lokuphila.

(2) UMthetho unokuvumela ukuba isigwebo sentambo sibekwe kuphela kumntu onetyala lokubulala esebenzisa imeko ezimbi; kwaye—

(a)

uMthetho kufuneka uvumele inkundla yamatyala ukuba ithathe isigqibo sokuba ingasothula na isigwebo sentambo okanye ayinakusothula;

(b) isohlwayo singenziwa kuphela ngokuhambelana nesigwebo sokugqibela senkundla efanelekileyo;

(c)

isohlwayo asifanelanga ukuba sibekwe kumntu—

(i) obeneminyaka engaphantsi kwamashumi amabini nanye yobudala ngexesha ebesenza ityala;

(ii) oneminyaka engaphezu kwamashumi asixhenxe yobudala;

(d) isigwebo sentambo akufuneki sohlwaywe umntu ongumfazi; kwaye

(e)

umtu ogwetywayo kufuneka avunyelwe ilungelo lokufuna ukuxolelwa okanye ukuba isigwebo sakhe singabi sesentambo kuMongameli.

(3) UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka ukhusele ubomi babantwana abangekazalwa, kwaye looMthetho kufuneka uvumele ukuba isisu singachithwa kuphela ngokuhambelana naloMthetho. 49

Ilungelo lenkululeko yobuqu

(1) Wonke umntu unelungelo lenkululeko yomntu isiqu sakhe, elibandakanye ilungelo—

(a)

lokungabanjwa avalelwa enganikwanga ithuba lokuba avele enkundleni yamatyala; kwaye

(b) lokungangcungcuthekiswa nangaluphi na uhlobo.

(2) Akukho mntu onokuvalelwa entilongweni nje ngenxa yokoyiseka ukuzalisa isibophelelo. 50

Amalungelo abantu ababanjiweyo kunye nabavalelweyo

(1) Wonke umntu obanjiweyo—

(a)

aziswe ngexesha lokubanjwa isizathu sokubanjwa kwakhe;

(b) kufuneka, ngokungxamisekileyo—

(i) ngeendleko zeLizwe, ukuqhakamshelana nomlingane okanye iqabane, umntakwabo okanye ummeli wezoMthetho okanye nawuphina umntu akhetha ukumazisa ngokubanjwa kwakhe; kwaye

(ii) ngeendleko zakhe, ukubonisana ngasese nommeli wezoMthetho kunye nogqirha azikhethele yena kwaye kufuneka aziswe kwamsinyane ngalo eli lungelo;

(c)

lokuphathwa kakuhle angohlwaywa ngendlela ekhohlakeleyo, engenabuntu okanye ethoba isidima somntu;

(d)

kufuneka akhululwe kungekho miqobo ethile ngelixesha elinde isohlwayo okanye ukuxoxiswa kwetyala, okanye kubekho izizathu ezinyanzelisayo ukuba aqhubeke evalelwe; kwaye;

(e)

kufuneka avunyelwe ukufaka umgeni kuMthetho wokubanjwa komntu phambi kwenkundla kwaye kufuneka ngokukhawuleza akhululwe ukuba ukubanjwa kwakhe kungekho mthethweni.

(2) Wonke umntu obanjiweyo okanye ovalelweyo—

(a)

ebanjelwe okanye evalelelwe ukuba ayovela kwinkundla yamatyala; okanye

(b)

ebekwa isityolo;

kwaye ongekavulelwa kufuneka eze enkundleni masinyane kungekagqithi iiyure ezingamashumi amane anesibhozo ebanjiwe okanye evalelawe emva kokubanjwa okanye ukuvalelwa kwakhe, nokokuba hlambi eloxesha liphela ngoMgqibelo, ngeCawa okanye ngeeholide.

(3) Nabani na umntu ongaziswanga enkundleni kwisithuba seyure ezingamashumi amane anesibhozo okubhekiselwe kuwo kwicandelwana 2 kufuneka akhululwe ngokukhawuleza ngaphandle kokuba ukuvalelwa kwakhe kwandiswe yinkundla efanelekileyo

(4) Nabani na umntu obanjelwe okanye ovalelelwe isigwebo esinetyala unelungelo—

(a)

lokuthula;

(b)

lokwaziswa ngokukhawuleza—

(i) ilungelo lakhe lokuthula; kwa

(ii) neziphumo zokuthula nokungathuli;

(c)

ukunganyanzelwa ukuvuma nokwamkela; kwaye

(d)

ngemva kokuvela enkundleni okokukuqala emva kokubanjwa, kufuneka aziswe ngesizathu sokuba aqhubeke evalelwe okanye avulelwe

(5) Wonke umntu obanjiwe kuquka nosegwetyiwe, unelungelo—

(a)

lokwaziswa ngokukhawulezileyo isizathu sokuba avalelwe;

(b)

ngeendleko zakhe ukubonisana ngasese nommeli wezomthetho azikhethele yena, kwaye aziswe ngelilungelo ngokukhawulezileyo;

(c)

ukuthetha nokuhanjelwa—

(i) ngumlingane okanye iqabane;

(ii) mtakokwabo;

(iii) mcebisi wezenkolo amfunayo;

(iv) mmeli wezoMthetho amfunayo;

(v) ugqirha amfunayo; kwaye

(vi) kuxhomekeke kwimingcipheko efanelekileyo eyenzelwe ukulawulwa kakuhlu kweentilongo okanye indawo zokugciba ababanjwa, nabani na okhethiweyo;

(d) lokweemeko zokuvalelwa ezihambelana nesidima somntu, kuquka nethuba lokuzilolonga ngokomzimbakunye nokubonelelwa ngeendleko zeLizwe indawo yokuhlala efanelekileyo, izakhiwo zangasese, ucoceko lomntu, ukutya okunesondlo, izinto ezifanelekileyo zokufunda kunye nonyango; kwaye.

(e)

ukuvunyelwa ukufaka umgeni kuMthetho wokubanjwa komntu phambi kwenkundla kwaye kufuneka ngokukhawuleza akhululwe ukuba ukubanjwa kungekho mthethweni.

(6) Wonke umntu ovalelweyo elinde isohlwayo setyala alibekwayo yaye engekayi enkundleni kwade kwegqitha ixesha lokuba ngabe seyile enkundleni, kufuneka avulelwe kungekho miqobo okanye kubekho izizathu ezivakalayo ukwenzela ukuba ngemva kokuvulelwa—

(a)

uyaya ukuyoxoxiswa ityala;

(b) akaphazamisi ubungqina obusakusetyenziswa etyaleni; kwaye akenzi elinye ityala elifanayo engekabuyeli enkundleni kwakhona.

(7) Ukuba kukho izizathu ezifanelekileyo zokukholelwa ukuba umntu ubanjwe ngokungekho mththweni okanye ukubaakaziwa apho avalelwe khona, nawuphi na umntu unokuya kwiNkundla ePhakamileyo ayokwenza umyalelo.

(a)

ye habeas corpus, oko kukukuthi umyalelo ofuna ukuba umntu ovalelweyo akhululwe, okanye aziswe kwinkundla yamatyala ukuba kuqinisekiswe imfuneko yokuvalewla , okanye oveza intilongo avalelwe kuyo; okanye.

(b) ukuvakalisa ukuvalelwa okungekho mththweni nokuyalela umyalelo wokukhutshwa komntu ovalelweyo ngokukhawulezileyo; kwaye iNkundla ePhakamileyo ingenza noba zeziphi izigqibo ezifanelekileyo.

(8) Ukubanjwa okanye ukuvalelwa okuphambene neli candela, okanye apho imekoezichazwe kwelicandelo zingagqibelelanga, akukho emthethweni okokubanjwa okanye ukuvalelwa.

(9) Nabani na umntu obanjwe ngokungekho emthethweni okanye ovaleliweyo unelungelo lokuhlawulwa ngumntu onoxanduva ekubanjweni okanye ekuvalelweni kwakhe, kodwa uMthetho ungabakhusela abantu abalandelayoekubekweni elityala liphantsi kwelicandelo—

(a)

igosa lezoMthetho elisebenza kakuhle nangokwethembeka;

ngokufanelekileyo

(b) naliphi na igosa likarhulumente elisebenza ngokufanelekileyo nangokwethembeka nangaphandle kokungazinto okanye ukungahoyi umsebenzi. 51 Ilungelo lokuba nesithunzi nesidima somntu Wonke umntu unesidima sobomi bakhe babucalakunye nobasesidlangalaleni kwaye unelungelo lokuba esosidima sihlonitshwe yaye sikhuselwe. 52 Ilungelo lokhuseleko lomntu Wonke umntu unelungelo lengqibelelo yomzimba kunye neyengqondo, ukuquka ilungelo—

(a)

lenkululeko kuzo zonke iintlobo zobundlobongela lwasesidlangalaleni nangasese;

(b)

kuxhomekeke kuzibonelelo zoMgaqo-siseko, ukwenza izigqibo ngokuzala;

(c)

nokungasetyenziselwa luphando ngokufunisela malunga nezonyango okanye ezobunzululwazi, ngaphandle kwemvume yakhe ayinike eyiqonda kakuhle into avuma yona.

Inkululeko yokungathuthunjiswa nokungakhohlakalelwa okungenabuntu nesohlwayo esithoba isidima

Akukho mntu othuthunjiswa ngokomzimba, ngokwengqondo, okanye akhohlakalelwe okanye isohlwayo esithoba isidima somntu. 54

Inkululeko yokungakhobokiswa Akukho mntu makenziwe ikhoboka, asetyenziswe ngembophelelo.


Inkululeko yokunganyanzelwa ngomsebenzi Akukho mntu omakanyanzelwe ukusebenza.


Ukulingana nokungacaluli

(1) Wonke ubani ulingana nomnye phambi koMthetho kwaye unelungelo lokukhuselwa nelokuncedeka ngokulinganayo emthethweni.

(2) Abasetyhini kunye namadoda banelungelo elilinganayo kwezonyango, kuquka amalungelo alinganayo kumathuba ezepolitiki, ezoqoqosho, inkcubeko yosiko nentlalo.

(3) Wonke umntu unelungelo lokuphathwa ngendlela efanelekileyo, angacalulwa ngezizathu ezifana nobuzwe, ubuhlanga, umbala, iSizwe, indawo yokuzalwa, intlalo, ulwimi, inkolelo, ezepolitiki, umbono, inkcubeko, isiko, iSini, ubumi obuxela ukuba utshatile okanye awutshatanga, iminyaka yokuzalwa, ukukhulelwa, ukukhubazeka okanye imeko yezoqoqosho okanye yasekuhlaleni, okanye ukuba uzalelwe emtshatweni okanye hayi.

(4) Umntu kuthwa ucaluliwe xa kubhekiselwa koko kuxelwe kwicandelwana

(3)—

(a)

ukuba uyachaneka ngqo okanye ngokungathanga ngqo kwiimeko, umqobo okanye ukukhubazeka apho abanye abantu abangathinteki khona koko; okanye

(b) abanye abantu bafunyaniswa ilungelo okanye ukuncedakala okuthile yena angakufumaniyo.

(5) Ucalulo nolwaluphi na uhlobo oluchazwe kucandelwana (3) alifanelekanga ngaphandle kokuba kusekwa ukuba ulucalulo lulungile, lufanelekilile kwaye lufanelekile ngokolulawulo lwentando yeSininzi okususela ebuhleni, ubulungisa, isithunzi somntu, ukulingana kunye nenkululeko.

(6) URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo oMthetho kunye namanye amanyathelo okukhuthaza ukuphumelela kokulingana kunye nokukhusela okanye ukuqhubela phambili abantu okanye iindidi zabantu abaye bachaphazeleka ekucalulweni, kwaye—

(a)

amanyathelo afanelekileyo kufuneka athatyathwe kwiimeko zokulungiswa kwemfuno zokwenyani;

(b)

akukho mqathango onjalo ongathathwa njengobulungisa xa kulandelwa icandelwana (3).

57 Ilungelo labucala Wonke umntu unelungelo lokuba nemfihlo, nto leyo ebandakanya ilungelo—

(a)

lokungangenelwa kwikhaya, kwiindawo okanye ukuphendlwa kwempahla yakhe ngaphandle kwemvumo yakhe;

(b)

lokungasetshwa kwakhe isiqu, umzi, indawo okanye ipropati yakhe;

(c)

lokungathathwa kwezinto ezizezakhe;

(d)

lokungabhencwa konxibelelwano lakhe lwangasese/oluyimfihlo okanye

(e)

lokungabhengezwa kwemeko yakhe yezempilo.

58 Inkululeko yendibano nemibutho

(1) Wonke umntu unelungelo lokwenza indibano kwanokungena imibutho, kwanelungelo lokungangeni ndibano nemibutho

(2) akukho mntu omakanyanzelwe ukuba sembuthweni okanye ukuya kwindibano okanye intlanganiso. 59 Ilungelo lokubhiyoza nokukhalaza Wonke umntu unelungelo lokubhiyoza nokukhalaza 60 Inkululeko yesazela

(1) Wonke umntu unenkululeko yokubanesazela, ukuquka ilungelo—

(a)

inkululeko, yokucinga, yokupha umbono, unqulo okanye inkolelo; kwaye

(b) inkululeko yokuzibandakanya nokusabalalisa kwa nokubonisa ingcamango yakhe, umbono, inkolo okanye inkolelo, nokuba kusesidlangalaleni okanye ngasese kaye nokuba uyedwa okanye unabanye.

(2) Akukho mntu unokunyanzelwa ukuba enze isifungo esicgasene nenkolo okanye inkolelo yakhe, okanye enze isifungo ngendlela ephikisana nenkolo okanye inkolelo yakhe.

(3) Abazali kunye nabo bagcine abantwana abancinci banelungelo lokumisela, ngokuhambelana nenkolelo yabo ukukhuliswa kwabantwana abo ngokuziphatha nangokwenyaniso, ngaphandle kokuba oko akuhambisani namalungelo abo axelwe kulo Mgaqo-siseko, kuquka amalungelo abo kwimfundo, impilo, ukhuseleko kunye nentlalontle.

(4) Nawuphi na umbutho wezenkolo unako ukuzimisela iziko apho kunokwenziwa khona imiyalelo yenkolo, nokuba iziko lingafumana inkxaso yezemali evela kurhulumente. 61 Inkululeko yokuthetha neyokufikelela iindaba

(1) Wonke umntu unelungelo lenkululeko yokuthetha, okuquka—

(a)

inkululeko yokufuna, ukufumana nokunika izimvo kunye nolunye ulwazi;

(b) inkululeko yokudala nokuvelisa ngobuchule;kwa

(c)

nenkululeko kwezemfundo nenkululeko yokwenza uphando lwezobunzululwazi.

(2) Wonke umntu unelungelo lokufikelela kumaziko eendaba, inkululeko iquka ukukhuselwa kwemfihlo yomthombo wendaba wonondaba.

(3) Ukusasazwa kunye neminye imisasazo nonxibelelwano lobunzululwazi inenkululeko yokusungula, ngokuxhomekeke kwiinkqubo zelayisensi zikaRhulumente kuphela—

(a)

ukuba kuyimfuneko ukulawula amagagassi ezosasazo kunye nezinye indlela zokunikezelwa kwe siginali; kwaye

(b) ezizimeleyo kululawulo lukarhulumente, ezepolitiki okanye ezorhwebo.

(4) Onke amaziko eendaba alawulwa ngurhulumente kufuneka—

(a)

ukuba akhululeke ukuzikhethela ngokuzimeleyo umxholo wokuhlela kwabo ezosasazo,

(b)

ukungabi namkhethe; kwaye

(c)

aphe isithuba esilingeneyo solusasazo lwezimvo nemibono eyahlukeneyo.

(5) Inkululeko yokuzichaza nenkululeko yokufikelela kumaziko —


(a)

ukuphembelela ubundlobongela/udushe

(b)

ukukhuthazan inzondo okanye intetho eyohlwayela inzondo;

(c)

ukulimaza udumo nesidima somntu; okanye

(d)

ukukroboza ilungelo lomntu lemfihlo okanye impilo yakhe yangasese.

Ukufikeleleka kolwazi

(1) Bonke abemi baseZimbabwe okanye abahlala ngokusisigxina, kuquka neembumba ezisekwe ngokomthetho kunye nabamaziko eendaba eZimbabwe, banelungelo lokufikelela nakuluphi na ulwazi olugcinwe ngurhulumente namanqanaba akhe onke, ukuba ololwazi lufuneka ukuba lizalise iminqweno yoluntu.

(2) Wonke umntu, ukuquka amaziko endaba eZimbabwe anelungelo lokufikelela kulwazi/umbiko ogcinwe noba ngubani, ukuquka noRhulumende ukuba ololwazi luyafuneka ekuqhubeni umsenenzi wokukhusela ilungelo.

(3) Wonke umntu unelungelo lokulungisisa okanye ukususa ulwazi/mbiko ongeyonyani ugcinwe ngurhulumente okanye ngamanqanaba akhe ukuba ngaba oko kuphathelane naye umntu lowo.

(4) UMthetho kufuneka ugqitywe ukuphumeza eli lungelo, kodwa unokuthintela ukufikelela kulwazi/mbiko yezokhuselo, ukhuselo loluntu okanye imfihlo yobungcali, kwinqanaba lokuba umqobo ulungelelanise, uqikelele, uyimfuneko kwaye ulungele ululawulo lwentando yeSinintsi olususela ebulungiseni, isithunzi somntu, ukulingana kunye nenkululeko. 63 Ulwimi nenkcubeko Wonke umntu unelungelo—

(a)

lokusebenzisa noba loluphi ulwimi azikhethela lona; kwaye

(b)

athathe inxaxheba kwimicimbi yenkcubeko azikhethela yona;

Kodwa akukhomntu onokusebenzisa lamalungelo ngohlobo olungahambisani nesi Sahluko. 64

Inkululeko yobungcali, urhwebo okanye impangelo

Wonke umntu unelungelo lokukhetha ngokukhululekileyo ubungcali, urhwebo okanye impangelo, kodwa ke ukuqhutywa kobungcali, umsebenzi worhwebo okanye impangelo kuyakulawulwa nguMthetho. 65

Amalungelo abasebenzi

(1) Wonke umntu unelungelo lokusebenza ngendlela efanelekileyo nekhuselekileyo kunye nesemgangathweni kwa nokuhlawulelwa umvuzo owamkelekileyo.

(2) Ngaphandle kwabo bangamalungu oMkhosi wezokhuselo, wonke umntu unelungelo lokuyila nokuba yinxalenye kwimibutho yabasebenzi okanye abaqeshi abazikhethela yona, kunye nokuthatha inxaxheba kwimisebenzi esemthethweni yalemibutho.

(3) Ngaphandle kwabo bangamalungu oMkhosi wezokhuselo, wonke umsebenzi unelungelo lokuqhankqalaza, ukurhoxisa umsebenzi wakhe kunye nokuthatha nasiphi na esinye isenzo esifanayo, kodwa uMthetho unokunqanda ukusetyenziswa koko ukuba ugcine iinkonzo ezibalulekileyo.

(4) Wonke umqeshwa unelungelo lokusebenza phantsi kwemeko, ezamkelekileyo, ezifanelekileyo nezilungileyo.

(5) Ngaphandle kwabo bangamalungu oMkhosi wenkuselo, wonke umqeshwa, umqashi, imibutho yabaphangeli, nemibutho yabasebenzi kunye neyabaqashi banamalungelo oku—

(a)

lokubandakanya ekuthengiselaneni;

(b) ukuququzelela; kwa

(c)

nokuyila nokuba yinxalenye yemibutho ebamelayo.

(6) Oomama notata banelungelo elilinganayo lokuvuzwa ngokulinganayo emsebenzini.

(7) Oomama abaphangelayo banelungelo lokuba babhatalwe ngokupheleleyo ngelixesha behleli bekhulisa usana okwenyanga ezintathu. 66 Inkululeko yokuhamba nokuhlala

(1) Wonke ummi waseZimbabwe une—

(a)

lungelo lokungena eZimbabwe;

(b) lungelo lokhuseleko lokungagxothwa eZimbabwe; kwa

(c)

lungelo lokuba nepaspoti namanye amaxwebhu okuhamba.

(2) Wonke ummi waseZimbabwe naye wonke umntu ohleli eZimbabwe ngokusemthethweni unelungelo—

(a)

ukuhamba ngokukhululekileyo eZimbabwe;

(b) ukuhlala noba kuphi eZimbabwe; kwa

(c)

nokushiya iZmbabwe.

Amalungelo ezepolitiki

(1) Wonke ummi waseZimbabwe unelungelo—

(a)

lokhetho olukhululekileyo nolulungileyo lwayo nayiphi na i-ofisi yoluntu ekhethiweyo, eyenziwe ngokwemigaqo yalo Mgaqo-siseko okanye nayiphi na enye imithetho, kwaye

(b) nokuzikhethela ngokukhululekileyo iqela lezepolitiki.

(2) Kuxhomekeke kulo Mgaqo-siseko, wonke ummi waseZimbabwe unelungelo—

(a)

lokuyila, ukuba yinxalenye nokuthatha inxaxheba kwimicimbi yamaqela ezepolitiki abazikhethela wona;

(b) ukukhankanela ngokukhululekileyo nangoxolo noba leliphi iqela lezepolitiki;

(c)

ukuthathi nxaxheba ezolileyo kwimicimbi yezepolitiki; kwa

(d) ukuthathi nxaxheba emnye okanye ngokubambisana nabanye, kwiindibano nasemibuthweni eqhutywa ngoxolo khona ukuze ithintele, igade okanye ixhase imigaqo kaRhulumente okanye noba leliphi iqela lezepolitiki.

(3) Kuxhomekeke kulo Mgaqo-siseko, wonke ummi waseZimbabwe oneminyaka yobudala engaphezu kweshumi elinasibhozo unelungelo—

(a)

lokuvota kulunyulo nakwiireferendamu nonyulo laliphi na iziko lezowiso Mthetho elisekwe nguMgaqo-siseko, oko akwenze emfihlakalweni,

(b) nokuzigqatsela isikhundla eburhulumenteni, nelokuba kuso isikhundla eso ukuba unyulwe.

(4) Ngeenjongo zokukhuthaza ukuba kubekho amaqela amanintsi ezepolitiki kulawulo lwentando yesininzi, uMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uphe inkxaso yezemali kumaqela ezepolitiki. 68 Ilungelo lobulungisa bolawulo

(1) Wonke umntu unelungelo lokuxoxiswa ityala ngendlela esemthethweni, ekhawulezileyo, efanelekileyo, evisisekayo, elingeneyo engacaluliyo kwaye elandelwa ngemfanelo.

(2) Nawuphi na umntu onelungelo lakhe, inkululeko, umdla okanye ukulindela okusemthethweni okuye kwachaphazeleka kakubi ngempatho unelungelo lokunikezwa ngokukhawulezileyo kwaye ngokubhaliweyo izizathu zempatho.

(3) UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka uqinisekiswe ngokulandelwa kwalamalungelo, kwaye—

(a) ukubonelela ukuhlaziywa kweendlela zokuphathwa kwenkundla okanye apho kufanelekile iqumrhu elizimeleyo nelingenamkhethe;

(b) ukupha uxanduva kurhulumente ukuba aqinisekise ukuphumeza amalungelo akucandelwana (1) no (2); kwa

(c)

nokukhuthaze ukuxoxiswa kwamatyala ngendlela efanelekileyo.

69 Ilungelo lokuviwa ngokulungileyo

(1) Wonke umntu othyolwa ngetyala unelungelo lokuxoxiswa aviwe ngokufanelekileyo enkundleni kawonke-wonke, ezimeleyo nengenacalu-calulo.

(2) Ekumisweni kwamalungelo kunye neembophelelo zomntu, wonke umntu unelungelo lokuxoxiswa ngokukhawuleza kwinkundla kawonke phambi kwenkundla ezimele yodwa nengenamkhethe okanye elinye iqumrhu elisungulwe nguMthetho.

(3) Wonke umntu unelungelo lokufikelela enkundleni, ikomiti yoMthetho okanye iqumrhu elisungulwe nguMthetho.

(4) Wonke umntu unelungelo, ngendleko zakhe, ukuzikhethela ummeli wezoMthetho kwaye amelwe ngummeli wezoMthetho enkundleni yamatyala noba yeyiphi. 70

Amalungelo abarhanelwa

(1)

Wonke umntu otyolwa ngetyala unalamalungelo alandelayo—

(a)

lokuthathwa njengomntu ongenatya ade abe ufunyanwa enalo ityala;

(b) lokwaziswa ngeesityolo ngokukhawuleza, enikwa iincukatsha ezipheleleyo ukuze akwazi ukuziphendulela;

(c)

ukunikwa ixesha elaneleyo kwanezinto ezaneleyo zokulungiselela impendulo yokuzikhusela;

(d) lokuzikhethela igqwetha ngendleko zakhe, ze amelwe lilo;

(e)

ukumelwa ligqwetha likaRhulumente kwaye ngendleko;

(f)

ukwaziswa ngokukhawuleza lamalungelo axelwe ku (d) no (e);

(g) lokubakhona buqu xa kuxoxwa ityala lakhe;

(h) lokuveza nokuphikisa ubungqina;

(i)

lokuthula tu, kwa nokunganyanzelwa ukuba anike ubungqina obumbeka ibala;

(j)

lokuxoxwa wetyala lakhe ngolwimi lommangalelwa alwaziyo, ukuba oko akunakwenzeka ingxoxo zitolikwe ngolwimi lwakhe alwaziyo;

(k) lokungagwetywa ngesenzo okanye utyeshelo olwalungelo lwaphuloMthetho ngexesha lokwenzeka kwaso;

(l)

lokungagwetyelwa isenzo okanye utyeshelo olungelo lwaphulo Mthetho;

(m) lokungatshutshiselwa ityala elisisenzo okanye ukungenzi asele wagwetyelwa lona okanye wagwetywa ngenxa yalo okanye laxolelwa;

(n) lokuzuza esona sohlwayo silula kwezo zimiselweyo ukuba ngaba isohlwayo esimiselweyo solo lwaphulo Mthetho siye satshintshwa apha kwelixeshaliphakathi kokwenziwa kolwaphulo Mthetho nokugwetywa.

(2) Apho elicandelo lifunisa ukuba umntu aphiwe iinkcukacha—

(a)

Ezonkcukaca kufuneka umntu lowo aziphiwe ngolwimi alwaziyo; kwaye

(b) ukuba umntu lowo akakwazi kufunda nokubhala, uxwebhu lweenkcukacha zonke kufuneka azichazelwe ngohlobo oluzakumenza avisisise.

(3) Nakuphi ukuxoxiswa kwetyala, ubungqina obufunyenwe ngendlela eyaphula naliphi na ilungelo elixelwe kwesiSahluko mabungafakwa ukuba ukwamkelwa kobo bungqina kungabangela ukuba ukuxoxwa kwetyala kungamkeleki okanye ukwenzeka kobulungisa buphazamiseke ngendlela ethile.

(4) Nawuphi na umntu oye waxoxiswa ngokoMthetho unelungelo lokuhlawula ngokwemali efanelekileyo emiswe nguMthetho, kufuneka anikwe ikopi yenkqubo kwangexesha elifanelekileyo emva kokuphiwa isohlwayo sakhe.

(5) Nawuphi na umntu oye waxoxiswa wafunyanwa enetyala, unelungelo ngokubhekiselele kwimithetho efanelekileyo enokumiselwa nguMthetho, elo—

(a) kuba ityala lakhe liyojongwa yinkundla ephakamileyo; okanye

(b) abhenele kwinkundla esentla ngokubanjwa nangesohlwayo asiphiweyo. 71

Amalungelo empahla

(1) Kwelicandelo— “inzuzo yomhlalaphantsi” ithetha ipensheni, imali yesibongo okanye imali efanelekileyoebhatalwa—

(a) noba ngowuphi umntu ivela kwingxowa-mali yengeniso;

(b) umntu ngokwentsebenzo yakhe kumqashi wakhe;

(c)

umntu ngemva kokugula okanye ukonzakala; okanye

(d) umntu ngemva kokuba eyeke umsebenzi ngenxeni yobudala ngeminyaka okanye ukugula okanye nasiphi na esinye isizathu; kwaye kuquka ukuguqulwa kwempensheni, imali yesibongo okanye imali efanelelkileyo, okanye isibonelelo kunye nokubuyiselwa kwemali ehlawulwe kuloo pensheni, imali yesibongo okanye imali ezithile zezibonelelo; “impahla” ichaza nokuba yeyiphi na inkcazelo kunye nawaphi na amalungelo okanye umdla kumhlaba.

(2) Kuxhomekeke kwicandelo 72, wonke umntu unelungelo, noba kukweyiphi indawo eZimbabwe, lokufumana, ukubamba, ukuhlala, ukusebenzisa, ukudlulisa, ukuqeshisa okanye ukulahla zonke iintlobo zepropati, ngokuzimeleyo okanye ngokubambisana namaqela.

(3) Kuxhomekeke kwelicandelo nakwicandelo 72, akukhomntu omakavaleleke ukuba abe nempahla ngaphandle kokuba lemigaqo ilandelayo iyazaliseka ukuba—

(a)

ukwalelwa kuhambisana noMthetho wesicelo jikelele;

(b)

ukwalelwa kuyimfuneko kweyiphi na yezi zizathu zilandelayo—

(i) kwiminqweno yokukhusela, ukhuseleko loluntu, ulawulo loluntu, ukuziphatha komphathi, impilo yoluntu okanye idolophu kunye ngocwangciso lweLizwe; okanye

(ii) ukuze kuphuhliswe okanye kusetyenziswe loonto okanye nayiphi impahla ethile ngenjongo enokunceda uluntu;

(c)

umthetho udinga igunya lokufumana—

(i) ukunika isaziso esicacileyo ngenjongo yokufumana loo impahla kuwo wonke umntu onomdla okanye ilungelo lakhe kwipropati liya kuthinteka ukufumana;

(ii) ukubhatala isixa-mali yembuyekezo yokufumana okanye phambi kokufumana impahla ngexesha elibekiweyo; kwaye

(iii) ukuba ukufumaneka kuphikiswa, ukufuna inkundla yezoMthetho efanelekileyo phambi kokufumaneka kwe mpahla kungekegqithi

intsuku ezingamashumi amathathu zokwalelwa, ukuba kubekho ucwangco oluqinisekisa ukwala;

(d) uMthetho uvumela nabani na onepropati ukuba afake isicelo kwinkundla efanelekileyo ukuba kubuyiselwe ngokukhawuleza impahla leyo ukuba inkundla ayiqiniseki ngokufunyanwa kwayo; kwaye

(e)

uMthetho upha amandla nakubani othi impahla yeyakhe ukuba afake isicelo kwinkundla efanelekileyo ezimiseleyo—

(i) kubukho, uhlobo kunye nenani lomdla wabo kwipropati echaphazelekayo;

(ii) kwinqanaba lokunciphisa; kwa

(iii) nesi-mali yembuyekezo okufuneka beyibhataliwe;

kwa nokuba bafake isicelo enkundleni eyalela ukuhlawulwa ngokukhawuleza noba yeyiphi imbuyekezo

(4) Xa umntuunelungelo elixhasiweyo okanye elilingene nokuhlawula inzuzo yempesheni, uMthetho ohlinzekela ukuphela okanye ukuncipha kwelo lungelo uthathwa, ngenjongo yecandelwana 3, njengoMthetho onikizela ukufunyanwa kwepropati. 72 Amalungelo omhlaba wezolimo

(1) Kwelicandelo— “umhlaba wezolimo” uchaza umhlaba osetyenziswa okanye olungele ezolimo, ukutsho ukulima, amahlathi, amanzi okanye nayiphi na injongo yokulima, ukuquka—

(a) ukugcina nokufuya imfuyo, izilwanyana zasendle, iinkukhu okanye iinyosi; okanye

(b) amadlelo emfuyo okanye izilwanyana zasendle; kodwa awubandakanyi umhlaba woluntu okanye umhlaba ongaphakathi kwemida yezixeko zasezidolophini okanye kwilokishi elisekwe phantsi koMthetho oophathelene needolophu kunye nokucwangciswa kweLizwe okanye njengoko kuchaziwe kuMthetho; “umhlaba” ubandakanya indawo okulinywa kuyo; “indawo yezolimo” ithetha ukuba indawo yezolimo ebhalisiweyo okanye ehlukeneyo yomhlaba.

(2) Xa umhlaba wezolimo, okanye naliphi na ilungelo okanye umdla kulomhlaba ufuneka ngenjongo kawonke wonke; ukuqula—

(a)

ukuhlaliswa kwezolimo okanye ngokwezinye iinjongo;

(b)

ukulungiswa kwakhona komhlaba, amathathi, ukongiwa kwemveli okanye ukusetyenziswa ngemfanelo kwezilwanyana zasendle nobunye ubutyebi bezwe; okanye

(c)

ukuhlaliswa ngokutsha kwabantu abasuswa kwimihlaba yabo ukuba isetyenziswe kwezonto zixelwe kumhlathi u (a) okanye (b);

Ilungelo okanye umdla womhlaba unokuthi uthathwe ngurhulumente ngesaziso isishicilelwe kwiGazethi ekuchazeni ilungelo lomhlaba okanye umdla walo apho oko kulilungelo okanye umdla ka Rhulumente ngesihloko esigcweleyo ukususela ngomhla wokupapashwa kwesaziso.

(3) Xa umhlaba wezolimo, ilungelo okanye ilungelo lalomhlaba linyanzeliswa ukuthathwa ngenxeni yezizathu ezichazwe kwicandelwana —

(a)

kuxhomekeka kwicandelo 295(1) no (2), akukho ntlawulo ehlawulwa ngokubhekiselele ekuthengeni, kophuculo olwenziwe kuwo ngaphambi kokufunyanwa kwawo;

(b) Akukho mntu unokufaka isicelo enkundleni ukuzimisela nawuphi na umbuzo ophathelene nembuyekezo, ngaphandle kophuculo olwenziwe kulomhlaba ngaphambi kokufunyanwa kwawo, kwaye akukho nkundla inokwamkela nasiphi na isicelo esilolohlobo; kwaye

(c)

ukufunyanwa komhlaba akunakuphikiswa kumiswa ngelokuba kubekho ucalulo ekuphawulweni kwecandelo 56.

(4) Wonke umhlaba wezolimo owa—

(a)

candwayo kwishedyuli 7 yoMgaqo-siseko omdala; okanye

(b) phambi kokuba kufike usuku lokuqalisa, oluchazwe ngokuxela kwecandelo 16B(2)(a)(ii) okanye (iii) kuMgaqo-siseko omdala; yaqhubekeka unikwe uRhulumente, kwaye akukho ntlawulo ehlawulwa ngokubhekiselele nokufunyanwa kwawo ngaphandle kophuculo olwenziweyo kwawo ngaphambi kokufunyanwa kwawo.

(5) Kwamsinyane nje emva kokuba umhlaba sowuthathiwe kulandelwa indlela zixelwe kwicandelo (2), igosa elibona ngokubhaliswa kwetayitile , kufuneka kungekhange kubekho saziso simbi, aqinisekise ukubhaliswa kwetayitili ukuba isuswe kulo ubengumniniyo ngaphambilini kwaye ibhaliswe ngaphantsi kukarhulumente.

(6) Mthetho wasePalamente umbeka ityala nawuphi na umntu ongekho gunyeni lezoMthetho onokuthatha umhlaba wezolimo uxelwe kwelicandelo okanye nawuphi umhlaba karhulumente.

(7) Ngokubhekiselele ekufunyanweni komhlaba wezolimo ukuba kuhlaliswe kuwo abantu ngokutsha kwinkqubo yohlaliso lwabantu ngokutsha , ezi zinto zilandelayo kufuneka ziqaphelwe njengezona zinto zibaluleke kangangoko—

(a)

ngaphantsi kolulawulo locinezelo abantu baseZimbabwe boxuthwa umhlaba wabo kunye nobunye ubutyebi ngokungafanelekanga abaphiwa kwambuyekezo;

(b) abantu bancama kwanto bayokulwa ilwela umhlaba wabo kwa nenkululeko, nto leyo eyenza iZimbabwe yakhululeka ngonyaka ka 1980

(c)

abantu baseZimbabwe kufuneka bakwazi ukufumana kwakhona amalungelo abo omhlaba kwaye bazilawulele umhlaba wabo;

kwaye ngokunjalo—

(i) ulawulo oludala olocinezelo lunoxanduva lokuhlawula imbuyekezo yomhlaba owathathwayo kwahlaliswa kuwo ngokutsha abantu, kwingxowa yemali eyeneleyo emiselwe oko; kwaye

(ii) ukuba ululawulo lwangaphambilini olocinezelo luyoyisakala ukuhlawula lombuyekezo kulengxowa yemali, uRhulumente akangeni ndawo yena ekuhlawuleni lomhlaba wathathwayo ukuba kuhlaliswe ngokutsha kuwo abantu.

(8) Elicandelo lisebenza ngaphandle kokunyanzelisa umbophelelo wolulawulo lwangaphambilini olocinezelo ukuhlawula imbuyekezo yomhlaba uxelwa kwelicandelo owathathelwa ukuba kuhlaliswe kuwo abantu kutsha.

73 Amalungelo emveli esingqongileyo

(1) Wonke umntu unelungelo—

(a)

lokungqongwa yimveli engenabungozi kwimpilo yakhe okanye nakwintlao yakhe; kwaye

(b) lokuba imvelo emngqongileyo ikhuselwe ukuba kuncedeke izizukulwana zangoku nezizayo, ikhuselwe ngamanyathelo afanelekileyo ezoMthetho kwakunye namanye amanyathelo—

(i) anqanda ukungcoliswa kwa nokudodobaliswa kwemveli;

(ii) akhuthaza ulondolozo lwemveli, kanjalo

(iii) aqinisekisa uphuhliso lwezemveli oluhlala luqhuba kunye nokusetyenziswa kwamaziko ezemvelo logama kukhuthazwa uphuhliso olulungileyo lwezoqoqosho nentlalo.

(2) URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo oMthetho namanye amanyathelo ukuze kuqinisekiswe ukulandelwa kwalamalungelo axelwe kwelicandelo. 74 Inkululeko yokungagxothwa ngokungamkelakanga Akukhomntu uya kukhutshwa endlwini yakhe, okanye adilizelwe indlu yakhe ngaphandle komyalelo wenkundla okhutshwe ngemva kokuqwalasela zonke iimeko ezimalunga noko. 75 Ilungelo lemfundo

(1) Wonke ummi kunye nabani ongumhlali waseZimbabwe unelungelo—

(a)

lemfundo esisiseko esekwa yingxowa kaRhulumente, kuquka imfundo yabantu abadala esisiseko; kwa

(b)

nelemfundo ethe tyishi, ekufuneka uRhulumente athi ngamanyathelo afanelekileyo, ithi gqolo ukufumaneka nokufikeleleka.

(2) Wonke umntu unelungelo lokuseka nokugcina ngendleko zakhe, iziko lemfundo elizimeleyo lemigangatho efanelekileyo, ngaphandle kokucalula nawuphi na umqathango oxelwe nguMgaqo-siseko.

(3) UMthetho unokubonelela ukubhaliswa kwamaziko emfundo okubhekiselelwe kuwo kwicandelwana(2) kunye nokuvalwa kwale ntlangano eqhuba imiqathango efanelekileyo eyokubhalisa

(4) URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo oMthetho kunye namanye amanyathelo, ngezibonelelo ezikhoyo kuwo, ukufezekisa ukuqhubela phambili kwelungelo elichazwe kwicandelwana. 76 Ilungelo lokhathalelwa kwempilo

(1) Wonke ummi kunye nomhlali osisigxina eZimbabwe unelungelo lokufikelela kwiinkonzo zokunakekelwa kwezempilo ezisisiseko, kuquka iinkonzo zonyango lwezempilo.

(2) Wonke umntu ophila nesifo esingapheliyo unelunglo lokufumana iinkonzo zokunakekelwa kwezempilo ngokwesifo eso.

(3) Akukho mntu omakalelwe ukufunyaniswa unyango olungxamisekileyo nakweliphi na iziko lezempilo.

(4) URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo oMthetho kunye namanye amalungelo, ngezibonelelo ezikhoyo kuwo ukuze afezekise ukuthi gqolo kokufumaneka kwelilungelo lixelwe kwelicandelo. 77

Ilungelo lokutya kunye namanzi Wonke umntu unelungelo lokufumana—

(a) amanzi akhuselekileyo nacocekileyo; kwa

(b) nokutya okweneleyo;

kwaye uRhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo oMthetho kunye namanye amalungelo, ngezibonelelo ezikhoyo kuwo ukuze afezekise ukuthi gqolo kokufumaneka kwelilungelo. 78

Amalungelo omtshato

(1) Wonke umntu oneminyaka yobudala engaphezu kweshumi elinesibhozo unalo ilungelo lokuzifunela usapho.

(2) Akukho mntu unokunyanzelwa ukungena emtshatweni engafuni.

(3) Abantu besini sinye abavumelekanga ukutshata. INXALENYE 3 Ukucaciswa Kwamalungelo Athile 79 Ukusetyenziswa kwenxalenye 3

(1) Le nxalenye ichaza amalungelo athile kunye neenkululeko ukuze kuqinisekiswe ngokugqithisekileyo malunga nokusetyenziswa kwamalungelo kunye nenkululeko kwiintlobo ezithile zabantu.

(2) Le nxalenye akufunekanga ithathwe njengesinciphiso saliphi na ilungelo okanye inkululeko echazwe kwicandelo 2. 80 Amalungelo abasetyhini

(1) Wonke umntu ongumama unesidima esipheleleyo nesilinganayo nomntu oyindoda kwaye oku kuquka amathuba afanayo kwimicimbi yezepolitiki, ezoqoqosho nezentlalo.

(2) Abasetyhini banamalungelo alinganayo namadoda ngokubhekiselele ekugcinweni kunye nasekukhulisen kwabantwana, kodwa uMthetho wasePalamente unokulawula ukuba loomalungelo kufuneka esetyenziswe njani.

(3) Yonke imithetho, amasiko, inkolo nenkolelo zenkcubeko eziphambene namalungelo amabhinqa anikezelwe kuloMgaqo-siseko awanakuphumelela. 81 Amalungelo abantwana

(1) Wonke umntwana, nokokuba yinkwenkwe okanye intombazana oneminyaka yobudala engaphantsi kweshumi elinesibhozo, unelungelo—

(a)

lokuphathwa ngokulinganayo emthethweni, ukuquka nelungelo lokumanyelwa;

(b)

lokuthiywa igama nokuba nesibongo;

(c)

kwimeko yomntwana—

(i) ozalelwe eZimbabwe; okanye

(ii) ozalelwe ngaphandle kweLizwe laseZimbabwe kwaye engummi waseZimbabwe ngokubhalisa;

ukufumana isiqinisekiso sokuzalwa kwamsinyane;

(d) lokukhathalelwa lusapho okanye ngabazali, okanye lokufunyaniswa inkathalo efanayo xa ethe wasuswa kusapho lwakhe;

(e) ukukhuselwa ekuxhatshazweni kwezoqoqosho kunye nokuxhaphazwa ngokwesondlo, ukususela kwimisebenzi yabantwana kunye nokuphathwa kakubi, ukunganakwa okanye nayiphi na indlela yokusetyenziswa kakubi;

(f)

lezemfundo, kwiinkonzo zezempilo, ukutya okunesondlo kwanendawo yokuhlala;

(g) lokungaqeshwa kuMkhosi wamajoni okanye ukuthathi nxaxheba kunquzulwano okanye imfazwe;

(h) lokunganyanzelwa ukuthathinxaxheba kwimicimbi yeze politiki; kwa

(i)

lokungavalelwa ngaphandle kokuba bekungekho ngenye indlela, kwaye ukuba uthe wavalelwa —

(i)

avalelwe okwexesha elifutshane;

(ii) lokwahlukaniswa nabantu abaseluvalelweni abaneminyaka yobudala engaphezu kweshumi elinesibhozo; kwa

(iii) ukuphathwa nokugcinwa kwiimeko ezivumelekileyo ebantwaneni.

(2) Iminqweno yomntwana ibaluleke kakhulu kuyo yonke into ephathelene nomntwana.

(3) Abantwana banelungelo lokukhuselwa ngokwaneleyo kwiinkundla, ngokukodwa yiNkundla ePhakamileyo njengomntu ukhangele umntwana. 82 Amalungelo abantu abadala Abantu abaneminyaka yobudala engaphezu kwamashumi asixhenxe banelungelo—

(a) lokunyamekelwa nokuncediswa ziintsapho zabo kunye noRhulumente;

(b) lokufumana unyango lwempilo kunye noncedo lwezonyango oluvela kwuRhulumente; kwa

(c) lokufumana inkxaso yezemali ngendlela ngendlela yezokhuselo loluntu nentlalontle; kwaye uRhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo oMthetho kunye namanye amalungelo, ngezibonelelo ezikhoyo kuwo ukuze afezekise ukuthi gqolo kokufumaneka kwelilungelo. 83 Amalungelo abantu abakhubazekileyo URhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo, ngezibonelelo ezikhoyo kuwo, ukuqinisekisa ukuba abantu abakhubazekileyo bafumana amandla abo ngokwengqondo nangokwenyama, kuquka amanyathelo—

(a)

okubenza bakwazi ukuzimela;

(b) okubenza bakwazi ukuhlala neentsapho zabo kwaye bathathe inxaxheba kwimisebenzi yoluntu, eyokudala okanye ukuzonwabisa;

(c)

okubakhusela kwintlobo zonke zobundlobongela nokusetyenziswa kakubi;

(d)

okubenza bakwazi ukufikelela kwezonyango kwengqondo kunye nokunyangwa okusebenzayo

(e)

ukubabonelela ngeenkonzo ezizodwa zemfundo; kwa

(f)

ukubabonelela ngemfundo kunye noqeqesho oluxhaswe ngurhulumente apho kufuneka khona oloncedo.

84 Amalungelo aabo balwa imfazwe yenkululeko

(1) Abalwi bemfazwe yenkululeko, babalela—

(a)

abo balwayo kwiMfazwe yeNkululeko;

(b) abo abancedisana nabo babesilwa kwiMfazwe yeNkululeko; kwa

(c)

abo baye babanjwa, bavalelwa ngezizathu zokuthathinxaxheba kwezepolitiki ngexesha lemfazwe yenkululeko banelungelo lokuphiwa isidima esifanelekilyo ngegalelo labo ekukhululweni kweZimbabwe, kunye nentlalontle efanelekileyo njengempensheni kunye nokufikelela kwiinkonzo zempilo ezisiseko.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka unikezele isidima esifanelekileyo kumagqala emfazwe yenkululeko njengoko kuxelwayo kwicandelwana (1). INXALENYE 4 Ukunyanzeliswa Kwamalungelo Asiseko Kunye Nenkululeko Yoluntu 85

Ukunyanzeliswa kwamalungelo asiseko kunye nenkululeko yoluntu

(1) Nokuba ngowuphi umntu kwababantu balandelayo; ababalela—

(a)

noba ngubani ozifakela isicelo ngokwakhe;

(b)

noba ngubani ofaka isicelo emele omnye umntu ongakwaziyo ukuzimela ngokunokwakhe;

(c)

noba ngubani omele umbutho, okanye amava okanye iqela labantu abathile;

(d)

noba ngubani ofaka isicelo emele izimvo zabantu;

(e)

noba ngowuphi umbutho omele izimvo zabantu;

unelungelo lokuya nokuba kukweyiphi na inkundlaayokuxela ukuba ukunyanzekiswa kwamalungelo neenkululeko ezixelwe kwesiSahluko, akroboziwe okanye anganako ukukrobozeka, kwaye inkundla izakupha uncedo olufanelekileyookuquka ukubhatalwa kwemali yembonelelo kwabo bachazelekileyo ngokukrobozwa kwamalungelo lawo

(2) Ukuba umntu wophule uMthetho lonto ayithethi ukuba akanaukwazi ukuya kwinkundla ayokufuna ukuncedakala ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (1).

(3) Imithetho yeenkundla zonke kufuneka ibeke imigaqo ezakulandelwa xa kunoze kwenzeke ukuba umntu ofuna unceda ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (1), yaye lomithetho kufuneka iqinisekise ukuba—

(a)

ilungelo lokuya enkundleni ngaphantsi kwecandelwana(1) liyalandelwa;

(b)

ukulandelwa kwemigaqo, ukuquka nokukuqala kwayo kujongiwe;

(c)

nogama iinkundla zilandela imithetho elawula iinkundla, izinto imigaqo engabalulekanga ayilandelwa; kwaye

(d)

umntu onobuncutshe obuthile, ngemvumo yenkundla, angabanabo ubuhlobo nenkundla.

(4) Ukungabikho kwalemiththo kuthethwe ngayo kwicandelwana (3) akuthinteli ekubeni lemithetho ikucandelwana (1) ukuba inyanzekiswe yaye ixoxiswe enkundleni INXALENYE 5 Ukufakwa Komda Kumalungelo Asiseko Nakwiinkululeko Zoluntu 86

Umda wamalungelo neenkululeko

(1) Amalungelo asiseko neenkululeko ezixelwe kwesisahluko kufuneka asetyenziswe ngengqiqo nangonanzo olufanelekileyo lwamalungelo asiseko neenkululeko zabanye abantu.

(2) Amalungelo asiseko neenkululeko ezixelwe kwesiSahluko asenofakelwa imida kuphela ngokoMthetho wesicelo jikelele kwaye ukufakwa komda kube kulungile, kuqiqile, kuyimfuneko kwaye kwanesizathu kuluntu olunentando ngokucacileyo, kusemthethweni, kubalulekiswa isidima somntu, ulingano nenkululeko, kunanzwa lemiba elandelayo, equka—

(a)

uhlobo lwelungelo nenkululeko okuthethwa ngayo;

(b)

injongo yokufakwa komda welungelo, ngokukodwa, ukuba kubandakanya umdla kwezenkuselo, ukhuseleko loluntu, ucwangco, umdla woluntu, impilontle yoluntu, ukuhlelwa kweedolophi neminye imimimandla okanye umdla nje woluntu;

(c) ubumi nobungakanani bokuncitshiswa;

(d)

iimfuneko yokuba kubekho isiqinisekiso sokuqinisekisa ukunambithwa kwamalungelo neenkululeko nangubani na kungekho kuxhatshazwa kwamalungelo neenkululeko;

(e)

ubudlelwane phakathi kokuncitshiswa neyona njongo yako, ngokukodwa ukuba ingaba kwenza amalungelo neenkululeko ezifanelekileyo zifikeleleke; kwaye

(f)

ukuba ingaba kukho imiqobo engaphazamisa injongo yonciphiso.

(3) Akukho myalelo onganciphisa amalungelo alandelayo achazwe kwesiSahluko, kwaye akukho mntu ongawophula —

(a)

ilungelolobomi, ngaphandle kwakwiimeko ecaciswe kwicandelo 48;

(b)

ilungelo lesidima somntu;

(c)

ilungelo lokungatyakatywa okanye ukuxhatshazwa, ukuphathwa kakubi okanye ukuhlambalazwa okanye isohlwayo esikhupha isidima sommntu

(d)

ilingelo lokungakhobokiswa;

(e)

ilungelo lokuxoxiswa ngokufanelekileyo;

(f)

ilungelo lokufumana umyalelo we habeas corpus njengoko kunikwe kwicandelo 50(7)(a).

Imida yamalungelo ngexesha likaxakeka kawonke-wonke

(1) Ukwengezelela kwimida evunyelwe kwicandelo 86, amalungelo kunye neenkululeko ezichazwe kwiSahluko zingadluliselwa ngakumbi nguMthetho obhaliweyo onika amanyathelo okujongana neemeko ezivela ngexesha loxinzelelo loluntu, kodwa kuphelangokuvunyelwe lelicandelo kunye neShedyuli yesiBini

(2) UMthetho obhaliweyo ochazwe kwicandelwana ( 1 ) kunye nawuaphi na amanyathelo oMthetho athathwe phantsi lwalo myalelo, kufuneka ashicilelwe kwiGzethi.

(3) Nawuphi na umda obhaliweyo okhankaswe kwicandelwana (1) ubeka ilungelo elifanelekileyo okanye inkululeko esetyenziswe kwesiSahluko kufuneka ube mkhulu kuba kuyimfuneko ekhawulezileyo yemeko engxamisekileyo.

(4) Akukho Mthetho obonelela ngesibhengezo seemeko ezingxamisekileyo, kwaye akukho mithetho okanye omnye umlinganiselo owenziweyo ngenxa yesibhengezo esinjalo—

(a)

ukuhlawulela, okanye ukuvumela okanye ukugunyaza ukuhlawulwa, uRhulumente okanye amaziko kaRhulumente nakowuphi na umgangatho, okanye nawuphi na umntu, ngokubhekiselele nasiphi na isenzo esingekho emthethweni; okanye

(b) ukukhawulela naliphi na ilungelo okubhekiselelwe kulo kwicandelo 86(3), ugunyazise okanye uvumele naliphi na lamalungelo ukuba ophulwe. ISAHLUKO 5 Ulawulo Inxalenye1 INXALENYE 1 Ulawulo Olugunyazisiweyo 88

Ulawulo olugunyazisiweyo

(1) Igunya elilawulayo livela kubantu baseZimbabwe kwaye kufuneka lisetyenziswe ngokuhambelana naloMgaqo-siseko.

(2) Igunya elilawulayo laseZimbabwe lijongene noMongameli owenza oko, ngokubhekiselele kulo Mgaqo-siseko, kwiKhabinethi. ISAHLUKWANA2 Umongameli Noosekela Mongameli 89

U Mongameli

UMongameli yiNtloko yoMbuso noRhulumente kunye noMlawuli-jikelele wenkonzo yoMkhosi. 90

Imisebenzi kaMongameli

(1) UMongameli kufuneka agcine, aphendule, athobele kwaye awuhlonele loMgaqo-siseko njengoMthetho ophakamileyo weSizwe kwaye kufuneka aqinisekise ukuba loMgaqo-siseko kunye neminye imithetho igcinwa ngokwethembeka.

(2) UMongameli kufuneka enze oku—

(a)

akhuthaze ubunye kunye noxolo kulo lonke iLizwe ngenzuzo;

2013

(b)

aqaphela nokuhlonela iinjongo kunye nemilinganiselo yemfazwe yenkululeko;

(c)

aqinisekisa ukukhuselwa kwamalungelo oluntu kunye nenkululeko kunye nokulawulwa koMthetho; kwa

(d)

nokuhlonela ukuhlukahlukana kwabantu kunye noluntu lwaseZimbabwe.

Ukulungela ukunyulwa njengoMongameli neSekela-Mongameli

(1) Umntu unelungelo lokukhethwa njengo Mongameleli okanye iSekela likaMongameli ukuba—

(a)

ungummi waseZimbabwe ngokuzalwa okanye ngokwenzalana;

(b)

senelisile iminyaka engamashumi amane yobudala;

(c)

ungumhlali waseZimbabwe; kwaye

(d)

ubhalisile njengomvoti.

(2) Umntu akavumelekanga ukunyulelwa ubuMongameli okanye iSekela likaMongameli ukuba sele eseke waphatha isikhundla eso njengoMongameli okanye iSekela likaMongameli phantsi kwalo Mgaqo-siseko okwethemu ezimbini, nokokuba beziqhubekeka okanye zingaqhubekeki, kwaye njeenjongo yecandelwana iminyaka emithathu okanye engaphezulu ithathwa njengegcweleyo yexesha lolulawulo. 92

Unyulo lukaMongameli kunye neSekela-Mongameli

(1) Unyulo lukaMongameli kunye nooSekelaMongameli ababini kufuneka kwenziwe ngexesha elichazwe kwicandelo 158.

(2) Wonke umviwa okhethwe njengoMongameli kufuneka atyumbe abantu ababini ukuba bamisele ukukhethwa ngokubambisana naye njengamasekelaMongameli, kwaye omnye wabo abatyunjiweyo njengomviwa kusihlalo sobusekelaMongameli wokukuqala, kunye nomnye njengomviwa wakhe kwisihlalo sobusekelaMongameli wesibini.

(3) UMongameli kunye namasekela ache bakhethwa ngokubambisa ngabavoti ababhalisileyo kulo lonke elaseZimbabwe, kwaye yonyulo lwabo imiselwe nguMthetho woNyulo.

(4) Iimfuno zokubhalisa njengomvoti, kunyulo lukaMongameli kunye namasekelaMongameli zicacisiwe kwiShedyuli yeSine.

(5) Unyulo lukaMongameli kunye nosekelaMongameli kufuneka lwenzeke kunye nonyulo lwamalungu ePalamente, amabhungu akumaphondo kunye nezigqeba ezilawulayo kwinginqi. 93

Ukuphikisana nokunyulwa kukamongameli

(1) Ngokuxhomekeke kwelicandelo, nawuphi na umviwa onokukhathazeka ngeziphumo, angenza umngeni wokhetho lokunyulwa kukaMongameli okanye usekalaMongameli ngokufaka isikhalazo okanye isicelo kwiNkundla yoMgaqo-siseko zingadlulanga iintsuku ezisixhenxe emva komhla wokubhengezwa kweziphumo zonyulo.

(2) Unyulo lwesekelaMongameli lungaba nomngeni kuphela xa ngaba yena umviwa akafanelekanga ukhetho.

(3) Inkundla yoMgaqo-siseko kufuneka imamele kwaye iqinisekise isicelo phantsi kwecandelwana (1) kwisithuba seentsuku ezilishumu elinesine emva kokuba isikhalazo okanye isicelo sifakiwe kwaye isigqibo senkundla sinokugqibelela.

(4) Ekumiseni isikhalazo okanye isicelo phantsi kwecandelwana (1), iNkundla yoMgaqo-siseko iseno—

(a)

nokuxela ophumeleleyo;

(b)

kosula unyulo olo, luqalwe kutsha kungekegqithi iintsuku ezingamashumi asithandathu emva kwesimiselo sesikhalao; okanye

(c)

kwenza nawuphi na omnye umyallo ukuba ibone ufanelekile kwaye usamkeleka;

(5) Ukuba isikhalazo okanye isicelo phantsi kwecandelwana (1)—


(a)

inkundla yoMgaqo-siseko xa ithe yabeka ecaleni usuku lonyulo lokaMongameli, nonyulolamasekela omabaini kaMongameli luyanqunyaniswa ngoko nangoko;

(b)

inkundla yoMgaqo-siseko ibeke usuku lonyulo noba lelosekela Mongameli bobabini, ngokukhawulezileyo umongameli kufuneka atyumbe umntu okanye abantu ababini abanazo zonke iimfuneko ezifunekayo ukuba abe okanye babe ngosekela bakaMongameli.

Ukuqala umsebenzi kukaMongameli nooSekelaMongameli

(1) Abantu abonyulwe njengoMongameli kwa nooSekelaMongameli baqala lomsebenzi xa sebenze izifungo ngokulandelanayo kwabo,ezibekwe kwiShedyuli yesiThathu phambi kukaMgwebi oyiNtloko okanye kukamahluleli omdala—

(a)

ngosuku lwethoba bakumiselwe ukunyulwa; okanye

(b)

ukuba kuthe kwakho ukungavisisani malunga nokwamkelwa kweziphumo zonyulo, kwiyure ezingamashumi amane anesibhozo emva kokuba inkundla yoMgaqo-siseko ibaxele njengabaphumeleleyo ekhethweni.

(2) UMongameli ophetheyo uyaqhubeka ephethe kude kube uMongameli osanda kunyulwa ukuqala umsebenzi njengokuchazwe kwicandelwana (1).

(3) UsekelaMongameli othatha isihlalo sikaMongameli ngemva kokufa okanye ukushiya umsebenzi okanye ukususwa esihlalweni kukaMongameli, ukuqala umsebenzi ngemva kokuba senziswe izifungo phambi kuka Mgwebi oyiNtloko okanye mahluleli omdala okhoyo, njengokuxela kweShedyuli yesiThathu, izifungo ezo kufuneka azenze kwamsinyane ukuba kunokwenza kungegqithanga iiyure ezingamashumi amane nesibhozo emva kokuba isihlalo sikaMongameli sishiyeke singenamntu. 95

Ixesha elibekiweyo lokuba sesihlalweni kukaMongameli kunye nooSekela-Mongameli.

(1) Ixesha lokuba sesihlalweni sobuMongameli okanye oosekelaMongameli liqala ngalomhla kanye esenza izifungo zokuthatha umsebenzi njengokuchaza kwecandelo 94(1)(a) okanye 94(3).

(2) Ixesha lokuba sesihlalweni kukaMongameli okanye oosekela Mongameli liyaqhubeka kude abe—

(a)

abe uyawuyeka umsebenzi okanye uyasuswa esihlalweni; okanye

(b) ngemva kolunyulo wonyuliwe kwakhona ngokusemthethweni ukuba aqhubeke okanye konyulwe omnye uMongameli omtsha,

okanye ngaphandle kokuba ngokoMgaqo-siseko ixesha labo lokuba sesihlalweni yiminyaka emihlanu kwaye oko kuhambisana nobomi. 96

Ukurhoxa kuka Mongameli okanye uSekela-Mongameli

(1) UMongameli angarhoxa ngesaziso esibhalelwe uSomlomo, omele anike isaziso kuwonkewonke ngokushiya umsebenzi ngokukhawuleza, kwaye enjenjalo kungegqithanga iiyure ezingamashumi amane anesine okushushiywa komsebenzi.
