(2) UsekelaMongameli angashiya isikhundla sakhe ngesaziso esibhalelwe uSomlomo, omele anike isaziso kuwonkewonke ngokushiya umsebenzi ngokukhawuleza, kwaye enjenjalo kungehqithanga iiyure ezingamashumi amabini anesine okushiya umsebenzi. 97

Ukususwa kukaMongameli okanye iSekela likaMongameli kwi-ofisi

(1) I Senathi kunye neNdibano yeSizwe, ngesigqibo esisigxina esiphunyezwe ubuncinane kwisigamu sesibalo sabo bonke abangamalungu, singasombulula lo mbuzo ukuba ingaba uMongameli okanye iSekela likaMongameli kufanele asuswe esikhundleni ukuba —

(a)

utyolwa ngokungaziphathi kakuhle;

(b)

yoyisakala ukuthobela, ukuxhasa nokukhusela loMgaqo-siseko;

(c)

wophule ngesibomu lomnqophiso woMgaqosiseko; okanye

(d)

ukungakwazi ukwenza imisebenzi ye ofisi ngenxa yokungaphili ngokwasemzimbeni okanye ngokwengqondo, kwaye oko kufuneka kuphandwe ngokwelicandelo.

(2) Ekufikeleleni kwisigqibo sesisombululo ngokwecandelwana (1), ikomiti ebonangokuhamha kweMithetho neMimiselo kufuneka ityumbe ikomito ebambiseneyo ne Senethi kunye neNdibano yeSizwee qukethe amalungu alithoba abonakalisa ukubunjwa kwasePalamente, ukuphanda ukususwa esikhundleni uMongameli okanye iSekela likaMongameli njengoko kunjalo.

(3) Xa—


(a)

ikomiti yobambiswano etyunjwe ngokwemigaqo echazwe kwicandelwana

(2) ivumelana nokususwa esikhundleni kukaMongameli okanye iSekelaMongameli; kwaye

(b)

amaDoda kunye neNdibano yeSizwe, ukuba ngokubambiseneyo isibini sesithathu (2/3) samalungu onke aye apasisa isigqibo sokususwa kukaMongameli okanye iSekelaMongameli esikhundleni, ngokunjalo uMongameli okanye iSekelaMongameli uyasuswa esikhundleni ngokukhawulezileyo.

Ukhuseleko lukaMongameli ekutshutshisweni

(1) Ukuba usesesihlalweni, uMongameli akakwazi ukuba axoxiswe yinkundla yamatyala nayiphi na ngamatyala awenzileyo okanye angawenzanga yena ubuqu bakhe.

(2) Inkundla zamatyala zingamxoxisa umntu owake waba sesikhundleni sobuMongameli ngamatyala awenza engekabi kwesosikhundla okanye awenze ngexesha ekwesosikhundla sokuba nguMongameli.

(3) Izigqibo ezinokwenza namatyala ezikweliti ezenziwe nguMongameli engekabi kwesisikhundla okanye ngexesha ebekusikhundla sobuMongameli zike zibekwe ecaleni okwangoku esasehlalweni sobuMongameli.

(4) Amatyala abekwa umntu owake waba nguMongameli awenzayo okanye angawenzanga ngexesha engekabi sesikhundleni okanye ngexesha sekwesisikhundla sokuba nguMongameli, unako ukusebenzisa nabuphi ubunqina ngokwethembeka. 99

Iimisebenzi yooSekelaMongameli

OosekelaMongameli bancedisana noMongameli ekwenzeni imisebenzi yakhe neminye imisebenzi abanokuyiphiwa ebalela ukukhokhela noba leliphi isebe , iziko okanye uMthetho wasePalamente abanokuwuphiwa nguMongameli 100 IBambela likaMongameli

(1) Ngalo lonke ixesha apho uMongameli asuke engekho khona eZimbabwe okanye engakwazi ukwenza umsebenzi wakhe kakuhle ngenxeni yokungaphili okanye ezinye izizathu zimbi, loomisebenzi kufuneka yenziwe—

(a)

liSekela likaMongameli lokuqala;

(b) ukuba ngaba iSekela likaMongameli elokuqala alikwazi ukuthabatha lomsebenzi, iSekela likaMongameli lesibini lizakwenza lomsebenzi; okanye;

(c)

ukuba akukho sekela likaMongameli elinokuwenza loomsebenzi, ngokoMphathiswa—

(i) obekelwe isithuba esinjalo nguMongameli; okanye

(ii) onyulwe yiKhabinethi, ukuba akukho Mphathiswa obekhethelwe loomsebenzi nguMongameli njengokuchazwe kumhlathana (i).

(2) Ngaphandle ngokwezigqibo ezenziwe sisinintsi samalungu eKhabinethi, umntu owenza imisebenzi kaMongameli njengokuxelwe kwicandelwana (1) lomntu akanako ukusebenzisa amandla obuMongameli —

(a)

ukuthuma uMkhosi wezokhuselo;

(b)

ukwenza izigqibo nezivumelwano namazwe ngamazwe;

(c)

ukutyumba nokurhoxiswa ukutyunjwa kukasekela likaMongameli, uMphathiswa, sekela li kaMphathiswa okanye;

(d) ukutyumba okanye ukutyumba ngokutsha imisebenzi kooSekela Mongameli, uMphathiswa okanye sekelaMphathiswa, ukuquka ukulawula koMthetho wasePalamente okanye noba ngowaliphi isebe okanye ukosula nayiphi imisebenzi enokuba njalo. 101 Ukungenwa kwesikhundla ngemva kokufa, ukushiya umsebenzi okanye ukoyisakala kokusebenza kukaMongameli okanye iSekela lika Mongameli

(1) Ukuba uMongameli uye wafa, washiya umsebenzi okanye wasuswa esikhundleni—

(a)

usekelaMongameli wokukuqala nguye othatha isikhundla kude kufike ekugqibeleni kwexesha lokuba sesikhundleni;

(b)

usekelaMongameli wesibini uthathaisikhundla sokuba liSekela Mongamelei lokukuqala kude kufike ekugqibeleni kwexesha lokuba sesikhundleni;

(c)

ekuthabatheni isikhundla sokuba nguMongameli, owayesakuba liSekela Mongameli lokukuqala kufuneka atyumbe umntu onemfuneko

ezipheleleyo zokuba abe liSekela Mongameli lesibini kude kufike ekugqibeleni kwexesha lokuba sesikhundleni.

(2) Xa usekela Mongameli wokukuqala ethe wafa, wayeka umsebenzi okanye wasuswa—

(a)

iSekela likaMongameli elesibini lithabatha isikhundla sokuba liSekela likaMongameli elokukuqala; kwaye

(b)

uMongameli ngokukhawulezileyo kufuneka atyumbe umntu onemfuneko ezipheleleyo zokuba liSekela likaMongameli lesibini kude kufike ekugqibeleni kwexesha lokuba sesikhundleni.

102 Ukuvuzwa kukaMongameli kunye nooSekela-Mongameli

(1) UMongameli kunye nooSekelaMongameli kufuneka babenerholo, iimali zezibonelelo, ipensheni kunye neminye imivuzo echazwe kuMthetho wasePalamente.

(2) Irholo nezibonelelo zikaMongameli kunye nooSekelaMongameli kufuneka zihlawulwe kwiNgxowa-mali yeNgeniso.

(3) Umntu osephumile kwisihlalo sobuMongameli okanye esokuba ngusekelaMongameli kufuneka afumane oku —

(a)

umhlala-phantsi olingana nerholo likaMongameli ohleliyo okanye iSekela Mongameli, njengko kunjalo; kwaye

(b) izibonelelo ezinjalo kunye neminye imivuzo emiselwe phantsi koMthetho wePalamente. 103 Mongameli kunye nooSekela-Mongameli kwa nabo babeke babamba ezizikhundla abavumelekanga ukuba babe kwesinye isihlalo okanye benze omnye umsebenzi eburhulumenteni UMongameli nooSekelaMongameli kwanabani owake wabasesihlalweni sikaMongameli okanye esikasekela Mongameli akavumelekanga ukuba enze omnye umsebenzi wakwaRhulumente ngelixesha esesihlalweni okanye ngelixesha esafumana umhlala-phantsi kuRhulumente owokuba nguMongameli okanye iSekela likaMongameli; njengoko kunjalo. INXALENYE 3 Abaphathiswa, Sekela-Mphathiswa N e khabinethi 104 Ukutyunjwa kwabaPhathiswa naBasekeli babaPhathiswa

(1) UMongameli utyumba abaPhathiswa abanike amagunya nemisebenzi yabo, ukuquka ukulandelwa koMthetho wasePalamente okanye nowaliphi na isebe, kodwa ke uMongameli usengaipha uxanduva lokulawula uMthetho wasePalamente okanye isebe okanye iziko.

(2) UMongameli angatyumba oosekela babaPhathiswa ukuba bancedisane nabaPhathiswa kwimisebenzi yabo.

(3) AbaPhathiswa nooSekela babaPhathiswa batyunjwa kumalungu eSenethi okanye kumalungu eNdibano yeSizwe, kodwa ke babekhoyo abahlanu abatyunjwa ngokwekhono kumsebenzi wabo, abo bangatyunjwa ngaphandle kwasePalamente.

(4) Ekutyunjweni kwabaPhathiswa kwa nosekela babaPhathiswa, uMongameli kufuneka alinganise ngokwendawo basuka kuzo kwanangeSini.

(5) AbaPhathiswa kunye nooSekela babaPhathiswa abangengo malungu ePalamente bangahlala bathethe kodwa abanako ukuvota kwindlu yeSenethi kwiNdibano yeSizwe.

(6) Phambi kokuthabatha uxanduva , amandla nomsebenzi, umntu otyunjelwe ukuba nguMphathiswa kufuneka afunge njengoko kuchaziweyo kwiShedyuli yesiThathu. 105 IKhabhinethi

(1) Kukho ikhabhinethi ebalela uMongameli, njengentloko yeKhabhinethi, oosekela bakaMongameli kunye nabaPhathiswa njengokutyunjwa kwabo nguMongameli ukuba babe ngamalungu eKhabhinethi.

(2) Iindibano zeKhabhinethi zikhokhelwa nguMongameli, okanye ukuba akekho kube liSekela likaMongameli, ukuba alikho naye ibenguMphathiswa oxelwe kwicandelo100(1)(c). 106 Ukuziphatha kooSekela-Mongameli, abaPhathiswa nooSekelaMphathiswa

(1) Wonke usekela kaMongameli , uMphathiswa kwa nosekela Mphathiswa kufuneka baziphathe ngokwendlela yokuziphatha emiselwe kuMgaqo-siseko.

(2) Oosekela Mongameli, abaPhathiswa kwa nosekela babaPhathiswa akuvumelekanga ukuba benze oku besesesihlalweni—

(a)

ukwenza nawuphi na omnye umsebenzi obabhatalayo;

(b) benze nangayiphi na indlela engahambisaniyo nezikhundla zabo, okanye bazibeke esichengeni sayo nayiphi imeko engathi ibangele umngcipheko wokungquzulana koxanduva lwabo lwaseburhulumenteni neemfuno zabo, okanye.

(c)

basebenzise izikhundla zabo, okanye naluphina olunye ulwazi olusezandleni zabo besenzela ukutyebisa, okanye ukulungelwa ngendlela engeyiyo yakh nawuphi na omnye umntu.

(3) UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka umisele indlela yokuziphatha kwabo oosekela bakaMongameli, abaPhathiswa nooSekela babaPhathiswa. 107 Uxanduva looSekela-Mongameli, abaPhathiswa kwa nooSekela babaPhathiswa

(1) Ngokuxhomekeke kulo Mgaqo-siseko nabani ongusekelaMongameli, Mphathiswa kunye nesekela Mphathisa unoxanduva lokubambisana kunye nabanye, kwanoMongameli ekwenzeni imisebenzi yakhe.

(2) Nabani ongusekelaMongameli, Mphathiswa nonguSekela-Mphathiswa kufuneka aye kwiindibano zePalamente kunye nezekomitii yasebuPalamenteni khona ukuze bayokuziphendulela imibuzo imibuzo ejongene nabo ubuqu okanye eyobambiswano. 108 Ukumiselwa kwe-ofisi yabaPhathiswa kwa nosekela babaPhathiswa

(1) I-ofisi kaMphathiswa okanye eyesekela li kaMphathiswa ishiyeka ingenamntu xa—

(a)

2013

uMongameli eye wamsusa kwesosikhundla;

(b) eyewashiya umsebenzi ngokubhalela uMongameli isaziso;

(c)

uMongameli omntsha eye wangena esikhundleni sokuba nguMongameli;

(2) Ngokuxhomekeke kwicandelwana(4), uMphathiswaokanye iSekela li kaMphathiswa elalililungu lePalamente eetyunjwa ukuba abe nguMphathiswa okanye iSekela li kaMphathiswangokunjalo uyasishiya isikhundla sokuba ukuba uthe washiya eso sokuba lilungu lePalamente.

(3) Ngokuxhomekeke kwicandelwana(4), uMphathiswaokanye iSekela li kaMphathiswa elalililungu lePalamente eetyunjwa ukuba abe nguMphathiswa okanye iSekela li kaMphathiswangokunjalo uyasishiya isikhundla sokuba ukuba kuthe kwanemeko enokwenza oxuthwe isikhundla sokuba lilungu lePalamente.

(4) Ngokuxhomekeke kuloMgaqo-siseko, ukuba iPalamente ithe yachithwa, abaPhathiswa kwa nosekela babaPhathiswa bayaqhubeka besezikhundleni zabo ukuba uMongameli omntsha onyuliweyo akakaqalisi umsebenzi wakhe ngemva konyulo jikelele. 109 Ivoti yokususwa uRhulumente

(1) ISenethi kunye neNdibano yeSizwe, ngesivumelwano esidibeneyo esogqithiswe sisibini esithathwini senani labo lobulungu liphelele, asenokwegqithisela ivoti yokungathembi kuRhulumente.

(2) Isisombululo sokusombulula ivoti engathembekanga singasuswa kuphela xa—

(a)

uSomlomo enikwe iintsuku ezisinxhenxe zesaziso sokunyuswa;

(b) isaziso sokunyuswa sisayinwe kwisiqingatha samalungu onke awe Ndibano yeSizwe

(3) Iinjongo yokunika ivoti yokungabinathemba kurhulumente—

(a)

kufuneka ixoxwe izindluzombini zePalamente zidibene phakathi kweentsuku ezingamashumi amabini nanye emva kokuba uSomlomo efumene isaziso sokunyuswa;

(b) kufuneka bavotelwe kwiintlanganiso ezisixhenxe ezilandelelanayo emva kokuba zisusiwe; kungenjalo iba yilahleko.

(4) Apho iPalamente idlulisa ukuzithemba kuRhulumente, uMongameli kufuneka emva kweentsuku ezilishumi elinane emva kokuvota —

(a)

asuse bonke abaPhathiswa nooSekela babaPhathiswa ezikhundleni, ngaphandle kokuba babe besebevele sebeshiyile umsebenzi ngenxa yeziphumo, atyumbe abanye kwezozikhundla; okanye

(b) chitha ipalamente, kuthi ngemva kweentsuku ezingamashumi alithoba abizele unyulo jikelele

(5) Ukuba uMongameli akenzi ngokuhambelana necandelwana (4) kwisithuba seentsuku ezilishumi elinanye emva kokupasa kwevoti kuRhulumente, iPalament iyachithwa.

INXALENYE 4 Imisebenzi Yolawulo 110 Imisebenzi kaMongameli neKhabhinethi

(1) UMongameli unamagunya namandla awaphiwa nguloMgaqo-siseko kwanoMthetho wasePalamente okanye nawuphi na omnye uMthetho, ukuquka leyo efunakalayo ukuba iimisebenzi yeNtloko yeSizwe ifezekiswe.

(2) Ngokuxhomekeke kuloMgaqo-siseko, uMongameli unegunya—

(a)

wokuvuma nokusayina iiBhili;

(b)

ukwegqithisela kwiNkundla yoMgaqo-siseko iiBhili khona ukuze afumane iingcebiso ngoMthetho lowo;

(c)

wokubiza iindibano engxamisekileyo yamalungu eNdibano yeSizwe, Senethi okanye ePalamente ukuba kuzoxoxwa iindaba ezibalulekileyo;

(d)

wokutyumba abantu kwizikhundla njengokuchazwe kuMgaqo-siseko, oko kungumsebenzi kaMongameli;

(e) wokumemezela ukubakubekho ulunyulo ngokwezimiso zaloMgaqosiseko;

(f)

wokumemezela ukubakwenziwe iireferendamu nangawuphi na umba unokwenza nooMthetho;

(g) ukuthumela uMkhosi wezokhusela ukuba uyokugcina ucwangco;

(h) ukupha iimbasa nezidanga;

(i)

ukutyumba oonozakuzaku, amaqela wenkokheli ezinamandla okuthatha izigqibo kwezoMthetho, abameli beLizwe kumanye amazwe; kwa

(j)

ukwamkela abameli bakwamanye kweli.

(3) Ngokuxhomekeke kuloMgaqo-siseko, iKhabhinethi inoxanduva—

(a)

ukukhokhela iinkqubo zikaRhulumente;

(b) wokwenza iimisebenzi kaRhulumente ePalamente;

(c)

ukulungisa, ukuyila nokusebenzisa iimithetho;

(d)

ukulungisa nokusebenzisa imigaqo; kwa

(e)

ukucebisa uMongameli.

(4) Ngokuxhomekeke kuloMgaqo-siseko uMongameli angatyikitya izigqibo zovisiswano namanye kunye neemibutho yakumamazwe ngamazwe.

(5) Izigqibo zikaMongameli kufuneka zibhalwe phantsi ukuba zingokoMthetho.

(6) Ekuthabatheni iimisebenzi yakhe, uMongameli kufuneka acetyiswe yiKhabhinethi, ngaphandle kokuba yimisebenzi yakhe exelwe kwicandelwana (2) phezulu. 111 Imfazwe nokuthula

(1) UMongameli unamandla wokubiza imfazwe nawokugcina uxolo, kwaye kufuneka abikele ISenethi kwa neNdibano yeSizwe kungegqithanga iintsuku ezizxhenxe zokuhlala kwazo ezizindlu ekubeni esebenzise elogunya.

(2) Isenethi neNdibano yeSizwe , ngesigqibo esidibeneyo esipasiswe sisibini sesithathu (2/3) samalungu onke ePalamente , bangasikhaba isigqibo sokuba kubekho imfazwe.

(3) Ukuba isigqibo sokuba ichithwe imfazwe siyesangqinwa , uMongameli kufuneka enze konke anokukwenza ukuba aphume kwimfazo kwaye aqinisekise ukuba abemi baseZimbabwe bakhuselekile kunye nezixhobo. 112 Amandla okuxolela

(1) UMongameli, ngemva kokubonisana neKhabinethi, unako ukusebenzisa amandla okuxolela, okukukuthi —

(a)

ukuxolela nawuphi na umntu obekwa ityala lokwaphula noba ngowuphi na uMthetho,

(b) angamisa isohlwayo noba sesaliphi ityala okwexesha elithile ngezizathu ezithile,

(c)

ukupha isohlwayo esilula noba kulweliphi na ityala, okanye

(d) ukumisa isigwebo okwexesha elithile okanye ukosula sonke isigwebo okanye inxalenye yesigwebo noba sesaliphi ityala, kwaye usengabeka iimeko zokupha uxolelo, ukosulwa kwesigwebo,, ukuthotywa kwa nokumiswa kwesigwebo.

(2) Ukuba umntu ongumhlali waseZimbabwe uye wabanjwa kwelinye iLizwe ngezityholo zokwephula uMthetho kweloLizwe, uMongameli unawo amandla okuba atsho ukuba elotyala alibekwayo alithathwa njengetyala ngokoMgaqo-siseko waseZimbabwe.

(3) Ukunikwa koxolo okanye ukumiswa kwesigwebo okanye ukosulwa kwesigwebo kufuneka kubhengezwe kwi Gazethi. 113 Iimeko zikaxakeka kawonke-wonke

(1) UMongameli unokumemezela kwiGazethi ukuba kukho iimeko zongxunguphalo loluntu ezikhona kumaphondo okanye kwimimandla ethiile yezwe laseZimbabwe

(2) Isibhengezo semeko engxamisekileyo yongxunguphalo siphela ngemva kweentsuku ezilishumi elinane ukukuqala ngomhla wokupapashwa kwesibhengezo kwiGazethi ngaphandle, ngaphambi kokuphela kweloxesha , isimemezelo sivunyiwe ebuncinaneni sisibini kusithathu sobulungu bePalamente sendibano edibeneyo eye Senethi neNdibano yeSizwe.

(3) Ukuba iPalamente iyachithwa ngexesha leentsuku ezilishumi elinesine emva kokuba imeko yongxunguphalo loluntu iye yavakaliswa, isimemezelo siyeka ukusebenza emva kweentsuku ezingamashumi amabini, ukususela ngomhla wokupapashwa kwesibhengezo kwiGazethi, ngaphandle kokuba ngeloxesha ivunyiweyo luluninzi lwawo onke amalungu ePalamente entsha kwindibano edibeneyo eye Senethi kunye neNdibano yeSizwe.

(4) Isibhengezo semeko yongxunguphalo esivunyelwene phantsi kwicandelwana

(2) okanye (3) sihlala sisebenza okwenyanga ezintathu ukususela kumhla apho isimemezelo sapapashwayo kwiGazethi ngaphandle kokuba sichithwe ngaphambilini okanye saphela.

(5) Ukuba ukuvakaliswa kweemeko zongxunguphalo loluntu akuvunyelwanga emva kokuqwalaselwa yiPalamente, okanye ukuba nangaziphi isizathu asiqwalaselwanga yiPalamente phakathi kwexesha elikhankanywe kwelicandelo, uMongameli kufuneka kwiintsuku ezisixhenxe, ngokumenyezelwa kwiGazethi, arhoxise isimemezelo.

(6) Ukuba, ngesigqibo esadluliselwa luluninzi lwamalungu akhona kwindibano edibeneyoeyeSenethi kunye neNdibano yeSizwe, iPlamente izakugqiba ukuba isimemezelo songxunguphalo loluntu—

(a)

siqhubeke okwexesha elithile elingegqithi iinyanga ezintathu, ngaphandle kokulibazisa uMongameli kufuneka ,

Ngokumenyezelwe kwiGazethi, andise isibhengezo malunga neloxesha elongeziweyo;

(b)

kufuneka ichithwe okanye kufanele ifake isicelo kwinginqi encinci, uMongameli kufuneka ngaphandle kokulibazisa, ngokumenyezelwa kwiGazethi, arhoxise isimemezelo okanye ukubonelela ukuba isimemezelosihambelana nalondawo incinci.

(7) INkundla yoMgaqo-siseko, ngokusetyenziswa kwanangubani na, inokumisela—

(a) ukuqinisekiswa kweemeko zongxunguphalo loluntu;

(b) ukongezwa noba sesiphi na isibhengezo songxunguphalo loluntu.

(8) Nayiphi na inkundla inokumisela ukuqinisekiswa kwanoba ngowuphi na uMthetho owenziwe, okanye neliphina inyathelo elithathwe, ngenxa yokubhengezwa kwemeko yongxunguphalo loluntu. INXALENYE 5 Igqwetha-Jikelele 114 Igqwetha-jikelele

(1) Kukho iGqwetha-Jikelele elityunjwa nguMongameli.

(2) Umntu otyunjwe njengeGqwetha-Jikelele ukuqala umsebenzi ngemva kokuba enze izifungo zokwethembeka phambi kukaMongameli, okanye umntu ogunyaziswe nguMongameli ngokwenza izifungo ezikuShedyuli yesiThathu.

(3) Umntu ufanelekile ukuqeshwa njengeGqwetha-Jikelele ukuba ufanelekile ukuqeshwa njengoMgwebi kwiNkundla ePhakamileyo.

(4) Imisebenzi yeGqwetha-Jikelele —

(a)

ukuba abe ngumncebisi wezoMthetho kuRhulumente;

(b)

ukumela uRhulumente kwimimiselo yoluntu kunye noMgaqo-siseko;

(c)

ukuqulunqa uMthetho egameni likaRhulumente;

(d)

ukuphakamisa, ukukhusela nokugcina uMthetho wolawulo kwa nokhuseleko lomdla woluntu; kwaye

(e)

ukwenza eminye imisebenzi enokuphiwa iGqwetha-Jikelele eyiphiwa nguMthetho wasePalamente;

kwaye iGqwetha-Jikelel lingawenza lomsebenzi ngokwakhe okanye asebenzisane namanye amagosa amancinci asebenzisana nawo ngaphantsi kwemiyalelo yakhe uGqwetha –Jikelele.

(5) Gqwetha-Jikelele anga—

(a)

ngena kwiindibano zeKhabinethi, kodwa akanakuthatha inxaxheba ekuvoteni;

(b)

hlala kwaye athethe kwindibano yeSenethi kwa neyeNdibano yeSizwe, kodwa angathathinxaxheba ekuvoteni; kwaye

(c)

ngemvumo yenkundla efanelekileyo, angahlala njengomhlobo wenkundla leyo ixoxisayo oko kubandakanya noRhulumente.

115 Ukususwa esikhundleni kweGqwetha elikhulu UMongameli naninina angakwazi ukususa iGqwetha elikhulu esikhundleni.

ISAHLUKO 6 Inkundla Yowiso-Mthetho INXALENYE 1 Igunya Lenkundla Yowiso-Mthetho. 116 Inkundla yoWiso-mthetho. Inkundla yowiso Mthetho eZimbabwe ibandakanya iPalamente noMongameli esebenza ngokumiswe sesi sahluko. 117 Ukuma nobukhulu begunya lenkundla yowiso Mthetho.

(1) Igunya lenkundla yowiso Mthetho eZimbabwe lithathelwe ebantwini kwaye lingaphantsi, lisetyenziswe ngulomgaqo siseko wenkundla yowiso Mthetho.

(2) Igunya lenkundla yoMthetho linikezela kuwiso amandla—

(a)

okwenza izilungiso kulomgaqo siseko ngokungqinelana necandelo 328;

(b) okwenza imithetho yoxolo, uncwangco nolawulo olulungileyo eZimbabwe kwaye;

(c)

nokunikezela amandla oMthetho oyamileyo phezu kwelinye isebe okanye igunya ngokungqinelana necandelo 134. INXALENYE 2 iPalamente

118 IPalamente IPalamente iquka Isenethi kwaye neNkundla yoWiso Mthetho. 119 Indima yePalamente

(1) IPalamente kufuneka ikhusele loMgaqo Siseko kwaye ikhuthaze ulawulo lwedemokhrasi eZimbabwe.

(2)IPalamente inamandla okuqinisekisa ukuba izibonelelo zaloMgaqo Siseko zigciniwe kwaye uRhulumente namaziko onke nearhente zikarhulumente kwinqanaba zonke zenze ngokomgaqo nangemfuno zezwe.

(3)Kuba injongo kwicandelwana (2), amaziko onke nearhente zika rhulumente nobuzwe kwinqanaba zonke zingaphantsi kolawulo lwePalamente.

INXALENYE 3 Isenethi 120 Ukwakhiwa kweSenethi.

(1) ISenethi iqulethe amalungu asisibhozo kuthi lawo—

(a)

asisithandathu onyulwe kumaphondo ngokumelwa ngokulungelelana zihambelana necandelwana (2);

(b) ishumi elinesthandathu ziiNkosi ezenyulwe yinkundla yamaphondo yeenkosi kwiphondo ngalinye ngaphandle kwamaphondo angombaxa ehlukanisa izwe laseZimbabwe;

(c)

uMongameli neSekela Mongameli weBunga lenkosizeSizwe; kwaye

(d) ezimbini zanyulwe ngokwendlela emiselweyo nguMthetho woNyulo ukumela abantu abakhubazekileyo.

(2) Unyulo lweSenethi kufuneka luqutywe ngokungqinelana noMthetho woNyulo okufuneka uqiniseke ukuba iiSenethe ekubhekiselwe kwicandelwana (1)

(a) zonyulwe ngokulungelelana ko luhlu leqela—

(a)

apho esekelwe ziivoti ezifunyanwe ngumgqatswa omele umbutho weze politiki kwiphondo ngalinye kunyulo jikelele lwamalungu eNdlu yowiso Mthetho; kwaye

(b) a b a g q a t s w a a b a n g a m a d o d a n a b a n g o m a m a b a d w e l i s w e ngokutshintshiselanayo kuthi uluhlu ngalinye likhokhelwe ngugqatswa ongumama. 121 Iziqinisekiso nokunqunyanyiswa konyulwa kweSenethi

(1) umntu ufanelekile ukwanyulwa njenge Senethi kubhekiswe kwicandelwana 120(1)(a) okanye (d) xa—

(a)

ebhalisile ukuba ngumvoti, kwaye

(b) uneminyaka engamashumi amane obudala; Ngaphandle xa enqunyanyisiwe ngaphantsi kwecandelwano (4) okanye (5).

(2) Umntu ufanelekile unyulwa njengenkokheli yeSenethi ngokubhekiswe kwicandelwana 120(1)(b) xa—

(a)

ebambe iwofisi yeNkosi; kwaye

(b) ebhalisile ukuba ngumvoti; ngaphandle xa enqunyanyisiwe ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (4)okanye (5).

(3) umntu ufanelekile unyulwa njengeSenethi ngokubhekiwse kwicandalwana 120(1)(d) xa engunmtu okhubazekileyo ngokuchazwe nguMthetho woNyulo ngaphandle xa enqunyanyiswe ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (4) okanye (5)

(4) Umntu uyanqunyanyiswa kunyulo ekubeni yiSenethi xa—

(a)

enqunyanyiswe ngaphantsi kweShedyuli yeSine yokubhalisela ukuba ngumvoti; okanye

(b) kungegqithanga iminyaka emihlanu phambi konyulo, kufuneka aphume kwisihlalo seSenethi okakanye iNkundla yeNdibano ngokuyelelana.

(5) Umntu uyanqunyanyiswa kolonyulo kwiSenethi xa elilungu lePalamente. 122 UMongameli weSenethi

(1) Kwintlanganiso yokuqala emva kolonyulo jikelele kwaye ngaphi kwenqubo neyayiphina inkqubo iNkundla yeSenethi kufuneka yonyule igosa elongameleyo elaziwa ngokuba nguMongameli weSenethi.

(2)Sawube kukho isithuba kwiwofisi kaMongameli weSenethi , iSenethi ngaphndle kokulibazisa mayonyule umntu ozakuvala esosithuba.

(3)Umntu ufanelekile unyulwa njengoMongameli Senethi xa ebenguSenethi okanye efanelekile unyulwa abelilungu leSenethi..

(4) Unyulo kuwofisi kaMongameli weSenethi liqhutywa nguNobhala wePalamente ngaphantsi kolawulo lweKomishni yoNyulo yaseZimbabwe kusetyenziswa unyulo oluyimfihlo ngokungqinelelana nemithetho esisigxina kwaye iziphumo kufuneka zibhengezwe kuphumakunye.

(5)Ngaphambi kokuqalisa umsebenzi wakhe, uMongameli weSenethi kufuneka enze isifungo sentembeko phambi kweJaji eyiNtloko okanye ijaji ephezulu ngokumiswe kwiShedyuli yesiThathu.

(6) ISenethi enyulwe ukuba nguMongameli weNkundla weSenethi iyayeka ukuba yiSenethi kwaye kufuneka ashiye isikhundla ngokungqinelelana noMthetho woloNyulo.

(7) UMongameli weSenethi angarhoxa ngokwazisa ngobuso inkundla yeSenethi okanye xa inkundla ingahlalanga enze isaziso esibhaliweyo kuNobhala wePalamente.

(8) UMongameli weSenethi kufuneka ashiye iwofisi yakhe—

(a)

kwilanga apho iSenethi ihlale okukuqala emva konyulo jikelele;

(b) phezu kokwamkela nayiphi iwofisi kawonkewonke okanye aqeshwe kwenye indawo;

(c)

phezu kokuba lilungu lePalamente okanye uSomlomo wePalamente;

(d) phezu kokuba liSekela Mongameli, uMpathiswa, okanye iSekela Mpathiswa;

(e)

xa kungavela iimeko ezimnyanzelayo ukuba ashiye isikhundla sakhe xa ebeyiSenethi okanye;

(f)

xa isigqibo sokususwa kwakhe kwiwofisi sivunywe ngezimbini esithathwini enani elipheleyo lamalungu eSenethi.

123 ISekela-Mongameli weSenethi

(1) Msinyane emveni kokonyula uMongameli weSenethi kulandela unyulo jikelele, iSenethi kufuneka yonyule enye yeSenethi ukuba liSekela Mongameli weSenethi.

(2) Naninina xa kunesithuba kwiwofisi kaSekela Mongameli weSenethi inkundla yeSenethi ingalibazisanga mayonyule umntu ozakuvala esosithuba.

(3) Unyulo kwiwofisi kaSekela Mongameli weSenethi luqhutywa nguNobhala wePalamente ngaphantsi kolawulo lweKomishini yoNyulo besebenzisa unyulo olufihlakeleyo kwaye iziphumo zibhengezwe kuphumakunye.

(4)Ngaphambi kokuqalisa umsebenzi wakhe uSekela Mongmeli weSenethi kufuneka enze isifungo sentembeko phambi kweJaji eyiNtloko okanye ijaji ephezulu ngokumiswe yiShedyuli yesiThathu.

(5) USekela Mongameli weSenethi angarhoxa ngokwazisa ngobuso inkundla yeSenethi okanye xa inkundla ingahlalanga enze isaziso esibhaliweyo kuNobhala wasePalamente.

(6) ISekela Mongameli weSenethi angashiya iwofisi yakhe—

(a)

phezu kokuyeka ukuba yiSenethi;

(b) phezu kokuba siSithethi sasePalamente

(c)

phezu kokuba nguSekela Mongameli, uMphathiswa okanye uSekela Mphathiswa;

(d) xa isigqibo sokususwa kwakhe kwiwofisi sivunye sisibini esithathwini kwinani lonke lamalungu eSenethi, INXALENYE 4 Inkundla Yesizwe 124 UKwakhiwa kweNkundla yeSizwe

(1) Inkundla yezwe ibandakanya—

(a)

amalungu angamakhulu amabini aneshumi anyulwe ngevoti efihlakeleyo kumakhulu amabini aneshumi enqila iZimbabwe eyahlukaniswe ngazo, kwaye;

(b) kusukela kwikota ezimbini zePalamente zelanga elimisiweyo amashumi asisithandathu omama, abathandathu kwinqila ngayinye bonyulwe ngaphantsi kweqela lezepolitiki yokumelwa ngolungelelwano esekelwe zivoti ezifunyanwe ngumgqatswa omele iqela lezepolitiki kunyulo jikelele amalungu enqila zophondo.

(2) Unyulo lwamalungu eNkundla yeSizwe kufuneka luqhutywe ngokungqinelana noMthetho woNyulo.

(3) Isiqinisekiso sokubhalisa njengomvoti kwaye uvota kunyulo lwamalungu eNkundla yeSizwe. Zisekiwe kwiShedyuli yeSine. 125 IsiQinisekiso nokunqunyanyiswa kunyulo kwiNkundla yeSizwe

(1) Umntu ukulungele ukuvotelwa njengeLungu leNkudla yeSizwe xa—

(a)

ebhalisile ukuba ngumvoti; kwaye

(b) eneminyaka engamashumi amabini nanye obudala; ngaphandle xa enqunyanyisiwe ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (2) or (3).

(2) umntu uyanqunyanyiswa ekonyulweni ukuba liLungu leNkundla yeSizwe xa—

(a)

enqunyanyiswe ngaphantsi kweShedyuli yeSine yokubhalisela ukuba ngumvoti ; okanye

(b) kwiminyaka emihlanu phambi konyulo kufuneka ashiye isikhundla sakhe kwiSenethi okanye kwiNkundla yeSizwe ngokungqinelana necandelwana 129(1)(i) sawube efunyaniswe enetyala.

(3) umntu uyanqunyanyiswa kunyulo kwiNkundla yeSizwe xa eliLungu lasePalamente. 126 ISithethi seNkundla yeSizwe

(1) kwintlanganiso yokukuqala emva konyulo kwaye ngaphambi kwenkqubo zeNkundla yeSizwe kufuneka bonyule igosa elongamelayo elizakwaziwa njenge Sithethi.

(2) Xa kukho isithuba somsebenzi kwiwofisi kaSithethi, iNkundla yeSizwe ingalibazisanga kufuneka yonyule umntu ozakuvala esosithuba .

(3) Umntu ufanelekile unyulwa njengeSithethi okanye xa ebeliLungu leNkundla yeSizwe okanye ukulungele ukonyulwa ukubaliLungu leNkundla yeSizwe.

(4) Unyulo kwiwofisi kaSithethi kufuneka liqutywe nguNobhala wasePalamente ngaphantsi kolawulo lweKhomishini yonyulo yaseZimbabwe lusenziwa ngaphantsi kwemfihlo ngokungqinelelana nemithetho esisingxina kwaye neziphumo zibhengezwe ngokuphumakunye.

(5) Ngaphambi kokukuqala ukusebenza iSithethi kufuneka senze isifungo sentembeko phambi kweJaji eyNitloko okanye ijaji ephezulu ngokumiswa kwiShedyuli yesiThathu.

(6) ILungu leNkundla yeSizwe elonyulwe ukuba siSithethi liyayeka ukuba liLungu leNkundla yeSizwe kwaye isikhundla esesivelile makutyunjwe omnye ngokungqinelana noMthetho woNyulo .

(7) ISithethi singarhoxa ngokubhengeza ngobuso okanye xa iNkundla ingahlalanga angenza ingxelo ebhaliweyo kuNobhala wasePalamente.

(8) ISithethi kufuneka sishiye ;

(a)

kwimini apho iNkundla yeSizwe ihlala okokukuqala emva konyulo jikelele;

(b) sawube eyamkele nawuphi umsebenzi kawonkewonke okanye eqeshwe kwenye indawo

(c)

sawube eliLungu lasePalamente okanye uMongameli weSenethi;

(d) sawube enguSekela Mongameli, uMphathiswa okanye uSekela Mphathiswa;

(e)

xa kungavela imeko engamnyanzela ukuba ashiye isikhundla sakhe xa ebeliLungu lePalamente okanye

(f)

xa isigqibo sokususwa kwakhe singasatyelwa ngezimbini esithathwini kwinani lonke lamaLungu eNkundla yePalamente

127 ISekela leSithethi seNkundla yeSizwe

(1) Ngokukhawuleza okukhulu emveni kokonyula uSomlomo kulandela unyulo, iNkundla yeSizwe kufuneka yonyule elinye lamaLungu libeliSekela likaSomlomo.

(2) Naninina xa kunesithuba kwiwofisi kaSekela Somlomo, iNkundla yeSizwe ingalibazisanga kufuneka yonyule umntu ozakungena kwesosithuba.

(3) Unyulo lokungena kwiwofisi yeSekela Sithethi kufuneka liqhutywe nguNobhala wePalamente ngaphantsi kweliso leKhomishini yoNyulo yaseZimbabwe, ngonyulo oluyimfihlo ngokungqinelana nomyalelo omisiweyo kwaye iziphumo zibhengezwe kwangoko.

(4) Ngaphambi kokukuqala umsebenzi wakhe uSekela Somlomo kufuneka enze isifungo sokunyaniseka kwiwofisi phambi kweJaji eyiNtloko okanye ijaji enkulu ekhoyo ngokwengcwadi ezimiswe kwiShedyuli yeSithathu.

(5) ISekela Sithethi kuumele arhoxe ngokubhengeza ukurhoxa kwakhe ngokwakhe kwiNkundla yeSizwe okanye, xa engekho uSomlomo kuNobhala wePalamente.

(6) iSekela Somlomo kufuneka aphume kwiwofisi yakhe—

(a)

apho seyekile ukuba liLungu leNkundla yeSizwe;

(b) apho asawuba nguMongameli weSenethi;

(c)

apho asuwuba nguSekela Mongameli, uMphathiswa okanye uSekela Mphathiswa, okanye;

(d) xa isisombululo sokususa kwakhe kwiwofisi segqithiswe ngezimbini esithathwini samaLungu wonke eNkundla yeSizwe. INXALENYE 5 Ixesha Lengqesho Lamalungu Epalemente 128 Isifungo seLungu lePalamente

(1) Ngaphambi kokubana iLungu lasePalamente lithathe isihlalo salo iLungu lePalamente kufuneka lenze isifungo sokuba liLungu lePalamente ngokumiswa kuShedyuli yeSithathu.

(2) Isifungo okubhekiswe kwicandelwana (1) kufuneka senziwe phambi koNobhala wasePalamente. 129 Ingqesho yeLungu lasePalamente

(1) Ilungu lasePalamente lishiya isihlalo —

(a)

ekuchithweni kwePalamente;

(b)

ekubeni iLungu lirhoxile esihlalweni okanye ngokunika ingxelo ebhaliweyo kuMongameli weSenethi okanye iSithethi xa kungaba njalo;

(c)

ekubeni iLungu libe nguMongameli okanye uSekela Mongameli;

(d)

ekubeni iLungu libe nguMongameli weSenethi okanye iSithethi okanye iLungu lenye iNkundla ;

(e)

xa iLungu lingasafanelekanga ukuba ngumvoti;

(f)

xa ngaphandle kwemvume yeSithethi okanye uMongameli weSenethi iLungu alangabikho kwiNkundla apho aliungu okwentsuku ezingamashumi amabini nanye zilandelana kwaye iNkundla ngenkxalabo icala kwinani lamalungu wonke agqibe ukuba isihlalo asisenamntu;

(g)

xa iLungu lamkele iwofisi kawonkewonke okanye irhante esemthethweni, ingeka rhulumente, eyephondo, ibhunga lombxa, okanye uRhulumente wengingqi;

(h) xa iLungu laliligosa likawonkewonke, okanye iLungu le arhente esemthethweni eka rhulumente wephondo, ibhunga lombaxa, okanye uRhulumente wengingqi kwilanga ebengezwe njengeLungu lasePalamente okanye oyisakale ukuphuma kwesosikhundla, ukubaliLungu okanye ukuqeshwa kwintsuku ezingamashumi amathathu emva kwalomhla;

(i)

2013

xa iLungu lifumaneke linetyala—

(i) ityala elinokwephula ukuthembeka eZimbabwe , ukunganyaniseki, okanye ubudlobongela ngummba obalulekiyo; okanye

(ii) ukuziphatha ungaphandle kweLizwe xa ubuse Zimbabwe uyakube udale ityala elinokwephulwa kokuthembeka , ukunganyaniseki, okanye ubudlobongela ngummba obalulekileyo

kwaye egwetyelwe ukuhlala etrongweni inyanga ezisithandathu okanye ngaphezulu okanye engaphiwanga isohlayo semali okanye nasiphi isigwebo esingayisiso itrongo ngaphandle ngokwenza isibhheno isigwebo seLungu sibekwe bucala okanye isigwebo sokuhlala etrongweni inyanga ezisithandathu zincitshiswe okanye aphiwe esinye isigwebo ngaphandle kokuhlala etrongweni;

(j)

xa iLungu kubhengezwe ukuba litshonile ngaphantsi koMthetho osebenzayo eZimbabwe kwaye akancediswanga ukuguqula isimilo sakhe okanye akhululwe okanye xa iLungu lenze isigqibo ngoku semthethweni nababoleki bakhe ukuba isikweliti singachithwa okanye sibekwe ecaleni;

(k) xa iLungu seliyekile ukubaliLungu lombutho wezepolitiki ebeliLungu ekwenyulweni kwakhe ePalamente kwaye umbutho wakhe ngokubhalela iSithethi okanye uMongameli weSenethi utsho ukuba iLungu aliseyo nxalenye nayo;

(l)

xa iLungu belingeloLungu lombutho wezepolitiki ekonyulweni kwakhe ukuba liLungu lasePalamente wasesiba liLungu lombutho wezepolitiki;

(m) iLungu ngokuqinisekisiweyo ufumaneke sengophambaneyo okanye ekhubazeke ngokwasengqondweni ngaphantsi koMthetho osebenzayo eZimbabwe;okanye

(n) xa iLungu lifumeneke linetyala ngaphantsi koMthetho woNyulo kwaye kubhengezwe yiNkundla Ephakemeyo ukuba anqunyanyiswe ekubhalisweni njengomvoti okanye ekuvoteni nakuliphina unyulo.

(2) iLungu ekubhekiswe kulo ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (1)(i) lenze isibheno ngesigwebo salo angaqhubekeka kude kwenziwe isigqibo ngesibheno sakhe aqhubeke esenza umsebenzi wakhe njengeLungu ngaphandle xa iNkundla yoMthetho ingathi wakavalelwe etrongweni kusalindeleke iziphumo zesibheno.

(3) iLungu lasePalamente elibe ngusihlalo wenkundla yephondo uyashiya isikhundla sakhe abekuso ngaphambi kokuba ngusihlalo. INXALENYE 6 Uwiso Mthetho Namanye Amandla 130

Amandla nemisebenzi yeSenethi neNkundla yeSizwe

(1) Ngaphandle xa kubonelelwe kwiShedyuli yeSihlanu, ekusebenziseni igunya loMthetho zombini iSenethi neNkundla yeSizwe zinamandla okuqalisa, ukulungiselela,ukwenza ingqalelo okanye ukwala nawuphi uMthetho.

(2) ngaphezu kwemisebenzi evele inayo ngaphantsi kwaloMgaqo siseko, iSenethi neNkundla yeSizwe ziyakwazi ukuphatha nawuphi umsebenzi eziwunikwe okanye obekwe kuzo ngaphantsi kwawuphi uMthetho.

131 Imithetho yasePalamente nenkqubo yokuwiswa kwayo.

(1) Igunya lePalamente ngokoMthetho lisetyenziswe ngokuwiswa kwemithetho yasePalamente.

(2) UMthetho wasePalamente nguMthetho oyilwayo—

(a)

othiwe thaca wabuye wedluliswa zinkundla zombini zePalamente ; kwaye

(b) wavunywa ukusayinwa nguMongameli ngokungqinelana noMgaqo siseko.

(3) Amagama okuwiswa koMthetho ePalamente “ Awiswe yiPalamente nanguMongameli waseZimbabwe” okanye namazwi angqinayo .

(4) Inkqubo emayilandelwe yiNkundla yeSizwe neSenethi mayelana noMthetho oyilwayo imiswe kwiShedyuli yeSihlanu.

(5) Emveni kokuba uMthetho oyilwayo udluliswe zinkundla zombini ngokungqinelana neShedyuli yeSihlanu , uMongameli weSenethi , okanye iSithethi

(a) singenza uthiwe thaca kuMongameli ukuvunyelwa nokusayinwa kunye nesiqinisekiso esifunwa nguMgaqo siseko sihamba kunye noMthetho osayilwayo ;, kwaye

(b) Sinike uwonkewonke umhla apho uMthetho osayilwayo owathunyelwa kuMongameli.

(6) ukuba uMthetho osayilwayo wathiwa thaca kuMongameli ukuvunya noku sayinwa, kufuneka kwinsuku ezingamashumi amabini nanye , nokuba—

(a)

uvunyelwe abuye awusayine kwaye enze ukuba upapashwe kwigazethi kungalityaziswanga; okanye

(b) xa eqaphela ukuba uphikisana noMgaqo siseko okanye enezinye ingcamango ngawo, abuyisele loMthetho oyilwayo ePalamente ngoNobhala wasePalamente, kunye nezizathu ezibhaliweyo ngengcamango zakhe nesicelo sokuba loMthetho oyilwayo ubuye ujongisiswe .

(7) Apho uMthetho oyilwayo ubuyiselwe ePalamente ngokwecandelwana

(6)(b), ISithethi singalibazisanga sibize indibano yeNkundla yeSizwe, okufuneka—

(a)

ijongisise kwakhona loMthetho oyilwayo kwaye amkele ngokugcweleyo ingcamango zikaMongameli; okanye

(b) adlulise uMthetho oyilwayo kungekho silungiso ngokubini esithathwini uninzi amaLungu onke eNkundla yeSizwe; kwaye nokuba kanjani kungalityaziswanga iSithethi kufuneka senze ukuba uMthetho oyilwayo uthiwe thaca kuMongameli ukuvunywa nokusayinwa kwaye kufuneka anike uwonkewonke ingxelo yomhla apho uMthetho oyilwayo wathunyelwa kuMongameli.

(8) Xa loMthetho oyilwayo sowuthiwe thaca kuMongameli ngokwecandelwana

(7) wamkela ngokupheleleyo intsolo zikaMongameli, uMongameli kufuneka avumele loMthetho oyilwayo awusayine phakathi kwexesha elingamashumi amabini nanye kwaye enze ipapashwe kwiGazethi kungalibaziswanga, kodwa xa uMongameli esoloke enentsolo ngoMthetho oyilwayo, kufuneka phakathi kwexesha nokuba—

(a) amkele loMthetho oyilwayo awusayine nangona esola; okanye

(b) abhekise loMthetho oyilwayo kwiNkundla yoMgaqo siseko ukuze afumane ingcebiso ngokuba sisiseko kwawo’

(9) ngokuba sisingqiniso ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (8) iNkundla yoMgaqo siseko icebisa ukuba loMthetho oyilwayo usisiseko uMongameli amkele loMthetho abuye awusayine kwangoko kwaye awenze ukuba upapashwe kwiGazethi kungalityaziswanga .

(10) xa loMthetho oyilwayo uthiwe thaca kuMongmeli ukuba awamkele abuye awusayine ungakhatshwanga sisiqinisekiso esifuna nangawuphi uMthetho woMgaqo siseko , uMongameli angawuvumi loMthetho oyilwayo okanye awusayine kude kuveliswe isiqinisekiso kodwa kufuneka uNobhala wasePalamente aziswe kwangoko ukuba loMthetho oyilwayo awuhambanga nesiqinisekiso . 132 Ukuqalisa kweMithetho yasePalamente . UMthetho wasePalamente ukuqala ukusebenza ekuqaleni kosuku apho upapashwe khona kwiGazethi okanye ekuqaleni kolunye usuku lokuvakalisa kwiGazethi okanye ekuqaleni kolunye usuku oluthe lakhankanywa nguMthetho okanye omnye uMthetho othe wawiswa. 133 Ubhaliso lweMthetho yasePalamente

(1) Ekubeni uMongameli eyamkele wabuya wasayina uMthetho wasePlamente uNobhala wasePalamente kufuneka ahambise ikopi ingqinisiswe lutyikityo lukaMongameli netywina lika wonkewonke waseZimbabwe , kufuneka ibhaliswe kwiwofisi yoMbalisi weNkundla Ephakamileyo kwaye lokopi ibubugqina bezimiso zoMthetho ngaphandle kokuba uMthetho uhlaziyiwe ngaphantsi koMthetho wasePalamente obhekiselwe kwicandelwana (2).

(2) UMthetho wasePalamente ungabonelela ngoMthetho ongummiselo okanye inxalenye yayo ipapashwe ngendlela ehlaziyiweyo kwaye ibonelele ukuba —

(a)

sawube ipapashiwe inguqulelo ehlaziyiwey yiyo eyoqobo equlethwe ku

(b)

ikopi ehlaziyiweyo kufuneka ihanjiswe kwiwofisi yoMbalisi weNkundla Ephakamileyo ; kwaye ;

(c)

lokopi ehanjiswe kwiwofisi yoMbhalisi weNkundla Ephakamileyo ibubungqina obupheleleyo bezimiso zoMthetho equlethwe kuwo.

(3) Ukunyaniseka koMthetho wasePlamente okanye uMthetho ohlaziyiweyo awuxomekekanga elubhalisweni okanye ehanjiswe ngapantsi kwelicandelwana 134 UMthetho oncendisayo NgokoMthetho wePalamente, iPalamente isenga nika amandla okwenza imithetho ongummiselo ngokomda nejongo ebekwe kwacaca kuloMthetho, kodwa—

(a)

amandla angundoqo ePalamente okwenza uMthetho awanakwabiwa;

(b) imithetho engummsilo akufuneki yaphulwe okanye unciphise amalungelo neenkululeko ezisekwe kusibhengezo samalungelo;

(c)

iimithetho engummiselo kufuneka ibe ngengqinelana noMthetho wasePalamente;

(d) uMthetho kufuneka ukhankanye imida yamandla nemeko yoMthetho ongummiselo ongenziwa, imigaqo neemigangatho esebenzayo kuloMthetho omisiweyo;

(e)

iizixhobo zoMthetho akufuneki zibenesinyanzeliso ngaphandle xa zithe zapapashwa kwiGazethi;

(f)

uMthetho ongummiselo kufuneka ubekwe phambi kweNkundla yeSizwe ngokungqinelana nemiyalelo esisigxina kwaye ungeniswe kwiKomiti yezoMthetho yasePalamente ukuhlolisiswa.

INXALENYE 7 Inkqubo Zasepalamente 135 Intloko yePalamente.

(1) ISethethi sePalamente siyiintloko yePalamente kodwa kufuneka senze imisebenzi yaso ngokungqinelana nemithetho esisigxina.

(2) NgokoMthetho osisigxina umongameli wePalamente lisekela lePalamente kwaye uba libambela xa iSithethi nagasiphi isizathu asenelisi ukuqhuba umsebenzi. 136 Abantu abongamelayo ePalamente

(1) Umntu owongamelayo nakuyiphi indibano yeSenethi kufuneka—

(a)

nguMongameli weSenethi okanye xa engekho iSekela leSenethi ; okanye

(b) ekubeni ngabikho kuka Mongameli weSenethi neSekela lakhe kwiSenethi iLungu leSenethi elonyulwe ngokomsebenzi weSentha kodwa elolungu akufuneki libenguMphathiswa okanye iSekela Mphathiswa .

(2) Umntu owongamela nayiphi indibano iNkundla yeSizwe kufuneka—

(a)

iSithethi xa singekho iSekela leSithethi; okanye

(b) ekubeni ngabikho kweSithethi neSekela lakhe , iLungu leNkudla yeSizwe elonyulwe ngoko msebenzi weNkundla yeSizwe kodwa iLungu akufuneki libe nguMphathiswa okanye iSekela Mphathiswa.

(3) ISithethi ekubeni ngabikho uMongameli weSenethi kufuneka ongamele nakuyiphi intlanganiso ezidibeneyo zeNkundla yeSizwe neSenethi. 137 IKhoramu ePalamente ISenethi neNkundla yeSizwe kufuneka zenze immiselo esisigxina ubuncinane benanani lamaLungu okufuneka babekho xa kuqutya umsebenzi. 138 Ukuvota nelungelo lokuphulaphula ePalamente

(1) ngaphandle apho uMgaqo siseko ungabonelela ngenye indlela—

(a)

yonke imibuzo ecetywayo ukuze ibesisigqibo kwinkundla yasePalamente kufuneka igqitywe luninzi lwevoti lwamaLungu alondlu akhoyo kwaye evotile;

(b) Umntu owongamela nokuba kwiNkundla yasePalamente akanavoti yengxoxo okanye ivoti eyahlulayo nakowuphina umba ophambi kwePalamente.

(c)

xa iivoti nokuba kwiNdlu yasePalamente zahlukene ngokulinganayo nakusiphina isindululo, isindululo silahliwe.

(2) USekela Mongameli unelungelo lokuhlala kwaye athethe kodwa angavoti kwiZindlu zasePalamente zombini.

(3) AbaPhathiswa namaSekela abaPhathiswa banelungelo lokuhlala nokuthetha kwiZindlu Zombini zePalamente kodwa abanalungelo lokuvota kwiNdlu apho abangeyingo maLungu.

(4) AmaLungu asePalamente eKomiti yoMthetho nayiphina ikomiti edibeneyo yasePalamente banelungelo lokuhlala nokuthetha nokuba yiNdlu yasePalamente

ngenjongo yokwazisa okanye ukuxoxa nayiphina ingxelo yalo komiti ephambi kweNdlu yasePalamente kodwa ayinalungelo lokuvota kwiNdlu abangeyingo maLungu.

(5) Apho iLungu lasePalamente, ngaphandle ku kaMphathiswa okanye iSekela Mphathiswa azise uMthetho oyilwayo kwiNdlu apho yena aliLungu kwaye iNdlu seyedlulise leBhili, eloLungu lingekho, naliphina iLungu laloNdlu linelungelo lokuhlala nokuthetha kwiNdlu ngenjongo yokuqhuba iBhili yegqithe kuloNdlu kodwa akanalungelo lokuvota kwezinye iziNdlu. 139 Imiyalelo Emisiweyo

(1) Iinkqubo yeSenethi neyeNkundla yeSizwe zilawulwa yimithetho eyaziwa ngokuba yiMyalelo Emisiweyo, eyenziwa Yindlu iyodwa okanye idibene ngengcebiso yeKomiti yeMithetho neMiyalelo.

(2) Imyalelo Emisiweyo ingabonelela ngo—

(a)

kwedluliswa kweBhili;

(b) kutyunjwa nokusebenza kweekomiti nokwabiwa kwemisebenzi kuyo;

(c)

ndlela enye enamandla, amalungelo awodwa nokugonyeka kwendlu kungasetyenziswa kwaye igcinwe;

(d) kubuzwa kwabaPhathiswa namaSekela abaPhathiswa namaLungu asePalamente

(e)

indlela yokusebenza kwamaLungu asePalamente ;

(f)

ilungelo likawonkewonke lokubongoza iPalamente ; na

(g) ngokubanzi ummiselo nokuziphatha ngocwangco kwiingqubo phakathi kweezindlu zowisoMthetho.

(3) Inkqubo yePalamente neekomiti zayo ngokubonelelwa yiimithetho esisigxina kufuneka ikhuthaze ukuba izinto zibelubala ikhuthaze ukubandakanya kwamalunga onke emiibutho yezepoliltiki asePalamente nowonkewonke kwaye kufuneka bangathathi cala kwaye zibe nobulungisa.

(4) Nayiphi na ikomiti etyunjwe ngaphantsi kwemithetho eyingqiqo kufuneka ibonakalise ngokuseduze ukwakhiwa kweSini kwamalungu ePalamente okanye inkundla apho imithetho eyingqiqo esebenza khona. 140 INtetho kaMongameli nemiyalezo ePalamente

(1) UMongameli nangaliphi ixesha angakwazi ukwethula intetho kwiNdlu yePalamente okanye iziNdlu zombini zidibene.

(2) UMongameli angakwazi ukuthumela imiyalezo nokuba yiNdlu yasePalamente okanye umyalezo omila kunje ungafundwa nguSekela Mongameli okanye uMphathiswa noba ngowuphi umntu owongamela inkundla okuthethwa ngayo

(3) UMongameli angabakho ePalamente ukozophendula imibuzo nakuyiphi imiba ebonelelwe kwimithetho yengqiqo.

(4) Phantse kanye ngomnyaka uMongameli kufuneka ethule intetho kwiziNdlu zombini zasePalamente zidibene kwintetho yeSizwe, kwaye uSomlomo noMongameliweSenethi kufuneka enze amalungiselelo afanelekileyo ukuze iPalamente ikwazi ukwamkela lentetha.

141 Ukufikelela koluntu nokubandakanya ePalamente IPalamente kufuneka—

(a)

iququzelele ukubandakanya koluntu ekwenziweni koMthetho nezinye inkqubo nakweminye imisebenzi yeekomiti;

(b) ukuqiniseka ukuba yonke imibutho enomdla iyavelelwa ngoMthetho oyilwayo oqwalaselwayo yiPalamente ngaphandle xa oluvelelo lungafanelekanga kwaye lunganakwenzeka; kwaye;

(c)

inkqubo zenziwe lubala ibambe indibano zayo nezo ezekomiti esidlangalaleni naxa amanyathelo engatyashwanga—

(i) ukugcina ucwangco kwiinkqubo zePalamente;

(ii) ukulawula ukufikelela koluntu kubandakanya ukufikelela kwijelo leendaba ePalamente neekomiti zayo ;

(iii) ukushiya ngaphandle kubandakanya abendaba kwindibano zeekomiti; kwaye;

(iv) iveze indlela yokuhlola abantu kwaye nalapho kufanele ukwalela ukungena ePalamente okanye ukukhutshwa komntu ePalamente;

kodwa lomanyathelo kufuneka abesemthethweni , efanelekile kwaye elungile kwintlalo yedemokhrasi, ekhululekileyo esemthethweni, kuhlonitshwa uluntu, ulingano nenkululeko. 142 Ukunyaniseka kwenkqubo zasePalamente

(1) Isithuba kumalungu eSenethi okanye iNkundla yeSizwe okanye ukunqunyanyiswa kweLungu lasePalamente akuthinteli iSenethi okanye iNkundla yeSizwe ukuqhuba umsebenzi wayo.

(2) Naxa lomntu ongene iNkundla waze wavota kwiSenethi okanye kwiNkundla yeSizwe okanye wabandakanya kwinkqubo zeSenethi okanye zeNkundla yeSizwe ebenganalo ilungelo lokwenjenjalo lonto ayiyichithi into ibithethwa. INXALENYE 8 Ubude Bexesha , Ukuchithwa Nokuhlala Kwepalamente 143 Ubude bexesha nokuchithwa kwePalamente.

(1) IPalamente yonyulwa okwexesha eleminyaka emihlanu kusukela ngomhla uMongameli owonyuliweyo ethanjiswa kwaye eqala ukusebenza ngokumiswe kwicandelwana 94(1)(a), iPalamente ingechithiweyo ezinzulwini zobusuku ngaphambi kosuku lokukuqala lolwanyulo jikelele olubhengezwe ngokwemiqathango wecandelwana 144.

(2) UMongameli kufuneka ngokuvakalisa achithe iPalamente xa iSenethe neNkundla yeSizwe zahlukene ukuhlala, ngevoti ezimbini esithathwini kwinani lonke lamaLungu eNdlu ngayinye bevumelene ngesisombululo sokuyichitha.

(3) UMongameli ngokwenza isibhengezo ayichithe iPalamente xa iNkundla yeSizwe ngoku ngekho engqiqweni yale ukwedlulisa iBhili yolwabiwo ebhekiselwe kwicandelo 305.

(4) Isigqibo sokuchitha iPalamente ngokwecandelwano (3) ngokwenza isicelo naliphi iLungu lePalamente isengabekwa ecaleni ukuze iphononongwe yiNkundla yoMgaqo.

(5) Isicelo sophononongo sesigqibo sokuchithwa kwePalamente kufuneka isiwe kwiNkundla yoMgaqo phakathi kwentsuku ezisixhenxe emva kokuba isigqibo sipapashiwe, kwaye—

(a)

iNkundla yoMgaqo kufuneka imisele isicelo phakathi kwentsuku ezilishumi nane emveni kokuba ifakiwe; kwaye;

(b) de iNkundla yoMgaqo izimisele ukwenza isicelo isigqibo sokuchithwa kwePalamente sinqunyanyisiwe. 144 Ulonyulo jikelele lubangela ukuchithwa kwePalamente

(1) Apho iPalamente engakhange yenze izigqibo sokuchitha ngokwe candelo143(2), uMongameli kufuneka ngokwesibhengezo abize abuye amise imihla yonyulo jikekelele oluzakubanjwa phakathi kwentsuku ezimiselwe kwicandelo 158.

(2) Apho—

(a)

iPalamente yenze isigqibo sokuchitha ngokwe candelo 143(2); (b) uMongameli angachitha iPalamente ngokwe candelo 143(3);

(c)

uMongameli angachitha iPlamente kulandela ukugxothwa yiPalamente ngokwe candelo 109(4); okanye

(d) IPalamente ingechithiweyo kulandela ukugxothwa ngokwe candelo 109(5); UMongameli kufuneka ngokwe sibhengezo abize abuye amise imihla yonyulo jikelele omalubanjwe kungedlulanga iinsuku ezingamashumi alithoba emveni kokuba iPlamente yenze isigqibo okanye uMongameli angachitha iPalamente okanye iPalamente ibe ngechithiweyo ngokwemeko.

(3) iintsuku zonyulo jikekelele ezibhengezwe ngokwecandelwana (1) okanye

(2) kufuneka ilungiswe nguMongameli emveni kothethwano neKhomishini yoNyulo yaseZimbabwe. 145 Indibano yokukuqala yePalamente kulandela unyulo jikelele

(1) Indibano yokukuqala yePalamente emveni konyulo jikelele kufuneka yenzeke ngexesha nangomhla omiselwe nguMongameli, kodwa umhla akufuneki ubengaphambi kwentsuku ezingamashumi amathathu emveni kokuba eqale ukusebenza ngokwecandelo 94.

(2) Kude unyulo luka Mongameli weSenethi okanye iSithethi kuba kufuneka kubenjalo intlanganiso yokukuqala yeNkundla yePalamente kufuneka yonganyelwe nguNobhala wePalamente. 146 Indibano nexesha lekhefu Nganye indlu yasePalamente iyamisela ixesha nobude bexesha lendibano ngaphandle kwe ndibano yokukuqala namaxesha endibano, kodwa—

(a)

uMongameli angabiza iPalamente nangaliphi ixesha ukuzoqhuba umsebenzi okhethekileyo;

(b) akufuneki kwedlule insuku ezilikhulu namashumi asisibhozo phakathi kwendibano yeNkundla.

147 Ukuphelelwa kwebhili, isindululo, isibongozo neminye imisebenzi yokuchithwa kwePalamente Ekuchithweni kwePalamente zonke inkqubo okusathethwa ngazo ngeloxesha ziyapheliswa kwaye zonke ibhili, isindululo, isibongozo, neminye imisebenzi iyaphelelwa. INXALENYE 9 Imicimbi Ngokubanzi Enxulumene Nepalamente 148 Amalungelo nokugonyeka kwePalamente

(1) Umongameli weSenethi iSithethi namaLungu ePalamente banenkululeko yokuthetha kwikomiti zonke zePalamente kwaye logama kufuneka bathobele imithetho nemiyalelo yeNkundla echaphazelekayo abanatyala kwinkqubozo luntu okanye ubukrlemnqa ,ukubanjwa okanye ukuvalelwa okanye umonakalo nowayiphi ithethwayo, yaveliswa phambi kwePlamente okanye nayiphina ikomiti.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente unga—

(a)

bonelela ngamanye amalungelo, ukukhuseleka, namandla ePalamente namaLungu ayo namagosa;

(b) chaza isimilo esibonisa ukudela iPalamente lonto yenziwe ngamaLungu ePalamecnte okanye abanye abantu ; kwaye

(c)

bonelela ngelungelo lokuphendula ngokusebenzisa iSithethi okanye uMongameli weenetha ngokunjalo, abantu abalinyazwe ngokungekho emthethethweni ngokuthethwe ngabo ePalamente;

kodwa ke akhomthedtho ongavumela iPalamente okanye amaLungu okanye amagosa ukuwisa isigwebo solwaphulo Mthetho, ukuhlawuliswa imali, ngokwaphulwa kwamalungelo okanye ukudela iPalamente. 149 Ilungelo lokukhalaza ePalamente

(1) Wonke ummi nomhlali osisigxina wase Zimbabwe unelungelo lokukhalaza ePalamente ukunika ingqalelo nawuphi umba onegunya kuwo kubandakanya ukuwisa, ukulungisa okanye ukurhoxiswa koMthetho.

(2) Indlela izikhalazo ezithiwa thaca ngayo ePalamente nenyathelo iPalamente elithabathayo ekwedluliseni isikhalazo kufuneka limiselwe kuMthetho osisigxina. 150 Indawo yePalamente IPalamente ingahlala kwezinye indawo ngaphandle kweNdlu yePalamente eqhelekileyo kodwa ngezizathu zeemfuno zoluntu , ukhuselo okanye uncedo. 151 IKomiti kwimithetho eSisigxina nemiyalelo

(1) IPalamente kufuneka ityumbe ikomiti eyaziwa njenge Komiti yeMthetho eSisigxina neMiyalelo ngenjongo yoku—

(a)

baliliso ekulawuleni iPalamente ;

(b) ukuqulunga imithetho eSisigxina;

(c)

ukuqwalasela nokwenza izigqibo kwiimibayonke enokwenza nePalamente; kwaye;

(d) enze nawuphi umsebenzi odluliselwe okanye onyanzelwe nguMgaqo siseko okanye imithetho eSisigxina okanye nawuphi uMthetho.

(2) IKomiti kwimithetho eSisigxina nemiYalelo kufuneka iquke uSomlomo noMongameli weSenethi nenamaLungu ePalamente alandelayo—

(a)

ISekela likaSomlomo;

(b) ISekela likaMongameli weSenethi;

(c)

UMpathiswa onoxanduva ngezemali nabaPhathiswa ababini abatyunjwe nguMongameli;

(d) Inkokheli yenkqubo zikarhulumente endlini yoWiso Mthetho;

(e)

Ikokheli yeqela eliphikisa yo kiindlu ngayinye ;

(f)

Ababhexeshi bemibutho yonke emelweyo kwiNdlu ngayinye;

(g) uMongameli weSizwe lebhunga leenkosi;

(h) amaLungu amabini angengo baPhathiswa okanye amaSekela abaPhathiswa kuthi omnye onguSenethi otyunjelwe kwikomiti nguMongameli weSenethi kwaye omnye abeliLungu leNkundla YeSizwe etyunjwe nguSomlomo; kwaye;

(i)

amaLungu asisibhozo angeyingo baPhathiswa okanye amaSekela abaPhathiswa, amane onyulelwe kwiKomiti yiSenethi kuzokuthi abane bonyulwe yiNkundla yeSizwe.

(3) AmaLungu kufuneka atyunjwe okanye onyulwelwe kwikomiti kwimithetho eSisigxina nemyalelo ngoko ngoko emveni kokukuqala kweseshoni yokukuqala yePalamente ngayinye kwaye kufuneka bakhethwe ukuze ikomiti ibonakalise kwangangoko ukuma kweSini kwezepolitiki kwiNkundla zePalamente zidibene.

(4) IKomiti kwiMthetho eSisigxina neMiyalelo ityunjwa ngokwexesha lePalamente ngayinye.

(5) IKomiti kwiMthetho eSisigxina neyoMiyalelo ichotshelwa nguSomlomo okanye xa engekho nguMongameli weSenethi.

(6) Inkqubo emayilandelwe yiKomiti kwiMthetho eSisigxina neMiyalelo kufuneka imiselwe kw iimithetho eSisigxina

(7) Sawube kuvele isithuba sombenzi kwiKomiti yoMthetho oSisigxina noMyalelo iLungu kufuneka lonyulwe okanye lityunjwe ngoko nangoko ukugcwalisa esosithuba. 152 IKomiti yezoMthetho ePalamente

(1) Ngokukhawuleza okukhulu emveni kokukuqala kweseshoni ngayinye yePalamente, iKomiti kwiiMthetho engaquka amalungu ePalamente amathathu angengo baphathiswa okanye amaSekela abaPhathiswa.

(2) Inintsi lamaLungu eKomiti yoMthetho ePalamente kube ngabafanelekileyo ukusebenza njengamagqwetha eZimbabwe ngaphandle xa kukho ukusilela ababantu bafanelekile ukutyunjelwa kwiKomiti.

(3) IKomiti yoMthetho ePalamente kufuneka ihlolisise—

(a)

yonke iBhili ngaphandle kweBhili yoMgaqo nkqubo ngaphambilini kokuba ivotelwe okokugqibela kwiSenethi okanye kwiNkundla yeSizwe;

(b) nayiphina iBhili elungisiweyo emveni kokuba ihloliwe yikomiti ngaphambili kokuba leBhili yamkele ivoti yokugqibela kwiSenethi okanye kwiNkundla yeSizwe;

(c)

yonke imithetho epapashwe kwiGazethi;

(d) yonke iBhili eyilwayo ebhekiselwe kwiKomiti nguSekela Mongameli okanye uMphatiswa; kwaye

(e)

wonke uMthetho oyilwayo obhekiselwe kwiKomiti ephiwe igunya lokwenza uMthetho;

kwaye kufuneka anike ingxelo ePalamente okanye kuSekela Mongameli, kuMphathiswa okanye nabanina osemagunyeni eqwalasela nasiphina isibonelelo kwiBhili, uMthetho okanye uMthetho oyilwayo uyachasana okanye, xa utyunjiwe ungachasana nasiphi isibonelelo saloMgaqo siseko.

(4) Emva kokuhlolisisa nawuphina uMthetho okanye uMthetho oyilwayo iKomiti yoMthetho ePalamente kufuneka inike ingxelo ePalamente okanye kuSekela Mongameli, uMpathiswa okanye osemagunyeni ochaphazelekayo nokuba eqwalasela nasiphina isibonelelo nakowuphina uMthetho okanye xa utyunjiwe uzakuba ngonamagunya ekwenzeni uMthetho wePalamente.

(5) UMthetho wePalamente okanye uMthetho oSisigxina unokunika eminye imisebenzi kwiKomiti yoMthetho ePalamente— 153 Ukuvuzwa kukaMongameli weSenethi, uSomlomo namaLungu ePalamente

(1) Ukuvuzwa kukaSomlomo noMongameli weSenethi—

(a)

kufuneka kumiselwe kuMthetho wePalamente kwaye kufuneka ibeyintlawulo kwiNgxowa yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo;

(b) akufuneki incitshiswe xa esabambe iwofisi; kwaye

(c)

kufuneka baqubeke behlawulwa emveni kokuchithwa kude kufike ithuba lokuyeka ukuba sewofiSini.

(2) Umvuzo ohlawulwayo kumaLungu lePalamente kufuneka umiselwe kuMthetho wePalmente. 154 UNobhala wePalamente namanye amagosa

(1) IKomiti kwiiMthetho esiSigxina nemiyalelo ngokuvunyelwa yiNkundla yeSizwe,kufuneka ityumbe igosa elizakwaziwa njengoNobhala wePalamente ozakuba noxanduva, ngokumiswa nguMthetho osiSigxina,ukulawulwa nokuselwa iliso nguSomlomo ngolawulo lwemihla ngemihla yiPalamente.

(2) UNobhala wePalamente, utyunjwa okwekota yeminyaka esisithandathu kwaye angabuye atyunjwe kubekanye okweminyaka efanayo.

(3) UNobhala wePalamente ashiye iwofisi yakhe—

(a)

xa, ngokucetyiswa yiKomiti kwiMithetho eSisigxina neMmiselo, ngaphezu kwecala lenani lamaLungu eNkundla YeSizwe inike isisombululo sokubana uNobhala makasuswe;

(b) xa uNobhala kuzakufuka ashiye isikhundla sakhe xa ebelilungu lePalamente; okanye

(c)

nakusiphina isiganeko emveni kokubamba iwofisi kaNobhala okweminyaka elishumi elinesibini.

(4) IKomiti kwiMthetho eSisigxina neMmiselo kufuneka ityumbe amanye amagosa ePalamente xa kuyimfuneko.

(5) uNobhala wePalamente namanye amagosa ePalamente—

(a)

atyunjwa ngokwenkqubo zeKomiti yoMthetho oSisigxina neMmiselo ngexesha ngalinya; kwaye

(b) ngamagosa oluntu kodwa bengeyiyo nxalenye yeMmcimbi Yoluntu ISAHLUKO 7 INXALENYE 1 Indlela Yonyulo Neenkqubo 155 Iimigaqo yokuqhutywa konyulo

(1) Unyulo okufuneka lubanjwe rhoqo, kwaye nemvanyo apho loMgaqo siseko usebenza kufuneka—

(a)

ubenoxolo, lubengolukhululekileyo kwaye lilungile

(b) luqhutywe ngendlela efihlakeleyo

(c)

esekwe kwimfuna zeSinintsi nolungelwano lwevoti, kwaye

(d) kungabikho budlobongela neminye imikhwa egwenxa ephathelane nonyulo.

(2) URhulumente kufuneka athabathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo, kubandakanya amanyathelo oMthetho, ukuqinisekisa ukuba imigaqo esekwe kucandelwana (1) ukuba iyenziwa; ngakumbi—

(a)

aqiniseke ukuba bonke abemmi abafanelekileyo, apho kusitsho ukuba abemmi abafanelekileyo ngaphntsi kweShedyuli yeSine, babhalise njenga bavoti;

(b) kwaye aqinisekise ukuba wonke ummi ofanelekileyo ukuvota kulonyulo okanye imvo unethuba lokuvota kwaye kufuneka aququzelele ukuvota kwabantu abakhubazekileyo okanye abanemfuno ezikhethekileyo;

(c)

iqiniseke ukuba imibutho yonke yezepolitiki nabagqatswa abakhuphinasayo kunyulo okanye bebandakanya kwimvo babe nesizathu esifanelekileyo sokufikelela kwizixhobo nolwazi olufanelekileyo ukuze babandakanye ngempumelelo;

(d) ibonelele yonke imibutho yeze politiki nabagqatswa abakukhuphiswano lonyulo okanye bebandakanya kulumvo ngokuthembeka nokufikelela okulinganayo kwezobucwephesha nakumaphephandaba omabini oluntu nawabucala; kwaye

(e)

iqinisekise ukusonjululwa kwembambano zonyulo ngexesha.

156 Ukuphathwa konyulo neziimvo Kunyulo lonke nezimvo, iKomishini Yonyulo yaseZimbabwe kufuneka iqinisekise—

(a) nayiphina indlela yonyulo esetyenziswayo, kulula, ichanekile, inokuqinisekiswa, ikhuselekile, kwaye ilubala;

(b) iziphumo zonyulo okanye iizimvo ziyabhengezwa ngoko nangoko emva kokuvalwa konyulo; kwaye;

(c)

inkqubo ezifanelekileyo needlela zimiswa endaweni efanelekileyo—

(i) ukuphelisa ukudlobongela lonyulo neminye imikhuba egwenxa yonyulo; kwaye

(ii) ukuqinisekiswa kokugcinwa kwempahla yonyulo.

157 UMthetho Wonyulo

(1) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka ubonelele ngokuqhutywa konyulo neezimvo apho loMgaqo siseko usebenza, kwaye ingakumbi kwiimiba elendelayo—

(a)

ixesha lokucandwa kweenginqi zonyulo newadi ngokungqinelana necandelwa161;

(b) ubhaliso lwabavoti, neemfuneko zobhaliso kwiimiqulu ekhethekileyo yabavoti;

(c)

indlela yokuziphatha kwemibutho yeze politiki, abagqatswa nabanye abantu ababandakanya kunyulo okanye iimvo;

(d) inkqubo yokumelwa ngokulungelelana ngonyulo lwabantu kwezihlalo kwiSenethi ngokubhekiswe kwicandelwana 120(1) (a) kwaye nezihlalo ezingcinelwe abasetyini kwiNkundla yeSizwe ngokubhekiswe kwicandelwana 124(1)(b), kwaye inkqubo yokugcwalisa izithuba kwezozihlalo, izihlalo kufuneka zigcwaliswe ngabantu—

(i) abeqela lombutho omnye njegabo ngaphambilini ebebebambe izihlalo; kwaye

(ii) abeSini sinye njengabantu ngaphambilini ebebebambe izihlalo;

(e)

unyulo lwabameli lwabantu abakhubazekileyo ngaphntsi kwe candelwana 120(1)(d);

(f)

inkqubo yonyulo kuphondo nebhunga lombaxa norhulumente wenginqi;

(g) ingxaki zeziphumo zonyulo.

(2) Inkqubo yokumelwa ngokulungelelana ebonelelwe ngokwemiqathango ekucandelwana (1)(d) kufuneka uqiniseke ukumelwa okulingayo kwabasetyini phakathi kwamaSenethi ngokubhekiselwe kwicandelwana 120(1)(a).

(3) UMthetho woNyulo ubonelela ngokutyunjwa kwabagqatswa nakoluphina unyulo oluthatyathwayo nangona kwiintsuku ezilishumi nane emveni kopapasho lwesibhengezo sibiza unyulo. Unyulo kufuneka luqhutywe emva kwensuku ezingamashumi amathathu kokutyumba abagqatswa.

(4)Asikho isilungiso okufuneka senziwe kuMthetho Wonyulo okanye kuMthetho oncedisayo owenziwe ngaphantsi koMthetho ngaphandle iKhomishini Yonyulo eZimbabwe kubekho uthethwano kwaye naziphina ingcebiso ezenziwe yiKhomishini ziqwalaselwe ngokufanelekileyo.

(5) Emveni kokuba unyulo lubiziwe akho tshintsho kuMthetho Wonyulo okanye nawuphina uMthetho onxulumene nonyulo ochaphazela injongo yolonyulo. INXALENYE 2 Ixesha Lonyulo 158 Ixesha lonyulo

(1) Unyulo jikekelele kufuneka lubanjwe ukuze uvoto lwenziwe kungedlulanga—

(a)

intsuku ezingamashumi amathathu ngaphambi kokuphelelwa kwexesha leminyaka emihlanu elikhankanyiweyo kwicandelwana 143;

(b) apho iPalamente isedlulisa izisombululo zokuchitha ngokwemiqathango wecandelwana 143(2), intsuku ezingamashumi alithoba emveni kokuba zedlulile esosisombululo; okanye

(c)

apho iPalamente ichithiwe ngokwemiqathango ekwicandelwana 109(4) okanye (5) kulandela uvoto oluchitha uRhulumente, iintsuku ezingamashumi alithoba emveni kwesisombululo.

(2) Unyulo jikelele lweerhulumente zenginqi kufuneka lwenziwe kanye kanye nolukaMongameli nolwePalamete.

(3) Uvoto kunyulo kwiPalamente naku rhulumente wenginqi kufuneka lenziwe kwintsuku ezingamashumi alithoba emva kwezithuba zivelile ngaphandle xa izithuba zivele phakathi kwenyanga ezilithoba ngaphambi konyulo jikelele. sekufuneka lubanjwe, sawube kunjalo izithuba zingahlala zinganamntu kude kufike unyulo jikelele. 159 Ukugcwaliswa kwezithuba zonyulo Sakube kuvele isithuba nakuyiphina iwofisi yonyulo yoluntu esekwe ngokwemiqathango yaloMgaqo siseko ngaphandle kwewofisi apho icandelwana 158 lisebenza khona, lowo ogunyazisiweyo emiselwe ukuququzelela unyulo kulombutho kufuneka enze unyulo lubanjwe kwintsuku ezingamashumi alithoba ukugcwalisa isithuba. INXALENYE 3 Ukucandwa Kwemida Yonyulo 160 Inani lamaphondo ovoto namawadi

(1) Ngokwenjongo zokunyula amalungu ePalamente, iKomishini Yonyulo eZimbabwe yahlule Zimbabwe ibe ngamaphondo ovoto angamakhulu amabini neshumi;

(2) Ngokwenjongo zonyulo kaRhulumente wamaphondo, iKomishini Yonyulo yeZmbabwe kufuneka yahlule uRhulumente wamaphondo kumawadi ngokwenani lamaLungu okufuneka onyulelwe kurhulumente wamaphondo ochaphazelekayo. 161 Ukucandwa kwemida yonyulo

(1) Kanye kwiminyaka elishumi kumhla okanye ixesha elisisigxina elimiswe yiKhomishini okufuneka kwenzakale emveni kobalo lwabantu, iKhomishini Yonyulo yaseZimbabwe kufuneka iqhube ucando lwemida yovoto eyona ndlela iZimbabwe yahlulwe ngayo.

(2) Xa ukumisa imida yonyulo sekugqityiwe kungagqithanga iinyanga ezinthandathu ngaphambi kosuku lonyulo jikeklele imida emisiweyo ayichaphazeli unyulo, imida ibivele ikho ngoko ngaphambi kokumiswa yiyo esebenzayo.

(3) Imida yamaphondo ovoto kufuneka ibengohlobo lokuba xa kusenzeka, ngexesha lokumisa amanani alinganayo abavoti babhalisile kwiphondo lovoto ngalinye ngaphakathi eZimbabwe.

(4) Imida yamawadi kufuneka xa kusenzeka , ngexesha lokumiswa kwemida inani elichaphazelekayo.

(5) Ekumiseni imida yovoto—

(a)

imida yamawadi , iKhomishini Yonyulo eZimbabwe kufuneka iqinisekise ukuba akukho wadi eyahlukaniselwe phakathi koorhulumente bephondo ababini okanye ngaphezulu;

(b) imida yamaphondo ovoto, iKhomishini Yonyulo eZimbabwe kufuneka iqinisekise ukuba akukho wadi eyahlukaniswe phakathi kwamaphondo ovoto amabini okanye ngaphezulu.

(6) Ekwehlukaniseni iZimbabwe kumawadi kwaye namaphondo ovoto, iKhomishini Yonyulo eZimbabwe kufuneka ngokubonisa intlonipho nakuyiphina ummandla, inike ingcamango—

(a)

zokuma kweendalo;

(b) indlela zonxibelelwano zakummandla ochaphazelekayo ;

(c)

indlela abavoti ababhalisileyo abahleli ngayo ngokwendawo;

(d) naziphina iimfuno phakathi kwabavoti ababhalisileyo;

(e)

nakuyiphina imeko yokumiswa kwemida emva kokumisa imida okukuqala imida yonyulo ekhoyo; na

(f)

nabemi bayo;

kwaye ukunika isiphumo kwezingcamango, iKhomishini ingakwazi ukwesuka kumfuno zokubana amaphondo ovoto newadi kufuneka abe namanani abavoti alinganayo, kodwa iphondo lovoto okanye iwadi karhulumente wengingqi ezichaphazelekayo zingaba ngaphezu kwamashumi amabini ekhulwini okanye abavoti ababhalisileyo babengaphantsi kwamanye amaphondo ovoto okanye amawadi .

(7) Emveni kokumisa amawadi namaphondo ovoto, iKhomishini Yonyulo eZimbabwe kufuneka ingenise kuMongameli ingxelo yokukuqala iqulethe—

(a)

uluhlu lamawadi amaphondo ovoto, anamagama abalwe ngayinye nengcazelo yemida yabo;

(b) kwimapu okanye iimaphu ezibonisa amawadi namaphondo ovoto; kwaye

(c) naluphina ulwazi okanye inkcukacha iKhomishini ebona ziyimfuneko; kwaye uMongameli kufuneka akhuthaze ingxelo yomiso lokukuqala ukuba lithiwe thaca phambi kukaMongameli phakathi kwentsuku ezisixhenxe .

(8) kungekedluli intsuku ezilishumi nane emveni ingxelo yokumisa kokukuqala ithiwe thaca phambi kwePalamente—

(a)

UMongameli angayibuyisela emva ingxelo kwiKhomishini yoNyulo eZimbabwe iyoqwalaselwa kwakhona nakuwuphina umba okanye umcimbi;

(b) nayiphina indlu ingagqiba ukuba ingxelo ingabuyiselwa emva kwiKhomishini Yonyulo eZimbabwe ukuba iyoqwalaselwa kwakhona kwimiba okanye imicimbi okanye xa kungenzeka uMongameli kufuneka abuyisele emva kwiKomishini iyoqwalaselwa ngakummbi.

(9) Apho ingxelo yokumisa kokukuqala ibuyiselwe emva ngaphantsi kwecandelawna (8), iKhomishini yoNyulo eZimbabwe kufuneka inike ukuqwalaselwa ngakumbi kwimiba nemicimbi echaphazelekayo kodwa isigqibo seKhomishini kulomba sesokugqibela.

(10) Ngoko nangoko xa kusenzeka ukungqinelana neecandelwana (7) and

(9), iKhomishini yoNyulo eZimbabwe ingenise ingxelo yokungqibela yokumisa kuMongameli.

(11) Phakathi kwentsuku ezilishumi nane uMongameli kufuneka apapashe isibhengezo kwiGazethi ivakalisa amagama nemida ewadi namaphondo ovoto njengesimiso sokugqibela seKhomishini.

(12) Xa kukho ukungangqinelani kwenkcazelo phakathi kwemida nakweyiphi imida okanye amaphondo ovoto kwaye imephu okanye iimephu ezilungiswe yiKhomishini yoNyulo yaseZimbabwe inkcazelo yiyo emayo. ISAHLUKO 8 Iijaji Nenkundla INXALENYE 1 162 Amandla okugweba

Inkqubo Yenkundla

Amandla okugweba avela ebantwini baseZimbabwe kwaye axhomeke kwinkundla ezibandakanya—

(a)

INkundla yoMgaqo

(b) INkundla Ephez;ulu ;

(c)

INkundla Ephakemeyo;

(d) INkundla yabaSebenzi;

(e)

INkundlayoLawulo;

(f)

INkundla zomantyi ;

(g) Iinkundla zoMthetho wesintu ; ne

(h) zinye inkundla ezisekwe ngu okanye ngaphntsi koMthetho wePalamente. 163 Iijaji

(1) Iijaji zaseZimbabwe zibandakanya—

(a)

IJaji eyiNtloko, iSekela Jaji eyiNtloko, nezinye ijaji zeNkundla yoMgaqo;

(b) iiJaji zeNkundla Ephezulu;

(c)

iJaji enguMongmeli yeNkundla Ephezulu nezinye iijaji zalonkundla;

(d) iJaji enguMongameli yeNkundla yeziSebenzi nezinye iijaji zolonkundla

(e)

iJaji enguMongameli yeNkundla yoLawulo nezinye iijaji zalonkundla; na

(f)

abantu abongamela iinkundla zikamantyi, uMthetho wesintu, iinkundla zoMthetho wesintu, nezinye iinkundla ezisekwe ngu okanye ngaphantsi koMthetho wePalamente.

(2) IJaji eyiNtloko nguye oyintloko yeejaji kwaye nguye ophethe lonkundla yoMgaqo siseko neNkundla Ephakemeyo.

(3) iJaji onguMongameli weNkundla Ephezulu nguye ophethe lonkundla.

(4) iJaji enguMongameli yeNkundla yabaSebenzi iyintloko yalonkundla.

(5) IJaji enguMongameli yeNkundla yoLawulo iyintloko yalonkundla 164 Ukuzimela kwejaji

(1) Iinkundla zizimele kwaye zingaphantsi kuphela lolawulo laloMgaqo kwaye noMthetho okufuneka liwusebenzise ngokungenamkhethe, ngokukhawulezileyo kwaye ngaphandle koloyiko, ukhethe okanye ukucingela.

(2) Ukuzimela, ukungabinamkhethe nokusebenza kwenkundla zoMthetho zingundoqo ekulawulweni koMthetho nentando yeSinintsi, kwaye ngokoke—

(a)

nokuba ngurhulumente okanye iziko okanye iarhente zikarhulumente nakuyiphina inqanaba kwaye akukho namntu, ongaphazamisa ukusebenza kweenkundla;

(b) uRhulumente, esebenzisa eminye imithetho namanye amanyathelo, kufuneka ancedise kwaye akhusele iinkundla ukuqinisekisa ukuzimela, ukungathathi cala, isidima, ukufikeleleka nokusebenza kwaye ukuqinisekisa ukuba ziyathobela iimigaqo emiselwe kwicandelo 165.

(3) Umyalelo okanye isigqibo senkundla siyambopha uRhulumente nabantu bonke namaziko karhulumente nearhente apho osebenza khona, bawuthobele.

(4) Akhonto kwelicandelo okufuneka ibonakale njenge thintela UMthetho wePalamente ukwenza iimisebenzi ngaphandle kokugweba iinkqubo,kwilunga lejaji, kuxhomekeke ukuba umsebenzi wezonkqubo awuphikisani nokuzimela kwelungu lejaji echaphazelekayo ekuqhubeni umsebenzi wakhe woMthetho kwaye awuphikisani nokuzimela koMthetho ngokubanzi. 165 Imigaqo ekhokhela ijaji

(1) Ekusebenziseni igunya lolawulo, amalungu olawulo kufuneka akhokhelwe yiimigaqo elandelayo—

(a)

ubulungisa kufuneka benziwe kuwo wonke, kungakhathaleki ubume bomntu;

(b) ubulungisa bungalibaziseki kwaye ekugqibeleni amalungu agwebayo kufuneka enze umsebenzi wawo wolawulo ngokungasolisiyo kwaye ngokukhawuleza okufanelekileyo;

(c)

uxanduva leenkundla lubalulekile ngokugqithisileyo ekukhuseleni amalungelo oluntu neenkululeko nolawulo loMthetho.

(2) Amalungu eejaji ngabanye kwaye behlangene kufuneka bahloniphe babuye banike imbeko amagosa agwebayo njengabathenjwe luluntu kwaye kufuneka bazame ukukhulisa ukuzimela ukuze bagcine ukuthenjwa nguwonkewonke kwiinkqubo zolawulo.

(3) Ekwenzeni isigqibo esigwebayo,ilungu lejaji kufuneka senziwe ngokukhululekileyo kwaye kungenakuphazamiseka okanye impembelelo engafanelekanga.

(4) Amalungu ejaji akufuneki—

(a)

bazibandakanye kwimisebenzi yezepolitiki;

(b) babambe iwofisi okanye babengamalungu emibutho yezepolitiki;

(c)

acele imali ye okanye ukuba negalelo ukuya nakuwuphi umbutho weze politiki; okanye

(d) ukubakho kwiintlanganiso zepolitiki.

(5)Amalungu ejaji akufuneki acele okanye amkele naziphina izipho, izidlo, imboleko okanye isisa esingaphembelela ukuziphatha ekugwebeni okanye banike ukubonakalisa okungafanelekanga ekugwebeni.

(6) Amalungu ejaji kufuneka bakhokhelise umsebenzi wabo wokugweba ngaphezu kwayo yonke imisebenzi, kwaye akufuneki babandakanye nakuyiphina

imisebenzi ephazamisana kunye okanye ukufaka umsebenzi wabo wokugweba engozini.

(7) Amalungu ejaji kufuneka athathe amanyathelo afanelekileyo okugcina nokuphucula ubungcali bolwazi lwabo, izakhono nendlela yokuziphatha kwabo, kwaye kanye kufuneka bahlale bephambili ngokuziphuhlisa ngemithetho yasekhaya neyezizwe ngezizwe. 166 INkundla yoMgaqo

(1) inkundla yoMgaqo yiyo inkundla eyongamileyo eyaziwayo kwaye ine—

(a)

Ijaji eyintloko nesekela jaji eyintloko; kwaye

(b) nezinye ijaji ezintlanu zenkundla yomgaqo;

(2) Xa inkonzo zejaji ebambileyo zifunwa kwinkundla yoMgaqo okwexesha eliqingqiweyo, ijaji eyintloko ingatyumba ijaji okanye owayesakuba yijaji isebenze njengejaji yenkundla yoMgaqo okweloxesha.

(3) Imiba ephambi kwenkundla yoMgaqo—

(a)

ngephathelane ngokusolwa lolwaphulo lamalungelo oluntu asisiseko okanye inkululeko emiselwe kusahluko 4, okanye okuphathelane nonyulo lukaMongameli okanye uSekela Mongameli, kufuneka lumanyelwe zijaji zonke zenkundla;

(b) ngaphandle kwemiba ebhekiselwe kumhlathi (a),kufuneka umanyelwe ngobuncinane zijaji ezintathu zenkundla ; kodwa uMthetho wePalamente okanye imithetho yenkundla ingabonelela imiba engenelanayo imanyelwe yijaji enye okanye ezingaphezulu zenkundla.

(4) Ijaji okanye ezake zabazijaji ezatyunjwa ukuba zibambe ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (2) ingaqhubeka ihlala njenge jaji yenkundla yoMgaqo emva kokutyunjwa kwabo sekuphelelwe lixesha, ngomsebenzi wokujongana naziphina inkqubo eziqale ngaphambi kwabo ngexesha bebambile. 167 ULawulo lweNkundla yoMgaqo

(1) Inkundla yoMaqo—

(a)

yiyo inkundla ephezulu kuyo yonke imicimbi yomgaqo, nezigqibo zayo kuloomicimbi ibopha zonke iinkundla;

(b) yiyo egqibayo kuphela nemicimbi yomgaqo nemiba edibanisa izigqibo zomgaqo, ingakumbi iimbekiselo nokuphumeza ngaphantsi kwecandelo 131(8)(b) nomhlathi 9(2) oweShedyuli yeSihlanu; kwaye

(c)

yenza isigqibo sokugqibela nokuba umcimbi unokwenza noMgaqo okanye nokuba umba nokuba udibane nesigqibo somba woMgaqo.

(2) Ngenxa yaloMgaqo, kuphela iNkundla yoMgaqo inga—

(a)

cebisa nakusiphina isindululo soMthetho osiSeko, kodwa ingenza ngoluhlobo kuphela apho uMthetho ochaphazelekayo ubhekiselwe kuyo ngokwemiqathango yaloMgaqo;

(b) chophela kwaye igqibe ngeembambano ezinxulumene nonyulo kwiwofisi kaMongameli;

(c)

chophela kwaye igqibe ngeembambano ezinxulumene nokuba okanye hayi umntu ufanelekile ukubamba iwofisi kaSekela Mongameli; okanye

(d) gqiba nokuba iPalamente okanye uMongameli woyisakele ukuzalisekisa uxanduva loMgaqo.

(3) INkundla yoMgaqo yenza isigqibo sokugqibela nokuba uMthetho wePalamente usesisekweni, kwaye kufuneka iqinisekise nawuphina umyalelo ongqubana nomgaqo owenziwe ngenye inkundla ngaphambi kokubana lommiselo ungabanamandla.

(4) UMthetho wasePalamente ungabonelela ngokusebenza ngegunya leNkundla yoMgaqo kwaye ngalonjongo inganika amandla okwenza iimithetho yenkundla.

(5) IMithetho yeNkundla yoMgaqo kufuneka ivumele umntu, sawube kuzimfuno zobulungisa kwaye kunye okanye ngaphandle kwemvume yeNkundla Yomgaqo—

(a)

izise umba osisiseko ngqo kwiNkundla yoMgaqo;

(b) ukubhena ngqo kwiNkundla yoMgaqo kusuka nakuyiphina inkundla;

(c)

ukuvela njengo mhlobo wenkundla.

168 INkundla Ephezulu

(1) INkundla Ephezulu yinkundla eyongameleyo emisiweyo kwaye iqulethe—

(a)

Jjaji eyiNtloko neSekela Jaji eyiNtloko;

(b) iijaji zingabi ngaphantsi kwezimbini zeNkundla ephezulu; kwaye

(c)

naziphina ijaji ezengezelelweyo ezityunjwe ngaphantsi kwecandelwana

(2)

(2) xa umsebenzi wejaji eyongezelelweyo ufunwa kwiNkundla Ephezulu okwexesha eliqingqiweyo, iJaji eyiNtloko ingatyumba ijaji yeNkundla Ephakemeyo okanye owayesawuba yijaji yeNkundla Ephezulu okweloxesha.

(3) Ijaji okanye abake baba zijaji abatyunjwe ukuba babambe ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (2) ingaqhubeka ihlala njengeejaji zeNkundla Ephezulu emva kokuba ixesha labo liphelile, ngenjongo yokujongana nayiphina inkqubo eqale ngaphambi kwabo ngexesha besabambile. 169 ULawulo lweNkundla Ephezulu

(1) INkundla Ephezulu yinkundla yokugqibela yesibheno eZimbabwe, ngaphandle kwemiba apho iNkundla yoMgaqo inolawulo.

(2) Ngokululamela icandelwana (1), uMthetho wePalamente ungengeza ulawulo kwaye namandla kwiNkundla Ephezulu.

(3) UMthetho wePalamente ungabonelela ngokusetyenziswa kolawulo yiNkundla Ephezulu kwaye ngalomsebenzi inganikezela amandla imithetho yenkundla.

(4) IMithetho yeNkundla inganikezela kuMbhalisi weNkundla Ephezulu naliphina ulawulo lweNkundla namandla kumatyala oluntu—

(a)

ukwenza iimiyalelo kumatyala angaphikiswanga, ngaphandle kwemiyalelo echaphazela ubume okanye ugcino lwabantwana;

(b) ukugqiba okokukuqala okanye kwimiba engenelanayo, kuquka izicelo zemiyalelo, kodwa hayi iimiba echaphazela inkululeko eyanawuphina umntu; kodwa imithetho kufuneka inike nawuphina umntu ochaphazelekayo ngumyalelo wombhalisi okanye isigqibo selungelo lokubana sifunyanwe yijaji yeNkundla Ephezulu,

ongayingqinisekisa, ayihlengae okanye ayibeke ecaleni okanye awunike nawuphina umyalelo abona ufanele. 170 INkundla Ephakemeyo INkundla Ephakemeyo yiyo inkundla ephezulu emisiweyo kwaye ibandakanya—

(a)

IJaji eyiNtloko, iSekela Jaji eyiNtloko neJaji enguMongameli yeNkundla Ephakemeyo; kwaye

(b) ezinye iijaji zenkundla ephakemeyo ezingatyunjwa kusuka kwixesha ngexesha. 171 ULawulo lweNkundla Ephakemeyo

(1) INkundla Ephakemeyo—

(a)

inolawulo lwantlandlolo phezu kweemiba yoluntu neyobukrelemnqa kwiZimbabwe yonke;

(b) inolawulo lokusa iliso iinkundla zomantyi nezinye iinkundla ozayamileyo nokuphonononga izigqibo;

(c)

ingagqiba ngemiba yesiseko ngaphandle kwezo kuphela iNkundla yoMgaqo ingagqiba ngazo; kwaye

(d) inolawulo lesibheno engabe igunyaziswe nguMthetho wePalamente.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente ungabonelela ngokusebenza kolawulo yiNkundla Ephakemeyo kwaye ngalonjongo inganika amandla okwenza imithetho yenkundla.

(3) UMthetho wePalamente ungabonelela ngeNkundla Ephakemeyo ukuba yahlukaniswe ngamacandelo akhethekileyo, kodwa lonke ulwahlulo kufuneka lenelise ukusebenza ulawulo lwayo ongokubanzi, lweNkundla Ephakemeyo nakowuphina ummba othwe thaca phambi kwayo.

(4) Imithetho yenkundla inganika umbhalisi weNkundla Ephakemeyo amandla kumatyala oluntu—

(a)

ukwenza imiyalelo kumatyala angaphikiswanga, ngaphandla kwemiyalelo echaphazela ubume bento okanye ugcino nokulindwa kwabantwana;

(b) ukugqiba okokukuqala okanye imiba ehambelanayo, kuquka izicelo zemiyalelo, kodwa hayi imiba echaphazela inkululeko yoba nawuphina umntu; kodwa imithetho kufuneka inike nawuphina umntu ochatshazelwa ngumyalelo wombhalisi okanye isigqibo selungelo lokubana ibuye iphononongwe yijaji yeNkundla Ephakemeyo engayiqinisekisa, ilungiswe okanye ibekelwe ecaleni okanye inike nawuphina umyalelo okanye isigqibo abona sifanele. 172 INkundla yabaSebenzi

(1) INkundla yabaSebenzi emisiweyo kwaye ibandakanya —

(a)

ijaji enkulu; ne.

(b) iijaji zeNkundla abaSebenzi zingatyunjwa ngexesha ngexesha. [EliCandelwana lilungiswe liCandelo 3 loMthetho 10 oka 2017]

(2) INkundla zabaSebenzi inalo ulawulo phezu kwemiba yomsebenzi nengqesho ngokunikwa nguMthetho wePalamente

(3) UMthetho wePalamente ungabonelela ngokusebenza kolawulo lweNkundla yabaSebenzi kwaye ngalonjongo inganika amandla okwenza imithetho yenkundla. 173 INkundla yoLawulo

(1) INkundla yoLawulo yinkundla esesikweni kwaye ibandakanya—

(a)

ijaji endala; kwane;

(b) iijaji eziloluhlobo zeNkundla yoLawulo zingatyunjwa emva ngexesha ngexesha. [EliCandelwana lilungiswe liCandelo 4 loMthetho 10 oka 2017]

(2) INkundla yoLawulo inalo ulawulo phezu kwemiba yolawulo engaba iyinikwe nguMtheth wePalamente.

(3) UMthetho wePalamente ungabonelela ngokusebenza kolawulo yiNkundla yoLawulo kwaye ngalonjongo inganika amandla okwenza iimithetho yenkundla. 174 Ezinye iinkundla namaqumrhu

(1) UMthetho wePalamente ungabonelela ngokusekwa, ukwakhiwa nolwawulo lwe—

(a)

nkundla zomantyi,ukugweba amatyala oluntu nawobukrelemqa;

(b) nkundla yoMthetho wesintu apho ulawulo okokukuqala libandakanya ukusetyenziswa koMthetho wesintu;

(c)

ezinye iinkundla ezingaphantsi kweNkundla Ephakemeyo;na

(d) maqumrhu olamlo nezinye inkqubo ezingancedisa ukusombulula iimbambano.

(2) ngokuvumelana nelicandelo 188 (4) loMgaqo-siseko imivuzo, izibonelelo nezinye iinzuzo zeejaji zeNkundla yaBasebenzi kwaneNkundla yoLawulo abaziphetheyo okanye iofisi abayibamibileyo kusukela kumhla wokuqalisa kwalooMthetho azisoze zincitshiswe. [EliCandelwana lilungiswe liCandelo 5 loMthetho 10 oka 2017]

175 Amandla eenkundla kwimiba yomgaqo

(1) Apho iNkundla yenza ummiselo ophathelane ngokungafaneleki koMthetho ngokomgaqo okanye ukuziphatha kuka Mongameli okanye iPalamente, ummiselo awunamandla ngaphandle uqinisekiswe yiNkundla yoMgaqo.

(2) INkundla eyenza ummiselo yokungabi semthethweni kwisiseko ebhekiselwe kwicandelwana (1) inganika isigwebo sethutyana okanye isiqabu sethutyana kwiqela, okanye imise inkqubo, kusalindeleke isigqibo seNkundla yoMgaqo ngokufaneleka koMthetho okanye ukuziphatha okuchaphazelekayo.

(3) Nawuphina umntu onomdla oweneleyo angabhena okanye acele ngqo kwiNkundla yoMgaqo ukuqinisekisa okanye ukwahlukanisa ummiselo ophathelene nokungabi semthethweni kwisiseko yinkundla ngokwemiqathango ekucandelwana (1).

(4) Xa umba ophathelene nesiseko uvela nakuyiphina inkqubo phambi kwenkundla, umntu owongamela lonkundla anga, okanye xa kwenziwe isicelo naliphina ilungu kwiinkqubo, kufuneka ayibhekise kwiNkundla yoMgaqo ngaphandle aqwalasele ukuba isicelo asonwabisi okanye siyacaphukisa.

(5) UMthetho wePalamente okanye imithetho yenkundla kufuneka ibonelele iimbekiselo kwiNkundla yoMgaqo ngommiselo ophathelene nokungqubana nesiseko

owenziwe ngemiqathango yecandelwana (1) yinkundla ngaphandle kweNkundla yoMgaqo.

(6) Xa kugqitywa ngomba ophathelene nesiseko okwigunya layo inkundla kufuneka—

(a) bhengeze ukuba nawuphina uMthetho okanye ukuziphatha okungangqinelani noMgaqo awuphelelanga ngobukhulu bokungangqinelani;

(b) yenze ummiselo oyinyaniso kwaye olungeleleneyo, kuquka ummiselo olawula isiphumo esiqalela isibhengezo sokungquzulana nommiselo orhoxisa ngaphantsi kwemiqathango okanye kungekho miqathango isibhengezo sokugquzulana nakuliphina ixesha ukuvumela osemagunyeni ofanelekileyo alungise esisiphene. 176 Amandla emvelo eNkundla yoMgaqo, iNkundla Ephezulu neNkundla Ephakemeyo INkundla yoMqaqo, iNkundla Ephezulu neNkundla Ephakemeyo zinamandla emvelo ukukhusela nokulawula iinkqubo zazo kwaye nokuphuhlisa uMthetho oxhaphakileyo okanye uMthetho wesintu, kuqatshelwa iimfuno zobulungisa nemithetho yoMgaqo. INXALENYE 2 Ukutyunjwa Nexesha Lengqesho Lamalungu Agwebayo 177 Isiqinisekiso seejaji yeNkundla yoMgaqo

(1) Umntu unesiqinisekiso sokutyunjwa njengejaji yeNkundla yoMgaqo xa engummi waseZimbabwe, angaba neminyaka engamashumi amane kwaye enolwazi oluphangaleleyo ngoMthetho wesiseko kwaye ngaphezulu eneziqinisekiso ezilandelayo—

(a)

waba yijaji yenkundla enolawulo olungaqingqwanga kwiimithetho yoluntu neyobukrelemqa eLizweni apho uMthetho ungowama Roman-Dutch okanye amaNgesi, kwaye isiNgesi ilulimi olwaziwayo olusemthethweni; okanye;
