(b) okweminyaka elishumi nambini, nokuba eqhubeka okanye hayi enesiqinisekiso sokusebenza njengegqwetha—

(i) eZimbabwe; okanye;

(ii) eLizweni apho uMthetho oxaphakileyo ungowe Roman-Dutch okanye amaNgesi kwaye isiNgesi ilulimi olwaziwayo olusemthethweni kwaye unesiqinisekiso sokusebenza.

(2) Ukuze utyunjwe njengejaji yeNkundla yoMgaqo umntu kufuneka abe ngophilileyo kwaye abe ngumntu ofanelekileyo okuba sewofiSini njengejaji. 178 Isiqinisekiso seejaji zeNkundla Ephezulu

(1) Umntu unesiqinisekiso sokutyunjwa njengejaji yeNkundla Ephezulu xa engummi waseZimbabwe kwaye eneminyaka engamashumi amane okuzalwa, kwaye ukwengeza—

(a)

ebeyijaji yenkundla enolawulo olungaqingqwanga kwimiba yoluntu okanye eyobukrelemqa kwiLizwe apho uMthetho oqhelekileyo ungowe Roman-Dutch okanye owamaNgisi kwaye isiNgesi lulimi olwaziwayo olusemthethweni; okanye

(b) ngobuncinane okweminyaka elishumi, nokuba ngeqhubekekayo okanye hayi, ebenesiqinisekiso sokusebenza njengegqwetha—

(i) eZimbabwe; okanye

(ii) eLizweni apho uMthetho oxhaphakeleyo ungowe RomanDutch okanye owamaNgesi kwaye isiNgesi lulimi olwaziwayo olusmthethweni kwaye unesiqinisekiso sokusebenza.

(2) ukuze utyunjwe njengejaji yeNkundla Ephezulu umntu kufuneka abe ngophilileyo kwaye ibengumntu ofanelekileyo ukuba sewofiSini njenge jaji. 179 Iziqinisekiso zeejaji zeNkundla Ephakemeyo, iNkundla yabaSebenzi neNkundla yoLawulo

(1) Umntu ufanelekile ukutyunjwa njengejaji yeNkundla Ephakemeyo, iNkundla yabaSebenzi okanye iNkundla eLawulayo xa ubuncinane eneminyaka engamashumi amane yokuzalwa kwaye ngokwengeza—

(a)

eyijaji okanye ebiyijaji yenkundla ngolawulo olungaqingqwanga kwimiba yoluntu okanye eyobukrelemqa kwiLizwe apho uMthetho oxhaphakileyo ungowe Roman-Dutch okanye amaNgesi kwaye isiNgesi lulimi olusemthethweni olwaziwayo; okanye

(b) ngobuncinane ingaba yiminyaka esisixhenxe, nokuba engemanga okanye ufanelekile ukusebenza njengegqwetha—

(i) eZimbabwe;

(ii) kwiLizwe apho uMthetho oxhaphakileyo ungowe Roman-Dutch kwaye isiNgesi ilulimi olusemthethweni olwaziwayo; okanye

(iii) xa engummi waseZimbabwe, apho uMthetho oxhaphakileyo sisiNgesi kwaye isiNgesi lulimi olusemthethweni olwaziwayo; kwaye ngoku ufanelekile ukusebenza.

(2) Ukutyunjwa njengejaji yeNkundla Ephakemeyo, iNkundla yabaSebenzi okanye iNkundla eLawulayo umntu kufuneka abe ngofanelekileyo kwaye oqondileyo ukuba sewofiSini njenge jaji. 180 Ukutyunjwa kweejaji

(1) Ijaji eyintloko, iSekela-jaji eyiNtloko noMongameli weejaji yeNkundla ePhakemeyo nezinye iijaji zityunjwa nguMongameli ngokungqinelana neli candela.

(2) Iijaji eyintloko, iSekela-jaji ayintloko noMongameli weejaji yeNkundla ePhakemeyo zizakutyunjwa ngumongameli emveni kothethwano neKhomishini yeNkonzo eGwebayo.

(3) Xa ukutyunjwa kwejaji eyintloko, iSekela jaji eyiNtloko okanye uMongameli weejaji weNkundla ePhakemeyo ayingqinelani neengcebeso ezenziwe yiKhomishini yeNkonzo eGwebayo ngokusekwe kwicandelwana (2), uMongameli uzakwenza ukuba iSenethi yaziswe ngokungxama okukhawulezileyo xa kuvuma; Kuxhomekeke ekuthini, ekuphepheni ukuthandabuza, kuyabhengezwa ukuba isigqibo sikamongameli kolutyunjo asiguquki.

(4) Nanini na, xa kufanelekile ukutyumba ijaji ngaphandle kwejaji eyintloko, isekelajaji eyintloko okanye uMongameli wejaji yeNkundla ePhakemeyo iKhomishini yeNkonzo eGwebayo kufuneka—

(a)

2013

ibhengengeze esosikhundla; kwayeke

(b) imeme uMongameli nowonke-wonke ityumbe; kwaye

(c)

yenze udliwano-ndlebe nowonke-wonke nabagqatswa abafanelekileyo; kwaye

(d) ilunguselele uluhlu lwabantu abathathu abaqinisekileyo njengabafanele loo ofisi; kwaye ingenise kuMongameli, apho, ngapahantsi kwecandelwana

(5), uMongameli kufuneka atyumbe omnye wabagqatswa kwi ofisi echaphazelekayo.

(e)

ingenise uMongameli apho, ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (5), uMongameli kufuneka atyumbe omnye wabagqatswa kwiofisi echaphazelekayo.

(5) Xa uMongameli engaqaphela kubantu abangeniswe kuye ngokusekwe kwicandelwana (4)(e), akakho akulungeleyo ukutyunjelwa kwi ofisi, kufuneka afune ukuba iKhomishini yeNkonzo eGwebayo ingenise kwakhona olunye ulunhlu lwabantu abathathu abafanelekileyo. Apho ke uMongameli kufuneka atyumbe omnye wabagqatswa kwi ofisi echaphazelekayo.

(6) UMongameli kufuneka enze isibhengezo ngokuqeshwa konke ngaphantsi kwecandelo eliyakupapashwa kwiGazethi.

(7) Iiofisi zejaji enkulu yeNkundla yaBasebenzi nejaji enkulu yeNkundla yoLawulo kufuneka zingenwe ngenye ijaji okanye enye ijaji okanye ijaji ebambileyo yenkundla echaphazelekayo, kwaye ityunjwe yijaji eyintloko emva kothethwano noKhomishini yeNkonzo eGwebayo. [EliCandelwana lilungiswe liCandelo 6 loMthetho 10 oka 2017]

181 Ukutyunjwa kweejaji ezibambileyo

(1) Xa iwofisi yeJaji eyiNtloko ingenamntu okanye xa lowo osewofiSini engenelisi ukwenza imisebenzi, bengasenelisi ukwenza imisebenzi, isekela jaji elendelayo eyeNkundla yoMgaqo iyabamba njeJaji eyiNtloko.

(2) Xa iwofisi ye—

(a)

Jaji enguMongameli yeNkundla Ephakemeyo;

(b) ijaji enkulu yeNkundla yaBasebenzi; okanye;

(c)

ijaji enkulu yeNkundla yoLawulo;

ayinamntu okanye osewofiSini akasenelisi ukwenza imisebenzi yalowofisi, ijaji elandelayo ephezulu yenkundla echaphazelekayo iyabamba njengeJaji enguMongameli. [EliCandelwana lilungiswe liCandelo 7 loMthetho 10 oka 2017]

(3) Xa umsebenzi wejaji eyongezelelweyo yeNkundla Ephakemeyo, INkundla Yabasenzi okanye INkundla Elawulayo kufunakala okwexesha eliqigqiweyo uMongameli,esenza ngokucetyiswa yiKhomishini Yenkonzo Egwebayo, angatyumba owayesawuba yijaji ukubamba kulowofisi kungegqithi inyanga ezilishumi nanye,ixesha elingongezwa okwethuba elinye elenyanga ezilishumi nanye.

(4) Abantu abatyunjelwe ukubamba ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (3) bangaqhubeka ukuhlala njengejaji emveni ukutyunjwa kwabo kuphelelwe lixesha, ngenjongo yokujongana naziphina iinkqubo eziqale ngaphambi kwabo ngexesha besabambili.

182 Ukutyunjwa koomantyi namanye amalungu ejaji UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka ubonelele ngokutyunjwa koomantyi namanye amalungu agwebayo ngaphandle kweejaji, kodwa—

(a)

oomantyi kufuneka batyunjwe yiKhomishini Yenkonzo Egwebayo;

(b) amagosa oMthetho ngaphandle koomantyi okanye iijaji kufuneka zityunjwe ngemvume yeKhomishini Yenkonzo Egwebayo;

(c)

konke okutyunjwa kufuneka kube kwenziwe lubala kwaye kungena loyiko, umkhethe, ukucingela okanye kungathathwa cala.

183 Amalungu agwebayo okungamelanga atyunjwe kwinkundla ezingaphezulu kwenye Ngaphandle ngenye indlela ebonelelwe kulo Mgaqo siseko, umntu akufuneka atyunjwe njangelungu eligwebayo ngaphezu kwenkundla enye. 184 Ukutyunjwa kwejaji makubonise uluntu Ukutyunjelwa kwijaji kufuneka kubonise ngokubanzi iyantlukwano nesimo seSini eZimbabwe. 185

Isifungo sewofisi

(1) Ngaphambi kokuba iJaji eyiNtloko okanye iSekela Jaji eyiNtloko ingene ewofiSini kufuneka athabathe phambi kukaMongameli, isifungo sejaji ngendlela emiswe kwiShedyuli yeSithathu.

(2) Ngaphambi kokuba ijaji ngaphandlekwe kweJaji Eyintloko okanye iSekela Jaji eyiNtloko kufuneka ithabathe isifungo sejaji phambi kweJaji eyiNtloko okanye ijaji ephezulu ekhoyo ngendlela emiswe kwiShedyuli yeSithathu.

(3) IMithetho yePalamente apho ngaphantsi oomantyi namanye amalungu abagwebi, ngaphandle kwejaji, batyunjwa kufuneka bamiselwe isifungo okufuneka sithatyathwe ngalomalungu abagwebi. 186 Ixesha leejaji lokuba se ofisini

(1) Iijaji zeNkundla yoMgaqo zityunjwa okwexesha elingavuselelwayo elingedluli iminyaka elishumi nantlanu, kodwa—

(a)

kufuneka bathathe umhlala phantsi xa sebefikelele iminyaka engamashumi asisixhenxe yokuzalwa; kwaye

(b) emva kokugqiba ixesha lengqesho, bangatyunjwa njengejaji zeNkundla Ephezulu okanye iNkundla Ephakemeyo, ngokufuna kwabo, xa belufanele olutyunjo.

(2) Iijaji zeNkundla Ephezulu, iNkundla Ephakemeyo kwaye naziphina ezinye iijaji ezikwiwofisi kusukela kumhla wokungena ewofiSini bade bayofikisa iminyaka engamashumi asisixhenxe yokuzalwa,apho bethatha umhlalaphantsi.

(3) Umntu angatyunjwa njengejaji yeNkundla Ephezulu,iNkundla Ephakemeyo kwaye nayiphina inkundla okwexesha elisisigxina,kodwa xa umntu ethe watyunjwa,ngaphandle kokuba ebambile, uyayeka ukuba yijaji ekufikiseni iminyaka engamashumi asisixhenxe yokuzalwa nokuba ixesha lokutyunjwa kwakhe lingakapheli.

(4) Nokuba nangona ijaji irhoxile okanye ifikise iminyaka engamashumi asisixhenxe yokuzalwa,okanye kumba kwindawo yejaji yeNkundla yoMgaqo,

okanye ijaji okubhekiselwe kwicandelwana (3), ifikise ixesha lesiphelo lokubase wofiSini,kufuneka aqhubeke ukuhlala njengejaji ngokwenjongo yokusebenza naziphina iinkqubo eziqale ngexesha lakhe eseyijaji.

(5) Ijaji inga rhoxa kwiwofisi yayo nangaliphina ixesha ngokwenza isaziso esibhaliweyo kuMongameli sisegqithiswa yiKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo.

(6) Iwofisi yejaji ayinakubhangiswa ngexesha lakhe lengqesho. 187 Ukususwa kweejaji kwiwofisi

(1) Ijaji ingasuswa ewofiSini kuphela xa—

(a)

xa ingenelisi imisebenzi yewofisi yakhe, ngenxa yokungabikho ezinqondweni okanye ukukhubazeka ngokomzimba;

(b) ukungenelisi konke;okanye

(c)

ukuziphatha kakubi konke;

Kwaye ijaji ayinakususwa ewofiSini ngaphandle ngokungqinelana nelicandelo.

(2) xa uMongameli eqwalasela ukuba ukususa iJaji eyiNtloko kwiwofisi kufuneka iphandwe, uMongameli kufuneka atyumbe iqumrha kufunisiswe ngalomba.

(3) xa iKomiti yeNkonzo Egwebayo icebisa uMongameli ukuba umbuzo wokususwa nayiphina ijaji, kuquka neJaji eyiNtloko, kufuneka iphandwe, uMongameli kufuneka atyumbe iqumrhu kufunisiswe ngalomba.

(4) Iqumrhu elityunjwe ngaphantsi kwelicandelo kufuneka libandakanye phantse amalungu amathathu atyunjwe nguMongameli, apho—

(a)

phantse omnye kufuneka—

(i) abe ngosebenze njenge jaji yeNkundla Ephezulu okanye iNkundla Ephakemeyo eZimbabwe; okanye

(ii) abe ngosebenza okanye wake waba sewofiSini njengejaji yenkundla enolawulo olungaqingqwanga kwiimiba yoluntu okanye yobukrelemqa kwiLizwe apho uMthetho ungowe Roman-Dutch okanye owamaNgesi kwaye isiNgesi ilulimi olusemthethweni olwaziwayo;

(b) ingaba omnye kufuneka onyulwe kuluhlu lwabathathu okanye amagqwetha angaphezulu aneminyaka esisixhenxe amava okanye ngaphezulu abatyunjwe ngumbutho, esekwe ngaphantsi koMthetho wePalamente, omele amagqwetha eZimbabwe.

(5) Umbutho ojoliswe kwicandalwana (4)(b) kufuneka lilungise uluhlu olujoliswe kwelocandelwana xa sawube lifunwa nguMongameli.

(6) UMongameli kufuneka atyumbe omnye wamalungu equmru onyulwe ngaphantsi kwelicandelo abe ngusihlalo welo qumrhu.

(7) Iqumrhu elonyulo ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (2) okanye (3) kufuneka lifune umbuzo wokususa ijaji echaphazelekayo kwiwofisi kwaye sawube enjenjalo kufuneka apapashe iziphumo kuMongameli kwaye acebise nokuba okanye ijaji ingesuswa kwiwofisi.

(8) UMongameli kufuneka enze ngokugqinelana neezindululo zequmrhu ngokwemiqathango yecandelwana (7).

(9) Iqumrhu elityunjwe ngaphantsi kwelicandelo linamalungelo afanayo kwaye namandla njengeeKhomishini ngaphantsi kweKhomishini yoMthetho wophando, okanye nawuphina uMthetho ongathatha indawo.

(10) Xa umbuzo wokususa ijaji kwiwofisi ibhekiselwe kwiqumrhu ngaphantsi kwelicandelwana, ijaji iyanqunyanyiswa kwiwofisi kude uMongameli, ngokucetyiswa liqumrha arhoxise ukunqunyanyiswa okanye asuse ijaji ewofiSini.

(11) UMthetho wePalamente ungaxhobisa iKhomishini Yenkonzo Egwebayo okanye iqumrhu elityunjwe ngaphantsi kwelicandelo ukufuna nayiphina ijaji ukungenisa kuxilongo lonyango yibhodi yonyango etyunjwe ngalonjongo, ukuze iqinisekise isimo somzimba okanye impilo yengqondo yakhe. 188 Iimeko zengqesho nexesha lamalungu agwebayo

(1) Iijaji zinelungelo kwiimivuzo,izibonelelo nezinye iinzuzo ezimiswe kwixesha ngexesha yiKhomishini Yenkonzo Egwebayo ngemvume kaMongameli ayiphiwe ngemva kothethwano noMphathiswa onoxanduva ngezobulungisa kwaye ngokucetyiswa nguMphathiswa onoxanduva ngezimali.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka ubonelele ngeemeko zeengqesho zamagosa agwebayo ngaphandle kweejaji kwaye kufuneka uqiniseke ukuba ukuphakanyiswa, ukutshintshela nokugxothwa, kwaye nayiphina indlela zoluleko ezithatyathiweyo ezichaselene nabo kufuneka zenzeke—

(a) ngemvume zeKhomishini Yenkonzo Egwebayo; kwaye

(b) ngendlela elungileyo kwaye elubala engenaloyiko,umkhethe okanye ukucingela.

(3) Iimivuzo, izibonelelo nezinye iinzuzo zamalungu agwebayo yintlawulo kwiNgxowa Yengeniso Edityanisiweyo.

(4) Iimivuzo, izibonelelo nezinye inzuzo zamalungu agwebayo akufuneki zincitshiswe logama beqeshiwe okanye bebambile kwiwofisi. INXALENYE 3 Ikhomishini Yenkonzo Egwebayo 189 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo

(1) Kukho iKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo ebandakanya —

(a)

IJaji eyiNtloko;

(b) ISekela Jaji eyiNtloko;

(c)

IJaji enguMongameli yeNkundla Ephakemeyo;

(d) enye yejaji etyunjwe zijaji zeNkundla yoMgaqo, iNkundla Ephezulu, iNkundla Ephakemeyo, iNkundla yabaSebenzi neNkundla yoLawulo;

(e)

Igqwetha jikelele;

(f)

umantyi oyontloko;

(g) usihlalo weKhomishini yeNkonzo yoLuntu;

(h) amagqwetha amathathu asebenzayo anamava akufutshane neminyaka esisixhenxe etyunjwe ngumbutho, esekwe ngaphantsi koMthetho wePalamente, omele amagqwetha eZimbabwe;

(i)

omnye unjingalwazi okanye umhloli ophezulu woMthetho osekwe ngumbutho omele ininzi lwetishala zoMthetho kwi yunivesithi zaseZimbabwe okanye ukungabikho kombutho ololuhlobo, otyunjwe nguMongameli;

(j)

2013

umntu omnye ebuncinaneni neminyaka esisixhenxe osebenze eZimbabwe njengo mcwangcisi zimali woluntu okanye umphicothi, kwaye otyunjwe ngumbutho, osekwe ngaphantsi koMthetho wePalamente omele abantu aboluhlobo; kwaye

(k) umntu omnye oneminyaka esisixhenxe ebuncinaneni elawula ezengqesho, etyunjwe nguMongameli.

(2) IJaji eyiNtloko okanye ekubeni ngabingakho iSekela Jaji eyiNtloko wongamela intlanganiso zeKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo, kwaye ekungabikho kwabo bobabini nayiphina intlanganiso amalugu akhoyo onyula omnye wamalungu ukungomela intlanganiso.

(3) Amalungu eKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo ebhekiswe kumhlathi (d),

(h), (i), (j) no (k) wecandelwana (1) batyunjwa okwesithuba seminyaka essithandathu engavuselelwayo. 190 Imisebenzi yeKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo

(1) IKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo ingakwazi ukunika ingcebiso kurhulumente nakowuphina umcimbi enxulumene noMthetho okanye ulawulo loMthetho kwaye urhulumente anganika indlebe malunga nengcebiso enje.

(2) IKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo kufuneka ikhuthaze kwaye iququzelele ukuzimela noxanduva leejaji kwaye ukusebenza kakuhle, ngokuchanekileyo kwaye nolawulo lobulungisa olubala eZimbabwe, kwaye inamandla wonke alomsebenzi.

(3) IKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo ngemvume kaMpathiswa onoxanduva ngobulungisa, angenza immiselo, nangayiphina injongo emiselwe kwelicandelo.

(4) UMthetho wePalamente unganikeza kwiKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo umsebenzi onxulumene nengqesho, ingqeqesho neemmeko zomsebenzi wabantu abaqeshiweyo kwiNkundla yoMgaqo, iNkundla Ephezulu, iNkundla Ephakemeyo iNkundla yabaSebenzi, iNkundla eLawulayo nezinye inkundla. 191 Ukungabinamkhethe nokubalubala kweKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo IKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo kufuneka iqhube umsebenzi wayo ngendlela enobulungisa, ngoku ngenamkhethe nelubala. INXALENYE 4 Ngokubanzi 192 Ulawulo loMthetho UMthetho osetyenziswa zinkundla zaseZimbabwe nguMthetho obusebenza ngalomhla, ngokulandelayo uguqulwe kancinane. 193 Ulawulo lwenkundla yolwaphuloMthetho Kuphela ezinkundla ezilandelayo zingasebenza okanye zinganikwa ulawulo kumatyala olwaphulo —

(a)

INkundla yoMgaqo, iNkundla Ephezulu, iNkundla Ephakamileyo neenkundla zemantyi;

(b) inkundla okanye indibano egunyazisiweyo yokujongana namatyala angaphantsi koMthetho woluleko, ngobukhulu uluwulo lufanelekile ukunyanzeliswa koluleko. ISAHLUKO 9 Imigaqo yoLawulo loLuntu nobuNkokheli 194 Imithetho esisigxina nemigaqo elawula uwonkewonke

(1) Ulawulo lukawonkewonke kumanqanaba onke karhulumente, kuquka amaziko nearhente zikaRhulumente, namaqumrhu alawulwa ngurhulumente kwanezinye inkampani zikarhulumente,kufuneka zilawulwe ngemfuno yolawulo lwentando yeSininzi nemigaqo emiselwe kuloMgaqo siseko,kuquka imigaqo elandelayo—

(a)

umgangatho ophezulu wokuziphatha kufuneka ukhuthazwe kwaye ugcinwe;

(b) ukusetyenziswa kwezixhobo kakuhle nokongayo kufuneka kukhuthazwe;

(c)

ulawulo loluntu kufuneka lujoliswe kuphuhliso;

(d) iinkonzo kufuneka zibonelelwe ngokungenamkhethe,ngobulungisa,ngo kulinganayo kwaye kungathathi cala;

(e)

iimfuno zabantu kufuneka zisatyelwe ngexesha elifanelekileyo kwaye uluntu kufuneka lukhuthazwe ukubandakanya ekwakhiweni kweenkqubo;

(f)

ulawulo loluntu kufuneka libenoxanduva kwiPalamente nasebantwini;

(g) amaziko nearhente zikarhulumente kwinqanaba zonke kufuneka zisebenzisane;

(h) ulubala kufuneka lukhuthazwe ngoku bonelela uluntu ngolwazi oluchanekileyo, olufikelelekayo ngexesha;

(i)

ulawulo olulungileyo lwegqesho nophuhliso lwamakhono nokukhulisa ukuphakama kwabasebenzi;

(j)

ulawulo loluntu kemele lumelwe ngokubanzi ngabahlali abehlukeneyo baseZimbabwe;

(k) ingqesho, uqeqesho, nokukhulisa ukwenza kufuneka kusekwe ngokufaneleka, ukwenelisa, ukungathathi cala, okungenamkhethe, ulingano lotata nabasetyini nokubandakanywa kwabantu abakhubazekileyo; kwaye uRhulumente kufuneka athathe amanyathelo, kuquka amanyathelo oMthetho, ukukhuthaza ukuziphatha nemigaqo.

(2) Ukutyunjelwa kwiwofisi kumanqanaba onke karhulumente,kuquka amaziko karhulumente nearhente namaqumrhu alawulwa ngurhulumente nenkampani zoluntu,kufuneka zenzeyonke into ngokufanelekileyo. 195 Amaqumrhu oshishino alawulwa ngurhulumente

(1) Inkampani namanye amaqumrhu oshishino akarhulumente okanye alawulwa ngurhulumente kufuneka,ngokwengeza ekuthobeleni imigaqo emiselwe kwicandelwana 194(1), ukuqhuba imisebenzi ukuze bagcine ugqitho loshishino kwaye bamkele ngokubanzi imigangatho emihle yolawulo lequmrhu.

(2) Iinkampani namanye amaqumrhu oshishino ekubhekiselwe kwicandelwano

(1) kufuneka aseke iinkqubo zokuthenga ezinokhuphiswano, ezilubala nezingenamfihlo

196 Uxanduva lwamagosa oluntu nemigaqo yobunkokheli

(1) Igunya elabelwe igosa loluntu yintembeko yoluntu okufuneka isetyenziswe ngendlela—

(a)

engaguqukiyo kwiinjongo zaloMgaqo siseko;

(b) ebonisa intlonipho ebantwini kwaye nokulungela ukubakhonza kunokuba

(c)

ekhuthaza ukuzithemba koluntu kwiwofisi ebanjwe ligosa yoluntu.

(2) Amagosa oluntu kufuneka aziphathe esidlangalaleni nangasese ukuze baphephe nakuyiphina imbambano phakathi kwemfuno zabo nezasesidlangalaleni okanye imfuno zomsebenzi, kwaye bazikhwebule kwizimilo ezenyelisa iwofisi yabo.

(3) Amagosa oluntu akwindawo yobunkokheli kufuneka bahambe ngemiqathango yobukhokheli elandelayo—

(a)

umbono ophangaleleyo nokwenza isigqibo okungenamkhethe;

(b) ukuthembeka ekwenzeni umsebenzi woluntu ;

(c)

ukuba noxanduva kuluntu kwizigqibo nezenzo;

(d) ucwangco nokuzimisela ekunikezeleni kwenkonzo ebantwini. 197 Ixesha lengqesho lentloko zamaqumrhu alawulwa ngurhulumente UMthetho wasePalamente ungamisa imida ngexesha lengqesho lamagosa okanye iintloko zearhente ezilawulwa ngurhulumente okanye namanye amaqumrhu oshishino namashishini oluntu akarhulumente okanye alawulwa ngurhulumente . 198 UMthetho onyanzelisa Isahluko 9 UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka ubonelele amanyathelo okunyanzelisa izibonelelo zesiSahluko, kubandakanya amanyathelo—

(a)

afuna ukuba amagosa oluntu enze ingxelo rhoqo malunga ngempahla yabo;

(b) okusekwa kwendlela yokuziphatha okufuneka ilandelwe ngamagosa oluntu;

(c)

akhankanya imigangatho yolawulo loshishino oluhle okufuneka ilandelwe ngamaqumrhu alawulwa ngurhulumente namanye amaqumrhu oshishino akarhulumente okanye alawulwa ngurhlumente ngokupheleleyo;

(d) abonelela ngokohlwaywa kwabantu abanyasha izibonelelo zesiSahluko okanye nayiphina indlela yokuziphatha okanye imigangatho ebhekiselwe kumhlathi (b). ISAHLUKO 10 Inkonzo Yoluntu 199 INkonzo yoLuntu

(1) Kukho iNkonzo yoLuntu enye,enoxanduva lokulawula iZimbabwe.

(2) INkonzo yoLuntu iquka abantu abaqeshwe nguRhulumente ngaphandle—

(a)

kwamalungu enkonzo yokhuselo nezinye iinkonzo zokhuselo ezingasekwa;

(b) kweejaji, oomantyi nabanye abantu abongamela iinkundla ezisekwe nguMthetho wasePalamente;

(c)

kwamalungu eKhomishini asekwe nguloMgaqo siseko;

(d) kwanamagosa asePalamente; kwaye

(e)

kwanawuphina umntu apho iwofisi okanye isikhundla sakhe esichazwe, nguloMgaqo siseko okanye uMthetho wasePalamente,ongeyiyo nxalenye neNkonzo yoLuntu.

(3) UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka ubonelele ngokucwangciswa, ukwakhiwa,ulawulo,ummiselo, ingqeqesho kwaye, ngaphantsi kwecandelo 203, imiqathango yokusebenza yamalungu eNkonzo yoLuntu. 200 Ukuziphatha kwamalungu eNkonzo yoLuntu

(1) Amalungu eNkonzo yoLuntu kufuneka enze ngokungqinelana naloMgaqo siseko noMthetho.

(2) Akukho lungu leNkonzo yoLuntu elingathobela umyalelo obonakala ngokungekho emthethweni.

(3) Akukho lungu leNkonzo yoLuntu,ekwenzeni umsebenzi walo—

(a)

elingenza ngendlela ebonisa umkhethe;

(b) ngokukhetha iimfuno ezisemthethweni nezawuphina umbutho wepolitiki okanye enze; okanye

(c)

ukonakalisa iimfuno ezisemthethweni nezawuphina imibutho yezepolitiki okanye injongo zepolitiki.

(4) Amalungu eNkonzo yoLuntu akufuneki abambe izikhundla nezawuphina umbutho wezepolitiki.

(5) UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka wenze isibonelelo sokuqinisekisa sokungathathi cala kweNkonzo yoLuntu. 201 UMphathiswa onoxanduva ngeNkonzo yoLuntu UMongameli kufuneka atyumbe uMphathiswa onoxanduva ngeNkonzo yoLuntu. 202 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishini yeNkonzo yoLuntu

(1) Kune Khomishini yeNkonzo yoLuntu ebandakanya—

(a)

usihlalo nosekela sihlalo;

(b) ngobuncinane amalungu amabini ngobuninzi amahlanu; atyunjwe nguMongameli.

(2) Amalungu eKhomishini yeNkonzo yoLuntu kufuneka atyunjwe ngolwazi abanalo okanye amava ekulawuleni okanye nasekuboneleleni iinkonzo zoluntu. 203 Umsebenzi weKhomishini yeNkonzo yoLuntu

(1) IKhomishini yeNkonzo yoLuntu inemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukutyumba umntu ofanelekileyo nonako ukuba sesikhundleni kwiNkonzo yoLuntu;

(b) ngaphantsi kwecandelo 65(5), ukutywina nokulawula iimeko zokusebenza, kubandakanya imivuzo, izibonelelo nezinye inzuzo zamalungu eNkonzo yoLuntu;

(c) ukulawula namandla noqeqesha phezu kwamalungu eNkonzo yoLuntu;

(d) ukuphanda izikhalazo nokulungisa izikhalazo zamalungu eNkonzo yoLuntu ngokuphathathelene nezenzo ezisemthethweni nokungenzi;

(e) ukuphumeza amanyathelo okuqinisekisa ukwenza okuhle nokusebenzayo ngaphakathi nokuba kuhle ngokubanzi kweNkonzo yoLuntu;

(f) ukuqinisekisa ukuba amalungu eNkonzo yoLuntu benza imisenzi yabo kakuhle nokungenamkhethe;

(g) ukwazisa uMongameli noMphathiswa nakuwuphina umcimbi ophathelene nenkonzo yoluntu;

(h) ukukhuthaza kwiNkonzo yoLuntu izimilo nemigaqo emiselwe kuloMgaqo siseko; kwaye

(i) ukwenza nawuphina umsebenzi eliwunikwe okanye owiswe kwiKhomishini nguloMgaqo siseko okanye uMthetho wasePalamente.

(2) IKhomishini yeNkonzo yoLuntu ngemvume yoMphathiswa onoxanduva ngeNkonzo yoLuntu ingaba nolawulo nakuyiphina injongo emiselwe kwicandelwana (1).

(3) IKhomishini yeNkonzo yoLuntu kufuneka yenze umsebenzi wayo ngokungqinelana ngokubanzi nayiphina inkqubo engumyalelo uMphathiswa onoxanduva ngeNkonzo yoLuntu ayiphileyo.

(4) Ekumiseni imivuzo, izibonelelo nezinye iinzuzo zamalungu eNkonzo yoLuntu, iKhomishini yeNkonzo yoLuntu kufuneka yenze ngemvume kaMongameli ayiphiwe ngokuncetyiswa nguMphathiswa onoxanduva ngezamali kwaye emveni kothethwano noMphathiswa onoxanduva ngeNkonzo yoLuntu. 204 Onozakuzaku nabanye abameli abazinqununu baseZimbabwe UMongameli angatyumba abantu babengonozakuzaku okanye nabanye abameli abazinqununu baseZimbabwe kwamanye amazwe okanye babe ngonozakuzaku kwimibutho yamazwe kwaye nangaliphina ixesha angabasusa ababantu kwizikhundla zabo. 205 ONobhala abasisigxina

(1) ONobhala abasisigxina benkonzo batyunjwa nguMongameli emva kothethwano neKhomishini yeNkonzo yoLuntu.

(2) Ixesha lengqesho likaNobhala osisigxina lithuba elingaba yiminyaka emihlanu, ivuselelwa kanye ngenxa yokusebenza ngokuncomekayo nokuhambisa iinkonzo. ISAHLUKO 11 Iinkonzo Zokhuseleko INXALENYE 1; Izibonelelo Ezibanzi 206 Ukhuseleko lweSizwe

(1) Iinjongo zokhuseleko lweSizwe sase Zimbabwe kufuneka ziveze kwaye zibonise ukuzimisela kwabemi, uxolo, imvisiswano, nangempumelelo, kungekho loyiko.

(2) Ukhuseleko lweSizwesaseZimbabwe kunyanzelekile ukuba lukhuselwengulo Mgaqo-Siseko noMthetho weLizwe.

(3) Ngokubalulekileyo, ukukhuselwa kokhuseleko lweSizwekufuneka kwenziwe kuhambisana;

(a)

namalungelo asiseko, inkululeko,ukuxatyiswa kwedemokhrasi kwa nemigaqo eshicilelwe kuloMgaqo-Siseko; kwa

(b) nokuhlonelwa komthetho. 207 IiNkonzo zokhuseleko

(1) IiNkonzo zokhuseleko zaseZimbabwe ziquka ezi;

(a)

iNkonzo yoMkhosi.

(b) iNkonzo yamaPolisa.

(c)

iNkonzo yeeNtloli.

(d) iNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa; kwa

(e)

nezinye iinkonzo zokhuseleko ezithe zavunyelwa nguMthetho wePalamente.

(2) IiNkonzo zokhuseleko zingaphansti kwegunya lalo Mgaqo-Siseko, uMongameli weLizwe neKhabhinethi yakhe kufuneka bongamelwe yiPalamente.

(3) Ubulungu kwiiNkonzo zokhuseleko kufunekabubonise intlukano ngokobuntlanga kubantu base Zimbabwe. 208 Ukuziphatha kwamalungu eenkonzo zokhuseleko

(1) Amalungu eenkonzo zokhuseleko kufuneka enze okanye aziphathe ngendlela ehambisana kakuhle naloMgaqo-Siseko kwa noMthetho weLizwe.

(2) IiNkonzo zokhuseleko okanye amalungu azo, ekusebenzeni kwawo akufunekanga—

(a)

abe nomkhethe;

(b) afezekise iminqweno nokuba yeyeyowuphi umbutho wopolitiki;

(c)

aphikisane neminqweno esemthethweni yombutho wepolitiki;

(d) ukunyhasha amalungelo asiseko nenkululeko yomntu nokuba ngubani na.

(3) Amalungu eenkonzo zokhuseleko akufunekanga abe ngamalungu okanye asebenzele umbutho wepolitiki nokuba ngowuphi na.

(4) Amalungu eenkonzo zokhuseleko asasebenzayo akufunekanga abonakale sesebenzela iziko lomphakathi ngaphandle kokuba lixesha likaxakeka kawonke-wonke. 209 iBhunga loKhuseleko lweSizwe

(1) Kukho iBhungu lokhuseleko lweSizwe, elakhiwe ngohlobo lokuba uMongameli weLizwe ungusihlalo walo, namasekela akhe, abaPhathiswa namalungu eenkonzo zokhuseleko, nabanye ke abangabe bemiselwe nguMthetho wePalamente.

(2) Imisebenzi yeBhungu loKhuseleko lweSizwe yile;

(a)

ukuphuhlisa inkqubo yokhuseleko eZimbabwe;

(b) ukwazisa nokucebisa uMongameli malunga nokhuseleko lweSizwe; kwa

(c)

nokwenza nokuba yeyiphi na imisebenzi elinokuyimiselwa nguMthetho wePalamente.

(3) Abalawuli beenkonzo zokhuseleko kunyanzeleka bevele nengxelo njalo nje nxa ithe yafunwa liBhungu loKhuselekolweSizwe. 210 Indlela yokukhalaza ezimeleyo Ukuba umntu olilungu lika wonke-wonke eye akaphatheka kakuhle zizenzo zamalungu eenkonzo zokhuseleko kufuneka uMthetho wePalamente uveze indlela ezimeleyo emayisetyenziswe ukwamkela nokuphanda izikhalazo ezo zokungasebenzi kakuhle kwamalungu eenkonzo zokhuseleleko, kwaye kubekho indlela yokuwulungisisa umonakalo lowo. INXALENYE 2 Inkonzo Yomkhosi 211 INkonzo yoMkhosi

(1) INkonzo yoMkhosi yaseZimbabwe iquka; uMkhosi waphantsi, uMkhosi waphezulu, nezinye iiNkonzo ezingabe zisekwe nguMthetho wePalamente

(2) INkozo yoMkhosi yiyo kuphela evumelekileyo njengoMkhosi weSizwe ngokoMthetho waseZimbabwe.

(3) INkonzo yoMkhosi kufuneka ithobele amalungelo asiseko, inkululeko yabantu bonke, kwaye kufuneka ingabi namkhethe, njalo ibonise ukulithanda iLizwe, ukuthembeka eLizweni, ibonise ukuqeqesheka kwaye ilandele igunya labantu elisekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko.

(4) INkonzo yoMkhosi kufuneka uhlale ibonisa ukuqeqesheka nembeko njengokwemiqathango yamalungu oMkhosi wamajoni.

(5) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uveze indlela emayisetyenziswe yiNkonzo yoMkhosi ekulawulweni kwamalungu oMkhosi, nokuziphatha kwawokwaye kusetyenziswe icandelo 218, elithetha ngemiqathatngo emalunga nokusebenza kwalenkonzo nentsebenzo yamajoni. 212 Injongo yeNkonzo yoMkhosi Injongo yeNkonzo yoMkhosi kukukhusela izwe laseZimbabwe, abantu balo, nokukhusela iminqweno yeLizwe nomhlaba walo nokugxininisisa ukusetyenziswa kwaloMgaqo-Siseko. 213 Ukuthunywa koMkhosi

(1) Ngokubhekiselele kuloMgaqo–Siseko, kuphela uMongameli weLizwe, yena enguMlawuli-Jikelele woMkhosi nguye onamandla—

(a)

Awokuvumela ukuthunywa kwamajoni oMkhosi; okanye

(b) Onamandla nesiqinisekiso sokusebenza kwamajoni oMkhosi.

(2) Ngegunya likaMongameli weLizwe, amajoni asenokuthunywa ukusebenzela eZimbabwe—

(a)

ukukhusela iZimbabwe

(b) ukuncedisa amaPolisa ekugcineni ukuthula kuwonke-wonke; okanye

(c)

ukuncedisana namaPolisa namalungu namanye amalungu asekuhlaleni ngamaxesha likaxakeka okanye elentlekelele.

(3) Ngegunya likaMongameli weLizwe uMkhosi usenokuthunywa ukuyosebenzela ngaphandle kweLizwe—

(a)

ukuyogcina ukuthula noncwankco njengokufuna kwentlanganiso yaMazwe aManyeneyo omhlaba okanye nokuba ngowuphi umbutho wamazwe apho iZimbabwe elilungu kuwo;

(b) ukuyoncedisana nelinye iLizwe ukugcina nokukhusela umhlaba walo;

(c)

ukufezekisa izithembiso nezimvo zamazwe ewonke edibene;

(d) ukukhusela ukhuseleko lwabahlali baseZimabwe okanye iminqweno yeLizwe.

(4) Enanini lobubini esithathwini samalungu ePalamente ahleli edibene kwiNdibano ye Senethi okanye yeNdibano yeSizwe, iPalamente ivumelekile ukuphuma nesigqibo sokuba amajoni ebekhutshelwe ukuyosebenza ngaphandle kweLizwe awuyeke loomsebenzi ajike.

(5) Xa iPalamente ithe yagqiba ukuba amajoni mawajike, uMongameli weLizwe kufuneka ukuba azame kangangoko ukuba amajoni lawo ajike, ekwenzeni njalo uMongameli kufuneka aqinisekise ukhuseleko lobomi bamajoni lawo nabahlali baseZimbawe nezixhobo zeLizwe. 214 Uxanduva lwezopolitiki olumalunga nokuthunywa koMkhosi Ukuba uMkhosi uthunyelwa kwenye indawo ngokomsebenzi—

(a)

eZimbabwe ukuyakuncedisa ukugcina ukuthula kukawonke-wonke; okanye;

(b) ngaphandle kweLizwe laseZimbabwe; uMongameli weLizwe kufuneka azise iPalamente, ngokungxamisekileyo nangokucacileyo unobangela wokuba uMkhosi ube uyathunyelwa kwananjalo—

(i) apho athunyelwe khona ngokomsebenzi eZimbabwe, indawo abakuyo;

(ii) aphoasebenzela khona ngaphandle kweLizwe, iLizwe abakulo. 215 UMphathiswa ojongene noMkhosi UMongameli weLizwe kufuneka anyule uUMphathiswa ojongene noMkhosi . 216 Ukulawulwa koMkhosi

(1) UMthetho wePalamente usenokuthi;

(a)

uMkhosi ulawulwa nguMlawuli omnye; okanye

(b) inkonzo nganye yeMikhosi yokhuseleko, okanye nayiphi emibini nangaphezulu edibeneyo, iyakuba ngaphantsi kolawulo luka Mlawuli owahlukileyo

(2) UMlawuli ngamnye woMkhosi, naye wonke uMlawuli wenkonzo zoMkhosi, bonyulwa nguMongameli emveni kokuba eye wacebisana noMphathiswa ojongene noMkhosi.

(3) Abalawuli beeNkonzo zoMkhosi bonyulelwa ukuba bahlale kwesosihlalo okwesithuba esingegqithi iminyaka emihlanu, kwaye umntu akufunekanga asebenze kwesosihlalo ngaphezu kwezithuba ezimbini.

(4) Umntu owakhe wabasesihlalweni sika Mlawuli wenkonzo yoMkhosi angotyunjwa ayokubamba isihlalo sikaMlawuli woMkhosi, kodwa ke umntu owakhe wanguMlawuli woMkhosi akanako ukuba angayokuba ngu Mlawuli wenkonzo yoMkhosi okanye alawule nokokuba yeyiphi inkonzo yezokhuseleko.

(5) Bonke abaLawuli boMkhosi, kwanabo bonke abaLawuli beenkonzo zoMkhosi kufuneka balawule besebenzisa imiqathango neziyalelo ezisuka kuMphathiswa ojongene noMkhosi esenza into ayivunyelwe nguMongameli. 217 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishini yeNkonzo yoMkhosi

(1) Kukho iKhomishini yeNkonzo zoMkhosi enosihlalo okufuneka abe ngusihlalo we Khomishini yeNkonzo zoLuntu, nabanye abantu abangaba babini ebuncinaneni baze bangodlulisi isithandathu ebukhulwini abanyulwe nguMongameli weLizwe.

(2) Xa kunyulwa amalungu eKhomishini yeNkonzo yoMkhosi kufuneka kunyulwe abantu abanolwazi, abakuqhelileyo ukuphatha, abanolwazi ngemicimbi emalunga nesijoni, ingqeqesho yabo okanye nto imbi engenza balungele ukuwunikwa loomsebenzi, kwaye—

(a)

ebuncinaneni isiqingatha senanini labo bebonke kufuneka ibe ngabantu abangazange babakuMkhosi;

(b) ebuncinaneni omnye kubo kufuneka abe ngumntu owakhe wabamba isihlalo esiphezulu kuMkhosi woKhuslo okwesithuba esingaba ngunyaka kusiya kwemihlanu. 218 Imisebenzi yeKhomishiniyeNkonzoyo Mkhosi IKhomishini yeNkonzo yoMkhosi ijongene nale misebenzi—

(a)

ukuqasha abantu abakulungeleyo ukuba babenezikhundla kuMkhosi;

(b) ukulungisa intsebenzo neengxaki zoMkhosi, ezibalisa umrholo wamajoni, nezinye iimbonelelo zamalungu oMkhosi;

(c)

ukuqinisekisa ukuhleleka nokuphatheka kakuhle koMkhosikwa nokuba bahlale besemdleni wokusebenza;

(d)

ukuqinisekisa ukuba uMkhosi ubandakanya ngokupheleleyo kwimiqathango exelwa kwicandelo 208;

(e)

ukuqinisekisa uvano nokuvisisana phakathi koMkhosi kwawonke-wonke;

(f)

ukucebisa uMongamelikunye noMphathiswa woMkhosi ngezinto ezimalunga nawo uMkhosi kunye nabantu bonke nje; kwa

(g) nokwenza imisebenzi enikwe iKhomishini nguloMgaqo-Siseko okanye nguMthetho wePalamente.

(2) IKhomishini yeNkonzo yoMkhosi,ngokwe mvumo esuka kuMphathiswa ojongene noMkhosi, isenokuyenza imiqathango yokusebenza xa injongo yayo ingqinelana nokubhalwe kwicandelwana (1).

(3) Ekulungisiseni imirholo, nezinye izibonelo zamalungu oMkhosi, iKhomishini yeNkonzo zoMkhosi kufuneka yenze oko ngokwemvume ka Mongameli naye ecetyiswe nguMphathiswai wezemali emveni kokubonisana noMphathiswa woMkhosi. INXALENYE 3 Inkonzo Yamapolisa 219 INkonzo yamaPolisa nemisebenzi yayo

(1) Kukho iNkonzo yamaPolisa esebenza oku—

(a)

ukuphanda, ukukhangela nokulwisana nolwaphulo Mthetho;

(b) ukugcina ukhuseleko lweLizwe laseZimbabwe;

(c)

ukukhusela ubomi nempahla yabantu base Zimbabwe;

(d) ukugcina uMthetho nocwangco; kwa

(e)

nokuqinisekisa ukusebenza kwaloMgaqo-Siseko nokunyanzelisa uMthetho ngaphandle koloyiko nocalu-calulo.

(2) INkonzo yamaPolisa kufuneka yenze imisebenzi yalo incedisana—

(a)

nokokuba yeyiphi iNkonzo yobuchule kwezophando lweemfihlo engabe ivunyelwe nguMthetho;

(b) nokuba ngabaphi abantu abangabe bevunyelwe nguMthetho ngenjongo yokuphanda amatyala; kwa

(c)

namaqela amazwe ngamazwe alungiselelwe ukulwisana nolwaphuloMthetho.

(3) INkonzo yamaPolisa kufuneka ingabi namkhethe , ingathathe icala elithile kwimibutho yezepolitiki, ibonakalise ukuba ngeyezwe lonke , ithembeke kwaye ithande iLizwe, ibonise ukuqeqesheka kwaye ibalulekise abantu njengokuxela kwawo loMgaqoSiseko.

(4) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uqhube, ukusekwa nokuphathwa, ukuziphatha nokunyuselwa kwanokuthotywa kwamagosa, imiqathango yokusebenza, njengendlela echazwe kwicandelo 223, indlela yokusebenza kwamalungu eNkonzo yamaPolisa. 220 UMphathiswa weNkonzo yamaPolisa UMongameli weLizwe nguye onyula uMphathiswai ojongene neNkonzo yamaPolisa. 221 UMlawuli-Jikelele wamaPolisa

(1) INkonzo yamaPolisa ilawulwa nguMlawuli-Jikelele wamaPolisa otyunjwa nguMongameli emveni kokuba ethe wacebisana noMphathiswa wamapolisa.

(2) UMlawuli-Jikelele wamaPolisa uvunyelwe ukuba abambe esisihlalo okwesithuba esinye eseminyaka emihlanu isithuba ngasinye nogama usenokubuyiselwa kube kanye ekupheleni kwesithuba sokukuqala.

(3) Umntu owake wanguMlawuli-Jikelele weNkonzo yamaPolisa akavumelekanga ukuphinda alawule nokuba yeyiphi inkonzo yezokhuselo.

(4) UMlawuli-Jikelele wamaPolisa kufuneka alawule ngokwendlela ebhalwe phantsi esuka kuMphathiswa wamaPolisa esebenzisana kunye noMongameli. 222 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishini yeNkonzo yamaPolisa

(1) Kukho iKhomishini yeNkonzo yamaPolisa enosihlalo, okufuneka abe ngusihlalo weNkonzo yamaPolisa, kunye namalungu angaba mabini ebuncinaneni kodwa angogqithisi isithandathu.

(2) Amalungu eKhomishini yeNkonzo yamaPolisa kufuneka anyulwe ngenxeni yokuba bewazi kwaye bewuqhelile umsebenzi wokugcinwa uMthetho nobulungisa, ukuphathwa, okanye izinga lemfundo okanye nokuba yini engenza balungele ukuqashelwa lomsebenz; kwaye—

(a)

2013

Ebuncinaneni isiqingatha kwinani labo kufuneka ibe ngabantu abangengo malungu okanye abangazange baba ngamalungu eNkonzo yamaPolisa;

(b) ebuncinaneni omnye kubo kufuneka abengumntu owakhe wanesikhundla esiphezulu kwiNkonzo yamaPolisa okwekota enye nangaphezulu okweminyaka engaba mihlanu ebuncinaneni. 223 Imisebenzi yeKhomishini yeNkonzo yamaPolisa

(1) Imisebenzi yeKhomishini yeNkonzo yamaPolisa yile—

(a)

ukuqasha abantu abalungele ukuba ngamalungu nokuba nezikhundla esipoliseni;

(b) ukulungisisa iindlela zokusebenza ezibalisa, umrholo, nezinye izibonelelo, zamalungu akwiNkonzo yamaPolisa;

(c)

ukuqinisekisa ukonwaba nentlalontle yawo onke amalungu akwiNkonzo yamaPolisa namagosa ayo nokuba basebenze ngokuziphela qho;

(d) ukuqinisekisa ukuba amalungu eNkonzo yamaPolisaabandakanya kwimiqathango ixelwe kwicandelo 208;

(e)

ukuqinisekisa usetyenziswano phakathi kwamaPolisa nowonke-wonke;

(f)

ukucebisa uMongameli noMphathiswa malunga nayo yonke into enokwenza neNkonzo yamaPolisa;kwa

(g) nokwenza nokuba ngowuphi umsebenzi oyalelwe nguMgaqo-Siseko noMthetho wePalamente.

(2) IKhomishiniyeNkonzo yamaPolisa, ngemvumo kaMphathiswa ojongene neNkonzo yamaPolisa, ingayenza imiqathango yokusebenza ngeenjongo ezibhalwe kwicandelwana (1).

(3) Ekulungisiseni irholo, nezinye izibonelelo zamalungu eNkonzo yamaPolisa, iKhomishini yeNkonzo yamaPolisa kufuneka yenze konke ekwenzayo ngaphantsi kwesigqibo sika Mongameli emveni kokucebisana noMphathiswa ojongene nezemali ngemva kokuba naye sebonisene noMphathiswa weNkonzo yamaPolisa. INXALENYE 4 Inkonzo Yeentloli 224 Ukusekwa kweNkonzo yeeNtloli

(1) Nokuba yeyiphi iNkonzo yeeNtloli, engeyo Nkonzo yoMkhosi okanye eyamaPolisa, kufuneka iyilwe ngokoMthetho okanye ngeziyalelo zikaMongameli okanye iKhabhinethi ka Mongameli.

(2) INkonzo yeNtloli yeLizwe akufanelanga ibe yeyombutho othile wepolitiki, kufuneka ikhokhelise ukuthanda iLizwe, ibonakalise ukuqeqesheka nokuhleleka, ihlonele kwaye ithobele izimvo zikawonke-wonke njengokuxela kwawo loMgaqo Siseko. 225 UMphathiswa ojongene neNkonzo yeeNtloli UMongameli kufuneka atyumbe uMphathiswa ojongene neNkonzo yeeNtloli. 226 Ukulawulwa kweNkonzo yeeNtloli

(1) INkonzo yeeNtloli kufuneka ibe ngaphantsi kolawulo luka MlawuliJikelele weNkonzo yeeNtloli yena otyunjwa nguMongameli ukuba abambe esosihlalo okwesithuba seminyaka emihlanu enokuvuselelwa kanye kuphela.

(2) UMlawuli-Jikelele weNkonzo yeeNtloli kufuneka alawule elandela iziyalelo ezibhalwe phantsi zisuka kuMphathiswa ojongene neNkonzo yeeNtloli esebenza ngaphantsi kwegunyalikaMongameli.

(3) Umntu owakhe wanguMlawuli-Jikelele wenKonzo yokugcinwa kweMfihlo akavumelekanga ukuba alawule nokokuba yeyiphi na inkonzo yokhuselo. INXALENYE 5 Inkonzo Zababanjwa Nokulungisiswa Kwezimilo Zabo 227 Umsebenzi weNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa

(1) Kukho iNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa yona ejongene noku—

(a)

ukukhusela uluntu ukuze lungahlukunyezwa ngabaphuli boMthetho, ngohlobo lokuvalela nokufundisa abo bathe bagwetywa nguMthetho nabanye abajongelwe ukuba bavalelwe nguMthetho, ukwenzela ukuba balungisiswe izimilo zabo ze bamkeleke ekubuyeleni kwabo bevela ekuvalelweni; kwa

(b) nokuququzelela uku[phathwa kakuhle kwababanjwa kunye nokuba bafundiswe isimilo esisiso.

(2) INkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanwja kufuneka ingabi namkhethe, ingathathi cala kwimibutho yezopolitiki, ithande iLizwe, ithembeke, ibonise ukuqeqesheka kwaye ihlonele yaye ithobele loMgaqo-Siseko.

(3) UMthetho wePalamente nguwo omawuveze indlela yokuphathwa nokusebenza kwayo leNkonzo, ukusekwa kwayo, imiqathango yokusebenza kwamalungu ayo nembeko yawo kwaye, kulandelwa icandelo 231; indlela yokusebenza kwamalungu eNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa. 228 UMphathiswa ojonge neNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa UMongameli kufuneka atyumbe uMphathiswa ojongene neNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa. 229 UMlawuli-Jikelele weNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa

(1) INkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa isebenza ngaphantsi kolulawulo luka Mlawuli-Jikelele weNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa yena etyunjwe nguMongameli ngemeni kokucebisana noMphathiswa ojongene nayo leNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa

(2) UMlawuli-Jikelele weNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa utyunjelwa kwesisishlalo okwesithuba okwesithuba seminyaka emihlanu enokuvuselelwa kanye kuphela.

(3) Umntu owakhe wanguMlawuli-Jikelele weNkonzo yokuGcinwa kwabantu aboPhule uMthetho akufanelanga abe nguMlawuli wenyeiNkonzo yokhuseleko lwabantu

(4) UMlawuli-Jikelele weNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa kufuneka alawule njengokutsho kwemiqathango ebhalwe phantsi esuka kuMphathiswa ojongene neNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa naye esebenzisana noMongameli.

230 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishiniyeNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa

(1) Kukho iKhomishiniyeNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa yona enosihlalo, okufuneka ibe engusihlalo weKhomishini weNkozo zoLuntu, kwaye ibenawo namanye amalungu ebuncinaneni amabini kodwa angabi ngaphesi kwesithandathu atyunjwa nguMongameli.

(2) Amalungu eKhomishini yeNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa kufuneka akhethwe ngokwazi kwa nobugcisa babo kwimicimbi emalunga nokulawula, khuseleko lwabantu, inkcukacha zokuqeqeshwakwabo kwa nokufaneleka kwabo ukuqeshwelwa lomsebenzi; kwaye—

(a)

ebuncinaneni isiqingatha kwinani labo kufuneka ibe ngabantu abangewo malungu okanye abangazange baba ngamalungu eNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa;

(b) ebuncinaneni omnye kubo kufuneka abengumntu owakhe wanesikhundla esiphezulu kwiNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa okwekota enye nangaphezulu okweminyaka engaba mihlanu ebuncinaneni. 231 Imisebenzi yeKhomishini yeNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa

(1) IKhomishini yeNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa zabo ijongene noku—

(a)

qesha abantu abakulungeleyo ukuba nezikhundla kwiNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa;

(b) lungisa indlela yokusebenza, kubalisa irholo, nezibonelelo ezifanelekiyo zamalungu eNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa;

(c)

qinisekisa ukonwaba nentlalontle yawo onke amalungu akwiNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa nokuba basebenze ngokuziphela qho;

(d) qinisekisa ukuba amalungu eNkonzo yabaBanjwa nokuLungiswa kweZimilo zabo abandakanya kwimiqathango ixelwe kwicandelo 208;

(e)

qinisekisa usetyenziswano phakathi kwamalungu eNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa kunye no wowonke-wonke;

(f)

cebisa uMongameli noMphathiswa malunga nayo yonke into enokwenza neNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa; kwa

(g) kwenza nokuba ngowuphi umsebenzi oyalelwe iKhomishini nguMgaqoSiseko noMthetho wePalamente.

(2) IKhomishini yeNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabbanjwa, ngemvumo kaMphathiswa ojongene neNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa, ingayenza imiqathango yokusebenza ngeenjongo ezibhalwe kwicandelwana (1).

(3) Ekulungisiseni irholo, nezinye izibonelelo zamalungu eNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa, iKhomishini yeNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa kufuneka yenze konke ekwenzayo ngaphantsi kwesigqibo sika Mongameli emveni kokucebisana noMphathiswa ojongene nezemali ngemva kokuba naye sebonisene noMphathiswa weNkonzo yeeNtolongo noKwelulekwa kwaMabanjwa.

ISAHLUKO 12 Iikhomishini Ezizimeleyo Zokuxhasa Idemokrasi INXALENYE 1 Ngokubanzi 232 iKhomishini ezizimeleyo Ezi zizo iiKhomishini ezizimeleyo—

(a)

iKhomishini yoNyulo yase Zimbabwe;

(b) iIKhomishini yamaLungelo abantu yaseZimbabwe;

(c)

iIKhomishini yeSini yase Zimbabwe;

(d) iKhomishini yeeNdaba yaseZimbabwe;

(e)

iKhomishiniyoXolo noXolelwaniso lweSizwe.

233 Iinjongo zeeKhomishini ezizimeleyo IiKhomishini ezizimeleyo zineenjongo ezilandelayo ngaphezu kwezo ziziphiwe nganye nganye—

(a)

ukuxhasa nokuzikisa ngamandla kumalungelo abantu nedemokhrasi;

(b) ukukhusela ulongamo nemfuno zabantu;

(c)

ukuphuhlisa uMgaqo-Siseko;

(d) ukuphuhlisa ukwethembeka noxanduva kumaziko kawonke-wonke;

(e)

ukukhusela ukubaluleka kwenkululeko yentando yeSinintsi kuSizwe namaziko wonke namasebe onke kaRhulumente, namaqumrhu alawulwa nguRhulumente, kwa

(f)

nokuqinisekisa ukuba ubulungisa obungachanekanga buyalungisiswa.

234 Abaqeshwa beeKhomishini ezizimeleyo IiKhomishini ezizimeleyo zinawo amandla okuqesha abaqeshwa bazo ngokoMthetho, ukulawula iimeko zabo zenkonzo. 235 Ukuzimela kweeKhomishini

(1) IiKhomishini ezizimeleyo—

(a)

zizimele zodwa ngoko ke ziyazilawula zona azilawulwa nokuba nguban na;

(b) kufuneka zenze ngokwemfuneko yaloMgaqo-Siseko; kwaye

(c)

kufuneka zenze imisebenzi yazo ngaphandle koloyiko, isisa, nomkhethe; nogama ke zihambisene nezigqibo zasePalamente ekuqhubeni umsebenzi wazo.

(2) ISizwe namaziko onke namasebe kaRhulumente nokuba kukweliphi inqanaba, ngokoMthetho namanye amanyathelo, kufuneka zincedise iiKhomishini ezizimeleyo kwaye kufuneka zikhusele ukuzimela kwazo, ukungabi nacala kwazo, imfezeko, nokusebenza kwazo.

(3) Akekho umntu omakaphazamise ukusebenza kweeKhomishini ezizimeleyo.

236 Amalungu eKhomishini ezizimeleyo angathathi cala kwezopolitiki

(1) Ekusebenzeni, amalungu eKhomishini ezizimeleyo akufunekanga—

(a) enze ngokunomkhethe;

(b) aphuhlise iminqweno yamaqela athile ezepolitiki kungakhathalekile nokuba leliphi;

(c) aphazamise ukusebenza ngokusemthethweni kweqela nokuba leliphi lezopolitiki; okanye

(d) ukuxhaphaza amalungelo asiseko nenkululeko yomntu nokuba ngubani.

(2) Abantu abangamalungu eqela lezopolitiki ekuqeshweni kwabo yiKhomishini ezimeleyo kufuneka babuyeke ubulungu beqela lezopolitiki ngokungxamisekileyo kwaye nokuba sekutheni akufunekanga bogqithise iintsuku ezingamashumi amathathu emveni kokuqeshwa.

(3) Xa ilungu leKhomishini ezimeleyo—

(a) liye laba lilungu leqela okanye umbutho wezopolitiki; okanye

(b) liqeshwe yiKhomishini lililungu leqela okanye umbutho wezopolitiki, laze loyisakala ukuyeka ubulungu kwade kwegqitha iintsuku ezingamashumi amathathu; kunyanzelekile loomntu ayeke ukuba lilungu leKhomishini leyo. 237 Ukuqeshwa nokususwa e-ofiSini kwelungu leKhomishini

(1) Ngenxeni yenjongo zokutyunjwa kwabantu betyunjelwa nokokuba yeyiphi iKhomishini ezimeleyo, iKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango kufuneka—

(a) Ibhengeze isikhundla eso;

(b) Kumenywe uwonke-wonke ukuba enyule;

(c) kwenziwe udliwanondlebe no wonke-wonkelwabo bacingeleka ukuba bangakulungela ukutyunjwa oko;

(d) kwenziwe uluhlu lwenani elifanelekileyo lwabatyunjwa ukwenzela uqesho; kwaye

(e) lihambisa uluhlu lalomagama kuMongameli.

(2) Ilungu leKhomishini ezimeleyo lingakhutshwa e-ofisini kuphela ngesizatho sokuba elolungu—

(a) alikwazi ukusebenza imisebenzi yalo ngenxeni yokugula ngumzimba okanye ingqondo;

(b) aliwazi umsebenzi walo ngokupheleleyo;

(c) linetyala lokuziphathi kakuhle;

(d) alikulungelanga ukuqeshwa yiloKhomishini.

(3) Inkqubo yokukhutshwa kwabagwebi e-ofisisni yiyo esetyenziswayo ukukhupha ilungu leKhomishini ezimeleyo e-ofisini. INXALENYE 2 Ikhomishini Yonyulo Lwasezimbabwe. 238 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishini yoNyulo yaseZimbabwe

(1) Kukho iKhomishini okuthwa yiKhomishini yoNyulo yaseZimbabwe equka—

(a)

usihlalo otyunjwe nguMongameli emveni kokucebisana neKhomishini yeNkonzo Egwebayo neKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo; kwa

(b) namanye amalungu asibhozo atyunjwe nguMongameli kuluhlu lwabatyunjwa abangaphezu kweshumi elinambini ebelihanjiswe yiKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo.

(2) Usihlalo weKhomishiniyoNyulo yaseZimbabwe kufuneka abe ngumntu ongumgwebi okanye umntu owake wangumgwebi ngaphambili okanye umntu ofaneleka ukutyunjwa njengomgwebi.

(3) Xa ukutyunjwa kukasihlalo weKhomishini yoNyulo yaseZimbabwe kungahambelani nengcebiso yeKhomishini yokuxoxwa kwamTyala, uMongameli kufuneka aqinisekise ukuba iKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo iyaxelelwa ngokungxamisekileyo.

(4) Amalungu eKhomishiniyoNyulo kufuneka ibe ngabemmi baseZimabwe abanyulwe ngenxeni yemfezeko namava abo nokuziphatha kwabo emicimbini kawonke-wonke nakumacandelo abucala.

(5) Amalungu eKhomishini yoNyulo yase Zimbabwe atyunjelwa ukuba sesihlalweni okwesithuba seminyaka emithandathu kwaye aseno kuphinda babuyiselwe esihlalweni okweminyaka eminye esithandathu kuphela kwakhona, kwaye akekho umntu ovumelekileyo ukusebenzela iKhomishini emveni kokuba ekhe walilungu ithemu enye nangaphezulu, nokuba iithemu ezo bezohlukene okanye ziqhubekeka, yiminyaka elishumi nambini leyo. 239 Imisebenzi yeKhomishini yoNyulo yaseZimbabwe IKhomishini yoNyulo yase Zimbabwe imisebenzi yayo yile—

(a)

ukulungiselela, ukuqhuba nokujonga;

(i) kulunyulo lwe-ofiSini kaMongameli nolwePalamente;

(ii) kulunyulo lwamabhunga ephondo kunye namagosa kunye namabhunga olawulo lweendawo;

(iii) kulunyulo lwamalungu ebhungaleSizwe neeNkosi olusekwe licadelo 285; kwa

(iv) kuvoto loMthetho ocetywayo okanye ukulungisiswa koMgaqoSiseko;

nokuqinisekisa ukuba unyulo novoto luqhutywa kakuhle, ngokukhululekileyo, ngobulungisa, ngokusekuhleni nangokungqinelana noMthetho;

(b) ukujonga ulunyulo lukaMongameli weSenethi neSthethi nokuqinisekisa ukuba ulunyulo olo luqhutywa kakuhle ngokungqinelana noMthetho;

(c)

nokubhalisa abavoti;

(d) ukuqokelela uluhlu neencwadi zamagama wabavoti;

(e)

ukuqinisekisa ugcino nesiphatho esisiso soluhlu neencwadi zabavoti;

(f)

ulungisisa ulunyulo, iiwadi neminye imida yonyulo;

(g) ukuyila, ukushicilela nokusasaza amaphepha okuvota, bavumele imo nokufumaneka kwamabhoksi okuvota, ukuseka nokusebenza kwindawo zokuvotela;

(h) ukuqhuba nokujonga ukuqhutywa kwenkqubo zokufundisa ngokuvota;

(i)

ukuvumela nabo bazele ukuzojonga ukuvoto novoto lwezimvo;

(j)

ukupha imiyalelo kwabo baqeshwe siSizwe okanye kukuhlala ukuqinisekisa ukuqhutywa kakuhle kolunyulo, ngokukhululekileyo, ngokulungileyo ngendlela eyiyo nesebuhleni nokuba loluphi ulunyulovoto kunye nolunyulo lwentando yeSinintsi; kwa

(k) ukwamkela nokujonga izikhalazo zika wonke-wonke nokulungisa apho kukhalazwe khona. 240 Ukunqunyanyiswa ekutyunjweni kwiKhomishini yoNyulo yaseZimbabwe Ukongeza amalunga nabantu okuthethwe ngabo kwicandelo 320(3), ababantu balandelayo akufunekanga batyunjwe yi Khomishini yoNyulo lwaseZimbabwe—

(a)

amagosa kawonke-wonke, ngaphandle kwabagwebi

(b) abaqeshwa bamabhunga amaphondo neelali; kwa

(c)

amalungu nabaqeshwa bamaqela ezoMthetho kwii arhente ezilawulwa nguRhulumente.

241 Khomishini yoNyulo yaseZimbabwe kufuneka ize nengxelo yonyulo novoto nezimvo Ngaphandle kwengxelo okufuneka iyinikele ngokomqathango okwicandelo 323, iKhomishini yoNyulo yaseZimbabwe kufuneka ngokungxamisekileyo, ngokusebenzisa uMphathiswa ofanelekileyo, inike ingxelo kwiPalamente ngokuqhutywa kolunyulo lonke kwa novoto lwezimvo zabantu. INXALENYE 3 ikhomishini yamalungelo abantu basezimbabwe

242 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishini yamalungelo oluntu baseZimbabwe

(1) Kukho iKhomishini eyaziwa ngokuba yi Khomishini yamaLungelo oLuntu yaseZimbabwe; eno—

(a)

sihlalo otyunjwe nguMongameli ngemva kokucebisana neKhomishini yeNkonzo yokuxoxiswa kwamaTyala neKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo;

(b) namanye amalungu asisibhozo atyunjwe nguMongameli kuluhlu lwabatyunjwa abangaphezu kweshumi elinambini ebelihanjiswe yiKomiti yeMiyalelo nemiQathango emisiweyo

(2) Usihlalo weKhomishini yamaLungelo oLuntu baseZimabwe kufuneka ibe ngumntu okulungeleyo kwaye owakhe wasebenzela njengegqwetha okweminyaka esisxhenxe eZimbabwe.

(3) Xa ukutyunjwa kukasihlalo weKhomishini yomaLungelo yoLuntu yase Zimbabwe kungahambisani nengcebiso yeKhomishini yeNkonzo yokuxoxiswa kwamaTyala, uMongameli kufuneka abangele ukuba iKomiti yeMiyalelo nemiQathango emisiweyo ixelelwe ngokungxamisekileyo.

(4) Amalungu eKhomishini yamaLungelo oLuntu yaseZimbabwe kufuneka akhethwe ngokwethembeka kwabo, nolwazi nokuvisisa nobungcali bokukhuthaza malunga namalungelo abantu. 243 Imisebenzi yeKhomishini yamaLungelo abantu base Zimbabwe

(1) IKhomishini yamaLungelo loLuntu yaseZimabweisebenza oku—

(a)

ukukhuthaza ukuqonda nokuhlonipha amalungelo nenkululeko yabantu kumanqanaba onke oluntu;

(b) ukukhuthaza ukukhuseleka, uphuhliso nokufikeleleka kwamalungelo abantu nenkululeko;

(c)

ukujonga, ukuhlola, nokuqinisekisa isikhumbuzo malunga namalungelo abantu;

(d) ukwamkela nokuhlola izikhalazo ezisuka kuwonke-wonke nokuthabatha amanyathelo malunga nezikhalazo ezo ngokufanelekileyo;

(e)

ukukhusela uwonke-wonke ekuxhatshazweni kwamandla nokungalawuli kakuhle kukaRhulumente namaziko asebenza ukunceda uwonke-wonke kunye namalungu aloomaziko;

(f)

ukuphanda ngokusebenza kwabo basemagunyeni nokuba ngubani na, ukuba kuye kwenzeka ukuba nokuba leliphi lamalungelo nenkululeko ebalwe kwisibhengezo samalungelo elithe lanyhanshwa lelogunya okanye loomntu;

(g) ukuba inkululeko namalungelo abantu aye anyhashwa, iKhomishini yamalungelo abantu iyakuqinisekisa ukulungiswa komonakalo ngokufanelekileyo kuquka nengcebiso yokubanjwa kwezoni;

(h) ukuyalela UMlawuli-Jikelele wamapolisa ukuba aphande amatyala abatyolwa abathe bagxobha amalungelo abantu nenkululeko kwaye axele kuleKhomishini iziphumo zophando olo;

(i)

ukucebisa iPalamente ngamanyathelo asebenzayo ekukhuthazeni amalungelo nenkululeko yabantu;

(j)

ukuqhuba uphando malunga namalungelo nenkululeko yabantu nobulungisa bentlalo;

(k) ukuhambela nokuhlola—

(i) iintilongo, iindawo okuvalelwa kuzo abantu, iinkampu zeembacu nezinye izibonelelo ezifanana nezi; kwa

(ii) neendawo okuhlala kuzo abantu abakhubazeke ngokwasengqondweni; ukuqinisekisa iimeko abantu abagcinakale ngaphantsi kwazo kwezondawo, nokuncoma okanye ukugxeka malunga neemeko eezo abantu bagcinakale ngazo kuMphathiswai ojongene nolawulo lwezondawo.

(2) UMlawuli-Jikelele wamapolisa kufuneka athobele nokuba ngowuphi umyalelo awunikwe yiKhomishini yamaLungelo abantu baseZimabwe njengokutsho kwecandelo lesi (1)(h). 244 Iingxelo ezisiwa nezenziwe yiKhomishini yamaLungelo oluntu baseZimabwe

(1) IKhomishini yamaLungelo abantu baseZimabwe isenokufuna ukuba nokuba ngubani, nokuba ngawaphi amaziko, okanye nokuba leliphi i-arhente, elingabe liqashwe nguRhulumente ukuba—

(a)

2013

azise iKhomishini ngamanyathelo athathiweyo ekufezekiseni amalungelo nenkululeko yabantu njengokuxelwe kwi Sibhengezo Samalungelo; kwaye;

(b) anike iKhomishini ulwazi elufunayo ukuze yenze ingxelo ekufuneka iyoyingenisa nokuba kukweyiphi indibano yamaLungelo abantu engabe inamaqela alapha okanye asuka kwamanye amazwe.

(2) Ngaphezu kwengxelo elindeleke ukuba iyinikele njengokutsho kwecandelo 323, iKhomishini yamaLungelo abantu baseZimbabwe, ngokusebenzisa uMphathiswa ofanelekileyo, kufanele ingenise iingxelo eya kwiPalamente malunga namalungelo nenkululeko yabantu, okufuneka ngoluvo lweKhomishini, isiwe ePalamente. INXALENYE 4 Ikhomishini Yesini Ezimbabwe 245 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishini yeSini yaseZimbabwe

(1) Kukho iKhomishini eyaziwa ngokuba yiKhomishini yeSini yase Zimbabwe ebandakanya—

(a)

usihlalo otyunjwe nguMongameli emveni kokuba ebonisane neKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo;

(b) amanye amalungu asisibhozo, wona—

(i) asixhenkxe awo atyunjwa nguMongameli ebonyula kuluhlu lwabatyunjwa abangaphezi kweshumi nambini elingeniswe yikomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo;

(ii) k w a n o m n y e o t y u n j w a k w i B h u n g a l e N k o s i , a t y u n j w e nguMongameli.

(2) Amalungu eKhomishini yeSini yaseZimbabwe kufuneka bakhethwe ngenxeni yemfezeko yabo, ulwazi nokuvisisa kwabo ngezinto ezimalunga neSini, ngokwamanqanaba entlalo, amasiko, uqoqosho nezopolitiki, kwaye izini kufanele zimelwe ngokulinganayo kwiKhomishini. 246 Imisebenzi yeKhomishini yeSini yaseZimbabwe IKhomishini yeSini yaseZimbabweisebenza oku—

(a) Ukujongisisa ngezinto ezinokwenza nolingano ngokweSini njengokutsho kwaloMgaqo-Siseko;

(b) ukuphanda malunga nolwaphulo lwamalungelo anxulumene neSini;

(c)

ukwamkela nokuqhalela izikhalazo ezisuka kuwonke-wonke nokuthabatha amanyathelo afanelekileyo malunga nezikhalazo ezo ngokufanelekleyo;

(d) ukwenza uphando ngemicimbi enxulumene nokulinganiswa kwezithuba ngokweSini nentlalo, nokucebisa malunga notshintsho omalwenziwe kwimithetho nezenzo ezingabe zibangela ucalucalulo ngokweSini;

(e)

ukucebisa amaziko kawonke-wonke nabucala malunga namanyathelo okufuneka athabathwe ukuze kuqinisekiswe ulingano lweSini;

(f)

ukucebisa malunga nenkqubo zokuqinisekisa ukufumaneka kolinganisswa ngokweSini;

(g) ukubanjwa kwabo abophule amalungelo anxulumene neSini;

(h) Ukuqinisekisa ukulungisisa okufanelekileyo ukuba kuya kwanolwaphulo

lwamalungelo anxulumene neSini; kwa

(i)

Ukwenza nokuba yini enokukhuthaza ukulinganiswa kwezithuba zonke ngokweSini.

247 Iingxelo ezisuka kwiKhomishini yeSini yase Zimbabwe Ngaphezu kwengxelo elindeleke ukuba ingeniswe yiKhomishini yeSini njengokutsho kwecandelo 323, ngokusebenzisa uMphathiswa ofanelekileyo, kufanele, ingenise iingxelo kwiPalamente malunga nezinto ezinxulumene neSini, nezo ekufanele leKhomishini izazi, yazise iPalamente ukuba iyokuziqwalasela. INXALENYE 5 Ikhomishini Yeendaba Yase Zimbabwe 248 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishini yeenNdaba yaseZimabwe

(1) Kukho iKhomishini eyaziwa ngokuthi yiKhomishini yeeNdaba yaseZimbabwe equka—

(a)

usihlalo oqashwe nguMongameli emveni kothethwano neKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo;

(b) amanye amalungu asibhozo atyunjwe nguMongameli ethathela kuluhlu labatyunjwa abangaphezu kweshumi nambini elingeniswe yiKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo.

(2) Amalungu eKhomishini yeeNdaba yaseZimbabwe kufanele baqeshwe ngenxeni yemfezeko, ubuchule ekuphatheni nolwazi novisiso lwamalungelo abantu nokwenza okuhambisana kakuhle neemicimbi yeeNdaba. 249 Imisebenzi yeKhomishini yeeNdaba yaseZimbabwe

(1) IKhomishini yeeNdaba yaseZimbabwe isebenza—

(a) ukuxhasa, ukukhuthaza nokuphuhlisa ukukhululeka kwamaziko eeNdaba;

(b) ukukhuthaza nokuqinisekisa izenzo nokuziphatha okulungileyo kwabo abasebenza kumaziko eeNdaba;

(c) ukujongisisa ngezosasazo ngokwemfuneko kawonke-wonke nokuqinisekisa ukuba izimvo ezimele uluntu lwaseZimbabwezinamkhethe kwaye zibalulekisa abemi beli;

(d) ukukhuthaza uqulonqo lwemigaqo nemiqathango yokusebenza kwabasebenzi abaqeshwe kumaziko eendaba, ukuba ayikho imigaqo nemiqathango yokusebenza leyo, iyakuyiyila iyinyanzelise;

(e) ukwamkela nokungqalela izikhalazo zika wonke-wonke, kwaye apho kufanele khona, ithabathe inyathelo elifanelekileyo kwiintatheli nabanye abaqeshwa bakumaziko eeNdaba okanye abosasazo xa bethe bophula uMthetho nemigaqo yabo yokusebenza;

(f) ukuqinisekisa ukuba abantu baseZimabwe bayafikelela ngokwenezayo kulwazi;

(g) ukukhuthaza ukusetyenziswa nokuphuhliswa kwelwimi zonke zase Zimbabwe;

(h) ukukhuthaza ukwamkeleka nokusetyenziswa kwethekhinoloji entsha ekusasazeni iiNdaba;

(i)

ukukhuthaza iyantlukwano nokhuphiswano olungenamkhethe nolulungileyokumaziko eendaba; kwa

(j)

nokuqhutywa kophando malunga nenkululeko ekubhalweni nokuthethwa kweeNdaba, nokukhuthaza utshintsho loMthetho malunga nalemiba.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente usenokunika iKhomishini yeeNdaba eZimbabwe ukuba—

(a)

iqhube uphando nokubuzisisa

(i) nokuba yeyiphi inkqubo ebonakalisa ukuphazamisa inkululeko yokupapashwa kweeNdaba; kwa

(ii) ngokusebenza kweziko leeNdaba.

(b) ukucebisa okanye ukuthabatha amanyathelo akanelekileyo okuqeqesha abasebenzi bakumaziko eendaba abathe bafumaneka bophule uMthetho okanye imiqathango yokusebenza kwabo.

(3) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uvele nendlela zokulawula ukusebenza kwamaziko eendaba. 250 Iingxelo zeKhomishini yeeNdaba yase Zimbabwe Ngaphezu kwengxelo elindeleke ukuba ingeniswe yiKhomishini yeeNdaba yase Zimbazwe njengokutsho kwecandelo 323, ngokusebenzisa uMphathiswa ofanelekileyo, kufanele, ingenise iingxelo kwiPalamente malunga nezinto ezinxulumene nanaziko eendaba, nezo ekufanele leKhomishini izazi, yazise iPalamente ukuba iyokuziqwalasela. INXALENYE 6 Ikhomishini Yesizwe Yoxolo Noxolelwaniso 251 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishini yoXolo noXolelwaniso lweSizwe

(1) Okokukuqala ngemva kweminyaka elishumi ngemva komhla wokukuqala kwayo, kukho iKhomishini eyaziwa ngokuba yiKhomishiniyoXolo nemVisiswanoyeSizwe; yona ino—

(a)

sihlalo otyunjwe nguMongameli ngemva kokubonisanane Khomishiniye Nkonzooxiswa kwama Tyala kwa neKomiti yemiGaqo nemi Qathango emisiweyo;

(b) namanye amalungu asibhozo atyunjwe nguMongameli kuluhlu lwabatyunjwa abangaphezu kweshumi elinambini ebelihanjiswe yiKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo

(2) Usihlalo weKhomishini yoXolo nemVisiswano yeSizwe kufuneka ibe ngumntu okulungeleyo kwaye owakhe wasebenzela njenge gqwetha okweminyaka esisxhenxe eZimbabwe .

(3) Xa ukutyunjwa kukasihlalo weKhomishini yoXolo nemaVisiswano yeSizwe kungahambisani nengcebiso yeKhomishini yeNkonzo yokuxoxiswa kwamaTyala, uMongameli kufuneka abangele ukuba iKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo ixelelwe ngokungxamisekileyo.

(4) Amalungu eKhomishini yeSizwe yoXolo noXolelwaniso kufuneka akhethwe ngokwethembeka kwabo, nolwazi nokuvisisa nobungcali bokukhuthaza malunga namalungelo abantu.

252 Imisebenzi yeKhomishini yoXolo nemVisiswano yeSizwe IKhomishini yoXolo nemVisiswano yeSizwe isebenza oku—

(a)

ukuqinisekisa ubulungisa ngemva kongquzulwano, ukuphilisa noxolelwano;

(b) ukuphuhlisa noku phumeza iinkqubo ezikhuthaza ukuphila kweSizwe, ubunye, nomanyano eZimbabwe nokusombulula iimpikiswano ngoxolo;

(c)

ukubangela uxolelwano ngohlobo loku khuthaza abantu ukuba bathethe inyani malunga nokwayizolo nokuququzelela ubulungisisa apho konakele khona kubonelelwa nobulungisa;

(d) ukuphuhlisa iinkqubonamaziko eSizwe ukuququzelela incoko phakathi kwamaqela ezopolitiki, uluntu, imibutho namanye amaqela, ukuthintela iimpikiswano ezingaske zivuse ungquzulwano;

(e)

ukuphuhlisa iinkqubo zokuqinisekisa ukuba abantu abaphila ngaphantsi kwentshutshiso, utyakatyo nezinye iindlela zokuxhatshazwa bayalufumana unyango lwengcinga noxhaso ukuze babuyiseleke esimeni sakakade;

(f)

ukwamkela nokuqwalasela izikhalazo ezisuka kuwonke-wonke nokuzilungisisa kangangoko;

(g) ukuphuhlisa iindlela zokukhawlezisa ukubona apho kungasake kubekho impikiswano, nokuthabatha amanyathelo afaneleyo okuthintela impikiswano leyo;

(h) ukenza nokuba yini ukuze kuthinteleke ungquzulwano nokukhuthaza uxolo;

(i)

ukuxolelanisa nokulamla impikiswano phakathi koluntu, imibutho, amaqela nabantu; kwa

(j)

nokucebisauMthetho ukuze uqinisekise uncedo, kuquka neenkcukacha, luyafunyanwa ngabantu abachaphazelekayo kungquzulwano, ubhubhane okanye nokuba yini ehambelana nezizinto.

253 Iingxelo zeKhomishini yoXolo nemVisiswano yeSizwe Ngaphezu kwengxelo elindeleke ukuba ingeniswe yiKhomishini yeSizwe yoXolo noXolelwaniso njengokutsho kwecandelo 323, ngokusebenzisa uMphathiswa ofanelekileyo, kufanele, ingenise iingxelo kwiPalamente malunga nezinto ezinxulumene nanaziko eendaba, nezo ekufanele leKhomishini izazi, yazise iPalamente ukuba iyokuziqwalasela. ISAHLUKO 13 Amaziko Okulwisa Urhwaphilizo Nolwaphulomthetho INXALENYE 1: Ikhomishini Yokulwisa Urhwaphilizo 254 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishini yokuLwisa uRhwaphilizo

(1) Kukho iKhomishini eyaziwa ngokuba yiKhomishini yokuLwisa uRhwaphilizo yona equka—

(a)

usihlalo oqeshwe nguMongameli ngemva kokubonisana neKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo; kwa

(b) amanye amalungu asibhozo atyunjwe nguMongameli ethathela kuluhlu labatyunjwa abangaphezu kweshumi nambini elingeniswe yiKomiti yemiGaqo nemiQathango emisiweyo.

(2) Amalungu eKhomishini yokuLwisa uRhwaphilizo kufanele baqeshwe ngenxeni yemfezeko, ubuchule ekuphatheni nolwazi lwabo lomsebenzi ekuqhubeni uphando lwamatyala nokuziphela emsebenzini abawenzayo; kwaye

(a)

Ebuncinaneni kubekho nokuba mnye oqeqeshwe wakulungela ukusebenza njengomcebisi wezoMthetho eZimbabwe, kwaye eneminyaka engaba ngaphezu sisixhenxe ekulomsebenzi;

(b) Ebuncinaneni kubekho abemnye oqeqeshwe wakulungela ukuba ngumcwangcwisi zimali okanye umphicothi kawonke-wonke; kwaye

(c) Ebuncinaneni abemnye oneminyaka elishumi elichule ekuphandeni ngolwaphulo Mthetho. 255 Imisebenzi yeKhomishini yokuLwisa uRhwaphilizo yaseZimbabwe

(1) IKhomishini yokuLwisa uRhwaphilizoyaseZimbabweisebenza—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

(e)

(f)

(g)

(h)

ukuphanda nokubhenca amatyala okrhwaphilizo kumacandelo kawonkewonke nabucala ukulwisa ukrwaphilizo, ubusela,ukusetyenziswa gwenxa, ukuxhatshazwa kwamandla okuphatha, nokunye ukuziphatha okungafanelekanga kuwonke-wonke nabucala ukukhuthaza ukunyaniseka nengqengqesho emalini kumacandelo kawonke-wonke nabucala ukwamkela nokuqwalalsela izikhalazo ezisuka kuwonke-wonke nokuthatha amanyathelo malunga nezikhalazo ezo njengokuba ibaona kufanele ukuyalela umlwawuli-jikelele wamapolisa ukuba aphande amatyala acingeleka ukuba ngaworhwaphilizo kwaaye eze nengxelo kwiKhomishini yezipnumo zophando. ukusa amatyala kwi Gunya lokuMangala leSizwe ukuze amangalelwe ukufumana uncedo oluvela kumalungu eNkonzo yamaPolisa ukwenza isincomo kuRhulumente nabanye abantu malunga namanyathelo okuphucula imfezeko noxanduva nokuthintela ukuziphatha okungafanelekanga kumacandelo kawonke-wonke nabucala.

(2) uMlawuli-Jikelele wamapolisa kufuneka athobele iziyalelo ezinikelwe kuye yiKhomishini yokuLwisa uRhwaphilizonjengoko kuxeliwe kwicandelwana (1)(e).

(3) Urhulumente kufuneka aqinisekise, esebenzisa iindlela zoMthetho nezinye, ukuba iKhomishini yokuLwisa uRhwaphilizo inamandla okucebisa ukubanjwa nokuqinisekisa ukumangalelwa kwabantu abacingelwa ukuba benze urhwaphilizo, ukutxhaphaza amandla okuphatha nokunye ukuziphatha okungafanelekanga okungaphantsi kolawlo lweKhomishini. 256 Indlela izibonelelo ezithile zesahluko 12 emazisetyenziswe ngayo yiKhomishini yase Zimbabwe yokuLwisa uRhwaphilizo Izahluko zesi 234, 235, nesesi 237 zisetyenziswa yiKhomishini yase Zimbabwe eyoukuLwisa uRhwaphilizo namalungu ayo ngendlela emazisetyenziwe ngayo yi Khomishini ezimeleyo esekwe sisahluko 12.

257 Iingxelo zeKhomishini yaseZimbabwe eyokuLwisa uRhwaphilizo Ngaphezu kwengxelo elindeleke ukuba ingeniswe yiKhomishini yokuLwisa uRhwaphilizo eZimbazwe njengokutsho kwecandelo 323, ngokusebenzisa uMphathiswa ofanelekileyo, kufanele, ingenise iingxelo kwiPalamente malunga nezinto ezinxulumene nanaziko eendaba, nezo ekufanele leKhomishini izazi, yazise iPalamente ukuba iyokuziqwalasela. INXALENYE 2 Igunya Lesizwe Lokutshutshisa 258 Ukusekwa nemisebanzi yeGunya leSizwe lokuTshutshisa Kukho iGunya leSizwe lokuTshutshisa elinoxanduva wokuqalisa nokumangalelwa kolwaphulo Mthetho limele urhulumente nokwenza eminye imisebenzi eyimfuneko okanye ehambisana nokumangala mayelana noko. 259 UMtshutshisi-Jikelele namanye amagosa

(1) Kukho uMtshutshisi-Jikelele oyintloko yeGunya leSizwe lokuTshutshisa.

(2) I ofisi ka Mtshutshisi-Jikelele isebenza ngaphansti kuka kawonke-wonke kodwa ayiso nxalenye ye Nkonzo yoLuntu.

(3) UMtshutshisi-Jikelele uqashwa nguMongameli ngengcebiso yeKhomishiniyeNkonzo yoXoxiso lwamaTyala ilandela indlela umgwebi atyunjwe ngayo.

(4) UMtshutshisi-Jikelele kufuneka ibe ngumntu oqeqeshelwe wakulungela ukuba ngumgwebi weNkundla ePhakamileyo kuzo zonke.

(5) UMtshutshisi-Jikelele uhlala e-ofiSini ikota yeminyaka esithandathu kwaye sivuselelwa kube kanye esosithuba sokuba sesikhundleni eso.

(6) Engekangeni e-ofiSini uMtshuthsisi-Jikelele kufuneka enze isifungo phambi kuka Mongameli okanye umntu othunywe nguMongameli ngendlela exelwe kwishedyuli yesithathu.

(7) Izibonelelo ezimalunga kukukhutshwa kuka Mgwebi zizo ezisetyenziswayo ukukhupha uMtshutshisi-Jikelele e-ofiSini.

(8) Iimeko zomsebenzi kaMtshutshisi-Jikelele, kuquka nembatalo yakhe, kufuneka zenziwe ngoMthethowePalamente, kodwa imbatalo yakhe akufunekenga yohliswe ukuba uMtshtshisi-Jikelel esesofiSini.

(9) Umvuzo kaMtshutshisi-Jikelele usuka kwiingxowa-mali zeSizwe ezingenisiweyo zadityaniswa.

(10) UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka uvele nendlela emakuqeshwe ngayo ibhodi emayiqeshe abancedisi baka Mtshutshisi-Jikelele ekwenzeni imisebenzi yakhe, kwaye kufanele uvele—

(a)

neemfuneko zabo bantu ukuba bakwazi ukungena kwesisikhundla;

(b) iimeko zokusebebnza kunye nohlobo kufuneka baziphathe ngalo kulomsebnzi;

(c)

nokuba ekwenzeni imisebenzi yabo, abobantu kufuneka ibengabantu abazimeleyo, abangenamkhethe, kwaye bathobele uMthetho noMtshutshisi-Jikelele

(d) nolwakhiwo nocwangciso lwe Gunya likaMtshutshisi Jikelele; kwa

(e)

ngokubanzi ngokusebenza okusemgangathweni nobuhle begunya likaMtshutshisi Jikelele.

(11) UMtshutshisi-Jikelele usenokuyalela uMlawuli-Jikelele wamapolisa ukuba aphande kwaye eze nengxelo malunga, njengoluvo luka Mtshutshisi-Jikelele, netyala elikhoyo okanye elicingelekayo, kwaye uMlawuli-Jikelele wamaPolisa kufuneka athobele loomyalelo. 260 Ukuzimela kukaMtshutshisi-Jikelele

(1) Ngokubhekiselele kuloMgaqo-Siseko, uMtshutshisi-Jikelele—

(a)

uzimele kwaye akufanelanga athobele okanye alawulwe ngomnye umntu; kwaye.

(b) kufuneka enze imisebenzi yakhe engenamkhethe kwaye engenaloyiko.

(2) UMtshutshisi-Jikelele kufuneka adale, kwaye azise uwonke-wonke, imigaqo okufanele yena ayisebenzise ekuxoxweni kwamatyala. 261 Ukuziphatha kwamaGosa eGunya leSizwe lokuTshutshisa

(1) UMtshutshisi-Jikelele namagosa eGunya leSizwe lokuTshutshisa kufuneka enze njengokwemfuneko yalo Mgaqo-Siseko;

(2) Ekwenzeni imisebenzi yakhe, akukho Gosa le Gunya leSizwe lokuTshutshisa eliseno;

(a)

kwenza umsebenzi walo ngokukomkhethe;

(b) phumelelisa iminqweno yeqela lezopolitiki nokuba leliphi;

(c)

likhethe izimvo nokuba zisemthethweni ezamaqela ezopolitiki, okanye;

(d) ukugxobha amalungelo asiseko nenkululeko yomntu nokuba ngubani.

(3) Amagosa eGunya leSizwe lokuTshutshisa akufanelanga asebenzele okanye abe ngamalungu amaqela okanye imibutho yezopolitiki.

(4) UMthetho wePalamente usenokwenza eminye imigaqo yokuqinisekisa ukuba amagosa egunyaleSizwe lokumanagala awathabathi cala kwezepolitiki. 262 Ingxelo kaMtshutshisi-Jikelele kwiPalamente qho ngonyaka UMtshutshsisi-Jikelele kufuneka axele ePalamente, ngokusebenzisa uMphathiswa ofanelekileyo, achazeingxelo yonyaka yentsebenzo ye Gunya leSizwe lokuTshutshisa, ngxelo leyo ekufuneke ingacothi yogqithe iinyanaga ezithandathu ngemva kokukuqala konyaka olandela unyaka oxelwayo. 263 Amanye amandla okutshutshisa UMthetho wePalamente usenokunika abanye abantu abangasingabo abeGunya leSizwelokumanagala, amandla okumangala, kodwa loomandla awanakungqubana namandla eliGunya ngokwalenxalenye.

ISAHLUKO 14 Urhulumente Wophondo Nowasekuhlaleni INTSHAYELELO Njengoko kunqwenelekile ukuba;

(a) ukukhuseleka kobunye beSizwe saseZimbabwe nokuthintelwa kweendlela zonke zokuchitha ubunye nenkqubekeko;

(b) ukuncedisa kwabemmi nabahlali bonke baseZimabwe kwidemokrasi, no

(c)

nokwabiwa ngokulinganayo kwezixhobo zeSizwe nokuthatah inxaxheba kwabahlali kwinkqubo zophuhliso lweengingqi zabo;

Kufuneka kubekho ugqithiso lwamandla ukwenzela ukwehlisa amanqanaba kaRhulumente eZimbabwe. INXALANYE 1 264 Ukunikwa kwabantu bonke amandla karhulumente kunye noxanduva

(1) Xa kunokwenzeka, amagunya kaRhulumente kunye noxanduva kufuneka kuphuhliswe ngamabhunga ephondo kunye namagosa asekuhlaleni anobuchule bokufezekisa loo mfanelo ngokufanelekileyo nangempumelelo.

(2) Iinjongo zokwegqithiswa kwamandla noxanduva luka Rhulumente enikwa amabhunga amaphondo namagosa asekuhlaleni zezi;

(a)

ukunika abantu amandla obuRhulumente basekuhlaleni nokuphucula inxaxheba yabantu ekuthatheni izigqibo kuzinto ezibachaphazelayo;

(b) ukukhuthaza uRhulumente onedemokrasi, eyondeleleneyo, onoxanduva nosebenzayo eLizweni laseZimbabwe liphela;

(c)

ukukhusela nokunyanzela ubunye beSizwe, uxolo eLizweni, nomanyano olungenakwahlulwa eZimbabwe;

(d) ukuqaphela amalungelo asekuhlaleni ukuze bazenzele imicimbi yabo kunye nophuhliso lwabo;

(e)

ukuqinisekisa ukuba izixhobo zeSizwe zabiwa ngokulinganayo;

(f)

ukutshintshela iinxaxheba nezixhobo zikaRhulumenete weSizwe ukwenzela ukuseka immo eyomeleleyo ngasemalini yamabhunga ophondo nooRhulumente basekuhlaleni. 265 Imigaqo ebanzi karhulumentu wephondo nowasekuhlaleni

(1) Iikhansisli zamaphondo noorhulumente basekuhlaleni kufuneka; ngokufikeleleka kubo—

(a)

baqinisekise ulawulo olufaneleyo ngokukhuthala, ubulungisa nokondelela;

(b)

benze imisebenzi abayithunywe nguloMgaqo-Siseko noMthetho we Palamente;

(c)

benze imisebenzi yabo ngohlobo lokuba aliphazamisi amanye amanqanaba kaRhulumente;

(d) basebenzisane ngakumbi;

(i) ekuxelelaneni nasekucebisaneni ngezonto bahambisana kuzo;

(ii) ukungqinelanisa nokulungelelanisa imisebenzi yabo;

(e)

ukugcina uxolo, ubunye, nokumanyana kweSizwe saseZimbabwe;

(f)

ukukhusela intlalontle kawonke-wonke;

(g) nokuqinisekisa ukumelwa kwabantu ngokulungileyo nokulinganayo kwiindawo zabo zolawulo.

(2) Onke amalungu amagunya asekuhlaleni kufuneka onyulwe ngabavoti ababhalisileyo kwezondawo bahlala kuzo.

(3) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uze neendlela ezifanelekileyo zokuququzelela ulungelelano phakathi kukarhulumete weSizwe, namabhunga ephondo noorhulumente basekuhlaleni. 266 U k uz ipha t ha k w ab aseb en z i b aka R h u l u m en te w ep h o n d o nowasekuhlaleni

(1) Abasebenzi bamabhunga ephondo, awe dolophu nawasekuhlaleni kufanele benze ngokwemfuneko yaloMgaqo Siseko noMthetho.

(2) Akukho msebenzi webhunga lephondo ledolophu okanye likaRhulumente wasekuhlaleni osenokuthi xa esenza umsebenzi wakhe—

(a)

Abonakalise ukuba nomkhethe;

(b) aphumelelise iminqweno yamaqela okanye imibutho yezopolitiki okanye;

(c)

abangele ukuphunyeleliswa kweminqweno yamaqela ezopolitiki okanye;

(d) agxobhe amalungelo asiseko nenkululeko yomntu noba ngubani na.

(3) Abasebenzi bamabhunga ophondo needolophu noorhulumente basekuhlaleni kufuneka bangabambi izihlalo zolulawulo nokokuba kukweliphi na iqela lezepolitiki.

(4) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uvele nendlela zokuqinisekisa ukungabi namkhethe kwezopolitiki kubasebenzi beebhunga lophondo, ibhunga ledolophu noorhulumente basekuhlaleni. INXALENYE 2 Amaphondo Namabhunga Ephondo Naweembaxa 267 Amaphondo nezithili zase Zimbabwe

(1) I Zimbabwe yohlulwe yaba ngalamaphondo—

(a)

iphondo elingumbaxa elakwaBulawayo;

(b) iphondo elingumbaxa elase Harare;

(c)

phondo lasemaNyika;

(d) iphondo lasemaShoneni eliphakathi;

(e)

iphondo lasemaShoneni eliseMpumalanga;

(f)

iphondo lasemaShoneni eliseNtshonalanga;

(g) iphondo laseMaswingo;

(h) iphondo lasemaNdebeleni eliseNtla;

(i)

iphondo lamaNdebele eliseZantsi;

(j)

iphondo eliPhakathi naphakathi;

Imida yalamaphondo iimiswe nguMthetho wePalamente.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente—

(a)

kufanele uvele nendlela yokwaba amaphondo abezizithili;

(b) kusenokuvela nendlela yokutshintsha kwemida yamaphondo nezithili; ngemva kokucebisana neKhomishini yoNyulo yase Zimbabwe nabantu abakulomaphondo nezozithili. 268 Amabhunga amaphondo

(1) Kukho amabhunga ophondo kuphondo ngalinye, ngaphandleni kwamaphondo eedolophu, aquka— usihlalo webhunga, ovotelwe ngokwemigaqo yecandelo lesi 272;

(a)

amalungu eSenethi onyulwe ngamaphondo achaphazelekayo;

(b) iiNkosi zeeSenethi ezimbini ezimbini ezonyulwe kumaphondo achaphazelekayo njengokutsho kwecandelo lesi 120(1)(b);

(c)

uMongameli noSekela kaMongameli weBhunga leSizwe leeNkosi, abanendawo ezingaphantsi kwephondo elichaphazelekayo;

(d) onke amaLungu eNdinano yeSizwe asuka kwiindawo zingaphansti kwelophondo;

(e)

amalungu angabafazi aweNdibano yeSizwe abonyulwe ngokwemigaqo yecandelo lesi 124(1)(b)kuluphondo elichaphazelekayo;

(f)

osodolophu noosihlalo, kukungakhathalekile nokuba babizwa ngeliphi na igama, bamagunya edolophu zonke awasezi lalilini nasezidolophini kuluphond oluchaphazelekayo;

(g) nabantu abalishumi abenyulwe ngendlela yomlinganiselo wokumelwa okuthethwe ngawo kwicandelwana (3);

(h) abantu abalishumi abanyulwe ngenkqubo yolungelelwano elibhekiselwe kwelicandelwano 3).

(2) Umntu olungele ukunyulwa ukuze abe libhunga lephondo ngokomgaqo wecandelwana (1)(f) ukuba ngaba loomntu ukulungele ukunyulwa njengeLungu leNdibano yeSizwe.

(3) Unyulo lwamabhunga ephondo kufuneka lenziwe ngokwemfunekoyo Mthetho woNyulo, okufuneka uqinisekise ukuba oceba abaxelwe kwicandelwana lesi

(1)(f) banyulwa ngaphantsi koluhlu leqela lomlinganiselo wokumelwa—

(a)

osekelwe ziivoti zokuvotela abagqatswa abamela amaqela ezopolitiki kuphondo elichaphazelekayo kunyulo lika wonke-wonke lokunyulwa kwamaLungu eNdibano yeSizwe;

(b) ngohlobo lokuba abagqatswa abangamadoda nabangabafazi babe kuluhlu ngokufananayo, uluhlu ngalunye lube lukhokhelwe ngumgqatswa ongumfazi.

(4) isihlalo selungu lebhunga yephondo esixelwe—

(a)

2013

kumhlathi (b), (c), (e) okanye (g) womhlathana (1) ivuleka ngokuske ilungu lishiye isihlalo salo sasePalamente;

(b) kumhlathi (d) wecandelwana (1) sivuleka ngokuske ilungu liyeke ukuba ngosodolophu okanye usihlalo wegunya lokuhlala kuphondo elichaphazelekayo;

(c)

kumhlathi (f) wecandelwana (1), sivuleka kwiimeko ezixelwe kwicandelo 129, njengohlobo lwenziwa kumaLungu ePalamente.

269 Amabhunga eeDolophu

(1) Kuphondo ngalinye elasedolophini kukho ibhunga ledolophu elibalela(a)

Kwimeko yakwaBulawayo kukho usodolophu wakwaBulawayo, ongusihlalo weBhunga ledolophu yakwaBulawayo

(b) eHarare—

(i) kukho usodolophu wedolophu yase Harare ongusihlalo weBhunga ledolophu ase Harare;

(ii) nosodolophu okanyeusihlalo wedolophu engeyesibini ngobukhulu kwi kululawulo lwedolophu, ongusekela kasihlalo webhunga ledolophu yaseHarare;

(c)

onke amaLungu eNdibano yeSizwe aneendawo zingaphantsi kwephondo elichaphazelekayo.

(d) abafazi abangamalungu eNdibano yeSizwe aonyulwa ngokuxelwe kwicandelo lesi 124(1)(b) besuka kuphondo eliyidolophu echaphazelekayo.

(e)

Amalungu eSenethi onyulwe kwidolophu mbaxa echaphazelekayo.

(f)

Oosodolophu noosekela boosodolophu kwanosihlalo nosekela babo, nokokuba babizwa kanjani, banamagunya kwidolophu ezichaphazelekayo.

(2) Isihlalo selungu lebhunga—

(a)

umhlathi (a) okanye (b) wecandelwana (1) ivuleka ngokuske ilungu liyeke ukuba ngusodolophu, umsekeli kasodolophu okanye usihlalo,

(b) umhlathi (c), (d) okanye (e) wecandelwana (1) ivuleka ngokuske ilungu lisuke esihlalweni sasePalamente;

(c)

umhlathi (f) wecandelwana (1) sivuleka ngokuske ilungu liyeke ukuba ngusodolophu, umsekeli kasodolophu, usihlalo okanye umsekeli kasihlalo, wegunya lokuhlala phakathi kwephondo ledolophu elichaphazelekayo

270 Imisebenzi yamabhunga ephondo needolophu

(1) Ibhunga lephondo neledolophu linoxanduva lophuhliso lamaphondo alo ngokwentlalo noqoqosho kuquka—

(a)

ukuceba nophunyezo lophuhliso lwentlalo noqoqosho ephondweni lalo;

(b) ukulungelelanisa nokuphunyezwa kwenkqubo zikaRhulumente ephondweni layo;

(c)

ukuceba ngophunyezo lwamanyathelo okukhusela, ukuphuhlisa nolawulo lwezixhobo zobutyebi ephondweni lalo;

(d) ukukhuthaza ukhenketho ephondweni nokuphuhlisa izibonelelo zoko;

(e)

ukujonga nokuhlalutya ubutyebi koluphondo olo; kwa

(f)

nokwenza eminye imisebenzi, equka imisebenzi yoMthetho, engabe ibekwe kuyo nguMthetho wePalamente.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uvee nokusekwa, ulwakhiwo, nabasebenzi bebhunga lephondo eliyidolophu, nendlela emaba sebenze ngayo.

(3) Amalungu ebhunga lophondo eliyidolophu anoxanduva, ngokuhlangeneyo okanye ngalinye, okwenza imisebenzi yalo kubahlali bephondo okanye iSizwe. 271 Iikomiti zamabhunga ephondo Ukuze basebenze kangcono, amabhunga amaphondo nedolophu asenokuseka iikomiti kodwa ikomiti nganye kufuneka zichotshelwe lilungu elixelwe kwicandelo 268(1)(f) okanye 269(1)(f). 272 Oosihlalo bamabhunga amaphondo

(1) Ukuhlala kwalo kokukuqala emveni konyulo lukawonke wonke, ibhunga lephondo kufuneka linyule usihlalo lithathela kuluhlu labantu ababini abakulungeleyo ukuba ngosihlalo, lomagama abe eziswe—

(a)

liqela lezopolitiki elililo elifumene izihlalo ezinintsi kwiNdibano yeSizwe yephondo elichaphazelekayo; okanye

(b) ukuba akukho qela lezopolitiki njengoko kuxeliwe kumhlathi (a), elona qela lezopolitiki lifumene inani elingaphezulu leevoti ephondweni kunyulo lwamalungu eNdibanoyeSizwe.

(2) Umntu ulungela unyulo lukasihlalo webhunga lophondo xa engumntu olungele unyulo lwamalungu neNdibano yeSizwe.

(3) I-ofisi kasihlalo webhunga loluphondo yi-ofisi kawonke-wonkekodwa ayisonxalenye yeNkonzo zoLuntu.

(4) Engekaqalisi imisebenzi yakhe, usihlalo webhunga loluphondo kufuneka enze isifungo sokunyaniseka nese-ofisi phambi kukamabhalana webhunga loluphondo njengokuxelwe kwishedyuli yesithathu.

(5) Usihlalo webhunga loluphondo angarhoxa ngokwazisa ukurhoxa kwakhe ngokwakhe kwibhunga loluphondo.

(6) Usihlalo webhunga yephondo kufuneka aphume e-ofiSini—

(a)

ngomhla wendibano yokukuqala webhunga loluphondoemva kolunyulo lukawonke;

(b) ukuba eye akakulungela ukuba lilungu lebhunga loluphondo;

(c)

ukuba ukukhutshwa kwakhe kuye kwaqinisekiswa ngezihlalo zovoto ezimbini ebuthathwini zezihlalo zizonke zamalungu ebhunga loluphondo ebuncinaneni; okanye
