(d) ukuba ekhutshwa e-ofisini yinkundla exelwe kwicandelwana (7).

(7) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uvele nendlela yokusekwa kwenkundla ezimeleyo esebenza ukususa oosihlalo bamabhunga amaphondo kwi-ofisi, kodwa kufuneka ukususwa kwenziwe kuphela ukuba umtu;

(a)

uyohluleka ukwenza imisebenzi yakhe ngenxeni yokugula emzimbeni okanye ezingqondweni;

(b) umsebenzi akawazi kwaphela;

(c)

akaziphathanga kakuhle kwaphela;

(d) wenze ityala eliquka ukungabinanyani; okanye

(e)

Ephule uMthetho ngabom, kuquka nemithethwana yamagunya asekuhlaleni.

(8) Usihlalo webhunga lophondo akaphumi e-ofiSini yakhe ngaphandle ngokwezizatho ezixelwe kwelicandelwana. 273 Izibonelelo ezibanzi malunga namabhunga ephondo needolophu

(1) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka wenze amalungiselelo okanye izibonelelo, ezingqinelana nesisahluko, zokuseka nokusebenza kweebhunga lamaphondo needolophu, ingakumbi—

(a)

Iinkqubo zeebhunga lamaphondo needolophu;

(b) Imisebenzi yoosihlalo bamabhunga amaphondo needolophu;

(c)

Imeko yokusebenza kwebhunga lamaphondo needolophu; kwa

(d) Ukuqeshwa, iimeko zokusebenza nokususwa kwabaqeshwa bamabhunga amaphondo needolophu.

(2) UMthetho woNyulo kufuneka wenze izibonelelo, ezingqinelana nesisahluko singauguqulwanga, zokuzalisa izithuba kwizihlalo zamalungu eebhunga lamaphondo ezixelwe kwicandelo 268(1)(f) nee ofisi zoosihlalo beebhunga lamaphondo needolophu, zithuba ezo ekufuneka zizaliswe—

(a)

ngabantu beqela linye nelalowo osukileyo esihlalweni okanye e-ofiSini; kwaye

(b) ngaphandle koosihlalo, ibe ngumntu weSini sinye nalowo usukileyo esihlalweni okanye e-ofiSini. INXALENYE 3 Urhulumente Wasekuhlaleni 274 Amagunya entlalo yasezidolophini

(1) Kukho amagunya asekuhlaleni ezidolophini amela nalawula imicimbi yabantu ezidolophini zase Zimbabwe.

(2) Amagunya okuhlala kwasezidolophini aphethwe ngamabhunga aquka ooceba abanyulwe ngabavoti ababhalisiweyo kwidolophu ezichaphazelekayo zichotshelwe ngoosodolophu okanye oosihlalo abanyuliweyo.

(3) Intlobo ezohlukeneyo zamagunya asekuhlaleni zisenokusekwa zidolophu ezohluka-hlukeneyo, kwaye iidolophu ezimbini kunyuka zisenokufakwa ngaphantsi kolawulo lwegunya lasekuhlaleni elinye. (4 Ukulungela nendlela yokuqhutywa kwenkqubo zonyulo lwabantu abaxelwe kwicandelwana (2) kufuneka kuvezwe nguMthetho woNyulo.

(5) UMthetho wePalamente usenokunika usodolophu okanye usihlalo webhunga igunya lasekuhlaleni amandla okulawulaa, kodwa usodolophu okanye usihlalo onikwe loomandla kufuneka avotelwe ngqo ngabavoti ababhalisiweyo baloondawo yaloo yeloogunya lasekuhlaleni elisekwayo. 275 Amagunya asekuhlaleni eMaphandleni

(1) Kukho amagunya okuhlala aseMaphandleni, asekwe ngendlela exelwe lelicandelo, esekelwa ukumela nokulawula imicimbi yabantu baseMaphandleni.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uveze—

(a)

ukusekwa kwamagunya okuhlala kwaseMaphandleni;

(b) unyulo, omalwenziwe ngabavoti baseMaphandleni ababhalisiweyo, lweebhunga elilawulayo imicimbi yamagunya asekuhlaleni exelwe kumhlathi (a);

(c)

unyulo loosihlalo, kungakhathalekile nokokuba babizwa ngeliphi igama, kufuneka bachophele amabhunga axelwe kumhlathi (b); kwa

(d) nokulungela umsebenzi kwamalungu ebhunga axelwe kumhlathi (b).

(3) Iintlobo ezahlukeneyo zamagunya okuhlala eMaphandleni asenokusekwa kwezinye iindawo zaseMaphandleni, kwaye iindawo ezingaba mbini kunyuka zisenokubekwa ngaphansi kolawulo lwegunya lokuhlala elinye. 276 Imisebenzi yamagunya asekuhlaleni

(1) Ngokubhekiselele kuloMgaqo-Siseko noMthethowePalamente, igunya lasekuhlaleni linelungelo lokuphatha imicimbi yasekuhlaleni eyabantu abakuloondwo esekwe ligunya elo, kwaye linamandla apheleleyo okwenzenjalo.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente usenokunika igunya lasekuhlaleni imisebenzi; equka—

(a)

amandla okwenza imithetho, imimiselo nemigaqo yolawulo oluqiqileyo lweendawo ezisekiweyo.

(b) Amandla okwenza irhafu ukwenzela ingxowa yemali yokuba incediseke ekwenzeni imisebenzi yalo yobugunya. 277 Unyulo lwabalawuli asekuhlaleni

(1) Unyulo looceba bamagunya asekuhlaleni kufuneka lwenziwe —

(a)

ukuba lunyulo lukawonke loosodolophu nooceba, lwenziwa kunye nonyulo lukawonke-wonke lokunyula amaLungu ePalamente noMongameli;

(b) ukuba ngaba lunyulo olungasolilo lukawonke wonke, lwenzelwa ukuzalisa izithuba emveni kokuba kuye kwakho isithuba esithile, kwaye lwenziwe ngokungxama kangangoko.

(2) Unyulo loosodolophu noosihlalo bamagunya asekuhlaleni, ngaphandle koosodolophu noosihlalo abaphiwe amandla okulawula ngendlela exelwe kwicandelo 274(5), kufuneka lwenziwe ekuchopheni kokukuqala kwamabhunga achaphazelekayo ngemva konyulo lukawonke.

(3) Ngaphandle kokuba njengoko kuchaziwe kwicandelwana (2) okanye uMthetho wePalamente, oosodolophu, oosihlalo nooceba bamagunya asekuhlaleni baqala imisebenzi yabo ngomhla wethoba ngemva kokubhengezwa kweziphumo zonyulo lukawonke olunyule ooceba. 278 Ukuthathwa kwezihlalo ngamalungu alawula ekuhlaleni

(1) Isihlalo sika sodolophu, usihlalo okanye uceba wegunya lasekuhlaleni sithathwa ngokuba asinamntu njengokuxelwe kwicandelo 129, njengendlela eyenziwa kwiLungu lePalamente, nokuba sesiphi isingqiniso seSithethi okanye uMongameli we Senethi kwicandelo 129(1)(k) ecaciselwe njengesingqiniso sika Mphathiswa onoxanduva lukaRhulumente wasekuhlaleni.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uveze indlela yokusekwa kwenkundla ezimeleyo esebenza ukususa oosodolophu, oosihlalo nooceba, kodwa kufuneka basuswe kuphela ukuba ngaba—

(a)

boyisakele ukwenza imisebenzi yabo njenxeni yokugula emzimbeni okanye ezingqondweni;

(b) abasebenzi kakukhe kwaphela;

(c)

abanziphathanga kakuhle kwaphela;

(d) bafumaneke benetyala eliquka ukungabinanyani, urhwaphilizo okanye ukuxhaphaza amandla e-ofisi; okanye

(e)

ukuphula uMthetho ngabom, kuquka noMthetho wegunya lasekuhlaleni.

(3) Usodolophu, usihlalo okanye uceba wegunya lasekuhlaleni akasuki esihlalweni ngaphandle ngokwendlela exelwe leli candela. 279 Inkqubo zikaRhulumente wasekuhlaleni UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka uveze inkqubo emazilandelwe ngamabhunga kaRhulumente waswekuhlaleni. ISAHLUKO 15 Inkokheli Zemveli 280 Ubunkokheli bemveli

(1) Iziko, iwonga nendima yenkokheli zemveli ngaphantsi koMthetho wesintu ziyaziwa

(2) Inkokheli yemveli inoxanduva lokwenza ezenkcubeko, ezesintu nemicimbi yesintu emayenziwe yiNkosi, okanye umntu oyintloko okanye isibonda xakungabakho imfuneko kwindawo yakhe. 281 Imigaqo emayilandelwe zinkokheli zemveli

(1) Inkokheli zemveli kufuneka—

(a)

zenze ngokungqinelana naloMgaqosiseko nemithetho yaseZimbabwe;

(b) balandela amasiko aphathelene nobunkokheli bemveli kwaye benze imisebenzi yabo ngenjongo izikolo bunkokheli bemveli elaziwa ngayo nguloMgaqo-siseko; kwaye

(c)

baphathe wonke umntu okwindawo yabo ngokulinganayo nangobulungisa.

(2) Iinkokheli zemveli akufuneki—

(a)

zibe ngamalungu nowuphina umbutho wezepolitiki okanye nangayiphina indlela zibandakanye kwipolitiki;

(b) zenzengendlela enomkhethe;

(c)

zikhuthaze iimfuno nezawuphina umbutho wezepoliktiki okanye;

(d) zinyhashe amalungelo asisigxina neenkululeko nezawuphina umntu.

282 Imisebenzi yeenkokheli zemveli

(1) Inkokheli zemveli zinemisebenzi elandelayo kwindawo ezilawula kuzo—(a)

ukukhuthaza nokugcina ubume bamasiko endawo zabokwaye, ngokuthengqo, ukhuthaza izimilo ezincomekayo zosapho;

(b) ukuthatha amanyathelo okungcina inkcubeko, amasiko, imbali nemveli yendawo zabo, kuquka indawo ezingcwele;

(c)

ukuququzelela uphuhliso;

(d) ngokungqinelana noMthetho wasePalamente, ukulawula Umhlaba waseMaphandleni nokukhusela okusinqongileyo;

(e)

ukusombulula iimbambano phakathi kwabantu kwindawo zabo ngokungqinelana noMthetho wesintu; kwaye

(f)

ukwenza nawuphina umsebenzi awunikwe okanye onyanzelwe kubo nguMthetho wasePalamente .

(2) Ngaphandle ngokubonelelwa kuMthetho wasePalamente, iinkokheli zemveli zinegunya, ulawulo namandla phezukoMhlaba waseMaphandleni okanye nezinye iindawo.

(3) Ekwenzeni imisebenzi yazo, inkokheli zemveli azikho ngaphantsi kwemiyalelo okanyeuluwulo okanye igunya, ngaphandle xakubhaliwe eMthethweni wasePalamente.

(4)UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka ubonelele ukulawula ukuziphatha kweenkokheli zemveli. 283 Ukutyunjwa nokususwa kweenkokheli zemveli UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka ubonelele ngokulandelana, ngokungqinelana nenkcubeko, amasiko, izithethe nenkqubo zeendawo vezichaphazelekayo—

(a)

ukutyunjwa, ukunqunyanyiswa ukulandelelana nokususwa kwenkokheli zemveli;

(b) ukudalwa nokuvuselelwa kobukhosi; kwaye

(c)

ukusonjululwa kweembambano ezimalunga nokutyunjwa, ukuqunyanyiswa, ukulandelelana nokususwa kwenkokheli zemveli;

kodwa—

(i) ukutyunjwa, ukususwa , nokunqunyanyiswa kweeNkosi kufuneka kwenziwe nguMongameli ngokucetyiswa libhunga lephondo leNkosi ngokusebenzisa Ibhunga Lezwe leNkosi noMphathiswa onoxanduva neenkokheli zemveli kwaye ngokungqinelana nenkqubo zemveli nezithethe zeendawo ezichaphazelekayo;

(ii) iimbambano ezimalunga nokutyunjwa, ukunqunyanyiswa nokususwa kwenkokheli kufuneka kusonjululwe nguMongameli ngokucetyiswa libhunga lephondo leNkosi ngokusebenzisana noMphathiswa onoxanduva ngenkokheli zemveli;

(iii) uMthetho kufuneka ubonelele ngamanyathelo okuqinisekisa ukuba yonke lemiba iphathwa ngobulungisa kwaye kungajongwanga ingcamango zepolitiki;

(iii) uMthetho kufuneka ubonelele ngamanyathelo okukhusela isidima samaziko emveli nokuzimela kwawo kuphazamiseko kwezepolitiki.

(iv) uMthetho kufuneka ubonelele ngamanyathelo okukhusela isidima samaziko emveli nokuzimela kwawo kuphazamiseko kwezepolitiki. 284 Ukuvuzwa nezibonelelo zeenkokheli zemveli

(1)UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka ubonelele ngemivuzo neenzuzo ezisisigxina zenkokheli zemveli ngemvumo kaMongameli ayiphiwe ngokucetyiswa nguMphathiswa onoxanduva ngezemali kwaye emveni kothethwa nonoMphathiswa onoxanduva ngenkokheli zemveli.

(2) Ukuvuzwa kweenkokheli zemveli kufuneka kuhlawulwe kwaye kubhatalwe Yingxowamali-yoNgeniso Edityanisiweyo.

(3) Ukuvuzwa kwenkokheli zemveli akufuneki kuncitshiswe logama eseofisini okanye ebambile kwiofisi echaphazelekayo . 285 Ibhunga LeSizwe neembizo zeeNkosi

(1) Kukho iBhunga LeSizwe leNkosi elisekwe ngokungqinelana noMthetho wasePalamente, ukumela iiNkosi eZimbabwe.

(2) UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka useke kwiphondo ngalinye, ngaphandle kwamaphondo angombaxa, indibano yephondo yeNkosi equka iiNkosi kwelophondo.

(3) Xakusenzeka iiNkosi kwiphondo ngalinye kufuneka zimelwe ngokulinganayo kwiBhungaLeSizwe leNkosi.

(4) Unyulo lukaMongameli, iSekela-Mongameli namalungu eBhunga LeSizwe leNkosi kufuneka liqhutywe yiKhomishini yeNkonzo yoLuntu.

(5) Nganye indibano yephondo leNkosi kufuneka yonyulo, ngokungqinela nanoMthetho woNyulo, iiNkosi zimela iphondo kwiSenethi ngokwemigangatho yecandelo 120(1)(b).

(6) UMongameli noSekela-Mongameli weBhunga LeSizwe leeNkosi bonyulwa okwexesha leminyaka emihlanu kwaye bakulungele ukubuya bonyulwe okwexesha elifanayo kodwa umntu osabambe okweekota ezimbini njengoSekela-Mongameli ukulungela ukunyulwa njengoMongameli.

(7) UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka ubonelele—

(a)

ngonyulo leeNkosi kwiBhunga LeSizwe leeNkosi, neziqinisekiso nokunqunyanyiswa kwabagqatswa konyulo;

(b) isifungo kwi ofisi kufuneka senziwe ngamalungu eBhunga LeSizwe leNkosi neendibano zephondo;

(c)

ixesha lokuba se ofiSini lamalungu eBhunga LeSizwele eNkosi;

(d) ukuvuzwa, umhlala phantsi nezinye inzuzo zikaMongameli, uSekelaMongameli namalungu eBhunga LeSizwe leeNkosi;

(e)

inkqubo emayilandelwe kwiimbizo zeBhunga LeSizwe leeNkosi neendibano zephondo zeenkosi; kwaye

(f)

ukusekwa koonobhala beBhunga LeSizwe leeNkosi neendibano yephondo leeNkosi.

286 Imisebenzi yeBhunga leSizwe neendibano zephondo leeNkosi

(1) IBhunga LeSizwe leNkosi kwaye, ngaphakathi kwephondo, iBhunga LeSizwe leeNkosi linemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a) ukukhusela, ukukhuthaza nokuphuhlisa izithethe namasiko eZimbabwe;

(b) ukumela izimvo zeenkokheli zemveli nokugcina isidima nobume bamaziko emveli;

(c)

ukukhusela, ukukhuthaza nokuphakamisa izimvo zeenkokheli zemveli;

(d) ukunika ingqalelo yokumela ikhalazo ezenziwe kuyo zinkokheli zemveli;

(e)

ukuchaza nokunyanzelisa ngokulungileyo izimilo ezamkelekayo nokuphuhlisa amakhono obunkokheli;

(f)

ukuququzelela ukusonjululwa kwembambano phakathi kweenkosi zemveli ezichaphazelekayo.

(g) ukwenza nawuphina umsebenzi angawuphiwa okanye unyanzeliswe nguMthetho wasePalamente.

(2) UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka uqinisekise ukuba—

(a)

IBhunga LeSizwe leNkosi neendibano zamaphondo onke eeNkosi ayenelisa ukwenza imisebenzi yayo ngokukhululekileyo neyiyo; kwaye

(b) abantu abaqeshwe liBhunga LeSizwe leeNkosi neendibano zephondo leeNkosi benze imisebenzi yabo ngezazela kwaye bengathathi icala. 287 Isidima neKomiti yokuziphatha UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka ubonelele ngokuseka, ubulungu nenkqubo yeKomiti yeSidima seeNkosi, ukwenza imisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukuphuhlisa nokunyanzelisa isidima nokuziphatha okwamkelekileyo kwicala lenkokheli zemveli;

(b) ukusombulula iimbambano phakathi kweenkosi zemveli;

(c)

ukujongana nezikhalazo ezichasene neenkokheli zemveli. ISAHLUKO 16 Umhlaba Wezolimo

288 Ukuchazwa kwesahluko 16 Kwesisahluko umhlaba— “umhlaba wolimo” unentsingiselo enikwe licandelo 72 289 Imigaqo elawula umhlaba wezolimo Ukuze silungisise umonakalo owavela nababelungu ababesithathele iLizwe lethu, nokubuyisela umhlaba wethu kubanikazi bawo nokufikeleleka kobutyebi baseZimbabwe kubanikazi bawo, imiqathango emalunga nomhlaba wokulima kufuneka uqhutywe ngalemigaqo elandelayo—

(a) Umhlaba bubona butyebi bemveli sinabo

(b) Ngokwecandelo 72, wonke ummi wase Zimbabwe unelungelo lokufumana, ukusebenzisa, ukutshinstha, ukubolekisa, nokulima umhlaba kungakhathalekanga ngabala lakhe.

(c) Ukwahlulwa komhlaba kufuneka kube kulungile, kwaye kuvelise ulingano lweSini kwanezimvo zomphakathi.

(d) Ukulinywa komhlaba kufuneka kongeze isivuno notyalo-mali kumhlaba wezolimo.

(e) Ukusetyenziswa komhlaba wokulima kufuneka kukhuthaze lokutya, impilo entle nesondlo kudale nemisebenzi, kwaye kukhusele imvelo ukuze izizukulwane ezilandelayo nazo zifumane;

(f) Akukhomntu omakacinezeleke ukufumana isithuba sokusebenzisa umhlaba wezolimo. 290 Ukuqhubekeka kwamalungelo eSizwe kumhlaba wezolimo

(1) wonke umhlaba wezolimo—

(a)

ochazwe kwishedyuli 7 kuMgaqo-siseko omdala; okanye

(b) ngaphambi komhla wokukuqala, exelwe ngokwemiqathango yecandelo 16B(2)(a)(ii) okanye (iii) yomgaqo-siseko wakudala;

(2) nokuba kokuphi ukungangqinelani kwezinto nokuba zeziphi ezi—

(a)

kwisaziso esichazwe kwishedyuli 7 kulaMgaqo-siseko wakudala; okanye

(b) isaziso esimalunga nomhlaba wokulima esipapashwe ngokwemiqathango yecandelo 16B(2)(a)(ii) kuMgaqo-siseko omdala; kwaye nesiqinisekiso somhlaba esixela okanye esimelwe kukuxela, kwaneempazamo ezixelwe kwisaziso, asiphazamisi ukusebenza kwecandelwana (1) okanye ukungasebenzi kwesiqinisekiso somhlaba. 291 Ukuqhubekeka kwamalungelo okuphatha umhlaba wezolimo Ngokwemfuneko yalo Mgaqo-siseko, wonke umntu, ngaphanbi komhla wokukuqala, wokusebenzisa okanye ukuhlala emhlabeni, wokulima ngokubolekwa okanye ngezinye izivumelwano neSizwe ukuvunyelwa ukusebenzisa umhlaba ngaphambi okanye ngomhla wokukuqala, ngokwaloomboleko okanye ezinye izivumelwano. 292 Ukhuseleko lokusetyenziswa komhlaba wokulima ISizwe kufuneka sithathe amanyethelo alungileyo, kuquka namanyathelo asemthethweni, ukukhusela ukusetyenziswa komhlaba ngumntu wonke ofumane umhlaba ngokusemthethweni. 293 Ukupheliswa komhlaba wokulima siSizwe

(1) Rhulumente usenokwehlisa ixabiso lomhlaba, ngokutshintsha ubunikazi bomhlaba ngokwemboleko okanye ngokupha ilingelo lobunikazi baloomhlaba, kodwa ukupheliswa okunjalo kufanele kwenziwe ngokwemiqathango echazwe kwicandelo 289.

(2) uRhulumente akufanelanga anike umhlaba onye kubantu ababini kuquka nabantwana bakhe.

(3) uMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka uxela indlela yokunikwa komhlaba, kwaye uMthetho owenjenjalo kufuneka ube usebenza ngokwemiqathango exelwe kwi candelo 289. 294 Ukupheliswa kobumnikazi bomhlaba wokulima ngabanikazi okanye abahlali bawo Ngokwemida efakwe nguMthetho phezu komhlaba, umnikazi womhlaba wokulima unelungelo lokutshintsha okanye ukubolekisa ngelungelo lomhlaba wokulima.

295 Ukubhataleka komntu obekade engumnikazi womhlaba wokulima

(1) Nabani umntu ongummi wase Zimbabwe othathelwe umhlaba wakhe nguRhulumente ngaphambi komhla wokukuqala kufuneka abhatalwe nguRhulumente ngomhlaba lowo kungakhathalekile nokuba loluphi uphuhliso ebelwenziwe kuloomhlaba.

(2) Nokuba ngubani othathelwe umhlaba nguRhulumente welinye iLizwe ngaphambi komhla wokukuqala kwaye onamalungelo eprophathi akhuselwe sisivumelwano sikaRhulumente waseZimbabwe, kufuneka abhatalwe nguRhulumente ebhatalelwa umhlaba wakhe lowo okanye nokuba loluphi uphuhliso ebelwenzile kuloomhlaba njengokwesivumelwano sakhe noRhulumente

(3) Nakuba ngubani, ngaphandle komntu oxelwe kwicandelwana (1) okanye

(2), onomhlaba othathwe nguRhulumente ngaphambi komhla wokukuqala kufuneka abhatalwe nguRhulumente ebhatalelwa uphuhliso alwenzileyo kuloomhlaba ngexesha othathwe ngalo umhlaba.

(4) Imbatalo ebhatalwa ngokwe candelwana (1), (2) nelesi (3) kufuneka yenziwe ngokwemiqathango yoMthetho wasePalamente. 296 Ukusekwa nokwakhiwa kweKhomishini yomhlaba yaseZimbabwe

(1) kukho ikhomishonshini emayiobizwe ngokuba yoKhomishini yoMhlaba yaseZimbabwe—

(a)

enosihlalo nomsekeli kasihlalo;

(b) kwanamalungu amabini abuncinaneni angodlulisi isixhenkxe ebukhulwini; abenyulwe nguMongameli

(2) Amalungu eKhomishini yoMhlaba eZimbabwe kufuneka—

(a)

ikhethwe ngenxa yesidima , nokusebenza okuhle kwabo, nolwazi lokuvisisisa, nokuziphatha kwabo ekulawuleni umhlaba .

(b) babonise iintlobo ngentlobobantu base Zimbabwe ikakhulu imifuno zamaphondo nokulingana kwesini.

(3) Isahluko 237 sisebenza ukukhutshwa kwamalungu ekhomishini yomhlaba yase Zimbabwe eofiSini njengokwendlela emakukhuthswe ngayo ikhomishini ezimeleyo esekwe sisahluko 12. 297 Imisebenzi yeKhomishini yoMhlaba yaseZimbabwe

(1) Ikhomishini yomhlaba yase Zimbabwe isebenza—

(a)

ukulawulwa komhlaba kaRhulumente wokulima okulungileyo nokungenamkhethe;

(b) ukuphicotha umhlaba wezolimo ngamaxesha athile.

(c)

ukuncoma kurhulumente malunga.

(i) ukuthathwa komhlaba onomnikazi ukuze usetyenziswe siSizwe;

(ii) ukufikeleleka komhlaba wokulima ngokulinganayo, ikakhulu— A.

ngokushenxisa zonke iintlobo zocalucalulo, ikakhulu ucalulo lweSini;

B.

ukunyanzelisa nokuba ngowuphi uMthetho othintela inani lomhlaba omawunikwe umntu okanye indlu;

(iii) ukusetyenziswa komhlaba nobungakanani bawo omawufunyanwe ngumntu.

(iv) ukululazwa kokutshintshwa kobumnikazi bomhlaba

(v) iindlela zokusetyenziswa komhlaba.

(vi) ukubhataleka okulungileyo ngokusemthethweni womhlaba wokulima nophuhliso olwenziweyo

(vii) ukwabiwa komhlaba wokulima;

(d) ukuphanda nokulungisa izikhalazo ezimalunga nokulawulwa kokwabiwa komhlaba wokulima.

(2) IKhomishini yoMhlaba yaseZimbabwe, ngokwemvumo kaMphathiswa ojongene nomhlaba isenokwenza imigaqo ngokwesizatho nokuba sesiphi esixelwe kwicandelwana (1).

(3) iKhomishini yoMhlaba yaseZimbabwe kufuneka yenze imisebenzi yayo ngokwemigaqo nokuba yeyiphi ebhalwe nguMphathiswa ojongene nomhlaba

(4) ekwenzeni imisebenzi yayo iKhomishini yoMhlaba yaseZimbabwe kufuneka ikhokhelwe yimiqathango exelwe kwicandelo 289.

(5) uRhulumente kwanamziko onke karhulumente kwaneearhente zonke zikarhulumente kumanqanaba onke, ngokusemthethweni, kufuneka zincedise iKhomishini yoMhlaba yaseZimbabwe ukusebenza kwanokukhusela ukuzimela, ukungabinacala nemfezeko yayo.

(6) urhulumente kufuneka enze izibonelelo, ngokusebenzisa uMthetho nezinye iindlela ezilungileyo ukuqinisekisa ukuba—

(a)

iKhomishini yoMhlaba yaseZimbabwe yenza imisebenzi yayo ngokufanelekileyo izimele;

(b) abantu abaqashwe yiKomishini yoMhlaba yaseZimbabwe benze imisebenzi yabo ngokungenamkhethe nangokulungileyo. ISAHLUKO 17 Imali INXALENYE 1 Ulawulo Lwemali 298 Imigaqo yolawulo lwemali kawonke-wonke

(1) Imigaqo elandelayo kufuneka isetyenziswe ukukhokhela imiba emalunga nemali eZimbabwe—

(a)

Kufuneka kubekho inyaniso noxanduva kwimicimbi yemali;

(b) Isixokelelwano semali kawonke-wonke kufuneka sibhekiswe ekuphuhliseni iSizwe, ikakhulu—

(i) Umthwalo werhafu kufuneka wabiwe ngokulungileyo;

(ii) Ingeniso eliqokelelwe eSizweni kufuneka labiwe ngokulinganayo phakathi kwamanqanaba karhulumente osembindini, nowephondo nowasekuhlaleni;

(iii) Kwaye, inkcitho-mali kufuneka ibhekiswe ekuphuhliseni iZimbabwe, kwaye isibonelelo esikhethekileyo kufuneka senzelwe amaqela neendawo ezithethiweyo;

(c)

Imithwalo neenzuzo zokusetyenziswa kobutyebi kufuneka zabiwe ngokulinganayo phakathi kwezizukulwana ziphilayo nezizayo;

(d) Iimali zikawonke-wonke kufuneka zisetyenziswe ngokucacisekileyo, ngokunengqiqo, ngokongekileyo nangempumelelo;

(e)

Ukulawulwa kwemali kufuneka kubenoxanduva, kwaye iingxelo zemali kufuneka zicace;

(f)

Kwaye ukubolekwa kwemali neentengiselwano eziquka ityala leSizwe kufuneka zenziwe ngokucacileyo nangokwe nzuzo yeZimbabwe.

(2) Akukho rhafu esenokuwiswa ngaphandle ngaphantsi kwegunya elikhankanyiweyo laloMgaqo-Siseko okanye uMthethowePalamente. 299 Ukongamela kwePalamente ezimalini zeLizwe kuye nenkcitho-mali

(1) IPalamente kufuneka ibe ijonge kwaye yongameleinkcitho-mali eyenziwa siSizwe neeKhomishini zonke namaziko nama-arhente karhulumente kumabanga onke, kuquka neebhodi zoMthetho, namaqumrhu alawulwa nguRhulumente, amabhunga ephondo nedolophu kwanamagunya asekuhlaleni, ukwenzela ukuqinisekisa ukuba—

(a)

zonke iimali zichaziwe;

(b) yonke inkcitho yenziwe kakuhle;

(c)

nokuba yeyiphi imida kwa neemeko zolwabiwo ziqwalaselwe;

(2) uMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uvele nendlela ematyetyenziswe yiPalamente ukuqwalasela nokulawula inkcitho-mali exelwe kwicandelwana (1). 300 Imilinganiselo yoboleko lukaRhulumente, amatyala kunye neziqinisekiso zikaRhulumente

(1) UMthethowePalamente kufuneka wenze imilinganiselo malunga—

(a)

nemboleko eyenziwa siSizwe;

(b) netyala lika wonke-wonke; kwa

(c)

namatyala kunye neembopheleleko zokuhlawulwa okanye ukuhlawulwa kwayo kuqinisekiswa nguRhulumente;

kwaye loomilinganiselo akufuneki itsitywe kungekho gunya elisuka kwindibano yeSizwe.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka umisele imingqaliselo kunye neemeko apho uRhulumente angaqinisekisa khona imali.

(3) Kwiintsuku ezingamashumi asithandathu emva kokuba uRhulumente enze isigqibo semboleko okanye isiqinisekiso, uMphathiswa onoxanduva lwezemali kufuneka enze kuba imigaqo yayo ipapashwe kwi Gazethi.

(4) Mphathiswa wezemali—

(a)

Ebuncinaneni kabini ngonyaka, axelele iPalamentemalunga nokusebenza(i) kwamatyala enziwe siSizwe;

(ii) kwanamatyala aqinisekiswe siSizwe;

(b) Ngaxeshalinye nelokuqikelelwa kwengxowa-mali nekcitho engeniswa phambi kweNdibano yeSizwe njengokwecandelo 305, kwiPalamenteinkcazo epheleleyo yeSizwe saseZimbabwe. 301 Ulwabiwo lweengeniso phakathi kwamaphondo karhulumente kunye nawasekuhlaleni

(1) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uveze oku—

(a)

ukwabiwa kweemali ngendlela elungileyo phakathi kwamabhunga ephondo nawamaphondo needolophu; kwa;

(b) nokunye ukwabiwa emakwenziwe kumagunya asemaphondweni nawasekuhlalenineemeko zonke ekwabelwane ngazo.

(2) UMthetho oxelwe kwicandelwana (1) kufuneka uqaphele, ngaphezu kwazo zonke izinto—

(a)

imfuneko zeSizwe;

(b) okuba sesiphi isibonelelo emasenziwa malunga netyala leSizwe namanye amaqhina eSizwe;

(c)

nawaphi na amalungiselelo okufuneka enziwe ngokubhekiselele kumatyala ezwe nezinye izibophelelo zika wonke wonke;

(d) imfuneko yokubonelela ngeenkonzo ezisisiseko, kuquka nezemfundo kwanezempilo, amanzi, iindlela, nombane kwiindawo ezibaliweyo;

(e)

amandla apheleleyo wokusebenza kakuhle kwamabhungu akumaphondo needolophu kunye namagosa asekuhlaleni;

(f)

kuphuhliswa kwanezinye iimfuneko kumabhunga amaphondo needolophu; kwa

(g) ukungalingani kwezithuba zoqoqsho ngaphakathi kwamaphondo.

(3) Akufuneki ibe ngaphantsi kweephesenti ezintlanu ingxowa-mali yongeniso eqokelelwe enyakeni wemali emayabelwe amaphondo namagunya asekuhlaleni njengesabelo sabo soonyaka. INXALENYE 2 Ingxowa-Mali Engenisiweyo Yadityaniswa 302 INgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo Kukho imali engeniswayo idityaniswe okuyiyo emayisetyenziswe ukubhatala zonke iirhafu, nemalimboleko nezinye zonke iimali zikaRhulumente, kungakhathalekile ukuba zisuke phi, ngaphandle xa uMthethowePalamente—

(a)

vumela kwaye ufuna ukuba zisetyenziswe kweminye imisebenzi esekwe ngokwenjongo ethile; okanye

(b) uvumela ukuba igosa elinikwe ezimali lizithathe lizigcine, okanye ligcine inxalenye yayo ukwenzela ukuba lihlangabezane neendleko zalo. 303 Ukutsalwa kwemali kwi Ngxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo

(1) Akukho mali esenokutsalwa kwiNgxowamali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo ngaphandle kokuba itsalelwa ukuyosebenzai imisenezi evunyelwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko okanye uMthethowePalamente.

(2) Imali etsalwe kwiNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo kufunekwa ibhatalwe kuyenamntu ofanelekileyo.

(3) UMthethowePalamente kufuneka uchaze indlela—

(a)

zokutsalwa kwemali kwiNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo neezinye iimali zikawonke-wonke; kwa

(b) nokuba imali le ekwi Ngxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo nezinye iimali zingagcinwa kwaye zityalwe kanjani ukuba zizale. 304 Iindleko eziphezu kweNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo

(1) Onke amatyala eSizwe kufuneka iindleko zawo zibhatalwe yiNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisisweyo.

(2) Iindleko ezidalwa kukuqokelelwa nokusetyenziswa kwaleNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso kufuneka zibhatalwe yiyo leNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo.

(3) Ngenxeni yenjongo yecandelwana (1)— “amatyala” aquka neemali ezizalayo, neendleko ezinganyukiyo, ukubhatalwa kwamatyala kwaneemali zonke ezihamba kunye nemali-mboleko ekuqinisekiseni kwengxowa-mali yongeniso edityanisisweyo kwanezinye iindleko ezibangelwe yimali mboleko leyo. INXALENYE 3 Ukuvunyelwa Kokusetyenziswa Kwemali Esuka Kwingxowa-Mali Yongeniso eDityanisiweyo 305 Izabelo ezisuka kwingxowa-mali yongeniso

(1) Qho ngonyaka uMphathiswa wezemali kufuneka axelele iNdibano yeSizwe ingxelo yengxowa-mali eqikelwayo kwanendleko zikarhulumente ezonyaka olandelayo.

(2) Iziqikelelo zemali yongeniso kufuneka zingeniswe kwiNdibano yeSizwe ngokwe candelwana (1) ngomhla indibano eyakuthi ihlale ngawo iintsuku ezingamashumi amathathu zingekapheli ngemva kokuba unyaka wosetyenziso lwemali uqalile, kokwa ke ukuba ipalamente isachithiwe ngehloba elenza kube nzima ukuqikelela phambi kwePalamente ngelooxesha, kufuneka isiwe kwiNdibano yeSizwe iintsuku ezingamashumi amathatha zingekapheli ngemva kokuhlala kwayo kokukuqala ngemva kokuchithwa.

(3) Iziqikelelo ezahlukileyo kufuneka zinikwe malunga nemali engenisiweyo neyeendleko, zinikwa;

(a)

iKhomishini nganye esekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko;

(b) i-ofisi kaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele;

(c)

igunya leSizwe lokuXoxiswa kwamaTyala;

(d) iBhunga lamaKhosi; kwa

(e)

namanye amaziko axelwe nguMthetho wePalamente.

(4) Ekubeni iNdibano yeSizwe iye yavuma isiqikelelo seendleko yonyakaka wemali, kungabalwa indleko ezinikwe iNgxowa-mali nguloMgaqo-Siseko okanye uMthetho wePalamente, uMphathiswa wezemali kufuneka abangele ukuba iBhili yaziwe kwiNdibano yeSizwe kwaye looBhili kufuneka—

(a) ichaze imali emayikhutshwe yiNgxowa-mali ukuhlangabezana neendleko ezo zonke ezivunyiweyo; kwaye.

(b) yaabe imali ngokwendlela exelwe kwisiqikelelo, ngaphantsi kweevoti ezohlukeneyo ezeentloko zendleko ezahlukeneyo ezivunyiweyo.

(5) Ukuba imali exelwe nguMthetho woKwaba imali ayoneli okanye iindleko iyafuneka ngesizathu sokuba akukho mali ekhutshiweyo, uMphathiswa wezemali kufuneka abangele ukuba esinye isiqikelelo sisiwe kwiNdibano yeSizwe kwaye ukuba indibano iye yasivuma isiqikeleleo uMphathiswa wezemali kufuneka abangele ukuba iBhili yesiqikelelo isiwe kwiNdibano yeSizwe esiyokuxela imali ekufanele ikhutshwe kwiNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo. 306 Ukuvunyelwa kwendleko ngaphambi kokuba imali yabiwe

(1) UMthetho wePalamente usenokuvumela ukuba oMongameli agunyazise ukutsalwa kwemali kwiNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo ukuhlangabezana nendleko ebingalindelekanga kwaye engenaso nesibonelelo okanye uMthetho ovumela ukutsalwa oko; kodwa

(a)

uMthetho wePalamente lowo akufuneki uvumele ukutsalwa kemali engaba ngaphezu kwephesenti enye okanye enye enenxalenye yemali eqikelelwe kuMthetho wokwaBiwa owegqithileyo.

(b) yonke imali etsalwe ngaphantsi koMthetho kufuneka ibalwe kwiziqikelelo ezongeziweyo okanye iindleko ezongeziweyo isiwe phambi kweNdibano yeSizwe ngokungxamisekileyo, kwaye ukuba iNdibano isivumile isiqikelelo eso, imali iyakuvela kwiNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo ngoMthetho wesiqikelelo esongeziweyo.

(2) Ukuba uMthetho wesaBiwo esongeziweyo uye waqala ukusebenza ekuqaleni konyaka wezemali, uMthetho wePalamente usenokuvumela uMongameli ukuba avumele ukuba imali itsalwe ukuze ihlangabezane neendleko zeenyanga ezine zokukuqala konyaka wokusetyenziswa kwemali, kodwa

(a)

uMthethowePalamente lowo akufunekanga uvumele ukutsalwa kwemali engaba sisinye ebuthathwini yesiqikelelo sonyaka wemali owegqithileyo;

(b) nokuba yeyiphi imali etsalwe ngaphantsi koMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka ibhalwe kuMthetho wokwaBiwa waloonyaka kuthethwa ngawo, ngaphantsi kweevoti ezahlukeneyo kwanentloko zeendleko ezahlukeneyo.

(3) Ukuba iPalamente iye yachithwa isisbonelelo semali singekenziwa, uMthethowePalamente usenokuvumela uMongameli ukuba avumele ukutsalwa kwemali kwiNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo ukwenzela ufumana imali yokuhlangabezana neezondleko kude kwedlule iinyanga ezintathu ngemva kokuba iNdibano yeSizwe ihlale okokukuqala ngemva kokuchitheka kwayo, kodwa yonke imali etsaliweyo kufuneka ibhalwe kuMthetho wokwaBiwa kwemali ngaphantsi kweevoti ezahlukeneyo kwiintloko zendleko ezahlukeneyo. 307 Iiindleko ezingavumelekanga

(1) Ukuba kuye kwaqapheleka ukuba kuye kwasebenza imali eninzi ukuhlangabezana neendleko ebezimalunga nayo loomali ngokwale nxalenye, okanye imali iye yasetyenziswa kwizinto ebezingabalelwanga kuyo njengokwalenxalenye, uMphathiswa wezemali kufuneka aveze iBhili kwiNdibano yeSizwe ecela imvumo yendleko engavunywanga.

(2) IBhili exelwe kwicandelwana (1) kufuneka isiwe kwiNdibano yeSizwe ngokungxamisekileyo kwaye ingegqithisi iintsuku ezisithandathu emva kokuba indleko engavunywanga ikhutshiwe.

INXALENYE 4 Ukugcinwa Kakuhle Kweemali Zikawonke-Wonke Kwane Mpahla Yabo 308 Imisebenzi yobumnini beemali zikawonke-wonke kwanempahla

(1) Kwelicandelo— “iimali zika wonke-wonke” ziquka nemali ezigcinwa, kunye, nezeSizwe okanye nokuba leliphi iziko likarhulumente kuquka namanqanaba karhulumente awephondo nawasekuhlaleni, kwaneebhodi zoMthetho kwanamaziko alawulwa ngurhulumente. “impahla kawonke-wonke” ichaza nokuba yeyiphi impahla esezandleni zeSizwe, okanye nokuba leliphi iziko likarhulumemnte kuquka namanqanamba ephondo kwanawasekuhlaleni akarhulumente kwaneebhodi zoMthetho kwanamaziko alawulwa ngurhulumente.

(2) Luxanduva lukabai nobani ukukhusela iidleko zikawonke-wonke ukuqiniseka ukuba ezomali zisetyenziswa ngokufanelekileyo nangokusemthethweni nangokwamanani alinganiselwe nguMthetho.

(3) Luxanduva lukabani nabani ogcine okanye olawula impahla ukuba loompahla iselugcinweni olufanelekileyo nokuqinisekisa ukuba ayimoshakali kwaye ayisetyenziswa kakubi.

(4) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uvele neendlela emazikhawlezise ukubona xakuthe kwakho ulwaphulo lwamacandelwana (2) no (3) kwanokugwetywa kwabo bathe bafumaneka besophula lamacandelo kwaye ukuba kusenokwenzeka kwaye nangokufanelekileyo, impahla okanye imali leyo isetyenziswe kakubi ithathwe kuloomntu. NXALENYE 5 Umphicothi-Ncwadi Jikelele 309 UMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele nemisebenzi yakhe

(1) kufuneka kubekho Umbali-J-ikelele, oneofisi efikelelekayo kuwonke-wonke kodwa nogama engenanto yokwenza kwiNkonzo zika Wonke.

(2) Iimisebenzi kaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele yile—

(a)

ukubala iimali, iindlela zemali, nokuphathwa kweemali kumacandelo onke, amaziko namanqanaba karhulumente kumabhunga ephondo nawedolophu kwanamagunya asekuhlaleni onke.

(b) ngokomyalelo kaRhulumente, ukubala ukusetyenziswa kwemali kweebhodi zoMthetho okanye amaqumrhu alawulwa ngurhulumente;

(c)

ukupha imiyalelo yokulungisisa iziphoso zokuphathwa kweemali nempahla kawonke-wonke;

(d) ukwenza nokuba ngowuphi umsebenzi awunikwe nguMthetho wePalamente. 3. Amagosa kawonke-wonke kufuneka basebenze bengqinelana nemiyalelo abayinikwe nguMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele njengokwecandelo (2)(c).

310 Ukuqeshwa kukaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele

(1) Mphicothi-ncwadi jikelele utyunjwa nguMongameli ngemvumo yePalamente.

(2) Mphicothi-ncwadi jikelele kufuneka ibe ngummi waseZImbabwe onyulwe ngenxeni yobuhlakani bakhe, kwaye kufuneka ibe ngumntu oqeqeshwe wakulungela ukuba nguMbali enze loomsebenzi iminyaka elishumi ebuncinaneni.

(3) Ithemu kaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele iyiminyaka engogqithi iminyaka esithandathu kwaye umntu owakhe wasebenza lomsebenzi ithemu nokuba inye kunyuka akavumelekanga ukuphinde asebenze lomsebenzi, kude iminyaka yokusebenza kwakhe ibe lishumi elinambini.

(4) Engekangeni e-Ofisisni, uMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele kufuneka enze isifungo sokunyaniseka nese-ofisi phambi kuka Mongameli, okanye umntu othunywe nguMongameli, ngokwendlela exelwe kwishedyuli yesithathu. 311 Ukuzimela kukaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele Ekwenzeni imisebenzi yakhe uMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele uzimele ulawulwa nguMthetho kuphela. 312 Umvuzo kaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele

(1) UMthethowePalamente kufuneka uveze imbatalo neembonelelo zikaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele kwaye zivunywe nguMongameli ngemva kokubonisana noMphathiswa wezemali.

(2) Imali yokubhatala uJbali-jikelele isuka kwiNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo kwaye akufuneki ukuba yehliswe ngexesha lakhe ese-ofisini. 313 Ukukurhoxiswa kukaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele

(1) UMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele angarhoxiswa e-ofiSini kuphela xana—

(a)

engakwazi ukwenza imisebenzi yese-ofiSini yakhe ngenxeni yokungaphili engqondweni okanye emzimbeni;

(b) esoyisakala kwaphela ukwenza umsebenzi wakhe; okanye

(c)

engaziphathi ngendlela eyiyo .

(2) Ukuba uMphathiswa wezemali, engqinelene nekomiti ephatha iimali zikawonke-wonke, uye waxela kuMongameli ukuba umba wokukhutshwa kukaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele e-ofiSini kufuna ukuphandwa, uMongameli kufuneka aqeshe iqela emaliphandisise ngaloomba.

(3) Iqela elithe laqeshwa njengomyalelo wecandelwana (2) kufuneka libe nabantu noba bathathu ebuncinaneni abaqeshwe nguMongameli, kwaye

(a)

ebuncinaneni omnye wesisithathu funeka ibe ngumntu ngumntu owakhe wangumgwebi; kwaye

(b) ebuncinaneni omnye wabo funeka abe ebenyulwe kuluhlu lwabantu abathathu abanyulwe liziko elisekwe nguMthetho ukuze limele izibalo zikawonke-wonke eZimbabwe.

(4) Iziko elixelwe kwicandelwana (3)(b) kufuneka linyule uluhlu elixelwe kwelicandelwana kuphela xa lithunywe ukwenzenjalo nguMongameli.

(5) Iqela eliqashwe ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (20 kufuneka ligxilise kumba wokususwa kukaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele e-ofiSini, lisakugqiba, lise ingxelo

kuMongameli ebonisa ukuba ukuba uMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele kufanele akhuthswe; kwaye ukuba isigqibo sithi makakhutshwe, uMongameli kufuneka amsuse uMphicothincwadi jikelele e-ofiSini.

(6) Iqela eliqeshwe ngaphantsi kwecandelwana (2) linamalungelo namandla alinganayo naweekhomishini ngaphantsi koMthetho weKhomishini yoPhando (isahluko 10:07) okanye nokuba ngowuphi uMthetho osenokumela oko. 314 Abancedisi bakaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uveze indlela yokuqeshwa kwebhodi emayiqashe abancedisi bakaMphicothi-ncwadi jikelele ekwenzeni imisebenzi yakhe kwaye kufuneka uphinde uveze—

(a)

Iimfuneko zokulungela lomsebenzi;

(b) imiqathango yokusebenza, ukuziphatha nembeko yabo;

(c)

ukuzimela, ukungabinamkhethe nokuzimesela kwabobantu kulomsebenzi; kwa

(d) nemmo, ubuhle namadla nendlela yokusebenza kweofisi kaMphicothincwadi jikelele. INXALENYE 6. Ngokubanzekileyo 315 Iizibalo nezinye izivumelwano zika rhulumente

(1) UMthetho wasePalamente kufuneka uvele nemigaqo yokubalwa kwempahla nemisebenzi yeSizwe namaziko onke karhulumente kumabanga onke, ngendlela yokuba ukubalwa oko kwenzeke ngendlela ecacileyo, engenamkhethe nenyanisekileyo.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uze nendlela yothethwano phakathi kwezivumelwano zeSizwe ezilandelayo—

(a)

izivumelwano zosetyenziswano ezidibeneyo

(b) izivumelwano zosetyenziswano zokwakha izakhiwo

(c)

izivumelwano zosetyenziswano zamalungelo okurhumba amatye aligugu namanye amalungelo anjalo;

kufuneka zicace kwaye zinyaniseke. 316 Ukulawulwa kweebhodi zoMthetho UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uze neendlela ezilungileyo nezisebenzayo zokusebenza kweebhodi zoMthetho kwaye, ingakumbi, eziqinisekisa ukuba amagosa aziinkokheli asebenza ixesha elimiswe yimilinganiselo yexesha lokusebenza kwabo elinokuvuselelwa ngokuxhomekeke kwintsebenzo yabo abobantu. 317 IBhanki enguVimba yaseZimbabwe

(1) Kukho iBhanki enkulu kunazo zonke, ebizwa ngokuba yiBhanki enguVimba yaseZimbabwe, injongo yayo—

(a)

kukulawula nokuhlela ukusetyenziswa kwemali;

(b) ukukhusela imali yaseZimbabwe ukuze iphuhlise kwaye ikhulise uqoqosho lwaseZimbabwe; kwa

(c)

2013

nokudala kwanokwenza imigaqo yosetyenziswa kwemali.

(2) UMthetho wePalamente kufuneka uze nemmo yokulawulwa kweBhanki enguVimba yaseZimbabwe kwaye inike eminye imisebenzi emayenziwe yileBhanki. ISAHLUKO 18 Izibonelo Ezibanzi Nezongeziweyo INXALENYE 1 Iziboneleo Ezibanzi Njengokweekhomishini 318 Ukusetyenziswa kwenxalenye 1 Le inxalenye isetyenziswa ziiKhomishini ezisekwe ngulo Mgaqo-Siseko, kunye namalungu onke eziKhomishini. 319 IiKhomishini emazibe ziibhodi zamaqumrhu IiKhomishini zingumzimba wamaqumrhu alandelelanayo alungele ukumangala nokumangalelwa ngamagama azo. 320 Ubulungu kwiiKhomishini nemiqathango yokusebenza kwamalunguayo

(1) Ngaphandleni kwendlela exelwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko, onke amalungu eKhomishini, aqeshelwe ukusebenza ithemu yeminyaka esisihlanu esenokuvuselelwa kube kanye kuphela.

(2) Amalungu eeKhomishini, angengawo la;

(a)

amalungu eeKhomishini ezizimeleyo

(b) inkonzo yeKhomishini yokuXoxiswa kwamaTyala;

(c)

iKhomishini yokulwisana noRhwaphilizo; kwa

(d) neKhomishini yokwabiwa komhlaba weLizwe; ezisebenza ngendlela evunyelwe nguMongameli.

(3) Ngokubhekiselele kuloMgaqo-Siseko, amalungu ePalamente namalungu amabhunga namalungu amabhunga ephondo nedolophu, amagunya asekuhlaleni, namaqumrhu alawulwa nguRhulumente awakulungelanga ukuba ngamalungu eKhomishini.

(4) Ukuba kuye iKhomishini yanosihlalo no sekela, kufanele ibe ngabantu bezini ezahlukeneyo.

(5) Ngaphambi kokuqalisa umsebenzi, amalungu eeKhomishini kufuneka afunge phambi kukaMongameli, okanye umntu othunywe nguye uMongameli, besenza izifungo zokunyaniseka nezomsebenzi njengokuxelwe kwishedyuli yesi thathu.

(6) Amalungu eeKhomishini anyanzelekile kwimivuzo, izibonelelo, neezinye iinzuzo ezisenoku xelwa nguMthetho wePalamente, kwaye loomivuzo akufanelanga irhoxiswe kwixesha lokusebenza kwelungu.

(7) Imivuzo neenzuzo zamalungu eeKhomishini zibhatalwa yi Ngxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo.

321 Imisebenzi neeNkqubo zeKhomishini

(1) UMthetho wePalamente usenokunika iKhomishini imisebenzi kwaye isenokulawula indlela iKhomishini emayisebenze ngayo, ukuba ngaba ukuzimela kweKhomishini akuchaphazelekanga.

(2) UMthetho wasePalamente oxelwe kwicandelwana (1) ayayivumela iKhomishini ukuba yenze imisebenzi yayo, kodwa iKhomishini akufunekanga isebenzise amandla ayo ukuqesha, okanye ukubonisa okanye ukucebisa malunga, nomsebenzi nokuba ngowuphi osekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko.

(3) Ngokubhekiselele kuloMgaqo-Siseko, nokuba sesiphi isigqibo seKhomishini sifunisa isivumelwano soninzi lwamalungu eKhomishini ayabe ekhoyo kweloxesha lokwenza isigqibo eso.

(4) UMthetho we Palamente usenokuvela nemiqathango emayisetyenziswe yiKhomishini, nangoluphi na uhlobo engabe ingaveliselwanga ukusetyenziswa ngalo iKhomishini ngokwayo, kodwa ke loomiqathango kufuneka ibonise ulingano kwaye ibalulekise ukungabinamfihlo ekwenzeni imisebenzi yeKhomishini. 322 Ukubhataleka kwee Khomishini IPalamente kufuneka iqinisekise ukuba kuhlala kukho imali eyoneleyo eyokubhatalela iiKhomishini ukuze zenze imisebenzi yazo ngokufanelekileyo. 323 IiKhomishini kufuneka zixele qho ngonyaka kwiPalamente.

(1) Zonke iiKhomishini kufuneka zixele ingxelo yazo yonyaka ePalamente, zenjenjalo zisebenzisana noMphathiswa onoxanduva koko, lengxelo yonyaka funeka ichaze ngokugcweleyo imisebenzi eyenzileyo, ngxelo leyo ekufuneka ibe inikiwe inyanga yoKwindla kunyaka olandelayo ingekapheli.

(2) UMthethowePalamente usenokufunisa ukuba iKhomishini inike ezinye iingxelo ngaphezu kwezo zentsebenzo yonyaka njengoko kuxelwe kwicandelwana

(1), kwaye kufuneka ibe ixeliwe indlela emayingeniswe ngayo lengxelo. INXALENYE 2 NGOKUBANZEKILEYO 324 Ukusebenza okukhutheleyo noxanduva malunga noMgaqo-siseko Yonke imisebenzi kufuneka yenziwe ngokukhuthala nangokungxamisekilyo. 325 Ukubhataleka kweeBhodi ezimalunga noMgaqo-Siseko namanye amaziko

(1) URhulumente kufuneka aqinisekise ukuba iimbatalo ezoneleyo ziyafumaneka—

(a)

kwiiKhomishini namanye amaziko asekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko, ukuze asebenze ngokufanelekileyo;

(b) kwiPalamente, ukuqinisekisa ukuba yona neekomiti zihlangabezana kangangoko; kwa

(c)

namanye amaziko eSizwe nakaRhulumente, ukuze akwazi ukufezekisa imisebenzi exelwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko.

(2) IiKhomishini namanye amaziko asekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko kufanele anikwe umtya owaneleyo owokuza neengcinga kwikomiti yePalamente njengoko iimbatalo yabo iyakusoloko yabelwe kunyaka ngamnye. 326 UMthetho wesintu kuizizwe-ngezizzwe

(1) UMthetho wesiNtu uyinxalenye yoMthetho weSizwe saseZimbabwe, ngaphandle kokuba uyaphikisana naloMgaqo-Siseko okanye uMthetho wasePalamente.

(2) Ekuchazeni uMthetho, iinkudla zonke zamatyala, kufuneka zamkele nokuba loluphi uchazo Mthetho olungqinelana noMthetho wesintu wezizwe ngezizwe osenokusebenza eZimbabwe, kungqinelana neenkcazo ezingabe zingangqinelanagna naloo Mthetho 327 Iindibano, imiqophiso neezivumelwano zamazwe ngamazwe

(1) Kwelicandelo— “Imibutho yamazwe-ngamazwe” ichaza imibutho enobulungu bamazwe azimeleyo angaba mabini nangaphezulu okanye umbutho omele amazwe azimelyo angaba mabini nangaphezulu. “Imiqophiso yamazwe ngamazwe” ixela indibano, imiqophiso okanye izivumelwano ezibhaliweyo zeLizwe, uRhulumente okanye umbutho weLizwe elingabalinye nangaphezulu.

(2) Umqophiso wamazwe ngamazwe owenziwe nguMongameli okanye ngaphantsi kwegunya likaMongameli—

(a)

awunakuyibophelela iZimbabwe ngaphandle kokuba uvunyelwe yiPalamente; kwaye

(b) awuyonxalenye yoMthetho waseZimbabwe ngaphandle kokuba uye wafakwa emiThethweni yeLizwe nguMthetho we Palamente.

(3) Isivumelwano esingengomqophiso wamazwe kodwa—

(a)

sibe senziwe nguMongameliokanye ngaphantsi kwegunya lakhe uMongameli phakathi kwakhe neminye imibutho yamanye amazwe;

(b) esifunisa ukukhutshwa kwemali yi Zimbabwe; asinakuyibophelela iZimbabwe ngaphandle side sivunyelwe yiPalamente.

(4) UMthetho wePalamente usenokuthi amacandelwana (2) no (3)—

(a)

angasetyenziswa nokokuba kukowuphi umqophiso wamaZwe nakweliphi uhlobo; okanye

(b) usebenza ngezilungiso ezimalunga nomqophiso wamazwe nokuba ngowuphi nokuba leliphi uhlobo.

(5) IPalamente isenokusombulula ibhengeze ukuthi nokuba ngowuphi umqophiso wamazwe okanye uhlobo lwemiqophiso awunakufumana mvume kwicandelwana (2), kodwa isisombululo esilelohlobo asisebenzi kwimiqophiso efunisa—

(a)

imali esuka kwiNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo; okanye

(b) utshintsho loMthetho waseZimbabwe.

(6) Xa kuhlalutywa uMthetho, iinkundla zoMthetho kufuneka zisebenzise uhlalutyo olufanelekileyo loMthetho olungqinelana neendibano, imiqophiso nezivumelwano zamazwe-ngamazwe emazithotyelwe yiZimbabwe.

328 UkuLungiswa koMgaqo-Siseko

(1) Kwelicandelo— “iBhili yoMgaqo-Siseko” ichaza iBhilielungisa loMgaqo-Siseko; “izibonelelo zemilinganiselo” zichaza izibonelelo zaloMgaqo-Siseko eziyimilinganiselo kwixesha elingahlalwa ngumntukwi ofisi kawonkewonke.

(2) UMthethowePalamente omawulungise loMgaqo-Siseko kufuneka wenzenjalo ngokwendlela yemiqathango.

(3) IBhili yoMgaqo-Sisekoayinakunikelwa kwi Senethi, iNdibano yeSizwe ngokwemiqathango yecandelo 131 ngaphandleni kokuba uSithethi uye wanika isaziso engekoneli amalanga angaphezu kwamashumi alithoba kwiGazethi yemiqathango eqondileyo yeBhili.

(4) USithethi akugqiba ukunikela isaziso seBhili yoMgaqo-Siseko ngokwemiqathango yecandelwana (3), iPalamente kufuneka imeme amalungu kawonke-wonke ukuze avele nezimvo malunga neBhili entsha leyo kwiintlanganiso zikawonke-wonke nangokusebenzisa izingeniso ezibhaliweyo, kwaye kufanele ibize iintlanganiso zomntu wonke kwaye iququzelele ukuba uwonke-wonke akwazi ukwenzenjalo.

(5) IBhili yoMgaqo-Siseko isenokuvunyelwa, ziivoti ezingaba sisibini esithathwini zamalungu eNdlu nganye kwiNdibano yeSizwe neSenathe.

(6) Xa kuye kwenzeka ukuba iBhili yoMgaqo-Siseko ifune ukulungisa nokuba sesiphi sezibonelelo zesahluko 4 okanye 16—

(a)

Kungekagqithi iinyanga ezintathu ngemva kokukwamkelwa yiNdibano yeSizwe neSenethi ngokungqinelana necandelwlana (5), kufuneka ingeniswe kwizimvo zeSizwe; kwaye

(b) Ukuba iye yamkelwa lunintsi lwabavoti kuvoto-zimvo, uSithethi weNdibano yeSizwe usenokubangela ukuba singeniswe kuMongameli ngokukhawulezisa, naye okufaneleke ayamkele kunye nokuyiphawula ngotyikityo.

(7) Nangona enye yezibonelelo zelicandelo, isilungiso kwizibonelelo zemilinganiselo, umsebenzi wayo ungowokwengezelela ubude bexesha emalihlalwe ngumntu e-ofiSini kawonke-wonke, ayinakusebenza emntwini osebenze kuloo ofisi, okanye i-ofisi engaba ngenye kodwa ihambelana naleyo, nokuba kunini ngaphambi kolungiso.

(8) Amacandelwana (6) no (7) akufanelanga alungiswe omabini kwiBhili yoMgaqo-Sisekookanye lamacandelwana anikelwe ebantwini kuvoto-zimvo lunye.

(9) Elicandelo lisenokulungiswa kuphela ngokulandela imiqathango exelwe kumacandelwana (3), (4), (5) no (6), ngokungathi elicandelo likuSahluko 4.

(10) Xa kusiyokwethulwa iBhili yoMgaqo-Siseko phambi kukaMongameli ukuze ayiphawule ngokuyityikitya kufuneka ikhatshwe—

(a)

sisiqiniseko kuSithethi esokuba iBhili ivotelwe yavunywa ziivoti ezingaba sisibini esithathwini ebuncinaneni kwintlanganiso yeSizwe; kwa

(b) nesiqinisekiso esisuka kuMongameli weSenethi esingqina ukuba kuvoto lokugqibela iBhili iye yavunywa ngokuvotelwa ngamalungu angaba sisibini esthathwini ebuncinaneni kumalungu endlu yeSenethi.

329 Ukuqalisa koMgaqo-Siseko, izibonelelo zotshintsho Ishedyuli sesi 6 ixela ukuqalisa kwaloMgaqo-Siseko, ukukurhoxiswa koMgaqoSiseko omdala kwanokusebenza komyalelo omtsha woMgaqo-Sisekoosekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko.

INXALENYE 3 Utoliko 330 Ukusetyenziswa kwenxalenye 3 Le inxalenye ijongene notoliko lwalo Mgaqo-Siseko ngaphandle kokuba kuye kwanyanzeliswa yimeko. 331 Imigaqo emayisetyenziswe ekutolikeni uMgaqo-Siseko. Icandelo 46 liyasebenza, ukutolika loMgaqo-Siseko ngaphandle kwecandelo 4. 332 Iinkcazelo Kulo Mgaqo-Siseko— “UMthetho wePalamente” uchaza

(a) kuwiswa koMthetho okuvunye yiPalamente waze wasiwa kuMongameli ukuze awuphawule ngotyikityo ngokwendlela efunwa nguloMgaqo-siseko; okanye

(b) uMthetho obukade usebenza njengoMthetho wePalmente eZimbabwe kutshanje phambi kosuku lokukuqala, kuquka—

(i)

inkqubo yokuhlaziywa kommiselo woMthetho eZimbabwe eyenziwa ngo 1996 ngaphantsi kwegunya leNgqokelela yoMmiselo woMthetho noMthetho wohlaziyo (isahluko 1:03)

(ii)

uMthetho owisiweyo nokuba ungabe uye washiywa ngaphandle kwinkqubo yommiselo yohlaziyo exelwe kumhlathana (i), oqhubeka usetyenziswa nangona ungekho kulonkqubo;

(iii)

noMthetho owiswe yiPalamente yase Zimbabwe ngemva kwenkqubo yohlaziyo oluxelwe kumhlathana (i) ngaphambi komhla wokukuqala;

“ukuziphatha kwamalungu alawulayo” kuquka nokuba sesiphi isigqibo, ukwenza kwamagosa kawonke-wonke okanye umntu owenza imisebenzi yokuncedisa uwonke-wonke kwaye ukwehluleka kwaloomntu ukwenza izigqibo ezinjalo okanye ukwenza kanjalo; “ukulungisa” kuquka ukohluka, ukutshintsha, ukuguqula ukongeza nokukhupha okanye ukuziqhelanisa; “unyulo lwexeshana” luchaza unyulo lokuzalisa isithuba esiye savela ngesaquphe kwiPalamente kwigunya lasekuhlaleni; “iNkosi’ ichaza iNkosi exelwe kuSahluko 15; “iNkonzo yoluNtu” intsingiselo yalo yile echazwe licandelo 199; iKhomishini” intsingiselo yalo yile echazwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko;

“iKomiti yeMithetho neziyalelo ezimisiweyo” ichaza ikomiti enegama elisekwe ngaphantsi kwecandelo 151; “umhlaba woluntu”uchaza umhlaba woluntu osikwe ngaphantsi koMthetho wePalamente waphathwa ngokoMthetho wesintu ngabahlali bekhokhelwa yiNkosi. “iBhili yoMgaqo-siseko” ichaza iBhili ethi xa iye yawiswa ibenamandla okutshintsha nokuba zeziphi izibonelelo zaloMgaqo-Siseko; “inkundla yoMgaqo-Siseko” yinkunlda yoMgaqo-Siseko esekwe licandelo 162(a); “ityala loMgaqo-Siseko” lityala elimalunga noMgaqo-Siseko elimalunga nokutolikwa, ukukhuselwa kwawo, nokunyanzelisa kwawo uMgaqoSiseko; “uMthetho wesintu” nguMthetho wesintu wecandelo nokuba leliphi kwanoluntu nokuba loluphi olwase Zimbabwe; “uMthetho woluleko” nguMthetho obhaliweyo olawula ululeko lwamalungu lwamagosa aqeqeshiweyo, kuquka nabo abangamalungu angaphelelanga, ngexeesha besebenzela iinkonzo malunga nokwehluleka kwabo ukwenza umsebenzi ngendlela; “uMkhosi yokhuselo” uchaza—

(a) uMkhosi wasemanzini, owaphantsi nowasemoyeni;

(b) inkonzo yamapolisa;

(c) inkonzo yokugcinwa kwababanjwa nokulungisiswa kwezimilo zabo; okanye

(d) nequmrhu nokuba leliphi elisekelwe ukusebenzela uwonkewonke ngaphantsi koMthethowePalamente laze labhengezwa ngulooMthetho ukuba linguMkhosi olulekileyo “umhla wokukuqala” ngumhla oyakuthi loMgaqo-Siseko uqale ukusebenza ngokupheleleyo njengokomhlathi 3(2) weshedyuli lesithandathu; “uMthetho woNyulo” nguMthethowePalamenteolawula unyulo ngokwendlela exelwe kuloMgaqo-Siseko; “unyaka wemali” uxela ixesha eliziinyangaeziyi 12 eliphela ngomhlaka 31 disemba; “itheko” liquka amandla nomsebenzi; “iGazethi’ yiGazethi kaRhulumente evumelekileyo kuquka nesongezelelo nokuba sesiphi esaloGazethi; ‘unyulo lukawonke” lunyulo lomntu wonkei—

(a) oluka Mongameli, amasekela kaMongameli, kwanamalungu ePalamente;

(b) olwamalungu amaqumrhu alawulayo kumagunya asekuhlaleni; “uRhulumente’ nguRhulumente wase Zimbabwe; “amaqumrhu alawulwa nguRhulumente” iqumrhu elilawulwa siSizwe okanye umntu omele iSizwe ekusebenzeni kwalo, isenokuba ngobumnini bezabelo ezininzi kwiqumrhu okanye kangangoko;

“iNdlu’ yiSenathe okanye iNdibano yeSizwe; “iKhomishini ezimeleyo” yikhomishini exelwe kwicandelo 232; “umgwebi” kuchazwa umgwebi wenkundla yoMgaqo-Siseko, iNkundla ePhakamileyo, iNkundla ePhezulu, iNkundla yabaSebenzi, okanye iNkundla yoLawulo; “iKhomishini yokuXoxwa kwamaTyala” yiKhomishini enelogama esekwe licandelo 189; “uMthetho”—

(a) uchaza nokuba sesiphi isibonelelo saloMgaqo-Siseko okanye uMthethowePalamente;

(b) isibonelelo nokuba sesiphi esikulo Mthetho; okanye

(c) nokuba ngowuphi uMthetho ongabhalwanga kodwa osetyenziswayo eZimbabwe, kuquka noMthetho wesintu; kwaye lamagama athi ngokoMthetho, ngokusemthethweni kufuneka acaciswe ngokufanelekileyo “ingcali yoMthetho” ngumntu oye wavumeleka ukusebenzela uMthetho eZimbabwe; “igunya lasekuhlaleni” libhunga elixelwe kwinxalenye 3 nesahluko 14; “ilungu” malunga—

(a) Ikhomisshoni okanye ibhodi esekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko, equka usihlalo nosekela wakhe;

(b) Ibhodi yoMthetho, amabhunga ophondo okanye aweedolophu okanye igunya lasekuhlaleni, ngumntu oqeshelwe okanye onyulelwe kwigunya, ibhodi, okanye nokuba leliphi igunya eli;

(c) ibhodi yoMthetho, igunya lophondo okanye eledolophu; okanye

(d) umntu onoxanduva lokulawula kweenkqubo zoMthethokumabhunga ophondo nawedolophu okanye namagunya asekuhlaleni; “ilungu lepalanmente” kuchazwa amalungu wendlu yeSenethi, okanye aweNdibano yeSizwe; “ibhunga leedolophu’ libhunga elisekwe licandelo 269 lisekelwa iphondo eliyidolophu; “iphondo eliyidolophu” lixela iphondo eliyidolophu lakwaBulawayo kwanephondo eliyidolophu elase Harare; “uMphathiswai” ngumntu owenza imisebenzi kaMphathiswa noba ngawaliphi isebe; “uMthetho weSizwe’ nguMthethowePalamente okanye isixhobo soMthetho esenziwe ngaphantsi koMthethowePalamente; “isifungo” siquka nesiqinisekiso; “ixesha likaxakeka kawonke-wonke” lixesha apho ekuye kwakhutshwa kwaye kwasetyenziswa ixesha likaxakeka ngaphantsi kwecandelo 113; “umntu” kuxelwa umntu emnye okanye beliqela; “uMongameli” nguMongameli waseZimbabwe;

“uMongameli weSenethi” nguMongameli wendlu yeSenethi onyulwe ngokwecandelo 122; “ibhunga lophondo” libhunga elisekwe licandelo 268 linyulelwa amaphondo angasingawo amaphondo azidolophu; “i-ofisi karhulumente” yi-ofisi ebhatalwayo esebenzela urhuluente neSizwe;

“igosa likarhulumente” ngumntu osebenzela kwi-ofisi zikarhulumente;

“inkonzo yokhuseleko” yinkonzo yokhuseleko exelwe kwicandelo 207; “iNkosi yeSenethi’ yinkosi enyulelwe kwindlu yeSenethi ngokwecandelo 120(1)(b) okanye (c); “ukuhlala” lixesha lokuba indibano yeSizwe okanye eyeSenethi ihlale intlanganiso eqhubekeka yokuquka nexesha nokuba leliphi lokuba indibano yeSizwe okanye eyeSenethi neekomiti; “umhla wokuhlala” ngumhla waphakathi kweveki oxelwe kwimiqathango emisiweyo yendibano yeSizwe okanye eyeSenethi; “uSithethi” siSithethi seNdibano yeSizwe esinyulwe ngokwecandelo 126; “imiQathango emisiweyo” malunga nezinto emazenziwe—

(a) yiSenethi, ichaza imiqathango emiselwe amalungu eSenethi;

(b) yindibano yeSizwe ichaza imiqathango emisiweyo eyeNdibano yeSizwe;

(c) yindibano yeSizwe idibene neyeSenethi ichaza imiqathango edityanisiweyo, eyenziwe ngaphantsi kwecandelo 139; “ibhodi yoMthetho” –

(a) yiKhomishini esekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko.okanye

(b) ibhodi esekelwe ngaphantsi koMthetho wePalamente enemisebenzi ethile echazwe ngulooMthetho, onobulungu obungabe buphelele okanye buyimxalenye babo abatyunjwa nguMongameli okanye abantu abatyunjwe nguMongameli, SekelaMongameli, uMphathiswa sekela lika Mphathiswa nokuba yeyiphi ibhodi yoMthetho okanye iKhomishini evunyelwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko.

“isixhobo soMthetho” sisixhobo nasiphi esinamandla oMthetho esenziwe nguMongameli, uMsekeli kaMongameli, uUMphathiswa umsekeli kaUMphathiswa okanye nokuba ngubani othe wanikwa igunya nguloMgaqo-siseko okanye nguMthetho wasePalamente;

“irhafu” iquka irhafu yasemdeni weLizwe, izinga okanye ityala lemali; “Zimbabwe” ichaza iLizwe lase Zimbabwe; “inkokheli yesintu” ngumntu oqeshelwe loonto ngokwe candela 238; “iKhomishini yoNyulo eZimbabwe’ ichaza iKhomishini njengokuxelwe kwicandelo 238; “iKhomishini yamaLungelo abaNtu eZimbabwe” yikhomishini eneligama esekwe licandelo 242. 333 Iimbekiselo zezahluko, amacandelo nakanjalo Nokuba yeyiphi imbekiselo kuloMgaqo-Siseko, engahambisani ne—

(a)

sahluko, icandelo okanye ishedyuli kufuneka ithathwe njengembekiselo yesahluko, icandelo okanye ishedyuli saloMgaqo-Siseko.

(b) candelwana, kufuneka lithathwe njenge mbekiselo yecandelwana yecandelo elenzelwe loombekiselo;

(c)

mhlathi, kufuneka ucaciswe njengembekiselo yomhlathi weshedyuli, icandelo, icandelwana okanye inkcazelo eyenzelwe loombekiselo;

(d) umhlathana, kufuneka ucaciswe njengembekiselo yomhalathi okanye umhlathana oxelwayo. 334 Amagama obunye aquka nobuninzi nawobuninzi achaze ubunye KuloMgaqo-Siseko, amagama achaza ubunye aquka nobuninzi njengoko nawobuninzi abala nobunye. 335. Iitafile nezihloko

Imixholo esetafileni nezihloko zezahluko, iinxalenye, amacandelo nezinye izindululo zoMgaqo siseko aziyonxalenye noMgaqo siseko kwaye zifakiwe ukuze zibe sisiqiniso kuphela. 336 Iitafile neezihloko

(1) kuloMgaqo-Siseko njalo nje xa kuthethwa ngeemihla—

(a)

ukukuqala okanye ukubizwa ngomhla othile, loomhla awunakubalwa kwimihla yexesha elibalwayo;

(b) yokugqibela kufuneka ibalwe kwixesha elibalwayo.

(2) Ngokubhekiselele kuloMgaqo-Siseko, ukuba ixesha lokwenza nokuba yini ngokwaloMgaqo-Siseko liphele ngosuku lwangomGqibelo, iCawe okanye osuka lokuphumla kukawonke-wonke, ixesha kufuneka okanye loonto emayenziwe yenziwe ngomhla olandelayo ongengo mGqibelo, iCawe okanye usuku lokuphumla kuka wonk wonke.

(3) Imbekiselo yenyanga kuloMgaqo-Siseko kufuneka icaciswe njengembekiselo yenyanga esekhalendeni, kwaye nexesha leenyanga liqala ngomhla okukuqala ngalo enyangeni kusiyakuma ngomhla ofanayo wenyanga okuphela ngayo ixesha elo.

(4) Imbekiselo yaloMgaqo-Siseko engalungelanga kuba ngunyaka kufuneka icaciselwe njengeenyanaga ezingamashumi amabini nesibini. 337 Iimbekiselo kwabasebenzela ezi-ofiSini Njalo nje loMgaqo-Siseko xa ubhekisela emntwini osebenzela e-ofiSini, loombekiselo iquka nomntu owenza imisebenzi yaloo ofisi ngokusemthethwni. 338 Iimbekiselo zePalamente Ukuba loMgaqo-Siseko uye wafuna ingxelo okanye ezinye iinkcukacha ukuba zingeniswe ePalamente, ingxelo okanye ezonkcukacha kufuneka zingeniswe kwiSenethi okanye kwiNdibano yeSizwe. 339 Ingcebiso nothethwano

(1) Njalo nje ukuba loMgaqo-Siseko uye wafunisa ukuba umntu okanye igunya lenzele umntu okanye igunya locebiso loomntu okanye elogunya kufuneka—

(a)

amxelele lomnye, ngembalelwano, eyonanto emayenziwe kwaye kufuneka anike lowo omnye ulwazi olwaneleyo ukuze loomntu avisisise immo nomsebenzi wocebiso; kwaye

(b) afumanise lomnye isithuba esaneleyo sokwenza oko akucetyisiweyo; kwaye naye loomntu okanye elogunya kufuneka balandele olocetyiso.

(2) Qho ukuba loMgaqo-Siseko uye funa ukuba umntu okanye igunya ukuba lithethane nommnye umtu, loomntu okanye elogunya kufuneka—

(a)

lixelele lomnye ngembalelwano, loongcebiso emayenziwe ngulowo omye kwaye anike ulwazi olupheleleyo ukwenzela lowo omnye avisise imo nomsebenzi wengcebiso leyo;

(b) linike lomnye isithuba esaneleyo esokuba enze isincomo kwanezimvo malunga necebiso leyo; kwa

(c)

linike ingcamango efanelekileyo kwimibono enokwenziwa ngulo omnye malunga necebiso leyo;

kodwa umntu okanye igunya linyanzeliswa ukuba lilandele imiboni yalo omnye umntu. 340 Ukutyunjwa

(1) Ngaphandle kokuba kuye kwatsho loMgaqo-Siseko, amandla angaphantsi kwaloMgaqo-Siseko awukutyumba umntu ukuba asebenzele e-ofiSini aquka namandla;

(a)

awukuba umntu aphinde atyunjwe kwakhona kuloo ofisi;

(b) ukutyumba umntu onyuswe ngezihlalo zomsebenzi okanye asiwe kwenye i-ofisi;

(c)

ukutyumba umntu ukuba asebenzele kulooofisi;

(e)

ukulungisa imiqathango yokusebenza kwaloomntu e-ofiSini, kuquka iimbatalo, nexesha lokusebenza kwaloomntu kwanezinye iinzuzo zalomntu mhla wayeka ukusebenzela i-ofisi leyo; kwa

(f)

ngokwaloMgaqo-Siseko, ukumisa okanye ukurhoxisa umntu ekusebenzeleni kuloo ofisi.

(d) ukutyumba umntu ukuba asebenzele kulooofisi nangona kukho omnye kodwa engekho esekuphumleni;

(2) Ukuba kuye kwenzeka ukuthi abantu ababini nangaphezulu baye basebenzela iofisi enye ngesizathu sokuba osebenzela i-ofisi leyo ebeye ekuphumleni oyena oqashe mva nguye oyakakuba ngumnikazi waloofisi emnye.

(3) Ngaphantsi kwaloMgaqo-Siseko, uMthetho wePalamente kufuneka utyumbe isekela elingaba linye nangaphezulu lomntu osebenzela i-ofisi engaphantsi kwaloMgaqoSiseko kwaye kufuneka uvele nemisebenzi emayenziwe kwanemiqathango yokusebenza.

(4) Ukuba kuye kwatyunjwa isekela lontu osebenzela i-ofisi ngokwaloMgaqoSiseko, isekela elo kufuneka lenze yonke imisebenzi yomnikazi we-ofisi ngesizatho sokuba umnikazi akakwazanga kuyenza.

(5) Ngaphantsi kwezibonelelo nokuba zeziphi zaloMgaqo-Siseko ezivela nexesha okanye nemilinganiselo yexesha lokusebenzela kwi-ofisi, umntu oye waphuma e-ofiSini yaloMgaqo-Siseko, ukuba ufanelekile uvunyelwe ukuphinda atyunjwe okanye onyulwe kwakule-ofisi. 341 Ukurhoxa

(1) Wonke umntu oye watyunjwa ukusebenzela e-ofiSini esekwe nguloMgaqoSiseko usenokurhoxa e-ofisini leyo ngokubhala isaziso esiya kumntu ebemtyumbele ukusebenzela iofisi, kodwa ukuba;

(a)

2013

nguMongameli, isaziso sibhekiswa kwiSithethi;

(b) uMongameli weSenethi okanye usekela wakhe, isaziso sibhekiswa kumabhalane wePalamente okanye saziswe kwiSenethi;

(c)

uSithethi okanye isekela leSithethi seNdibano yeSizwe, isaziso kufuneka sisiwe kumabhalane wePalamente okanye kwaziswe iNdibano yeSizwe;

(d) kwilungu leSenethi, isaziso sisiwa kwiSithethi;

(e)

ilungu leNdibano yeSizwe, isaziso sisiwa kwiSithethi;

(f)

ilungu leBhunga lephondo, isaziso sisiwa kusihlalo webhunga;

(g) ilungu lebhunga ledolophu, isaziso sisiwa kusodolophu wephondo;

(h) ilungu legunya lokuhlala, isaziso sisiwa kwiNkulu yamagosa asigqeba eyebhunga.

(2) Ukurhoxa komntu ebesenzela i-ofisi esekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko kukuqala ngomhla obhalwe kwisaziso, okanye ukuba akukhosuku okanye ixesha elibhaliweyo, ngomhla esiye safunyanwa ngaso ngulowo obhalelweyo okanye lowo othunywe ngobhalelweyo. 342 Ukusetyenziswa kwemisebenzi, nakanjalo

(1) Amandla, ulawulo okanye ilungelo elinikwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko lisenokusetyenziswa, kwanomsenenzi onikwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko, kufuneka usetyenziswe ngendlela efanelekileyo.

(2) Onke amaziko asekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko anamandla apheleleyo okufezekisa iinjongo zemisebenzi yawo kwanokuyenza loomisebenzi.

(3) Ukuba amandla, olawulo nelungelo ziye zanikwa nguloMgaqo-Siseko amanye amandla namalungelo akulungeleyo ukunikwa ukuze lamandla aphunyezwe ayakubalwa njengokuba anikiwe nawo. 343 Umntu uthathwa njengomntu osebenzela i-ofisi kawonke-wonke xa kutheni Ngokwenjongo yaloMgaqo-Siseko, umntu akanakuze athathwe njengokuba usebenzela i-ofisi kawonke-wonke ngenxeni yokuba efumana umhlala phatshi, isiqingatha somrholo, okanye ezinye iinzuzo ezifana nezi ebhatalwa kumsebenzi ebewenze ngaphambili owase ofiSini kawonke-wonke. 344 Ikhoramu nemisebenzi yezithuba kwiibhodi zoMgaqo-Siseko

(1) Ibhodi esekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko isenokusebenza ukuba kukho isithuba esinye nangaphezulu kuxhomekeka ukuba amalungu ebhodi avumela ukusebenza oko ayikhoramu.

(2) Ngaphandle kokuba loMgaqo-Siseko okanye uMthetho olawula ibhodi leyo yenze izibonelelo ezahlukileyo, isiqingatha samalungu ebhodi nokuba yeyiphi esekwe nguloMgaqo-Siseko senza ikhoramu.

(3) Nokuba sesiphi isingqiniso kuloMgaqo-Siseko kwii voti ze—

(a)

siqingatha samalungu ebhodi anobulungu obulinani elingangenwa ngu 2;

(b) isibini esithathwini samalungu ebhodi anobulungu obulinani elingangenwayo ngu 3, okanye

(c)

iikota ezintathu zamalungu ebhodi anobulungu obulinani elingangenwayo ngu 4;

kufuneka ichazwe ixele ukuba inani leevoti akufuneki libe ngaphantsi kwenani elipheleleyo elandelayo ngaphezu kwesiqingatha esinye, isibini esithathwini okanye iikota ezintahu, zamalungu ebhodi

(4) Nokuba sesiphi isingqiniso kumalungu ewonke awePalamente sisingqiniso kuwo wonke amalungu ePalamente ngalo umzuzu. 345 Ukungangqinelani kwemibhalo kuloMgaqo-Siseko Ukuba kuye kwanokungangqinelani kwemibhalo yaloMgaqo-Siseko, owesingisi umbhalo waloMgaqo-Siseko uyayongamela yonke eminye. ISHEDYULI YOUKUKUQALA (Icandelo 4) Iflegi Yesizwe, Ingoma Yesizwe Uphawu Lwesizwe Netywina Likawonke-Wonke INXALENYE 1; IFLEGI YESIZWE

INXALENYE 2 UMHOBE WESIZWE Inguqulelo yesiXhosa 1. Phakamisani flegi yethu yaseZimbabwe Eyavela ngedabi lenkululeko Ngegaz elininz’ elamagorha ethu Lisikhusele nakwiintshaba zonke Malisikelelwe iLizwe laseZimbabwe 2.

Jongan’iZimbabwe yon’ihonjisiwe Ngeentaba ezintle nemilamb’emihle imvula inethe nezityalo zande ‘basebenzi bavun’ abantu bahluthe Malisikelelwe iLizwe lase Zimbabwe

3\. Nkosi sikekelela iLizwe laseZimbabwe ILizwe lemveli yethu thina sonke Kusuk’ eZambezi kusiy’ eLimpopo Iinkokheli zibenenyaniso Malisikelelwe iLizwe laseZimbabwe

INXALENYE 3 Uphawu Lwesizwe

INXALENYE 4 Itywina Likawonke-Wonke

ISHEDYULI YESIBINI (Icandelo 87) Ukufakwa Kwemida Kumalungelo Ngexesha Likaxakeka Kawonke-Wonke Ukutolikwa kweShedyuli yokukuqala 1. Kule shedyuli— “ibanjwa” ngumntu obanjiweyo phantsi koMthetho wongxamiseko obangela ukuvalelwa; “uMthetho ongxamisekileyo” uthetha uMthetho obhaliweyo onika amanyathelo athatyathwa ukujongana nayiphi na imeko evela ngexesha loxinzelelo loluntu; “amalungelo oluntu okanye inkululeko” kuthetha ilungelo okanye inkululeko echazwe kwiCandelo 2 leSahluko 4; “inkundla yokufunisisa” kuthetha lenkundla ixelwe umhlathi 3(1). Ubungakanani besilinganiselo sokusetyenziswa kwamalungelo oluntu okanye iinkululeko 2. (1) UMthetho wonxunguphalo unako ukukhawulela noba ngawaphi amalungelo neenkululeko, kodwa kuphela ngokwesimiselo esixelwe kwicandelo 87.

(2) Ukuba imeko yonxunguphalo lukawonke iye yaxelwe njengokuchazwe kwicandelo 113 malunga nayiphina indawo kwelaseZimbabwe, uMthetho wonxunguphalo ungangakhawuleli amalungelo nenkululeko yoluntu kuziganeko zonxunguphalo ezenzeke noba kuphi kweli laseZimbabwe njengokuxela kwaleShedyuli. Inkundla yokufunisisa ngamabanjwa 3. (1) UMthetho wonxunguphalo ovumela ukukhawulelwa kokubanjwa kufuneka uvumele ukusebenza kwenkundla efunisisa ngamabanjwa.

(2) Inkundla yolufunisiso kufuneka ityunjwe nguMongameli ecetyiswa yiKhomishini yeNkonzo zoxoxiso lwamatyala eye Judicial Service Commission kwaye ngemva kokubonisana neKomiti ebona ngemigaqo ebikiweyo.

(3) Inkundla yofunisiso kufuneka ibe—

(a)

nosihlalo, ongumahluleli okanye owake waba ngumahluleli; kwaye

(b) amalungu amabini, omnye wabo kufuneka—

(i) ungumgwebi okanye wake waba ngumgwebi okanye uyavumeleka ukuba atyunjwe;

(ii) uke waba ngumantyi eZimbabwe okwesithuba seminyaka esixhenxe ebuncikaneni; okanye

(iii) useke wabaligqwetha eZimbabwe okwesithuba seminyaka esixhenxe. Amalungelo angundoqo abavalelwa 4. (1) Bonke ababanjwa—

(a)

kufuneka baziswe ngokukhawulezileyo ngokubanjwa kwabo, kwaye ukuba kunokwenzeka kungaphelanga iintsuku ezisxhenxe baxelelwe ngembangela zokubanjwa kwabo

(b) ngokungxamisekileyo bavunyelwe oku—

(i) ngokwendleko zabo, bazifunele kwaye babonane ngasese nomcebisi wezoMthetho abazifunele yena; okanye

(ii) ukuba bayafuna banako ukubonana ngasese nomcebisi wezoMthetho abamphiwe nguRhulumente ngendleko zakhe uRhulumente; kwaye ngokukhawulezileyo baziswe ngamalungelo abo achazwe kwesisiqendu; kwaye

(c)

baphathwe ngesidima nesithunzi sokuba ngumntu.

(2) Ukuba esisiqendu sifunisa ukuba kubekho imiyalelo nemibiko ephiwa ababanjwa —

(a)

lomiyalelo kufuneka bayiphiwe ngolwimi aluvisisisayo umbanjwa; kwaye

(b) ukuba umbanjwa lowo akakwazi ukufunda okanye ukubhala, onke amaxwebhu anemiyalelelo kwa memibiko kufuneka kusetyenziswe nokokuba zeziphina iindlela zokuba ade avisisise. Ukuphononongwa kwamatyala abavalelwa 5. (1) Onke amatyala wababanjwa kufuneka afakwe kwinkundla ephonononga ngamatyal kwisithuba seentsuku ezilishumi emva kokuvalelwa kwakhe okokukuqala kwaye inkundla kufuneka ixelelwe igama lombanjwa, indawo apho agcinwe khonakunye nezizathu zokuvalelwa.

(2) Ityala lombanjwa wonke ovalelweyo kufuneka liphinde lihanjiswe kwinkundla ephonononga amatyala ngeentsuku ezingamashumi amathathu ukususela ngomhla othe wagqitywa ngalo ityala, okanye ngexesha elifutshane ukuba inkundla injalo.

(3) Inkundla yokuphonononga kufuneka iqhubekeke ngaphandle kokulibala ukuphonononga onke amatyala afakwe kuyo.

(4) Kuzo zonke iintetho zesikhalazo zophononongo lwamatyala, abavalelweyo aphononongwa amatyala wabo kufuneka bavunyelwe ukuba baxele ityala labo ngokwabo, ukuba bafuna—

(a)

ukuchazela igqwetha abaliphiwe nguRhulumente ngendleko zakhe uRhulumente; okanye

(b) ukuchazela igqwetha abazifunele lona ngendleko zabo.

(5) Ingcaciso kumhlathi (1) ukuya kwisithuba semihla elishumi kubandakanya ukubhekisela kwithuba elincinci lokuvalelwa kwelo lixa kwiintsuku ezilishumi, kwimeko yomntu ogcinwe kwisithuba seentsuku ezilishumi emva kokuvalelwa kokukuqala kwaye evalelwa kwakhona emva kweentsuku ezilishumi ngemveni kokukhululwa kwakhe. Iingcebiso zesikhalazo sophononongo 6. Emva kokuphonononga kwetyala, umgwebi wobuhlaziyi kufuneka enze iziphakamiselo ezibhaliweyo kwigunya eliyalela ukugcinwa eluvalelweni ukuba ngaba kufuneka aqhubeke nokuvalelwa, kunye negunya kufuneka lenze ngokuhambelana nesiphakamiselo sesigqeba. Ababanjwa abavulelweyo akufanelanga bavalewe kwakhona ngezizathu ezifana nezoebeke wazivalelelwa 7. (1) Ibanjwa elivulelweyo elubanjweni ngenxeni yengxelo yesikhalazo echaza ukuba akukho bungqina obupheleleyo obokuba avulelwe akufuneki avalelwe kwakhona ngezityolo ezifana nezo ebezityolwa ade avalelwe ngaphambilini.

(2) Ngenjongo yalomhlathi, umntu uthathwa ngokungathi uvalelwe kabini phantsi kwemeko ayebanjwe kuzo ukuba isigqeba sophononongo siye saxela ukuba ngokombono waso kukho izizathu ezitsha ezivakalayo zokuba avalewe kwakhona umntu lowo. Ukulondolozwa kokufikelela kwababanjwa kwinkundla 8. LeShedyuli ayinakuthi ithathwe njengophazamisa ilingelo lombanjwa lokufaka umngeni enkundleni ngokusemthethweni ngokuvalelwa kwakhe, nokuba ingaba ityala lakhe sele libekiwe phambi kwenkundla yokuphonononga amatyala.

ISHEDYULI YESITHATHU (Amacandelo 94, 104, 114, 122, 123, 126, 127, 128, 185, 259, 272, 310 no 320) Izifungo Nezingqiniso Ezinyanisekileyo IInotsi: 1. Umntu owenza noba zeziphin zezifungo zilandelayo ukuba uyathanda usenokwenza isifungo ezo esebenzisa lamazwi abhaliweyo kwezizifungo. 2. Umntu owenza noba zeziphi zezizifungo zilandelayo okanye owenza noba zeziphi izingqiniso ezilandelayo ukuba uyathanda unako ukungawasebenzisi lamagama abhalwe kwezizifungiso nezizingqiniso kodwa lawo anokuwasebenzisa abe kanti awayichithi le ibhalwe kwezizifungo nezingqiniso zilandelayo. Isifungo Okanye Isingqiniso Esinyanisekileyo Sokwethembeka Mna, ............................................ ndiyafunga[ndingqinisa ngokunyanisekileyo] ukuba ndiyakunyaniseka kuLizwe laseZimbabwe yaye ndiya kuwuthobela, ndiyihlonele iimithetho yaseZimbabwe. Ngoko ke ndincede Thixo. [Ungatsitywa xa kungqiniswa] Esisifungo okanye isingqiniso esinyanisekileyo senziwa phambi komntu okhethwe nguMthetho ofanelekileyo. Isifungiso Okanye Isingqiniso Esinyanisekileyo Sase Ofisini Mna,.............................., ............. ndiyafunga[ndingqinisa ngokunyanisekileyo] ukuba ndizakusebenzela iZimbabwe ngokwethembeka nangokunyanisekileyo kumsebenzi wam. Ngoko ke ndincede Thixo. [Ungatsitywa xa kungqiniswa ] Esisifungo okanye isingqiniso esinyanisekileyo senziwa phambi komntu okhethwe nguMthetho ofanelekileyo. Isifungiso Okanye Isingqiniso Esinyanisekile Sika Mongameli Kwa NoosekelaMongameli Mna, ................................................. ndiyafunga [ndingqinisa ngokunyanisekileyo] ukuba njengo Mongameli [okanye iSekela lika Mongameli] wase Zimbabwe

ndiyakuthembeka, ndiwuthobele, ndiwulandele kwaye ndiwuxhase uMgaqo-siseko kunye nawo wonke omnye uMthetho uMthetho weLizwe laseZimbabwe, kwaye— •

ndiyakukhuthaza konke oko kuyiqhubela phambili iZimbabwe, ndichasane nako konke okon kunokuthi kuyonakalise;

•

ndiwakhulise, ndiwakhuthaza amalungelo abo bonke abemi baseZimbabwe;

•

ndizakuyenza imisebenzi yam ngamandla ama onke neetalente zam zonke kangangoko ndikwaziyo nakangangoko ndinako nangokunyanisikileyo kwisikhokelo sesazela sam; kwaye

•

ndizakuzinikela kwintlalo-ntle yeZimbabwe kunye nabantu bayo.

Ngoko ke ndincede Thixo. [Ungatsitywa xa kungqiniswa] Esisifungo okanye isingqiniso esinyanisekileyo senziwa phambi kweJaji eNkulu okanye, ukuba akekho singenziwa phambi kweyinye iJaji elandela le enkulu. Isifungo Okanye Isingqiniso Esinyanisekileyo Si Kamphathiswa Mna, ................................................ndiyafunga [ndingqinisa ngokunyanisekileyo] ukuba ndiyakuthembeka kuLizwe laseZimbabwe, nakusihlalo sokuba nguMphathiswa, ndiya kuwuthobela, ndiwuhlonele kanjalo ndiwuxhase uMgaqo-siseko kunye nawo wonke omnye uMthetho, kanjalo ndiyathmbisa ukusibamba isikhundla sama njengoMPHATHISWA ngesihomo nesithozela ndibe ngumncebisi onyanisekileyo nothembekileyo ndingawudizi nokuba kungokuthe ngqo okanye ngokungathanga ngqo nawuphi na umcimbi olihlebo okanye imfihlo ephathiswe mna, ndize ndiyenze nemisebenzi yesikhundla sam ngenkathalo nakangangoko ndinako. Ngoko ke ndincede Thixo. [Ungatsitywa xa kungqiniswa] Esisifungo okanye isingqiniso esinyanisekileyo senziwa phambi kweJaji eNkulu okanye, ukuba akekho singenziwa phambi kweyinye iJaji elandela le enkulu. Isifungo Okanye Isingqiniso Esinyanisekileyo Sesekela Likamphathiswa Mna, ................................................ndiyafunga [ndingqinisa ngokunyanisekileyo] ukuba ndiyakuthembeka kuLizwe laseZimbabwe, nakusihlalo sokuba nguMphathiswa, ndiya kuwuthobela, ndiwuhlonele kanjalo ndiwuxhase uMgaqo-siseko kunye nawo wonke omnye uMthetho, kanjalo ndiyathmbisa ukusibamba isikhundla sama njene Sekela Mphathiswa ngesihomo nesithozela ndibe ngumncebisi onyanisekileyo nothembekileyo ndingawudizi nokuba kungokuthe ngqo okanye ngokungathanga ngqo nawuphi na umcimbi olihlebo okanye imfihlo ephathiswe mna, ndize ndiyenze nemisebenzi yesikhundla sam ngenkathalo nakangangoko ndinako. Ngoko ke ndincede Thixo. [Ungatsitywa xa kungqiniswa] Esisifungo okanye isingqiniso esinyanisekileyo senziwa phambi kweJaji eNkulu okanye, ukuba akekho singenziwa phambi kweyinye iJaji elandela le enkulu. Isifungo Okanye Isingqiniso Esinyanisekileyo Selungu Lepalamente Mna, ............................................... ndiyafunga [ndingqinisa ngokunyanisekileyo] ukuba ndiyakuthembeka kuLizwe lase Zimbabwe, ndiyakuwuthobela ndiwuhlonele uMgaqo-siseko kunye nawo wonke omnye uMthetho waseZimbabwe yaye ndiyenze

ngokuziphela imisebenzi yam njengelungu leSenethi/leNdlu yeNdibano yeSizwe [nokokuba yeyiphi efanelekileyo] nangokwethembeka kwaye ndize ndiyenze kangangoko ndinako. Ngoko ke ndincede Thixo. [Ungatsitywa xa kungqiniswa] Esisifungo okanye isingqiniso esinyanisekileyo senziwa phambi kukuMabhalane wasePalamente. Isifungo Okanye Isingqiniso Esinyanisekileyo Sejaji Mna ................................................ ndiyafunga wear [ndingqinisa ngokunyanisekileyo] ukuba ndiyakuthembeka kuLizwe laseZimbabwe kwaye nakusikhundla sokuba ……….. .............. , ndizakuhlonela, ndithobele kwaye ndikhusele uMgaqo-siseko, kwaye ndiyakwenza ubulungisa busebenze ngokufanayo kubo bonke abantu ngaphandle koloyiko okanye ukudlela indlala ngokungangqinelani loMgaqo-siseko kunye noMthetho. Ngoko ke ndincede Thixo. [Ungatsitywa xa kungqiniswa] Ukuba umntu owenza esisifungo okanye isingqiniso esinyanisekileyo uyiJaji, oku kungenziwa phambi kweNtloko Jaji okanye elinye igosa elidala elikhoyo. Ukuba kungenjalo senziwe phambi komntu okhethekileyo osuka kwiinkundla yezoMthetho efanelekileyo. Isifungo Okanye Isingqiniso Esinyanisekileyo Kumalungu Ebhungu Loomansipala Basezidolobheni Mna ................................................ ndiyafunga[ndingqinisa ngokunyanisekileyo] okokuba ndiyakuthembeka kuLizwe laseZimbabwe nakusihlalo sokuba lilungu lebhungu lophondo [okanye ibhungu lesixeko] kuluphondo lwase, [igama loluphondo] ndiyakuwuthobela ndiwuhlonele uMgaqo-siseko kunye nawo wonke omnye uMthetho, kanjalo ndibe ngumncebisi onyanisekileyo nothembekileyo kusiHlalo [okanye uSodolophi] we [utsho igama loluphondo] ngokukhululekileyo kwasye kangangoko ndinokwazi khona ukuze ululawulo loluphondo luqhutywe ngendlela efanelekileyo; kwaye ndingaze ndiwudize nokuba kungokuthe ngqo okanye okungathanga ngqo nawuphi na umcimbi olihlebo okanye imfihlo ephathiswe mna ngethuba ndibambe lomsebenzi njengelungu lebhungu , ndize ndiyenze nemisebenzi yesikhundla sam ngenkathalo nakangangoko ndinako. Ngoko ke ndincede Thixo. [Ungatsitywa xa kungqiniswa] Esisfungo okanye isingqiniso singenziwa phambi kwegosa elikhethekileyo elisuka kwiinkundla efanelekileyo.

ISHEDYULI YESINE(Icandelo 92, 121, 124, 125 no 158) Iziqinisekiso Zabavoti Iziqinisekis ozokubhalisa njengomvoti 1. (1) Ngaphantsi komhlathana (2) nakumhlathi 2,umntu ufanelekile ukubhalisa njengomvoti kumquluwa abavoti kuphondo labavoti xa—

(a)

engaphezu kweminyaka elishumine sibhozo yokuzalwa; kwaye

(b) ngummi waseZimbabwe.

(2) uMthetho woNyulo ungamisela iimfuno ezengezelelweyo zokuhlala ukuqinisekisa ukuba abavoti babhalisile kumquluwa bavoti ofanelekileyo, kodwa naziphi iimfuno eziloluhlobo kufuneka zingqinelane noMgaqo-siseko, ingakumbi necandelo 67. Ukunqunyanyiswa ekubhaliseni njengomvoti 2. Umntu uyaqunyanyiswa ukubhalisa njengomvoti—

(a) logama eselugcinwen inokuba enengulo yengqondo okanye enengqondo ezimfitshane ngaphantsi koMthetho wasePalamente onxulumene nempilo yengqondo;

(b) xa ebhengezwe ngumyalelo wenkundla njengongewukwazi ukulawula imicimbi yakhe, kusiya ngokwexesha lomyalelo usesisinyanzeliso; okanye

(c)

xa ukewafunyaniswa enetyala ngaphantsi koMthetho woNyulo kwaye wabhengezwa yiNkundla Ephakemeyo ukuba anqunyanyiswe ukubhalisa njengomvoti okanye ukuvota, okwexesha abhengezwe njengonqunyanyisiweyo , kodwa akufuneki kubengaphezu kweminyaka emihlanu.

ISHEDYULI YESIHLANU (Amacandelo 130 no 131) Inkqubo Yamatyala Neminye Imicimbi Yasepalamente UKUHLELWA KWEMIHLATHI INXALENYE 1 Ukungeniswa Kweebhili, Izindululo Nezibongozo Umhlathi 1. Utoliko lweShedyuli yeSihlanu. 2. Indlu yemvelaphi yamatyala. 3. Amalungu avumeleke ukushenxisa iiBhili nezindululo. 4. IPalamente akufuneki idibane neeBhili nemali okanye izindululo zemali okanyeizibongozo ngaphandle kokuba loonto ivele kuSekela-Mongameli, uMpathiswa okanye uSekela Mphathiswa. INXALENYE 2 Inkqubo Malunga Neebhili 5. 6. 7.

Ukwedluliselwa kweeBhili kwiZindlu. Ukungavisisani phakathi kweZindlu. IiBhili zemali.

INXALENYE 3 8. 9.

Iingxelo zeKomoto yoMthetho yasePalamente malunga neeBhili. Iingxelo zeKomiti yoMthetho yasePalamente malunga nezixhobo zomthetho. INXALENYE 1 Ukungeniswa Kweebhili, Izindululo Nemibongozo Utoliko lwe Shedyuli yesiHlanu 1. Kule Shedyuli— “iBhili yeMali” ichaza iBhili eyenza izibonelelo zo-;

(a)

kuwisa, ukunyusa nokwehlisa irhafu ngokwenzuzo yeSizwe;

(b) kulungelanisa imali esuka, nokuwisa, ukunyusa okanye ukuthoba nokuba leliphi ixabiso le, Ngxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo, okanye nokuba yeyiphi ingxowa-mali elawulwa nguRhulumente;

(c)

ukudibanisa nokwesula amatyala esizwe;

(d) ukuxolela ukwehluleka kweSizwe ukuqokelela irhafu; okanye

(e)

ukuxolela ukusetyenziswa kwemali okungavumelekanga nguRhulumente. INdlu yemvelaphi yamatyala

1\. (1) nokuba leliphi ityala livunyelwe ukuvela kwindibano yeSizwe.

(2) nokuba leliphi ityala, elingasililo elemali, livunyelwe ukuvela eMadodeni Amalungu avumeleke ukushenxisa amatyala nezindululo 3. NgokwaloMgaqo-Siseko neziyalelo ezimisiweyo—

(a)

nokuba leliphi ilungu leSenethi lisenokuvela neBhili okanye lishukumise nokuba sesiphi isindululo ukuze kuxoxwe ngaso kwiSenethi okanye inike isibhenokwiSenethi;

(b) nokuba leliphi ilungu leNdibano yeSizwe lisenokuvela neBhili nokuba yeyiphi kwiNdibano yeSizwe okanye lishukumise nokuba sesiphi isindululo ukuze kuxoxwe ngaso kwiNdibano yeSizwe okanye linike isibongozo kwiNdibano yeSizwe;

(c)

nokuba ngowuphi usekelaMongameli, uMphathiswa okanye usekela kaMphathiswausenokuvela neBhili okanye ashukumise nokuba sesiphi isindululo ukuze kuxoxwe ngaso okanye linike isibongozo kwiNdibano yeSizwe okanye eyeSenethi;

(d) nokuba leliphi ilungu eliye lavela neBhili kwiNdlu yePalamente, ngemva kokuba iBhili inikiwe yilooNdlu, uvumelekile ukuba emvakoko ase iBhili leyo kwenye iNdlu.

IPalamente akufuneki idibanane neeBhili zemali okanye izindululo zemali okanye izibongozo ngaphandle kokuba loonto ivele kuSekelaMongameli, uMphathiswa okanye usekelaMphathiswa 4. (1) Ngaphandle kokuba uSekelaMongameli eye wawacebisana noMphathiswa okanye usekela Mphathiswa, iNdlu yePalamente iseno—

(a)

kuqhubekeka ngeBhili nokuba yeyiphi kuquka nezilungiso zeBhili, ukuba iBhili leyo yeyemali;

(b) kuqhubeka nesindululo, kuquka nesilungiso sesindululo, esebenza, ngokwezimvo zikaMongameli weSenethi okanye uSithethi, ukuba isibonelelo senziwe sisenzelwa—

(i) ukuwiswa, ukwehliswa nokunyuswa kwerhafu kusenzela iseZwe;

(ii) alungelanise imali esuka, okanye ukuwiswa, iNgxowa-mali yoNgeniso eDityanisiweyo okanye nokuba yeyiphi ingxowa-mali elawulwa nguRhulumente;

(iii) ukudibanisa okanye ukucima amatyala email eSizwe;

(iv) ukusulwa kwetyala lerhafu yeSizwe;okanye

(v) ukugxeka usetyenziso lwemali ngokungafanelekanga kukaRhulumente; okanye

(c)

ukwamkela nokuba sesiphi isibongozo, ngokwezimvo zika sekelaMongameli weSenethi okanye uSithethi, sicele ukuba izibonelelo zenziwe njengokutsho komhlathi (b).

(2) Umhlathana (1) awusetyenziswa kwiBhili eqhubekayo, izindululo okanye izilungiso ezishunyisiweyo okanye isibongozo eSinikwe nguSekelaMongameli, uMphathiswa okanye sekelMphathiswa.
